[No.1] LOKI: A Comprehensive Synthetic Data Detection Benchmark using Large Multimodal Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: LOKI：使用大型多模态模型的综合合成数据检测基准
类别: datasets and benchmarks
作者: Junyan Ye;Baichuan Zhou;Zilong Huang;Junan Zhang;Tianyi Bai;Hengrui Kang;Jun He;Honglin Lin;Zihao Wang;Tong Wu;Zhizheng Wu;Yiping Chen;Dahua Lin;Conghui He;Weijia Li;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;Shanghai Artificial Intelligence Laboratory;Chinese University of Hong Kong;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;;Stanford University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27689
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09732
英摘: With the rapid development of AI-generated content, the future internet may be inundated with synthetic data, making the discrimination of authentic and credible multimodal data increasingly challenging. Synthetic data detection has thus garnered widespread attention, and the performance of large multimodal models (LMMs) in this task has attracted significant interest. LMMs can provide natural language explanations for their authenticity judgments, enhancing the explainability of synthetic content detection. Simultaneously, the task of distinguishing between real and synthetic data effectively tests the perception, knowledge, and reasoning capabilities of LMMs. In response, we introduce LOKI, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate the ability of LMMs to detect synthetic data across multiple modalities. LOKI encompasses video, image, 3D, text, and audio modalities, comprising 18K carefully curated questions across 26 subcategories with clear difficulty levels. The benchmark includes coarse-grained judgment and multiple-choice questions, as well as fine-grained anomaly selection and explanation tasks, allowing for a comprehensive analysis of LMMs. We evaluated 22 open-source LMMs and 6 closed-source models on LOKI, highlighting their potential as synthetic data detectors and also revealing some limitations in the development of LMM capabilities. More information about LOKI can be found at https://opendatalab.github.io/LOKI/
中摘: 随着AI生成内容的迅速发展，未来的互联网可能会被合成数据淹没，使得区分真实可信的多模态数据变得越来越具有挑战性。因此，合成数据检测已经引起了广泛的关注，大型多模态模型（LMMs）在这项任务中的表现也吸引了显著的兴趣。LMMs可以为其真实性判断提供自然语言解释，增强合成内容检测的可解释性。同时，区分真实和合成数据的任务有效测试了LMMs的感知、知识和推理能力。为此，我们引入了LOKI，这是一种新的基准，旨在评估LMMs在多种模态下检测合成数据的能力。LOKI涵盖了视频、图像、3D、文本和音频等多种模态，包含26个子类别、18000多个精心策划的问题，并且具有明确的难度级别。该基准包括粗粒度判断、多项选择题，以及细粒度异常选择和解释任务，从而可以全面分析LMMs。我们在LOKI上对22个开源LMMs和6个闭源模型进行了评估，突显了它们作为合成数据检测器的潜力，同时也揭示了一些在LMM能力发展中存在的局限性。有关LOKI的更多信息，请访问https://opendatalab.github.io/LOKI/
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.2] MOS: Model Synergy for Test-Time Adaptation on LiDAR-Based 3D Object Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: MOS：基于LiDAR的3D物体检测测试时适应性模型协同
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Zhuoxiao Chen;Junjie Meng;Mahsa Baktashmotlagh;Yonggang Zhang;Zi Huang;Yadan Luo;
机构: University of Queensland;University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;Hong Kong Baptist University;
类型: Oral
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29272
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14878
英摘: LiDAR-based 3D object detection is crucial for various applications but often experiences performance degradation in real-world deployments due to domain shifts. While most studies focus on cross-dataset shifts, such as changes in environments and object geometries, practical corruptions from sensor variations and weather conditions remain underexplored. In this work, we propose a novel online test-time adaptation framework for 3D detectors that effectively tackles these shifts, including a challenging cross-corruption scenario where cross-dataset shifts and corruptions co-occur. By leveraging long-term knowledge from previous test batches, our approach mitigates catastrophic forgetting and adapts effectively to diverse shifts. Specifically, we propose a Model Synergy (MOS) strategy that dynamically selects historical checkpoints with diverse knowledge and assembles them to best accommodate the current test batch. This assembly is directed by our proposed Synergy Weights (SW), which perform a weighted averaging of the selected checkpoints, minimizing redundancy in the composite model. The SWs are computed by evaluating the similarity of predicted bounding boxes on the test data and the independence of features between checkpoint pairs in the model bank. To maintain an efficient and informative model bank, we discard checkpoints with the lowest average SW scores, replacing them with newly updated models. Our method was rigorously tested against existing test-time adaptation strategies across three datasets and eight types of corruptions, demonstrating superior adaptability to dynamic scenes and conditions. Notably, it achieved a 67.3% improvement in a challenging cross-corruption scenario, offering a more comprehensive benchmark for adaptation. Source code: https://github.com/zhuoxiao-chen/MOS.
中摘: 基于LiDAR的三维物体检测在各种应用中至关重要，但在实际部署中往往由于领域转移而性能下降。虽然大多数研究关注的是跨数据集的转移，如环境和物体几何形状的变化，但来自传感器变化和天气条件的实际干扰仍然被忽视。在本工作中，我们提出了一种新颖的在线测试时自适应框架，该框架能够有效应对这些转移，包括一个具有挑战性的跨干扰场景，其中跨数据集转移和干扰同时发生。通过利用先前测试批次中的长期知识，我们的方法减轻了灾难性遗忘，并能够有效地适应各种转移。具体而言，我们提出了一个模型协同（MOS）策略，该策略动态选择具有多样知识的历史检查点，并将它们组装起来以最好地适应当前的测试批次。这种组装由我们提出的协同权重（SW）指导，SW执行所选检查点的加权平均，从而最小化复合模型中的冗余。SWs是通过评估测试数据上预测边界框的相似性和模型银行中检查点对之间的特征独立性来计算的。为了保持高效且信息丰富的模型库，我们丢弃SW得分最低的历史检查点，用新更新的模型替换它们。我们的方法在三个数据集和八种类型的干扰下与现有的测试时自适应策略进行了严格的对比测试，展示了在动态场景和条件下更出色的自适应能力。值得注意的是，在一个具有挑战性的跨干扰场景中，它实现了67.3%的性能提升，为自适应提供了一个更全面的基准。源代码：https://github.com/zhuoxiao-chen/MOS。

[No.3] Min-K%++: Improved Baseline for Pre-Training Data Detection from Large Language Models

类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Jingyang Zhang;Jingwei Sun;Eric Yeats;Yang Ouyang;Martin Kuo;Jianyi Zhang;Hao Frank Yang;Hai Li;

[No.4] GOLD: Graph Out-of-Distribution Detection via Implicit Adversarial Latent Generation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: GOLD：基于隐式对抗潜在生成的图域外检测
类别: learning on graphs and other geometries & topologies
作者: Danny Wang;Ruihong Qiu;Guangdong Bai;Zi Huang;
机构: University of Queensland;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27751
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05780
英摘: Despite graph neural networks' (GNNs) great success in modelling graph-structured data, out-of-distribution (OOD) test instances still pose a great challenge for current GNNs. One of the most effective techniques to detect OOD nodes is to expose the detector model with an additional OOD node-set, yet the extra OOD instances are often difficult to obtain in practice. Recent methods for image data address this problem using OOD data synthesis, typically relying on pre-trained generative models like Stable Diffusion. However, these approaches require vast amounts of additional data, as well as one-for-all pre-trained generative models, which are not available for graph data. Therefore, we propose the GOLD framework for graph OOD detection, an implicit adversarial learning pipeline with synthetic OOD exposure without pre-trained models. The implicit adversarial training process employs a novel alternating optimisation framework by training: (1) a latent generative model to regularly imitate the in-distribution (ID) embeddings from an evolving GNN, and (2) a GNN encoder and an OOD detector to accurately classify ID data while increasing the energy divergence between the ID embeddings and the generative model's synthetic embeddings. This novel approach implicitly transforms the synthetic embeddings into pseudo-OOD instances relative to the ID data, effectively simulating exposure to OOD scenarios without auxiliary data. Extensive OOD detection experiments are conducted on five benchmark graph datasets, verifying the superior performance of GOLD without using real OOD data compared with the state-of-the-art OOD exposure and non-exposure baselines.
中摘: 尽管图神经网络（GNNs）在建模图结构数据方面取得了巨大成功，但当前的GNNs仍难以应对离分布（OOD）测试实例带来的巨大挑战。检测OOD节点最有效的方法之一是向检测器模型暴露额外的OOD节点集，然而在实践中，获取额外的OOD实例往往非常困难。针对图像数据，最近的方法通过使用OOD数据合成来解决这一问题，通常依赖于预训练生成模型，如Stable Diffusion。然而，这些方法需要大量的额外数据以及适用于所有场景的预训练生成模型，而这些资源对于图数据来说并不可用。因此，我们提出了GOLD框架用于图结构的OOD检测，这是一种无需预训练模型的隐式对抗学习管道，通过合成OOD暴露来进行OOD检测。该隐式对抗训练过程采用一种新颖的交替优化框架：（1）训练一个潜在生成模型定期模仿进化中的GNN产生的ID嵌入；（2）训练GNN编码器和OOD检测器以准确分类ID数据，并增加ID嵌入与生成模型合成嵌入之间的能量差异。这种新颖的方法隐式地将合成嵌入转换为相对于ID数据的伪OOD实例，从而有效地模拟了OOD场景的暴露，而无需辅助数据。我们在五个基准图数据集上进行了广泛的OOD检测实验，验证了GOLD在无需使用真实OOD数据的情况下，相较于最先进的OOD暴露和非暴露基线方法具有更优的表现。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.6] ReDeEP: Detecting Hallucination in Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Mechanistic Interpretability
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: ReDeEP：通过机理可解释性检测检索增强生成中的幻觉
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: ZhongXiang Sun;Xiaoxue Zang;Kai Zheng;Jun Xu;Xiao Zhang;Weijie Yu;Yang Song;Han Li;
机构: Renmin University of China;;Kuaishou Technology;University of International Business and Economics;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27644
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11414
英摘: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) models are designed to incorporate external knowledge, reducing hallucinations caused by insufficient parametric (internal) knowledge. However, even with accurate and relevant retrieved content, RAG models can still produce hallucinations by generating outputs that conflict with the retrieved information. Detecting such hallucinations requires disentangling how Large Language Models (LLMs) utilize external and parametric knowledge. Current detection methods often focus on one of these mechanisms or without decoupling their intertwined effects, making accurate detection difficult. In this paper, we investigate the internal mechanisms behind hallucinations in RAG scenarios. We discover hallucinations occur when the Knowledge FFNs in LLMs overemphasize parametric knowledge in the residual stream, while Copying Heads fail to effectively retain or integrate external knowledge from retrieved content. Based on these findings, we propose ReDeEP, a novel method that detects hallucinations by decoupling LLM's utilization of external context and parametric knowledge. Our experiments show that ReDeEP significantly improves RAG hallucination detection accuracy. Additionally, we introduce AARF, which mitigates hallucinations by modulating the contributions of Knowledge FFNs and Copying Heads.
中摘: 检索增强生成（RAG）模型设计用于整合外部知识，从而减少因内部参数化知识不足而引起的幻觉。然而，即使使用准确且相关的检索内容，RAG模型仍然可以通过生成与检索信息相冲突的输出来产生幻觉。检测这些幻觉需要解开大型语言模型（LLMs）如何利用外部和参数化知识的内部机制。当前的检测方法往往侧重于这两种机制中的一个，或者未能分离它们交织的效果，使得准确检测变得困难。在本文中，我们研究了RAG场景下幻觉背后的内部机制。我们发现，当LLMs中的知识FFNs在残差流中过分强调参数化知识，而复制头无法有效保留或整合检索内容中的外部知识时，幻觉就会发生。基于这些发现，我们提出了ReDeEP，这是一种新颖的方法，通过解耦LLMs对外部上下文和参数化知识的利用来检测幻觉。我们的实验表明，ReDeEP显著提高了RAG幻觉检测的准确性。此外，我们引入了AARF，该方法通过调节知识FFNs和复制头的贡献来减轻幻觉。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.7] The Power of LLM-Generated Synthetic Data for Stance Detection in Online Political Discussions
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: LLM生成的合成数据在在线政治讨论立场检测中的力量
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Stefan Sylvius Wagner;Maike Behrendt;Marc Ziegele;Stefan Harmeling;
机构: Heinrich-Heine-Universität Düsseldorf;Heinrich-Heine Universität;Technische Universität Dortmund;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27816
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12480
英摘: Stance detection holds great potential to improve online political discussions through its deployment in discussion platforms for purposes such as content moderation, topic summarization or to facilitate more balanced discussions. Typically, transformer-based models are employed directly for stance detection, requiring vast amounts of data. However, the wide variety of debate topics in online political discussions makes data collection particularly challenging. LLMs have revived stance detection, but their online deployment in online political discussions faces challenges like inconsistent outputs, biases, and vulnerability to adversarial attacks. We show how LLM-generated synthetic data can improve stance detection for online political discussions by using reliable traditional stance detection models for online deployment, while leveraging the text generation capabilities of LLMs for synthetic data generation in a secure offline environment. To achieve this, (i) we generate synthetic data for specific debate questions by prompting a Mistral-7B model and show that fine-tuning with the generated synthetic data can substantially improve the performance of stance detection, while remaining interpretable and aligned with real world data. (ii) Using the synthetic data as a reference, we can improve performance even further by identifying the most informative samples in an unlabelled dataset, i.e., those samples which the stance detection model is most uncertain about and can benefit from the most. By fine-tuning with both synthetic data and the most informative samples, we surpass the performance of the baseline model that is fine-tuned on all true labels, while labelling considerably less data.
中摘: 立场检测在通过将其部署在讨论平台上以改进在线政治讨论方面具有巨大的潜力，用于内容审核、主题总结或促进更加平衡的讨论。通常，会直接使用基于变换器的模型进行立场检测，但这种模型需要大量的数据。然而，在线政治讨论中广泛多样的辩论话题使得数据收集尤为困难。尽管大语言模型（LLMs）重新激活了立场检测的应用，但在在线政治讨论中的部署仍面临挑战，如不一致的输出、偏见以及对抗性攻击的风险。我们展示了如何通过利用可靠的传统立场检测模型进行在线部署，并借助大语言模型的文本生成能力在安全的离线环境中生成合成数据，来提高在线政治讨论中的立场检测效果。（i）我们通过提示Mistral-7B模型生成特定辩论问题的合成数据，并证明使用生成的合成数据进行微调可以显著提升立场检测的表现，同时保持可解释性和与真实世界数据的一致性。（ii）利用合成数据作为参考，我们可以进一步提高性能，通过识别未标记数据集中最有信息量的样本，即立场检测模型最不确定且从中受益最多的样本。通过使用合成数据和最有信息量的样本进行微调，我们超越了仅在所有真实标签上进行微调的基础模型的表现，同时大大减少了需要标注的数据量。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.8] Union-over-Intersections: Object Detection beyond Winner-Takes-All
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 并集超越交集：超越赢家通吃的人工智能对象检测
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Aritra Bhowmik;Pascal Mettes;Martin R. Oswald;Cees G. M. Snoek;
机构: University of Amsterdam;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30208
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.18512
英摘: This paper revisits the problem of predicting box locations in object detection architectures. Typically, each box proposal or box query aims to directly maximize the intersection-over-union score with the ground truth, followed by a winner-takes-all non-maximum suppression where only the highest scoring box in each region is retained. We observe that both steps are sub-optimal: the first involves regressing proposals to the entire ground truth, which is a difficult task even with large receptive fields, and the second neglects valuable information from boxes other than the top candidate. Instead of regressing proposals to the whole ground truth, we propose a simpler approach: regress only to the area of intersection between the proposal and the ground truth. This avoids the need for proposals to extrapolate beyond their visual scope, improving localization accuracy. Rather than adopting a winner-takes-all strategy, we take the union over the regressed intersections of all boxes in a region to generate the final box outputs. Our plug-and-play method integrates seamlessly into proposal-based, grid-based, and query-based detection architectures with minimal modifications, consistently improving object localization and instance segmentation. We demonstrate its broad applicability and versatility across various detection and segmentation tasks.
中摘: 本文重新审视了物体检测架构中预测边界框位置的问题。通常，每个边界框建议或查询都旨在直接最大化其与真实值的交并比得分，随后通过“得胜者全拿”的非极大值抑制策略，仅保留每个区域得分最高的边界框。我们观察到这两个步骤都是次优的：第一步涉及将建议框回归到整个真实值上，即使具有大的感受野，这也是一个困难的任务；第二步忽略了除顶级候选框外其他框中的有价值信息。我们不将建议框回归到整个真实值上，而是提出一种更简单的做法：仅将建议框回归到其与真实值的交集区域。这避免了建议框需要超出其视觉范围进行外推，从而提高定位准确性。我们不再采用“得胜者全拿”的策略，而是将一个区域内所有框的回归交集取并集来生成最终的框输出。我们的即插即用方法能够无缝集成到基于建议、基于网格和基于查询的检测架构中，并且只需进行少量修改即可一致地提升物体定位和实例分割性能。我们展示了该方法在各种检测和分割任务中的广泛适用性和灵活性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.9] PointOBB-v2: Towards Simpler, Faster, and Stronger Single Point Supervised Oriented Object Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 点OBB-v2：向着更简单、更快、更强的单点监督定向物体检测
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Botao Ren;Xue Yang;Yi Yu;Junwei Luo;Zhidong Deng;
机构: Tsinghua University;Shanghai AI Laboratory;Southeast University;Wuhan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29665
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08210
英摘: Single point supervised oriented object detection has gained attention and made initial progress within the community. Diverse from those approaches relying on one-shot samples or powerful pretrained models (e.g. SAM), PointOBB has shown promise due to its prior-free feature. In this paper, we propose PointOBB-v2, a simpler, faster, and stronger method to generate pseudo rotated boxes from points without relying on any other prior. Specifically, we first generate a Class Probability Map (CPM) by training the network with non-uniform positive and negative sampling. We show that the CPM is able to learn the approximate object regions and their contours. Then, Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is applied to accurately estimate the orientation and the boundary of objects. By further incorporating a separation mechanism, we resolve the confusion caused by the overlapping on the CPM, enabling its operation in high-density scenarios. Extensive comparisons demonstrate that our method achieves a training speed 15.58x faster and an accuracy improvement of 11.60%/25.15%/21.19% on the DOTA-v1.0/v1.5/v2.0 datasets compared to the previous state-of-the-art, PointOBB. This significantly advances the cutting edge of single point supervised oriented detection in the modular track.
中摘: 单点监督导向对象检测已引起关注并取得了初步进展。不同于依赖单一示例或强大预训练模型（如SAM）的方法，PointOBB由于其无先验特征显示出潜力。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为PointOBB-v2的新方法，该方法通过训练网络生成非均匀正负样本的Class Probability Map (CPM)，从而生成伪旋转框，且无需依赖任何其他先验信息。具体而言，我们首先通过非均匀正负样本训练网络生成一个类概率图（CPM），展示了CPM能够学习对象的大致区域及其轮廓。然后，应用主成分分析（PCA）来准确估计对象的方向和边界。此外，通过引入分离机制，解决了CPM上重叠造成的混淆问题，使其能够在高密度场景下正常工作。广泛比较表明，我们的方法相比之前的最佳方法PointOBB，在DOTA-v1.0/v1.5/v2.0数据集上的训练速度提高了15.58倍，并且精度分别提高了11.60%、25.15%和21.19%。这显著推进了模块化轨道中单点监督导向检测的技术前沿。

[No.10] Black-Box Detection of Language Model Watermarks
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 黑盒检测语言模型水印
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Thibaud Gloaguen;Nikola Jovanović;Robin Staab;Martin Vechev;
机构: ETH Zurich;Swiss Federal Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30423
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20777
英摘: Watermarking has emerged as a promising way to detect LLM-generated text, by augmenting LLM generations with later detectable signals. Recent work has proposed multiple families of watermarking schemes, several of which focus on preserving the LLM distribution. This distribution-preservation property is motivated by the fact that it is a tractable proxy for retaining LLM capabilities, as well as the inherently implied undetectability of the watermark by downstream users. Yet, despite much discourse around undetectability, no prior work has investigated the practical detectability of any of the current watermarking schemes in a realistic black-box setting. In this work we tackle this for the first time, developing rigorous statistical tests to detect the presence, and estimate parameters, of all three popular watermarking scheme families, using only a limited number of black-box queries. We experimentally confirm the effectiveness of our methods on a range of schemes and a diverse set of open-source models. Further, we validate the feasibility of our tests on real-world APIs. Our findings indicate that current watermarking schemes are more detectable than previously believed.
中摘: 水印技术已成为检测由大型语言模型（LLM）生成的文本的一种有前途的方法，通过在LLM生成的内容中添加后期可检测的信号来实现。近期的研究提出了多种水印方案家族，其中一些方案专注于保持LLM的分布特性。这种保持分布的特性是由保留LLM能力的事实以及水印对下游用户而言内在的不可检测性所驱动的。然而，尽管有关不可检测性的讨论很多，但之前的研究并未在实际的黑盒环境中调查任何当前水印方案的实际可检测性。在这项工作中，我们首次解决了这一问题，开发了严格的统计测试方法，仅使用有限数量的黑盒查询即可检测所有三种流行的水印方案家族的存在，并估计其参数。我们在一系列方案和多种开源模型上进行了实验性验证，进一步证明了我们的测试方法在真实世界API上的可行性。我们的研究发现表明，当前的水印方案比先前认为的更具可检测性。

[No.11] I Can Hear You: Selective Robust Training for Deepfake Audio Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 我能听到你：选择性鲁棒训练在深度假音音频检测中的应用
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Zirui Zhang;Wei Hao;Aroon Sankoh;William Lin;Emanuel Mendiola-Ortiz;Junfeng Yang;Chengzhi Mao;
机构: Columbia University;Washington University in St. Louis;New York University;Pennsylvania State University;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/32111
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00121
英摘: Recent advances in AI-generated voices have intensified the challenge of detecting deepfake audio, posing risks for scams and the spread of disinformation. To tackle this issue, we establish the largest public voice dataset to date, named DeepFakeVox-HQ, comprising 1.3 million samples, including 270,000 high-quality deepfake samples from 14 diverse sources. Despite previously reported high accuracy, existing deepfake voice detectors struggle with our diversely collected dataset, and their detection success rates drop even further under realistic corruptions and adversarial attacks. We conduct a holistic investigation into factors that enhance model robustness and show that incorporating a diversified set of voice augmentations is beneficial. Moreover, we find that the best detection models often rely on high-frequency features, which are imperceptible to humans and can be easily manipulated by an attacker. To address this, we propose the F-SAT: Frequency-Selective Adversarial Training method focusing on high-frequency components. Empirical results demonstrate that using our training dataset boosts baseline model performance (without robust training) by 33%, and our robust training further improves accuracy by 7.7% on clean samples and by 29.3% on corrupted and attacked samples, over the state-of-the-art RawNet3 model.
中摘: 最近在人工智能生成声音方面的进展加剧了检测深度伪造音频的挑战，带来了诈骗和虚假信息传播的风险。为应对这一问题，我们建立了迄今为止最大的公共语音数据集，名为DeepFakeVox-HQ，包含130万样本，其中包括来自14个不同来源的27万个高质量深度伪造样本。尽管之前报道的准确率很高，现有的深度伪造语音检测器在我们的多样化数据集中表现挣扎，并且在现实中的损坏和对抗攻击下，其检测成功率进一步下降。我们对增强模型鲁棒性的因素进行了全面调查，并表明引入多样化的语音增强是有益的。此外，我们发现最佳检测模型往往依赖于高频特征，这些特征对人类不可感知，且容易被攻击者操控。为解决这一问题，我们提出了F-SAT：频率选择性对抗训练方法，专注于高频成分。实验证明，使用我们的训练数据集可以提升基准模型（未进行鲁棒训练）的表现33%，而我们的鲁棒训练方法在干净样本上进一步提高了准确性7.7%，在受损和攻击样本上则提高了29.3%，超过了最先进的RawNet3模型。

英摘: We present Uni$^2$Det, a brand new framework for unified and universal multi-dataset training on 3D detection, enabling robust performance across diverse domains and generalization to unseen domains. Due to substantial disparities in data distribution and variations in taxonomy across diverse domains, training such a detector by simply merging datasets poses a significant challenge. Motivated by this observation, we introduce multi-stage prompting modules for multi-dataset 3D detection, which leverages prompts based on the characteristics of corresponding datasets to mitigate existing differences. This elegant design facilitates seamless plug-and-play integration within various advanced 3D detection frameworks in a unified manner, while also allowing straightforward adaptation for universal applicability across datasets. 中摘: 我们提出了一种名为Uni$^2$Det的新框架，用于统一和通用的多数据集3D检测训练，能够在多种领域中实现稳健性能，并且能够泛化到未见过的领域。由于不同领域之间数据分布和分类学上的显著差异，简单地合并数据集来训练这样的检测器会面临重大挑战。基于这一观察，我们引入了多阶段提示模块，用于多数据集3D检测，该模块利用基于相应数据集特性的提示来缓解现有差异。这种优雅的设计使Uni$^2$Det能够在各种先进的3D检测框架中无缝集成，并且允许其方便地适应跨数据集的通用应用。我们在涉及KITTI、Waymo和nuScenes等多个数据集整合场景下进行了实验，结果显示我们的Uni$^2$Det在多数据集训练中的表现远超现有方法。值得注意的是，零样本跨数据集迁移的结果验证了我们所提出方法的泛化能力。

[No.13] State Space Model Meets Transformer: A New Paradigm for 3D Object Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 状态空间模型遇见变换器：三维物体检测的新范式
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: ChuXin Wang;Wenfei Yang;Xiang Liu;Tianzhu Zhang;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Dongguan University of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29524
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14493
英摘: DETR-based methods, which use multi-layer transformer decoders to refine object queries iteratively, have shown promising performance in 3D indoor object detection. However, the scene point features in the transformer decoder remain fixed, leading to minimal contributions from later decoder layers, thereby limiting performance improvement. Recently, State Space Models (SSM) have shown efficient context modeling ability with linear complexity through iterative interactions between system states and inputs. Inspired by SSMs, we propose a new 3D object DEtection paradigm with an interactive STate space model (DEST). In the interactive SSM, we design a novel state-dependent SSM parameterization method that enables system states to effectively serve as queries in 3D indoor detection tasks. In addition, we introduce four key designs tailored to the characteristics of point cloud and SSM: The serialization and bidirectional scanning strategies enable bidirectional feature interaction among scene points within the SSM. The inter-state attention mechanism models the relationships between state points, while the gated feed-forward network enhances inter-channel correlations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first method to model queries as system states and scene points as system inputs, which can simultaneously update scene point features and query features with linear complexity. Extensive experiments on two challenging datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our DEST-based method. Our method improves the GroupFree baseline in terms of AP50 on ScanNet V2 (+5.3) and SUN RGB-D (+3.2) datasets. Based on the VDETR baseline, Our method sets a new SOTA on the ScanNetV2 and SUN RGB-D datasets.
中摘: 基于DETR的方法通过迭代优化多层变压器解码器的对象查询，已经在室内三维物体检测任务中展现了令人鼓舞的性能。然而，变压器解码器中的场景点特征保持不变，导致后续解码器层的贡献微乎其微，从而限制了性能的提升。最近，状态空间模型（SSM）因其在通过系统状态和输入的迭代交互来实现线性复杂度的有效上下文建模能力而受到关注。受SSM的启发，我们提出了一种新的三维物体检测范式——交互式状态空间模型（DEST）。在交互式SSM中，我们设计了一种新颖的状态依赖参数化方法，使系统状态能够有效地作为查询用于室内三维检测任务。此外，我们引入了四种针对点云和SSM特性的关键设计：序列化和双向扫描策略在SSM内部实现了场景点之间的双向特征交互。状态间注意力机制建模了状态点之间的关系，而门控前馈网络则增强了跨通道的相关性。据我们所知，这是首次将查询参数化为系统状态，并将场景点作为系统输入的方法，能够在线性复杂度下同时更新场景点特征和查询特征。在两个具有挑战性的数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，我们的DEST基方法是有效的。与GroupFree基线相比，我们的方法在ScanNet V2数据集上的AP50指标提高了5.3%，在SUN RGB-D数据集上提高了3.2%。基于VDETR基线，我们的方法在ScanNetV2和SUN RGB-D数据集上均达到了新的最佳性能。

[No.14] UV-Attack: Physical-World Adversarial Attacks on Person Detection via Dynamic-NeRF-based UV Mapping
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: UV-攻击：基于Dynamic-NeRF的UV映射对人员检测的物理世界 adversarial 攻击
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Yanjie Li;Kaisheng Liang;Bin Xiao;
机构: Hong Kong Polytechnic University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28278
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.05783
英摘: In recent research, adversarial attacks on person detectors using patches or static 3D model-based texture modifications have struggled with low success rates due to the flexible nature of human movement. Modeling the 3D deformations caused by various actions has been a major challenge. Fortunately, advancements in Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) for dynamic human modeling offer new possibilities. In this paper, we introduce UV-Attack, a groundbreaking approach that achieves high success rates even with extensive and unseen human actions. We address the challenge above by leveraging dynamic-NeRF-based UV mapping. UV-Attack can generate human images across diverse actions and viewpoints, and even create novel actions by sampling from the SMPL parameter space. While dynamic NeRF models are capable of modeling human bodies, modifying clothing textures is challenging because they are embedded in neural network parameters. To tackle this, UV-Attack generates UV maps instead of RGB images and modifies the texture stacks. This approach enables real-time texture edits and makes the attack more practical. We also propose a novel Expectation over Pose Transformation loss (EoPT) to improve the evasion success rate on unseen poses and views. Our experiments show that UV-Attack achieves a 92.7% attack success rate against the FastRCNN model across varied poses in dynamic video settings, significantly outperforming the state-of-the-art AdvCamou attack, which only had a 28.5% ASR. Moreover, we achieve 49.5% ASR on the latest YOLOv8 detector in black-box settings. This work highlights the potential of dynamic NeRF-based UV mapping for creating more effective adversarial attacks on person detectors, addressing key challenges in modeling human movement and texture modification. The code is available at https://github.com/PolyLiYJ/UV-Attack.
中摘: 近年来的研究发现，针对使用补丁或静态3D模型基底纹理修改的人体检测器发起的对抗攻击，由于人体动作的灵活多变性，成功率普遍较低。各种动作引起的3D变形建模是一个主要挑战。幸运的是，神经辐射场（NeRF）在动态人体建模方面的进步为这一问题提供了新的解决方案。本文中，我们提出了UV-Attack这一开创性的方法，即使面对广泛的、前所未见的人体动作，也能实现高成功率的攻击。我们通过利用基于动态NeRF的UV映射来应对上述挑战。UV-Attack可以生成跨越多种动作和视角的人体图像，并且可以通过采样SMPL参数空间来创建新颖的动作。尽管动态NeRF模型能够建模人体，但修改服装纹理仍然具有挑战性，因为它们嵌入在神经网络参数中。为此，UV-Attack生成UV图而不是RGB图像，并修改纹理堆栈。这种方法允许实时纹理编辑，使攻击更加实用。此外，我们还提出了一种新颖的姿态变换期望损失（EoPT），以提高在未见过的姿态和视角下的逃避成功率。我们的实验表明，在动态视频设置下，UV-Attack对FastRCNN模型实现了92.7%的攻击成功率，显著优于仅在未见过的姿态和视角下有28.5%逃避成功率的AdvCamou攻击。同时，在黑盒设置下，UV-Attack对最新的YOLOv8检测器也实现了49.5%的逃避成功率。这项工作突显了基于动态NeRF的UV映射在创建更有效的针对人体检测器的对抗攻击方面的潜力，解决了建模人体运动和纹理修改的关键挑战。代码可在https://github.com/PolyLiYJ/UV-Attack获取。

[No.15] DECO: Unleashing the Potential of ConvNets for Query-based Detection and Segmentation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: DECO：挖掘基于查询检测与分割的卷积神经网络潜力
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Xinghao Chen;Siwei Li;Yijing Yang;Yunhe Wang;
机构: Huawei;ByteDance;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29537
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.13735
英摘: Transformer and its variants have shown great potential for various vision tasks in recent years, including image classification, object detection and segmentation. Meanwhile, recent studies also reveal that with proper architecture design, convolutional networks (ConvNets) also achieve competitive performance with transformers. However, no prior methods have explored to utilize pure convolution to build a Transformer-style Decoder module, which is essential for Encoder-Decoder architecture like Detection Transformer (DETR). To this end, in this paper we explore whether we could build query-based detection and segmentation framework with ConvNets instead of sophisticated transformer architecture. We propose a novel mechanism dubbed InterConv to perform interaction between object queries and image features via convolutional layers. Equipped with the proposed InterConv, we build Detection ConvNet (DECO), which is composed of a backbone and convolutional encoder-decoder architecture. We compare the proposed DECO against prior detectors on the challenging COCO benchmark. Despite its simplicity, our DECO achieves competitive performance in terms of detection accuracy and running speed. Specifically, with the ResNet-18 and ResNet-50 backbone, our DECO achieves $40.5\%$ and $47.8\%$ AP with $66$ and $34$ FPS, respectively. The proposed method is also evaluated on the segment anything task, demonstrating similar performance and higher efficiency. We hope the proposed method brings another perspective for designing architectures for vision tasks. Codes are available at https://github.com/xinghaochen/DECO and https://github.com/mindspore-lab/models/tree/master/research/huawei-noah/DECO.
中摘: 近年来，变换器及其变体在各种视觉任务中展现了巨大的潜力，包括图像分类、目标检测和分割。同时，最近的研究也揭示，在适当的架构设计下，卷积网络（ConvNets）也能与变换器相媲美，达到竞争性的性能。然而，此前没有任何方法探索如何利用纯粹的卷积来构建变换器风格的解码器模块，这对于编码器-解码器架构如检测变换器（DETR）至关重要。为此，本文探讨是否可以使用ConvNets替代复杂的变换器架构来构建基于查询的目标检测和分割框架。为此，我们提出了一种新颖机制，称为InterConv，通过卷积层实现对象查询与图像特征之间的交互。借助所提出的InterConv，我们构建了Detection ConvNet（DECO），该模型由骨干网络和卷积编码-解码架构组成。我们将所提出的DECO与先前的检测器在具有挑战性的COCO基准上进行了比较。尽管结构简单，我们的DECO在检测准确性和运行速度方面仍能达到竞争性表现。具体来说，使用ResNet-18和ResNet-50骨干网络时，DECO分别实现了66 FPS和34 FPS下的40.5%和47.8%的AP值。此外，所提出的方法还在分割一切任务中进行了评估，显示了类似的表现并具有更高的效率。我们希望所提出的方法能为设计视觉任务架构提供另一种视角。代码可在https://github.com/xinghaochen/DECO和https://github.com/mindspore-lab/models/tree/master/research/huawei-noah/DECO获取。

[No.18] Outlier Synthesis via Hamiltonian Monte Carlo for Out-of-Distribution Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于哈密尔顿蒙特卡洛的离群点合成方法用于分布外检测
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Hengzhuang Li;Teng Zhang;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29899
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.16718
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is crucial for developing trustworthy and reliable machine learning systems. Recent advances in training with auxiliary OOD data demonstrate efficacy in enhancing detection capabilities. Nonetheless, these methods heavily rely on acquiring a large pool of high-quality natural outliers. Some prior methods try to alleviate this problem by synthesizing virtual outliers but suffer from either poor quality or high cost due to the monotonous sampling strategy and the heavy-parameterized generative models. In this paper, we overcome all these problems by proposing the Hamiltonian Monte Carlo Outlier Synthesis (HamOS) framework, which views the synthesis process as sampling from Markov chains. Based solely on the in-distribution data, the Markov chains can extensively traverse the feature space and generate diverse and representative outliers, hence exposing the model to miscellaneous potential OOD scenarios. The Hamiltonian Monte Carlo with sampling acceptance rate almost close to 1 also makes our framework enjoy great efficiency. By empirically competing with SOTA baselines on both standard and large-scale benchmarks, we verify the efficacy and efficiency of our proposed HamOS.
中摘: 分布外（OOD）检测对于开发可信赖且可靠的机器学习系统至关重要。最近在使用辅助OOD数据训练方面的进展表明，这些方法能够有效提升检测能力。然而，这些方法严重依赖于获取大量高质量的自然异常样本。一些先前的方法试图通过合成虚拟异常样本来缓解这一问题，但由于单调采样策略和参数量大的生成模型，这些方法要么质量较差，要么成本较高。本文通过提出Hamiltonian蒙特卡洛异常合成（HamOS）框架解决了这些问题，该框架将合成过程视为从马尔可夫链中进行采样。仅基于分布内数据，马尔可夫链可以广泛遍历特征空间并生成多样且具有代表性的异常样本，从而让模型接触到各种潜在的OOD场景。Hamiltonian蒙特卡洛采样的接受率接近1也使得我们的框架具备很高的效率。通过在标准和大规模基准上与当前最先进的基线方法进行实证比较，我们验证了所提出的HamOS的有效性和效率。

[No.19] Pursuing Feature Separation based on Neural Collapse for Out-of-Distribution Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于神经坍缩追求特征分离以进行分布外检测
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Yingwen Wu;Ruiji Yu;Xinwen Cheng;Zhengbao He;Xiaolin Huang;
机构: ;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28462
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17816
英摘: In the open world, detecting out-of-distribution (OOD) data, whose labels are disjoint with those of in-distribution (ID) samples, is important for reliable deep neural networks (DNNs). To achieve better detection performance, one type of approach proposes to fine-tune the model with auxiliary OOD datasets to amplify the difference between ID and OOD data through a separation loss defined on model outputs. However, none of these studies consider enlarging the feature disparity, which should be more effective compared to outputs. The main difficulty lies in the diversity of OOD samples, which makes it hard to describe their feature distribution, let alone design losses to separate them from ID features. In this paper, we neatly fence off the problem based on an aggregation property of ID features named Neural Collapse (NC). NC means that the penultimate features of ID samples within a class are nearly identical to the last layer weight of the corresponding class. Based on this property, we propose a simple but effective loss called Separation Loss, which binds the features of OOD data in a subspace orthogonal to the principal subspace of ID features formed by NC. In this way, the features of ID and OOD samples are separated by different dimensions. By optimizing the feature separation loss rather than purely enlarging output differences, our detection achieves SOTA performance on CIFAR10, CIFAR100 and ImageNet benchmarks without any additional data augmentation or sampling, demonstrating the importance of feature separation in OOD detection. Code is available at https://github.com/Wuyingwen/Pursuing-Feature-Separation-for-OOD-Detection.
中摘: 在开放世界中，检测分布外（OOD）数据对于可靠的深度神经网络（DNNs）至关重要。这些数据的标签与分布内（ID）样本的标签不相交，因此通过定义在模型输出上的分离损失来放大ID和OOD数据之间的差异的一种方法可以实现更好的检测性能。然而，这些研究均未考虑扩大特征差异的问题，而这一点相比直接扩大输出差异可能更为有效。主要难点在于OOD样本的多样性，这使得描述其特征分布变得困难，更不用说设计损失函数来将它们与ID特征区分开来。在这篇论文中，我们基于一种名为神经坍缩（NC）的ID特征聚合性质巧妙地解决了这一问题。NC表示，在一个类内的倒数第二层特征几乎与对应类的最后一层权重相同。基于这一性质，我们提出了一种简单但有效的损失函数——分离损失，该损失函数将OOD数据的特征绑定在一个子空间中，这个子空间与由NC形成的ID特征的主要子空间正交。这样，ID和OOD样本的特征可以通过不同的维度进行分离。通过优化特征分离损失而非单纯扩大输出差异，我们的检测在CIFAR10、CIFAR100和ImageNet基准测试中达到了SOTA性能，且无需任何额外的数据增强或采样，证明了特征分离在OOD检测中的重要性。相关代码可在https://github.com/Wuyingwen/Pursuing-Feature-Separation-for-OOD-Detection获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.20] FakeShield: Explainable Image Forgery Detection and Localization via Multi-modal Large Language Models
来源: ICLR 2025

标题: FakeShield：通过多模态大型语言模型进行可解释的图像伪造检测与定位

英摘: The rapid development of generative AI is a double-edged sword, which not only facilitates content creation but also makes image manipulation easier and more difficult to detect. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FakeShield effectively detects and localizes various tampering techniques, offering an explainable and superior solution compared to previous IFDL methods. The code is available at https://github.com/zhipeixu/FakeShield.
中摘: 生成式AI的快速发展是一把双刃剑，它不仅促进了内容创作，还使得图像操控变得更加容易且难以检测。尽管当前的图像伪造检测与定位（IFDL）方法通常效果显著，但它们往往面临两大挑战：\textbf{1)} 黑盒性质，其检测原理不明，\textbf{2)} 对多样化的篡改方法（如Photoshop、DeepFake、AIGC编辑）缺乏广泛的泛化能力。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了可解释的IFDL任务，并设计了FakeShield，这是一个多模态框架，能够评估图像的真实性、生成篡改区域掩码，并基于像素级和图像级篡改线索提供判断依据。此外，我们利用GPT-4o增强现有的IFDL数据集，创建了多模态篡改描述数据集（MMTD-Set），用于训练FakeShield的篡改分析能力。同时，我们整合了一个领域标签引导的可解释伪造检测模块（DTE-FDM）和一个多模态伪造定位模块（MFLM），以应对各种类型的篡改检测解释，并通过详细的文本描述实现基于细节的伪造定位。广泛实验表明，FakeShield能够有效检测和定位多种篡改技术，相比之前的IFDL方法，提供了更加可解释和优越的解决方案。相关代码可在https://github.com/zhipeixu/FakeShield获取。

[No.21] R2Det: Exploring Relaxed Rotation Equivariance in 2D Object Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: R2Det：探索二维物体检测中的放松旋转不变性
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Zhiqiang Wu;Yingjie Liu;Hanlin Dong;Xuan Tang;Jian Yang;Bo Jin;Mingsong Chen;Xian Wei;
机构: East China Normal University;;Technische Universität München;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30402
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11760
英摘: Group Equivariant Convolution (GConv) empowers models to explore underlying symmetry in data, improving performance. However, real-world scenarios often deviate from ideal symmetric systems caused by physical permutation, characterized by non-trivial actions of a symmetry group, resulting in asymmetries that affect the outputs, a phenomenon known as Symmetry Breaking. Traditional GConv-based methods are constrained by rigid operational rules within group space, assuming data remains strictly symmetry after limited group transformations. This limitation makes it difficult to adapt to Symmetry-Breaking and non-rigid transformations. Motivated by this, we mainly focus on a common scenario: Rotational Symmetry-Breaking. By relaxing strict group transformations within Strict Rotation-Equivariant group $\mathbf{C}_n$, we redefine a Relaxed Rotation-Equivariant group $\mathbf{R}_n$ and introduce a novel Relaxed Rotation-Equivariant GConv (R2GConv) with only a minimal increase of $4n$ parameters compared to GConv. Based on R2GConv, we propose a Relaxed Rotation-Equivariant Network (R2Net) as the backbone and develop a Relaxed Rotation-Equivariant Object Detector (R2Det) for 2D object detection. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed R2GConv in natural image classification, and R2Det achieves excellent performance in 2D object detection with improved generalization capabilities and robustness. The code is available in \texttt{https://github.com/wuer5/r2det}.
中摘: Group不变卷积（GConv）赋予模型探索数据潜在对称性的能力，从而提高性能。然而，现实世界中的场景往往偏离由物理置换引起的理想对称系统，这种对称系统的特征是非平凡对称群的作用，导致输出出现不对称现象，这一现象被称为对称性破坏。传统的基于GConv的方法受限于组空间内的严格操作规则，假设在有限的组变换之后数据仍保持严格的对称性。这一限制使得它们难以适应对称性破坏和非刚性变换。受此启发，我们主要关注一个常见场景：旋转对称性破坏。通过在严格旋转不变群$\mathbf{C}_n$内放宽严格的组变换，我们重新定义了一个旋转松弛不变群$\mathbf{R}_n$，并引入了一种仅比GConv增加少量参数（约$4n$个）的新颖旋转松弛不变GConv（R2GConv）。基于R2GConv，我们提出了一种旋转松弛不变网络（R2Net）作为骨干网络，并开发了一种旋转松弛不变物体检测器（R2Det），用于2D物体检测。实验结果表明，提出的R2GConv在自然图像分类中非常有效，而R2Det在2D物体检测中表现出卓越的性能，具有增强的一般化能力和鲁棒性。代码可在<https://github.com/wuer5/r2det> 获取。

[No.22] Pursuing Better Decision Boundaries for Long-Tailed Object Detection via Category Information Amount
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过类别信息量追求更好的决策边界以改进长尾目标检测
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Yanbiao Ma;Wei Dai;Jiayi Chen;
机构: Xidian University;Heriot-Watt University;Xi'an University of Electronic Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29994
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03852
英摘: In object detection, the instance count is typically used to define whether a dataset exhibits a long-tail distribution, implicitly assuming that models will underperform on categories with fewer instances. This assumption has led to extensive research on category bias in datasets with imbalanced instance counts. However, models still exhibit category bias even in datasets where instance counts are relatively balanced, clearly indicating that instance count alone cannot explain this phenomenon. In this work, we first introduce the concept and measurement of category information amount. We observe a significant negative correlation between category information amount and accuracy, suggesting that category information amount more accurately reflects the learning difficulty of a category. Based on this observation, we propose Information Amount-Guided Angular Margin (IGAM) Loss. The core idea of IGAM is to dynamically adjust the decision space of each category based on its information amount, thereby reducing category bias in long-tail datasets. IGAM Loss not only performs well on long-tailed benchmark datasets such as LVIS v1.0 and COCO-LT but also shows significant improvement for underrepresented categories in the non-long-tailed dataset Pascal VOC. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate the potential of category information amount as a tool and the generality of our proposed method.
中摘: 在目标检测中，实例计数通常用于定义数据集是否表现出长尾分布，隐含地假设模型会在实例较少的类别上表现不佳。这一假设导致了对类别偏差（特别是在实例计数不平衡的数据集中）的研究大量增加。然而，即使在实例计数相对平衡的数据集中，模型仍然会表现出类别偏差，这清楚地表明仅凭实例计数无法解释这一现象。在这项工作中，我们首先引入了类别信息量的概念及其测量方法。我们观察到类别信息量与准确性之间存在显著的负相关关系，这表明类别信息量更准确地反映了类别的学习难度。基于这一观察，我们提出了信息量引导的角度_margin（IGAM）损失。IGAM的核心思想是根据每个类别的信息量动态调整其决策空间，从而减少长尾数据集中的类别偏差。IGAM损失不仅在长尾基准数据集LVIS v1.0和COCO-LT上表现良好，还在非长尾数据集Pascal VOC中对少代表类别也显示出显著的改进效果。全面的实验展示了类别信息量作为工具的潜力以及我们提出的方法的普适性。

[No.23] MMFakeBench: A Mixed-Source Multimodal Misinformation Detection Benchmark for LVLMs
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: MMFakeBench：面向低资源语言模型的混合来源多模态 misinformation 检测基准
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Xuannan Liu;Zekun Li;Pei Pei Li;Huaibo Huang;Shuhan Xia;Xing Cui;Linzhi Huang;Weihong Deng;Zhaofeng He;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08772
英摘: Current multimodal misinformation detection (MMD) methods often assume a single source and type of forgery for each sample, which is insufficient for real-world scenarios where multiple forgery sources coexist. The lack of a benchmark for mixed-source misinformation has hindered progress in this field. To address this, we introduce MMFakeBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for mixed-source MMD. MMFakeBench includes 3 critical sources: textual veracity distortion, visual veracity distortion, and cross-modal consistency distortion, along with 12 sub-categories of misinformation forgery types. Additionally, we propose MMD-Agent, a novel approach to integrate the reasoning, action, and tool-use capabilities of LVLM agents, significantly enhancing accuracy and generalization. 中摘: 当前的多模态虚假信息检测（MMD）方法通常假设每个样本只有一个来源和一种伪造形式，这在多种伪造来源共存的真实世界场景中是不够充分的。缺乏混合来源虚假信息的标准基准数据集已经阻碍了这一领域的进步。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了MMFakeBench，这是首个全面的混合来源MMD基准数据集。MMFakeBench包括3个关键来源：文本真实性失真、视觉真实性失真以及跨模态一致性失真，并且还涵盖了12种虚假信息伪造类型的子类别。我们进一步在零样本情况下对6种常见的检测方法和15种大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）进行了广泛的评估。结果显示，当前的方法在这一具有挑战性和现实性的混合来源MMD设置中表现不佳。此外，我们提出了MMD-Agent，这是一种新颖的方法，旨在整合LVLM代理的推理、行动和工具使用能力，显著提高了准确性和泛化能力。我们认为这项研究将激发未来更多针对更现实的混合来源多模态虚假信息的研究，并提供一个公平的虚假信息检测方法评估。

[No.25] An Undetectable Watermark for Generative Image Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 生成图像模型中的不可检测水印
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Sam Gunn;Xuandong Zhao;Dawn Song;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28619
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07369
英摘: We present the first undetectable watermarking scheme for generative image models. Undetectability ensures that no efficient adversary can distinguish between watermarked and un-watermarked images, even after making many adaptive queries. In particular, an undetectable watermark does not degrade image quality under any efficiently computable metric. Our scheme works by selecting the initial latents of a diffusion model using a pseudorandom error-correcting code (Christ and Gunn, 2024), a strategy which guarantees undetectability and robustness. We experimentally demonstrate that our watermarks are quality-preserving and robust using Stable Diffusion 2.1. Our experiments verify that, in contrast to every prior scheme we tested, our watermark does not degrade image quality. Our experiments also demonstrate robustness: existing watermark removal attacks fail to remove our watermark from images without significantly degrading the quality of the images. Finally, we find that we can robustly encode 512 bits in our watermark, and up to 2500 bits when the images are not subjected to watermark removal attacks. Our code is available at https://github.com/XuandongZhao/PRC-Watermark.
中摘: 我们提出了首个不可检测的生成图像模型水印方案。不可检测性确保了即使在进行了许多自适应查询之后，也没有高效的对手能够区分水标记和未水标记的图像。特别是，不可检测的水印不会在任何高效可计算的度量下降低图像质量。我们的方案通过使用伪随机纠错码（Christ和Gunn, 2024）选择扩散模型的初始潜在变量来实现，这是一种保证不可检测性和鲁棒性的策略。我们使用Stable Diffusion 2.1进行实验，证明了我们的水印保持图像质量和鲁棒性。我们的实验验证了这一点：与我们测试过的每一个先前方案相比，我们的水印不会降低图像质量。此外，实验还展示了鲁棒性：现有的水印去除攻击无法显著降低图像质量就将我们的水印从图像中移除。最后，我们发现可以稳健地在水印中编码512位，并且当图像不受水印去除攻击影响时，可以编码多达2500位。我们的代码可在https://github.com/XuandongZhao/PRC-Watermark获取。

[No.26] Unifying Unsupervised Graph-Level Anomaly Detection and Out-of-Distribution Detection: A Benchmark
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 统一无监督图级异常检测与分布外检测：一个基准

英摘: To build safe and reliable graph machine learning systems, unsupervised graph-level anomaly detection (GLAD) and unsupervised graph-level out-of-distribution (OOD) detection (GLOD) have received significant attention in recent years. To bridge the gap, in this work, we present a \underline{\textbf{U}}nified \underline{\textbf{B}}enchmark for unsupervised \underline{\textbf{G}}raph-level \underline{\textbf{O}}OD and anoma\underline{\textbf{L}}y \underline{\textbf{D}}etection (\ourmethod), a comprehensive evaluation framework that unifies GLAD and GLOD under the concept of generalized graph-level OOD detection. 中摘: 为了构建安全可靠的知识图谱机器学习系统，无监督图级异常检测（GLAD）和无监督图级分布外（OOD）检测（GLOD）在近年来受到了广泛关注。尽管这两条研究路线确实具有相同的目标，但由于不同的评估设置，它们在社区中独立研究，从而造成了一个阻碍方法从一方应用到另一方的差距。为了解决这一问题，在这项工作中，我们提出了一个统一的无监督图级分布外和异常检测基准（\ourmethod），这是一个全面的评估框架，将GLAD和GLOD统一在广义图级分布外检测的概念之下。我们的基准涵盖了4种实际异常和分布外检测场景下的35个数据集，方便了对18种代表性GLAD/GLOD方法的比较。我们进行了多维度分析，以探索现有方法的有效性、分布外敏感度范围、鲁棒性和效率，揭示了它们的优势和局限性。此外，我们还提供了\ourmethod 的开源代码库（https://github.com/UB-GOLD/UB-GOLD），以促进可重复研究，并基于我们的见解指出了未来研究的潜在方向。

[No.28] MMAD: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Large Language Models in Industrial Anomaly Detection
来源: ICLR 2025

标题: MMAD：工业异常检测领域多模态大型语言模型的全面基准

作者: Xi Jiang;Jian Li;Hanqiu Deng;Yong Liu;Bin-Bin Gao;Yifeng Zhou;Jialin Li;Chengjie Wang;Feng Zheng;

英摘: In the field of industrial inspection, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have a high potential to renew the paradigms in practical applications due to their robust language capabilities and generalization abilities. However, despite their impressive problem-solving skills in many domains, MLLMs' ability in industrial anomaly detection has not been systematically studied. To bridge this gap, we present MMAD, the first-ever full-spectrum MLLMs benchmark in industrial Anomaly Detection. [No.29] Swift Hydra: Self-Reinforcing Generative Framework for Anomaly Detection with Multiple Mamba Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: Swift Hydra：基于多个Mamba模型的自我强化生成框架异常检测系统
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Nguyen Hoang Khoi Do;Truc Nguyen;Malik Hassanaly;raed alharbi;Jung Taek Seo;My T. Thai;
机构: University of Florida;;National Renewable Energy Laboratory;Saudi Electronic University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29771
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06413
英摘: Despite a plethora of anomaly detection models developed over the years, their ability to generalize to unseen anomalies remains an issue, particularly in critical systems. This paper aims to address this challenge by introducing Swift Hydra, a new framework for training an anomaly detection method based on generative AI and reinforcement learning (RL). Through featuring an RL policy that operates on the latent variables of a generative model, the framework synthesizes novel and diverse anomaly samples that are capable of bypassing a detection model. These generated synthetic samples are, in turn, used to augment the detection model, further improving its ability to handle challenging anomalies. Swift Hydra also incorporates Mamba models structured as a Mixture of Experts (MoE) to enable scalable adaptation of the number of Mamba experts based on data complexity, effectively capturing diverse feature distributions without increasing the model's inference time. Empirical evaluations on ADBench benchmark demonstrate that Swift Hydra outperforms other state-of-the-art anomaly detection models while maintaining a relatively short inference time. From these results, our research highlights a new and auspicious paradigm of integrating RL and generative AI for advancing anomaly detection.
中摘: 尽管多年来开发了许多异常检测模型，它们在处理未见过的异常方面的泛化能力仍然存在问题，特别是在关键系统中。本文旨在通过引入Swift Hydra框架来应对这一挑战，该框架基于生成人工智能和强化学习（RL）训练异常检测方法。通过在生成模型的潜在变量上操作一个RL策略，该框架合成了新颖且多样的异常样本，能够绕过检测模型。这些生成的合成样本随后被用于增强检测模型，进一步提高其处理具有挑战性的异常的能力。Swift Hydra还结合了Mamba模型，这些模型结构为专家混合体（MoE），以根据数据复杂性可扩展地调整Mamba专家的数量，从而有效捕捉多样化的特征分布而不增加模型的推理时间。在ADBench基准上的实证评估表明，Swift Hydra在保持相对短的推理时间的同时，优于其他最先进的异常检测模型。从这些结果来看，我们的研究突显了将RL与生成人工智能相结合的新颖且前景广阔的范式，以推动异常检测的发展。

[No.31] Decoupled Graph Energy-based Model for Node Out-of-Distribution Detection on Heterophilic Graphs
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 异类图上节点离分布检测的解耦图形能量模型
类别: learning on graphs and other geometries & topologies
作者: Yuhan Chen;Yihong Luo;Yifan Song;Pengwen Dai;Jing Tang;Xiaochun Cao;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29852
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17912
英摘: Despite extensive research efforts focused on OOD detection on images, OOD detection on nodes in graph learning remains underexplored. The dependence among graph nodes hinders the trivial adaptation of existing approaches on images that assume inputs to be i.i.d. sampled, since many unique features and challenges specific to graphs are not considered, such as the heterophily issue. Recently, GNNSafe, which considers node dependence, adapted energy-based detection to the graph domain with state-of-the-art performance, however, it has two serious issues: 1) it derives node energy from classification logits without specifically tailored training for modeling data distribution, making it less effective at recognizing OOD data; 2) it highly relies on energy propagation, which is based on homophily assumption and will cause significant performance degradation on heterophilic graphs, where the node tends to have dissimilar distribution with its neighbors. To address the above issues, we suggest training EBMs by MLE to enhance data distribution modeling and remove energy propagation to overcome the heterophily issues. However, training EBMs via MLE requires performing MCMC sampling on both node feature and node neighbors, which is challenging due to the node interdependence and discrete graph topology. To tackle the sampling challenge, we introduce DeGEM, which decomposes the learning process into two parts: a graph encoder that leverages topology information for node representations and an energy head that operates in latent space. Extensive experiments validate that DeGEM, without OOD exposure during training, surpasses previous state-of-the-art methods, achieving an average AUROC improvement of 6.71% on homophilic graphs and 20.29% on heterophilic graphs, and even outperform methods trained with OOD exposure. Our code is available at: https://github.com/draym28/DeGEM.
中摘: 尽管在图像领域已经投入了大量研究精力进行OOD检测（异常数据检测），但在图学习中的节点OOD检测仍然未被充分探索。图节点之间的依赖关系阻碍了现有假设输入为独立同分布抽样的图像方法的直接应用，因为许多特定于图的独特特征和挑战并未被考虑进去，例如异质性问题。最近，GNNSafe 考虑到了节点依赖性，并将基于能量的方法适应到图域中，取得了最先进的性能，然而它存在两个严重的问题：1）它从分类logits中提取节点能量，而没有针对建模数据分布进行专门训练，这使得它在识别OOD数据方面效果不佳；2）它高度依赖能量传播，该传播基于同质性假设，在异质性图上会导致显著的性能下降，因为在异质性图中，节点与其邻居的分布往往不相似。为了应对上述问题，我们建议通过最大似然估计（MLE）训练EBMs（能量基础模型），以增强数据分布建模，并移除能量传播来克服异质性问题。然而，通过MLE训练EBMs需要在节点特征和节点邻居上执行MCMC采样，这由于节点间的相互依赖性和离散的图拓扑结构而颇具挑战。为了解决采样难题，我们引入了DeGEM，将其学习过程分解为两部分：一个图编码器利用拓扑信息生成节点表示，以及一个操作潜在空间的能量头。广泛实验验证表明，DeGEM 在训练过程中无需暴露于OOD数据，即可超越先前的先进方法，在同质性图上平均AUROC 提高了6.71%，在异质性图上提高了20.29%，甚至优于那些在训练时暴露于OOD数据的方法。我们的代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/draym28/DeGEM。

[No.32] Scale-Aware Contrastive Reverse Distillation for Unsupervised Medical Anomaly Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于尺度感知对比反向蒸馏的无监督医疗异常检测
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Chunlei Li;Yilei Shi;Jingliang Hu;Xiao Xiang Zhu;Lichao Mou;
机构: MedAI Tech;Technische Universität München;Technical University of Munich;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30228
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13828
英摘: Unsupervised anomaly detection using deep learning has garnered significant research attention due to its broad applicability, particularly in medical imaging where labeled anomalous data are scarce. While earlier approaches leverage generative models like autoencoders and generative adversarial networks (GANs), they often fall short due to overgeneralization. Recent methods explore various strategies, including memory banks, normalizing flows, self-supervised learning, and knowledge distillation, to enhance discrimination. Among these, knowledge distillation, particularly reverse distillation, has shown promise. Following this paradigm, we propose a novel scale-aware contrastive reverse distillation model that addresses two key limitations of existing reverse distillation methods: insufficient feature discriminability and inability to handle anomaly scale variations. Specifically, we introduce a contrastive student-teacher learning approach to derive more discriminative representations by generating and exploring out-of-normal distributions. Further, we design a scale adaptation mechanism to softly weight contrastive distillation losses at different scales to account for the scale variation issue. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, validating the efficacy of the proposed method. Code is available at https://github.com/MedAITech/SCRD4AD.
中摘: 基于深度学习的无监督异常检测由于其广泛的应用前景，特别是在标注异常数据稀缺的医学影像领域，已经引起了显著的研究关注。早期的方法利用生成模型如自动编码器和生成对抗网络（GANs），但由于过度泛化的问题，它们往往效果不佳。最近的方法探索了多种策略，包括记忆库、归一化流、自监督学习和知识蒸馏，以提高区分能力。在这之中，知识蒸馏，尤其是逆向蒸馏，显示出一定的潜力。遵循这一范式，我们提出了一种新颖的尺度感知对比逆向蒸馏模型，以解决现有逆向蒸馏方法中的两个关键限制：特征区分性不足以及无法处理异常尺度变化。具体而言，我们引入了一种对比学生-教师学习方法，通过生成和探索正常分布之外的数据来获得更具有区分性的表示。此外，我们设计了一种尺度适应机制，在不同尺度上柔和地加权对比蒸馏损失，以应对尺度变化问题。在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法具有最先进的性能，验证了所提方法的有效性。代码可在https://github.com/MedAITech/SCRD4AD获取。

[No.33] Manifold Induced Biases for Zero-shot and Few-shot Detection of Generated Images
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 生成图像零样本和少样本检测中的流形诱导偏差
类别: generative models
作者: Jonathan Brokman;Amit Giloni;Omer Hofman;Roman Vainshtein;Hisashi Kojima;Guy Gilboa;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;Fujitsu Research and Development Center;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30810
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15470
英摘: Distinguishing between real and AI-generated images, commonly referred to as 'image detection', presents a timely and significant challenge. Despite extensive research in the (semi-)supervised regime, zero-shot and few-shot solutions have only recently emerged as promising alternatives. Their main advantage is in alleviating the ongoing data maintenance, which quickly becomes outdated due to advances in generative technologies. We identify two main gaps: (1) a lack of theoretical grounding for the methods, and (2) significant room for performance improvements in zero-shot and few-shot regimes. Our approach is founded on understanding and quantifying the biases inherent in generated content, where we use these quantities as criteria for characterizing generated images. Specifically, we explore the biases of the implicit probability manifold, captured by a pre-trained diffusion model. Through score-function analysis, we approximate the curvature, gradient, and bias towards points on the probability manifold, establishing criteria for detection in the zero-shot regime. We further extend our contribution to the few-shot setting by employing a mixture-of-experts methodology. Empirical results across 20 generative models demonstrate that our method outperforms current approaches in both zero-shot and few-shot settings. This work advances the theoretical understanding and practical usage of generated content biases through the lens of manifold analysis.
中摘: 辨别真实图像与AI生成图像的能力，通常被称为“图像检测”，是一个及时且重要的挑战。尽管在半监督领域进行了广泛的研究，但在零样本和少样本解决方案方面，直到最近才出现有前景的替代方案。它们的主要优势在于缓解了持续的数据维护问题，因为生成技术的进步使这些数据迅速过时。我们识别出两个主要缺口：（1）方法缺乏理论基础，以及（2）零样本和少样本设置中的性能改进仍有很大空间。我们的方法基于理解并量化生成内容固有的偏差，利用这些量作为表征生成图像的标准。具体而言，我们探索由预训练扩散模型捕捉的隐式概率流形的偏差。通过评分函数分析，我们近似计算曲率、梯度以及概率流形上点的偏差，从而为零样本设置下的检测建立标准。我们进一步将贡献扩展到少样本设置，采用了专家混合的方法。在20个生成模型上的实证结果表明，我们的方法在零样本和少样本设置中均优于现有方法。这项工作通过流形分析的视角推进了生成内容偏差的理论理解和实际应用。

[No.34] A Sanity Check for AI-generated Image Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: AI生成图像检测的合理性检查
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Shilin Yan;Ouxiang Li;Jiayin Cai;Yanbin Hao;Xiaolong Jiang;Yao Hu;Weidi Xie;
机构: Xiaohongshu;University of Science and Technology of China;Hefei University of Technology;Alibaba Group;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29835
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19435
英摘: With the rapid development of generative models, discerning AI-generated content has evoked increasing attention from both industry and academia. In this paper, we conduct a sanity check on "whether the task of AI-generated image detection has been solved". To start with, we present Chameleon dataset, consisting AIgenerated images that are genuinely challenging for human perception. To quantify the generalization of existing methods, we evaluate 9 off-the-shelf AI-generated image detectors on Chameleon dataset. Upon analysis, almost all models classify AI-generated images as real ones. Later, we propose AIDE (AI-generated Image DEtector with Hybrid Features), which leverages multiple experts to simultaneously extract visual artifacts and noise patterns. Specifically, to capture the high-level semantics, we utilize CLIP to compute the visual embedding. This effectively enables the model to discern AI-generated images based on semantics or contextual information; Secondly, we select the highest frequency patches and the lowest frequency patches in the image, and compute the low-level patchwise features, aiming to detect AI-generated images by low-level artifacts, for example, noise pattern, anti-aliasing, etc. While evaluating on existing benchmarks, for example, AIGCDetectBenchmark and GenImage, AIDE achieves +3.5% and +4.6% improvements to state-of-the-art methods, and on our proposed challenging Chameleon benchmarks, it also achieves the promising results, despite this problem for detecting AI-generated images is far from being solved.
中摘: 随着生成模型的快速发展，辨别AI生成的内容正逐渐引起工业界和学术界的广泛关注。在本文中，我们对“AI生成图像检测任务是否已经解决”进行了验证。首先，我们展示了Chameleon数据集，该数据集包含了一些真正挑战人类感知能力的AI生成图像。为了量化现有方法的一般泛化能力，我们在Chameleon数据集上评估了9种现成的AI生成图像检测器。分析结果显示，几乎所有模型都将AI生成的图像分类为真实图像。随后，我们提出了AIDE（结合特征的AI生成图像检测器），该检测器利用多种专家同时提取视觉特征和噪声模式。具体而言，为了捕捉高层语义，我们利用CLIP计算视觉嵌入。这使得模型能够基于语义或上下文信息来辨别AI生成的图像；其次，我们选择了图像中的高频块和低频块，并计算低级块状特征，旨在通过低级特征如噪声模式、抗锯齿等来检测AI生成的图像。在现有基准测试AIGCDetectBenchmark和GenImage上的评估中，AIDE实现了对最先进的方法分别提高了3.5%和4.6%的效果，在我们提出的具有挑战性的Chameleon基准测试上也取得了令人鼓舞的结果，尽管检测AI生成图像的问题仍然远未解决。

[No.36] Can We Ignore Labels in Out of Distribution Detection?
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 我们可以在分布外检测中忽略标签吗？
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Hong Yang;Qi Yu;Travis Desell;
机构: Rochester Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28870
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14704
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection methods have recently become more prominent, serving as a core element in safety-critical autonomous systems. One major purpose of OOD detection is to reject invalid inputs that could lead to unpredictable errors and compromise safety. Due to the cost of labeled data, recent works have investigated the feasibility of self-supervised learning (SSL) OOD detection, unlabeled OOD detection, and zero shot OOD detection. In this work, we identify a set of conditions for a theoretical guarantee of failure in unlabeled OOD detection algorithms from an information-theoretic perspective. These conditions are present in all OOD tasks dealing with real-world data: I) we provide theoretical proof of unlabeled OOD detection failure when there exists zero mutual information between the learning objective and the in-distribution labels, a.k.a. 'label blindness', II) we define a new OOD task - Adjacent OOD detection - that tests for label blindness and accounts for a previously ignored safety gap in all OOD detection benchmarks, and III) we perform experiments demonstrating that existing unlabeled OOD methods fail under conditions suggested by our label blindness theory and analyze the implications for future research in unlabeled OOD methods.
中摘: 分布外（OOD）检测方法近年来越来越受到重视，已成为安全关键自主系统的核心要素之一。OOD检测的主要目的之一是拒绝可能导致不可预测错误且危及安全性的无效输入。由于标记数据的成本较高，近期的研究工作已经探讨了自监督学习（SSL）的OOD检测、无标记的OOD检测以及零样本的OOD检测的可行性。在本研究中，我们从信息论的角度识别了一组条件，以确保无标记OOD检测算法理论上的失败保证。这些条件存在于所有处理现实世界数据的OOD任务中：I）我们提供了理论证明，在学习目标与分布内标签之间不存在互信息的情况下，无标记OOD检测将失败，这被称为“标签盲视”；II）我们定义了一个新的OOD任务——相邻OOD检测，该任务用于测试标签盲视，并考虑到所有OOD检测基准中被忽视的安全差距；III）我们进行了实验，证明现有的无标记OOD方法在我们的标签盲视理论建议的条件下会失效，并分析了这对未来无标记OOD方法研究的影响。

[No.37] Prompt as Knowledge Bank: Boost Vision-language model via Structural Representation for zero-shot medical detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: Prompt 作为知识库：通过结构表示增强视觉-语言模型以实现零样本医疗检测
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Yuguang Yang;Tongfei Chen;Haoyu Huang;Linlin Yang;Chunyu Xie;Dawei Leng;Xianbin Cao;Baochang Zhang;
机构: School of Electronic Information Engineering;Beihang University;Communication University of China;360 AI Research;360 AI Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28543
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16223
英摘: Zero-shot medical detection can further improve detection performance without relying on annotated medical images even upon the fine-tuned model, showing great clinical value. Recent studies leverage grounded vision-language models (GLIP) to achieve this by using detailed disease descriptions as prompts for the target disease name during the inference phase. However, these methods typically treat prompts as equivalent context to the target name, making it difficult to assign specific disease knowledge based on visual information, leading to a coarse alignment between images and target descriptions. In this paper, we propose StructuralGLIP, which introduces an auxiliary branch to encode prompts into a latent knowledge bank layer-by-layer, enabling more context-aware and fine-grained alignment. Specifically, in each layer, we select highly similar features from both the image representation and the knowledge bank, forming structural representations that capture nuanced relationships between image patches and target descriptions. These features are then fused across modalities to further enhance detection performance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StructuralGLIP achieves a +4.1\% AP improvement over prior state-of-the-art methods across seven zero-shot medical detection benchmarks, and consistently improves fine-tuned models by +3.2\% AP on endoscopy image datasets.
中摘: 零样本医疗检测可以在不依赖标注医疗图像的情况下进一步提高检测性能，即使在微调后的模型上也是如此，显示出巨大的临床价值。最近的研究利用基于视觉-语言的模型（GLIP）在推理阶段通过详细疾病描述作为目标疾病名称的提示来实现这一点。然而，这些方法通常将提示视为与目标名称等效的上下文，使得根据视觉信息分配特定的疾病知识变得困难，导致图像与目标描述之间的粗略对齐。在本文中，我们提出了StructuralGLIP，它引入了一个辅助分支，逐层将提示编码到潜在知识库中，从而实现更上下文感知和精细的对齐。具体而言，在每一层中，我们从图像表示和知识库中选择高度相似的特征，形成结构表示，捕捉图像块与目标描述之间微妙的关系。然后，这些特征在不同模态间融合，以进一步增强检测性能。广泛的实验表明，StructuralGLIP在七个零样本医疗检测基准上的平均精度（AP）提高了4.1%，并在内镜图像数据集上的一致性改进为3.2%的AP。

[No.38] Fine-tuning can Help Detect Pretraining Data from Large Language Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 微调可以帮助检测大型语言模型中的预训练数据
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Hengxiang Zhang;Songxin Zhang;Bingyi Jing;Hongxin Wei;
机构: Southern University of Science and Technology;South University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29320
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10880
英摘: In the era of large language models (LLMs), detecting pretraining data has been increasingly important due to concerns about fair evaluation and ethical risks. Current methods differentiate members and non-members by designing scoring functions, like Perplexity and Min-k%. However, the diversity and complexity of training data magnifies the difficulty of distinguishing, leading to suboptimal performance in detecting pretraining data. In this paper, we first explore the benefits of unseen data, which can be easily collected after the release of the LLM. We find that the perplexities of LLMs shift differently for members and non-members, after fine-tuning with a small amount of previously unseen data. In light of this, we introduce a novel and effective method termed Fine-tuned Score Deviation(FSD), which improves the performance of current scoring functions for pretraining data detection. In particular, we propose to measure the deviation distance of current scores after fine-tuning on a small amount of unseen data within the same domain. In effect, using a few unseen data can largely decrease the scores of all non-members, leading to a larger deviation distance than members. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, significantly improving the AUC score on common benchmark datasets across various models.
中摘: 在大语言模型（LLMs）的时代，检测预训练数据变得越来越重要，因为人们越来越关注公平评估和伦理风险。当前的方法通过设计评分函数来区分成员和非成员，例如困惑度和Min-k%。然而，训练数据的多样性和复杂性增加了区分的难度，导致在检测预训练数据时性能不佳。在本文中，我们首先探讨未见过的数据的好处，这些数据可以在LLM发布后容易地收集。我们发现，在使用少量之前未见过的数据进行微调后，大语言模型对成员和非成员的困惑度有所差异。基于这一点，我们引入了一种新颖且有效的方法——微调得分偏差（FSD），该方法可以提高当前评分函数在预训练数据检测中的性能。特别地，我们提出在相同领域内使用少量未见过的数据微调后，测量当前评分的偏差距离。实际上，使用少量未见过的数据可以大幅降低所有非成员的评分，从而导致与成员相比更大的偏差距离。广泛的实验表明了我们方法的有效性，显著提高了各种模型在常见基准数据集上的AUC分数。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.39] CATCH: Channel-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Frequency Patching
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: CATCH：基于频域分段的通道感知多变量时间序列异常检测
类别: learning on time series and dynamical systems
作者: Xingjian Wu;Xiangfei Qiu;Zhengyu Li;Yihang Wang;Jilin Hu;Chenjuan Guo;Hui Xiong;Bin Yang;
机构: East China Normal University;Aalborg University;;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28489
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12261
英摘: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenging as heterogeneous subsequence anomalies may occur. Reconstruction-based methods, which focus on learning normal patterns in the frequency domain to detect diverse abnormal subsequences, achieve promising results, while still falling short on capturing fine-grained frequency characteristics and channel correlations. To contend with the limitations, we introduce CATCH, a framework based on frequency patching. We propose to patchify the frequency domain into frequency bands, which enhances its ability to capture fine-grained frequency characteristics. To perceive appropriate channel correlations, we propose a Channel Fusion Module (CFM), which features a patch-wise mask generator and a masked-attention mechanism. Driven by a bi-level multi-objective optimization algorithm, the CFM is encouraged to iteratively discover appropriate patch-wise channel correlations, and to cluster relevant channels while isolating adverse effects from irrelevant channels. Extensive experiments on 10 real-world datasets and 12 synthetic datasets demonstrate that CATCH achieves state-of-the-art performance. We make our code and datasets available at https://github.com/decisionintelligence/CATCH.
中摘: 多变量时间序列中的异常检测具有挑战性，因为可能存在异构子序列异常。基于重构的方法专注于在频域中学习正常模式以检测多样化的异常子序列，取得了令人瞩目的成果，但仍无法捕捉到精细的频率特征和通道间的关联性。为应对这些限制，我们引入了CATCH框架，该框架基于频域拼贴。我们提出将频域拆分为频带，从而增强其捕捉精细频率特征的能力。为了感知适当的通道关联性，我们提出了通道融合模块（Channel Fusion Module，CFM），该模块具备拼贴层面的掩码生成器和掩码注意力机制。受双层多目标优化算法驱动，CFM被鼓励迭代地发现适当的拼贴层面通道关联性，并聚类相关通道，同时隔离无关通道的影响。在10个真实世界数据集和12个合成数据集上的广泛实验表明，CATCH达到了最先进的性能。我们已在https://github.com/decisionintelligence/CATCH上开源了我们的代码和数据集。

[No.40] Glimpse: Enabling White-Box Methods to Use Proprietary Models for Zero-Shot LLM-Generated Text Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: Glimpse: 允许白盒方法使用专有模型进行零样本LLM生成文本检测
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Guangsheng Bao;Yanbin Zhao;Juncai He;Yue Zhang;
机构: Westlake University;Shanghai Polytechnic University;King Abdullah University of Science and Technology;

链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29149
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11506
英摘: Advanced large language models (LLMs) can generate text almost indistinguishable from human-written text, highlighting the importance of LLM-generated text detection. However, current zero-shot techniques face challenges as white-box methods are restricted to use weaker open-source LLMs, and black-box methods are limited by partial observation from stronger proprietary LLMs. It seems impossible to enable white-box methods to use proprietary models because API-level access to the models neither provides full predictive distributions nor inner embeddings. To traverse the divide, we propose **Glimpse**, a probability distribution estimation approach, predicting the full distributions from partial observations. Despite the simplicity of Glimpse, we successfully extend white-box methods like Entropy, Rank, Log-Rank, and Fast-DetectGPT to latest proprietary models. Experiments show that Glimpse with Fast-DetectGPT and GPT-3.5 achieves an average AUROC of about 0.95 in five latest source models, improving the score by 51% relative to the remaining space of the open source baseline. We release our code and data at https://github.com/baoguangsheng/glimpse.
中摘: 先进的大型语言模型（LLMs）可以生成几乎与人类撰写的文本无法区别的文本，突显了检测LLM生成文本的重要性。然而，当前的零样本技术面临挑战，因为白盒方法只能使用较弱的开源LLM，而黑盒方法受限于部分观察较强的专业LLM。似乎不可能让白盒方法使用专有模型，因为模型在API级别上的访问既不提供完整的预测分布，也不提供内部嵌入。为跨越这一鸿沟，我们提出了一种名为**Glimpse**的概率分布估计方法，能够从部分观察中预测出完整的分布。尽管Glimpse的方法很简单，但我们成功地将其扩展到最新的专有模型上，如Entropy、Rank、Log-Rank和Fast-DetectGPT。实验结果显示，在五个最新的源模型中，Glimpse与Fast-DetectGPT和GPT-3.5结合使用的平均AUROC约为0.95，相对于开源基线剩余的空间提高了51%的分数。这表明最新的LLM能够有效检测自己的输出，暗示高级LLM可能是最好的自我防护手段。我们已在https://github.com/baoguangsheng/glimpse发布了我们的代码和数据。

[No.44] AHA: A Vision-Language-Model for Detecting and Reasoning Over Failures in Robotic Manipulation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: AHA：一种用于检测和推理机器人操作中故障的语言-视觉模型
类别: applications to robotics, autonomy, planning
作者: Jiafei Duan;Wilbert Pumacay;Nishanth Kumar;Yi Ru Wang;Shulin Tian;Wentao Yuan;Ranjay Krishna;Dieter Fox;Ajay Mandlekar;Yijie Guo;
机构: University of Washington;Universidad Nacional de Ingeniería;NVIDIA;Nanyang Technological University;Unknown Institution;University of Michigan;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30106
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00371
英摘: Robotic manipulation in open-world settings requires not only task execution but also the ability to detect and learn from failures. While recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) and large language models (LLMs) have improved robots' spatial reasoning and problem-solving abilities, they still struggle with failure recognition, limiting their real-world applicability. We introduce AHA, an open-source VLM designed to detect and reason about failures in robotic manipulation using natural language. By framing failure detection as a free-form reasoning task, AHA identifies failures and provides detailed, adaptable explanations across different robots, tasks, and environments. We fine-tuned AHA using FailGen, a scalable framework that generates the first large-scale dataset of robotic failure trajectories, the AHA dataset. FailGen achieves this by procedurally perturbing successful demonstrations from simulation. Despite being trained solely on the AHA dataset, AHA generalizes effectively to real-world failure datasets, robotic systems, and unseen tasks. It surpasses the second-best model (GPT-4o in-context learning) by 10.3% and exceeds the average performance of six compared models including five state-of-the-art VLMs by 35.3% across multiple metrics and datasets. We integrate AHA into three manipulation frameworks that utilize LLMs/VLMs for reinforcement learning, task and motion planning, and zero-shot trajectory generation. AHA's failure feedback enhances these policies' performances by refining dense reward functions, optimizing task planning, and improving sub-task verification, boosting task success rates by an average of 21.4% across all three tasks compared to GPT-4 models.
中摘: 在开放环境中的机器人操作不仅需要任务执行，还需要检测和从失败中学习的能力。尽管近期视觉语言模型（VLMs）和大规模语言模型（LLMs）的进步提高了机器人的空间推理能力和问题解决能力，但它们在失败识别方面仍存在问题，限制了其在现实世界中的应用。我们引入了AHA，一个开源的VLM，用于通过自然语言检测和推理机器人操作中的失败。通过将失败检测框定为一种自由形式的推理任务，AHA能够识别失败并提供详细且适应性强的解释，适用于不同的机器人、任务和环境。我们使用FailGen对AHA进行了微调，FailGen是一个可扩展的框架，生成了首个大规模的机器人失败轨迹数据集——AHA数据集。FailGen通过程序化地对模拟成功的演示进行扰动来实现这一点。尽管仅基于AHA数据集进行训练，AHA仍能有效地泛化到现实世界的失败数据集、机器人系统和未见过的任务中。在多个指标和数据集上，AHA超越了第二好的模型（GPT-4o的上下文学习），并在六种比较模型中（包括五种最先进的VLMs）平均高出35.3%的表现。我们将AHA整合到三个利用LLMs/VLMs进行强化学习、任务和运动规划以及零样本轨迹生成的操作框架中。AHA的失败反馈通过细化密集奖励函数、优化任务规划和改进子任务验证，提升了这些策略的表现，与GPT-4模型相比，在所有三项任务中平均提高了21.4%的任务成功率。

[No.45] Adversarially Robust Out-of-Distribution Detection Using Lyapunov-Stabilized Embeddings
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题:  adversarialrobustness 基于李亚普诺夫稳定嵌入的出分布检测
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Hossein Mirzaei;Mackenzie W Mathis;
机构: EPFL;Swiss Federal Institute of Technology Lausanne;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30255
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10744
英摘: Despite significant advancements in out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, existing methods still struggle to maintain robustness against adversarial attacks, compromising their reliability in critical real-world applications. Previous studies have attempted to address this challenge by exposing detectors to auxiliary OOD datasets alongside adversarial training. However, the increased data complexity inherent in adversarial training, and the myriad of ways that OOD samples can arise during testing, often prevent these approaches from establishing robust decision boundaries. To address these limitations, we propose AROS, a novel approach leveraging neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs) with Lyapunov stability theorem in order to obtain robust embeddings for OOD detection. By incorporating a tailored loss function, we apply Lyapunov stability theory to ensure that both in-distribution (ID) and OOD data converge to stable equilibrium points within the dynamical system. This approach encourages any perturbed input to return to its stable equilibrium, thereby enhancing the model's robustness against adversarial perturbations. To not use additional data, we generate fake OOD embeddings by sampling from low-likelihood regions of the ID data feature space, approximating the boundaries where OOD data are likely to reside. To then further enhance robustness, we propose the use of an orthogonal binary layer following the stable feature space, which maximizes the separation between the equilibrium points of ID and OOD samples. We validate our method through extensive experiments across several benchmarks, demonstrating superior performance, particularly under adversarial attacks. Notably, our approach improves robust detection performance from 37.8% to 80.1% on CIFAR-10 vs. CIFAR-100 and from 29.0% to 67.0% on CIFAR-100 vs. CIFAR-10.
中摘: 尽管在离分布（OOD）检测方面取得了显著进展，现有的方法仍然难以在对抗攻击下保持鲁棒性，这使得它们在关键的实际应用中可靠性降低。以往的研究尝试通过在辅助OOD数据集和对抗训练的同时暴露检测器来解决这一挑战。然而，对抗训练固有的数据复杂性以及测试过程中OOD样本可能出现的各种情况，往往阻止这些方法建立稳健的决策边界。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种名为AROS的新颖方法，该方法利用神经常微分方程（NODEs）和李亚普诺夫稳定性定理，以获得用于OOD检测的稳健嵌入。通过引入一个定制化的损失函数，我们应用李亚普诺夫稳定性理论，确保在动态系统内，既分布内的（ID）数据又OOD数据都能收敛到稳定平衡点。这种方法鼓励任何扰动输入回到其稳定的平衡点，从而增强模型对对抗扰动的鲁棒性。为了不使用额外的数据，我们通过从ID数据特征空间的低概率区域采样生成假的OOD嵌入，以此来近似OOD数据可能存在的边界。为进一步增强鲁棒性，我们提出了在稳定特征空间之后使用正交二元层的方法，该方法最大化了ID样本和OOD样本平衡点之间的分离度。我们在多个基准上进行了广泛的实验验证了我们的方法，特别是在对抗攻击下的性能表现尤为出色。值得注意的是，我们的方法将CIFAR-10 vs. CIFAR-100的稳健检测性能从37.8%提高到了80.1%，并将CIFAR-100 vs. CIFAR-10的性能从29.0%提高到了67.0%。

[No.46] DiffGAD: A Diffusion-based Unsupervised Graph Anomaly Detector

英摘: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is crucial for identifying abnormal entities within networks, garnering significant attention across various fields. Traditional unsupervised methods, which decode encoded latent representations of unlabeled data with a reconstruction focus, often fail to capture critical discriminative content, leading to suboptimal anomaly detection. To address these challenges, we present a Diffusion-based Graph Anomaly Detector (DiffGAD). At the heart of DiffGAD is a novel latent space learning paradigm, meticulously designed to enhance its proficiency by guiding it with discriminative content. This innovative approach leverages diffusion sampling to infuse the latent space with discriminative content and introduces a content-preservation mechanism that retains valuable information across different scales, significantly improving its adeptness at identifying anomalies with limited time and space complexity. Our comprehensive evaluation of DiffGAD, conducted on six real-world and large-scale datasets with various metrics, demonstrated its exceptional performance.
中摘: 图异常检测（GAD）对于在网络中识别异常实体至关重要，引起了各个领域的广泛关注。传统的无监督方法侧重于通过重建来解码未标记数据的潜在表示，但往往难以捕捉到关键的区分性内容，导致异常检测效果不佳。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种基于扩散的图异常检测器（DiffGAD）。DiffGAD的核心是一种新颖的潜在空间学习范式，精心设计以通过引导其使用区分性内容来提升其专业性。这一创新方法利用扩散采样将区分性内容注入潜在空间，并引入了一种内容保留机制，能够在不同尺度上保留有价值的信息，显著提高了其在有限时间和空间复杂度下识别异常的能力。我们在六个真实世界的大规模数据集上进行了全面评估，采用多种指标验证了DiffGAD的卓越性能。
