[No.48] Multi-Perspective Data Augmentation for Few-shot Object Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 多视角数据增强在少样本目标检测中的应用
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Anh Khoa Nguyen Vu;Truong Quoc Truong;Vinh-Tiep Nguyen;Thanh Duc Ngo;Thanh-Toan Do;Tam V. Nguyen;
机构: ;University of Information Technology;University of Dayton;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28258
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18195
英摘: Recent few-shot object detection (FSOD) methods have focused on augmenting synthetic samples for novel classes, show promising results to the rise of diffusion models. However, the diversity of such datasets is often limited in representativeness because they lack awareness of typical and hard samples, especially in the context of foreground and background relationships. To tackle this issue, we propose a Multi-Perspective Data Augmentation (MPAD) framework. In terms of foreground-foreground relationships, we propose in-context learning for object synthesis (ICOS) with bounding box adjustments to enhance the detail and spatial information of synthetic samples. Inspired by the large margin principle, support samples play a vital role in defining class boundaries. Therefore, we design a Harmonic Prompt Aggregation Scheduler (HPAS) to mix prompt embeddings at each time step of the generation process in diffusion models, producing hard novel samples. For foreground-background relationships, we introduce a Background Proposal method (BAP) to sample typical and hard backgrounds. Extensive experiments on multiple FSOD benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Our framework significantly outperforms traditional methods, achieving an average increase of $17.5\%$ in nAP50 over the baseline on PASCAL VOC. Code is available at https://github.com/nvakhoa/MPAD.
中摘: 最近的少样本对象检测（Few-Shot Object Detection, FSOD）方法主要集中在为新类别增强合成样本，显示出对扩散模型兴起的良好前景。然而，这类数据集在代表性方面的多样性往往有限，因为它们缺乏对典型和困难样本的认知，特别是在前景与背景关系的背景下。为了应对这一问题，我们提出了一种多视角数据增强（Multi-Perspective Data Augmentation, MPAD）框架。针对前景-前景关系，我们提出了基于上下文学习的对象合成方法（In-Context Learning for Object Synthesis, ICOS），并调整边界框以增强合成样本的细节和空间信息。受大间隔原则的启发，支持样本在定义类别边界方面起着至关重要的作用。因此，我们设计了一种谐波提示聚合调度器（Harmonic Prompt Aggregation Scheduler, HPAS），在扩散模型生成过程中的每个时间步骤混合提示嵌入，从而生成困难的新样本。对于前景-背景关系，我们引入了一种背景建议方法（Background Proposal Method, BAP），以采样典型的和困难的背景。在多个FSOD基准上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法非常有效。我们的框架显著优于传统方法，在PASCAL VOC基准上平均提高了nAP50约17.5%。代码可在https://github.com/nvakhoa/MPAD获取。

[No.49] Language-Assisted Feature Transformation for Anomaly Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 语言辅助特征转换异常检测
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: EungGu Yun;Heonjin Ha;Yeongwoo Nam;Bryan Dongik Lee;
机构: SAIGE;LG;Saige Research;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/31115
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01184
英摘: This paper introduces LAFT, a novel feature transformation method designed to incorporate user knowledge and preferences into anomaly detection using natural language. Accurately modeling the boundary of normality is crucial for distinguishing abnormal data, but this is often challenging due to limited data or the presence of nuisance attributes. While unsupervised methods that rely solely on data without user guidance are common, they may fail to detect anomalies of specific interest. To address this limitation, we propose Language-Assisted Feature Transformation (LAFT), which leverages the shared image-text embedding space of vision-language models to transform visual features according to user-defined requirements. Combined with anomaly detection methods, LAFT effectively aligns visual features with user preferences, allowing anomalies of interest to be detected. Extensive experiments on both toy and real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of our method.
中摘: 本文介绍了LAFT，这是一种新颖的功能转换方法，旨在利用自然语言将用户知识和偏好融入异常检测。准确建模正常性的边界对于区分异常数据至关重要，但这一过程常常因数据有限或存在无关属性而具有挑战性。尽管无监督方法通常仅依赖数据而无需用户指导在这些场景下很常见，它们可能无法检测到特定感兴趣的异常。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了Language-Assisted Feature Transformation（LAFT），该方法利用视觉语言模型中的共享图像-文本嵌入空间，根据用户的定义要求转换视觉特征。结合异常检测方法，LAFT有效地将视觉特征与用户偏好对齐，使得特定感兴趣的异常能够被检测到。我们在玩具数据集和真实世界数据集上的广泛实验验证了该方法的有效性。

[No.50] Aligned Datasets Improve Detection of Latent Diffusion-Generated Images
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 对齐数据集提高检测潜在扩散生成图像的效果
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Anirudh Sundara Rajan;Utkarsh Ojha;Jedidiah Schloesser;Yong Jae Lee;
机构: University of Wisconsin-Madison;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28964
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11835
英摘: As latent diffusion models (LDMs) democratize image generation capabilities, there is a growing need to detect fake images. A good detector should focus on the generative models fingerprints while ignoring image properties such as semantic content, resolution, file format, etc. Fake image detectors are usually built in a data driven way, where a model is trained to separate real from fake images. Existing works primarily investigate network architecture choices and training recipes. In this work, we argue that in addition to these algorithmic choices, we also require a well aligned dataset of real/fake images to train a robust detector. For the family of LDMs, we propose a very simple way to achieve this: we reconstruct all the real images using the LDMs autoencoder, without any denoising operation. We then train a model to separate these real images from their reconstructions. The fakes created this way are extremely similar to the real ones in almost every aspect (e.g., size, aspect ratio, semantic content), which forces the model to look for the LDM decoders artifacts. We empirically show that this way of creating aligned real/fake datasets, which also sidesteps the computationally expensive denoising process, helps in building a detector that focuses less on spurious correlations, something that a very popular existing method is susceptible to. Finally, to demonstrate just how effective the alignment in a dataset can be, we build a detector using images that are not natural objects, and present promising results. Overall, our work identifies the subtle but significant issues that arise when training a fake image detector and proposes a simple and inexpensive solution to address these problems.
中摘: 随着潜在扩散模型（LDMs）普及了图像生成能力，检测假图像的需求也在增长。一个好的检测器应该专注于生成模型的独特特征，而忽略诸如语义内容、分辨率、文件格式等图像属性。现有的假图像检测器通常是以数据驱动的方式构建的，即训练一个模型来区分真实和虚假图像。现有研究主要探讨网络架构选择和训练方法。在这项工作中，我们提出除了这些算法选择外，还需要一个良好对齐的真实/虚假图像数据集来训练一个稳健的检测器。对于LDMs家族，我们提出了一种非常简单的方法来实现这一点：使用LDMs的自编码器重构所有真实图像，而不进行任何去噪操作。然后，训练一个模型来区分这些真实图像及其重构图像。这样生成的虚假图像在几乎所有方面（例如，大小、纵横比、语义内容）都与真实图像极其相似，这迫使模型寻找LDM解码器的痕迹。我们通过实验证明，这种创建对齐的真实/虚假数据集的方法，避免了昂贵的去噪过程，有助于构建一个较少依赖于虚假相关性的检测器，这是目前非常流行的一种方法所容易出现的问题。最后，为了展示数据集中对齐效果的有效性，我们使用非自然对象的图像构建了一个检测器，并展示了令人鼓舞的结果。总体而言，我们的工作指出了训练假图像检测器时出现的微妙但重要的问题，并提出了一种简单且成本低廉的解决方案来解决这些问题。

[No.51] Training-free LLM-generated Text Detection by Mining Token Probability Sequences
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 无训练数据的LLM生成文本检测通过挖掘令牌概率序列
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Yihuai Xu;Yongwei Wang;Yifei Bi;Huangsen Cao;Zhouhan Lin;Yu Zhao;Fei Wu;
机构: Zhejiang Gongshang University;Zhejiang University;Georgia Institute of Technology;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06072
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in generating high-quality texts across diverse domains. However, the potential misuse of LLMs has raised significant concerns, underscoring the urgent need for reliable detection of LLM-generated texts. Conventional training-based detectors often struggle with generalization, particularly in cross-domain and cross-model scenarios. In contrast, training-free methods, which focus on inherent discrepancies through carefully designed statistical features, offer improved generalization and interpretability. Despite this, existing training-free detection methods typically rely on global text sequence statistics, neglecting the modeling of local discriminative features, thereby limiting their detection efficacy. In this work, we introduce a novel training-free detector, termed \textbf{Lastde} that synergizes local and global statistics for enhanced detection. For the first time, we introduce time series analysis to LLM-generated text detection, capturing the temporal dynamics of token probability sequences. By integrating these local statistics with global ones, our detector reveals significant disparities between human and LLM-generated texts. We also propose an efficient alternative, \textbf{Lastde++} to enable real-time detection. Extensive experiments on six datasets involving cross-domain, cross-model, and cross-lingual detection scenarios, under both white-box and black-box settings, demonstrated that our method consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Furthermore, our approach exhibits greater robustness against paraphrasing attacks compared to existing baseline methods.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在生成跨领域高质量文本方面展现了非凡的能力。然而，LLM可能被不当使用的问题引起了广泛关注，突显了可靠检测LLM生成文本的迫切需求。传统的基于训练的方法在跨领域和跨模型场景中往往难以泛化。相比之下，无需训练的方法通过精心设计的统计特征来关注固有的差异，提供了更好的泛化能力和可解释性。尽管如此，现有的无需训练的检测方法通常依赖于全局文本序列统计，忽视了局部鉴别性特征的建模，从而限制了它们的检测效果。在本文中，我们介绍了一种新的无需训练的检测器，称为**Lastde**，它结合了局部和全局统计信息以增强检测能力。首次将时间序列分析应用于LLM生成文本的检测中，捕捉令牌概率序列的时间动态。通过将这些局部统计信息与全局统计信息集成，我们的检测器揭示了人类生成文本与LLM生成文本之间的显著差异。我们还提出了一个高效的替代方案**Lastde++**，以实现实时检测。在包含跨领域、跨模型和跨语言检测场景的六个数据集上进行的广泛实验，在白盒和黑盒设置下均证明了我们的方法能够持续获得最先进的性能。此外，我们的方法在对抗改写攻击方面的鲁棒性优于现有基准方法。

[No.52] TLDR: Token-Level Detective Reward Model for Large Vision Language Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 简而言之：面向大型视觉语言模型的 token 级侦探奖励模型
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Deqing Fu;Tong Xiao;Rui Wang;Wang Zhu;Pengchuan Zhang;Guan Pang;Robin Jia;Lawrence Chen;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04734
英摘: Although reward models have been successful in improving multimodal large language models, the reward models themselves remain brutal and contain minimal information. Notably, existing reward models only mimic human annotations by assigning only one binary feedback to any text, no matter how long the text is. In the realm of multimodal language models, where models are required to process both images and texts, a naive reward model may learn implicit biases toward texts and become less grounded in images. In this paper, we propose a $\textbf{T}$oken-$\textbf{L}$evel $\textbf{D}$etective $\textbf{R}$eward Model ($\textbf{TLDR}$) to provide fine-grained annotations to each text token. We first introduce a perturbation-based method to generate synthetic hard negatives and their token-level labels to train TLDR models. Then we show the rich usefulness of TLDR models both in assisting off-the-shelf models to self-correct their generations, and in serving as a hallucination evaluation tool. We show that TLDR automatically trains a token-level likelihood optimization, and can improve the base model's performance significantly. Finally, we show that TLDR models can significantly speed up human annotation by 3 times to acquire a broader range of high-quality vision language data.
中摘: 尽管奖励模型在提高多模态大型语言模型方面取得了成功，但这些奖励模型本身仍然非常残酷且包含的信息量很少。值得注意的是，现有的奖励模型仅通过为任何文本分配一个二元反馈来模仿人类注释，无论文本长度如何。在多模态语言模型领域，这些模型需要处理图像和文本，一个简单的奖励模型可能会学习到对文本的隐含偏见，并变得不那么依赖于图像。在本文中，我们提出了一种Token-Level Detective Reward Model（TLDR）来为每个文本标记提供精细粒度的注释。我们首先介绍了一种基于扰动的方法来生成合成的硬负样本及其标记的文本标记，用于训练TLDR模型。然后，我们展示了TLDR模型在辅助现成模型自我纠正其生成内容以及作为幻觉评估工具方面的丰富用途。我们表明，TLDR可以自动训练一种文本标记级别的似然性优化，并能显著提升基模型的表现。最后，我们展示了TLDR模型可以将人工注释的速度提高三倍，从而获取更广泛范围的高质量视觉语言数据。

[No.53] Towards a General Time Series Anomaly Detector with Adaptive Bottlenecks and Dual Adversarial Decoders
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 具有自适应瓶颈和双对抗解码器的一般时间序列异常检测器
类别: learning on time series and dynamical systems
作者: Qichao Shentu;Beibu Li;Kai Zhao;Yang Shu;Zhongwen Rao;Lujia Pan;Bin Yang;Chenjuan Guo;
机构: East China Normal University;;Huawei;Aalborg University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29176
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15273
英摘: Time series anomaly detection plays a vital role in a wide range of applications. Existing methods require training one specific model for each dataset, which exhibits limited generalization capability across different target datasets, hindering anomaly detection performance in various scenarios with scarce training data. Aiming at this problem, we propose constructing a general time series anomaly detection model, which is pre-trained on extensive multi-domain datasets and can subsequently apply to a multitude of downstream scenarios. The significant divergence of time series data across different domains presents two primary challenges in building such a general model: (1) meeting the diverse requirements of appropriate information bottlenecks tailored to different datasets in one unified model, and (2) enabling distinguishment between multiple normal and abnormal patterns, both are crucial for effective anomaly detection in various target scenarios. To tackle these two challenges, we propose a General time series anomaly Detector with Adaptive Bottlenecks and Dual Adversarial Decoders (DADA), which enables flexible selection of bottlenecks based on different data and explicitly enhances clear differentiation between normal and abnormal series. We conduct extensive experiments on nine target datasets from different domains. After pre-training on multi-domain data, DADA, serving as a zero-shot anomaly detector for these datasets, still achieves competitive or even superior results compared to those models tailored to each specific dataset. The code is made available at https://github.com/decisionintelligence/DADA.
中摘: 时间序列异常检测在广泛的应用中发挥着至关重要的作用。现有方法需要为每个数据集训练一个特定的模型，这在不同目标数据集上表现出有限的泛化能力，阻碍了在缺乏训练数据的各种场景中的异常检测性能。针对这一问题，我们提出构建一个通用的时间序列异常检测模型，该模型可以在大量跨领域的数据集上进行预训练，并随后应用于多种下游场景。不同类型领域的时间序列数据的巨大差异为构建此类通用模型带来了两大主要挑战：（1）在一个统一模型中满足不同数据集所需的不同信息瓶颈要求；（2）区分多个正常和异常模式，这对于各种目标场景中的有效异常检测至关重要。为应对这两个挑战，我们提出了一个具有自适应瓶颈和双对抗解码器的通用时间序列异常检测器（DADA），它能够根据不同的数据灵活选择瓶颈，并明确增强正常和异常系列之间的清晰区分。我们在来自不同领域的九个目标数据集上进行了广泛的实验。经过多领域数据的预训练后，DADA 作为这些数据集的零样本异常检测器，仍然取得了与专门为每个特定数据集训练的模型相当甚至更优的结果。相关代码已发布在 https://github.com/decisionintelligence/DADA。

[No.55] Humanizing the Machine: Proxy Attacks to Mislead LLM Detectors
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 机器人性化：代理攻击以误导大语言模型检测器
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Tianchun Wang;Yuanzhou Chen;Zichuan Liu;Zhanwen Chen;Haifeng Chen;Xiang Zhang;Wei Cheng;
机构: Pennsylvania State University;University of California, Los Angeles;Nanjing University;University of Virginia;NEC Laboratories;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29761
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19230
英摘: The advent of large language models (LLMs) has revolutionized the field of text generation, producing outputs that closely mimic human-like writing. Although academic and industrial institutions have developed detectors to prevent the malicious usage of LLM-generated texts, other research has doubt about the robustness of these systems. To stress test these detectors, we introduce a proxy-attack strategy that effortlessly compromises LLMs, causing them to produce outputs that align with human-written text and mislead detection systems. Our method attacks the source model by leveraging a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuned humanized small language model (SLM) in the decoding phase. Through an in-depth analysis, we demonstrate that our attack strategy is capable of generating responses that are indistinguishable to detectors, preventing them from differentiating between machine-generated and human-written text. We conduct systematic evaluations on extensive datasets using proxy-attacked open-source models, including Llama2-13B, Llama3-70B, and Mixtral-8*7B in both white- and black-box settings. Our findings show that the proxy-attack strategy effectively deceives the leading detectors, resulting in an average AUROC drop of 70.4% across multiple datasets, with a maximum drop of 90.3% on a single dataset. Furthermore, in cross-discipline scenarios, our strategy also bypasses these detectors, leading to a significant relative decrease of up to 90.9%, while in cross-language scenario, the drop reaches 91.3%. Despite our proxy-attack strategy successfully bypassing the detectors with such significant relative drops, we find that the generation quality of the attacked models remains preserved, even within a modest utility budget, when compared to the text produced by the original, unattacked source model.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的出现已经彻底改变了文本生成的领域，生成的输出与人类撰写的文字极为相似。尽管学术和工业机构已经开发出了检测器来防止LLM生成文本的恶意使用，但其他研究对此类系统的鲁棒性表示怀疑。为了测试这些检测器的可靠性，我们引入了一种代理攻击策略，这种策略能够轻松地攻破LLMs，使其生成的内容与人类撰写的文本一致，并误导检测系统。我们的方法在解码阶段利用了一种强化学习（RL）微调的人性化小型语言模型（SLM）攻击源模型。通过深入分析，我们证明了我们的攻击策略能够生成难以被检测器区分的响应，从而阻止它们区分机器生成的文本和人类撰写的文本。我们在广泛的数据库上进行了系统的评估，包括在白盒和黑盒设置下被代理攻击的开源模型Llama2-13B、Llama3-70B和Mixtral-8*7B。我们的发现表明，代理攻击策略有效地欺骗了领先的检测器，在多个数据集中的平均AUROC下降了70.4%，在单一数据集上的最大下降达到了90.3%。此外，在跨学科场景中，我们的策略也绕过了这些检测器，导致相对下降幅度高达90.9%，而在跨语言场景中，下降幅度更是达到了91.3%。尽管我们的代理攻击策略在相对下降幅度如此显著的情况下成功绕过了检测器，但我们发现，与原始未攻击源模型生成的文本相比，即使在有限的实用性预算内，受攻击模型的生成质量仍然保持不变。

[No.56] Cyclic Contrastive Knowledge Transfer for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 循环对比知识转移在开放词汇量目标检测中的应用
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Chuhan ZHANG;Chaoyang Zhu;Pingcheng Dong;Long Chen;Dong Zhang;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30109
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11005
英摘: In pursuit of detecting unstinted objects that extend beyond predefined categories, prior arts of open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) typically resort to pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) for base-to-novel category generalization. However, to mitigate the misalignment between upstream image-text pretraining and downstream region-level perception, additional supervisions are indispensable, eg, image-text pairs or pseudo annotations generated via self-training strategies. In this work, we propose CCKT-Det trained without any extra supervision. The proposed framework constructs a cyclic and dynamic knowledge transfer from language queries and visual region features extracted from VLMs, which forces the detector to closely align with the visual-semantic space of VLMs. Specifically, 1) we prefilter and inject semantic priors to guide the learning of queries, and 2) introduce a regional contrastive loss to improve the awareness of queries on novel objects. CCKT-Det can consistently improve performance as the scale of VLMs increases, all while requiring the detector at a moderate level of computation overhead. Comprehensive experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves performance gain of +2.9% and +10.2% AP50 over previous state-of-the-arts on the challenging COCO benchmark, both without and with a stronger teacher model.
中摘: 为了检测超出预定义类别的未限定对象，先前的开放词汇对象检测（OVD）研究通常依赖于预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）来进行基础到新类别的一般化。然而，为了缓解上游图像-文本预训练与下游区域级感知之间的不匹配问题，额外的监督信息是必不可少的，例如图像-文本对或通过自我训练策略生成的伪标注。在本工作中，我们提出了一种无需任何额外监督的CCKT-Det框架。该框架构建了从VLMs提取的语言查询和视觉区域特征之间的一种循环且动态的知识转移，迫使检测器紧密对齐于VLMs的视觉语义空间。具体而言，1) 我们预先筛选并注入语义先验以引导查询的学习，2) 引入区域对比损失以提高查询对新对象的认知能力。随着VLMs规模的增加，CCKT-Det可以持续提升性能，同时仅需适度增加计算开销。全面的实验结果表明，在挑战性的COCO基准测试上，我们的方法在没有更强教师模型的情况下分别实现了2.9%和10.2%的AP50性能增益，而在使用更强教师模型的情况下则进一步提升了性能。

[No.57] Activation Gradient based Poisoned Sample Detection Against Backdoor Attacks
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于激活梯度的后门攻击中毒样本检测
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Danni Yuan;Mingda Zhang;Shaokui Wei;Li Liu;Baoyuan Wu;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29418
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.06230
英摘: This work studies the task of poisoned sample detection for defending against data poisoning based backdoor attacks. Its core challenge is finding a generalizable and discriminative metric to distinguish between clean and various types of poisoned samples (e.g., various triggers, various poisoning ratios). Inspired by a common phenomenon in backdoor attacks that the backdoored model tend to map significantly different poisoned and clean samples within the target class to similar activation areas, we introduce a novel perspective of the circular distribution of the gradients w.r.t. sample activation, dubbed gradient circular distribution (GCD). And, we find two interesting observations based on GCD. One is that the GCD of samples in the target class is much more dispersed than that in the clean class. The other is that in the GCD of target class, poisoned and clean samples are clearly separated. Inspired by above two observations, we develop an innovative three-stage poisoned sample detection approach, called Activation Gradient based Poisoned sample Detection (AGPD). First, we calculate GCDs of all classes from the model trained on the untrustworthy dataset. Then, we identify the target class(es) based on the difference on GCD dispersion between target and clean classes. Last, we filter out poisoned samples within the identified target class(es) based on the clear separation between poisoned and clean samples. Extensive experiments under various settings of backdoor attacks demonstrate the superior detection performance of the proposed method to existing poisoned detection approaches according to sample activation-based metrics.
中摘: 这项工作研究了中毒样本检测的任务，旨在防御基于后门的数据中毒攻击。其核心挑战在于找到一个可泛化且区分力强的度量标准，以区分干净样本和各种类型的中毒样本（例如不同类型的触发器和不同的污染比例）。受后门攻击中常见现象的启发，即被植入后门的模型倾向于将目标类中的显著不同的干净样本和中毒样本映射到相似的激活区域，我们引入了一种新的视角，称为样本激活相对于梯度的圆分布（Gradient Circular Distribution，GCD）。基于GCD，我们发现了两个有趣的观察结果：一是目标类中的样本圆分布比干净类中的样本圆分布更为分散；二是目标类的圆分布中，中毒样本和干净样本被清晰地分离。受到上述两个观察结果的启发，我们开发了一种创新的三阶段中毒样本检测方法，称为基于激活梯度的中毒样本检测（AGPD）。首先，我们计算出训练于不可信数据集上的所有类别的GCD。然后，根据目标类与干净类之间圆分布分散度的差异来识别目标类。最后，基于中毒样本和干净样本之间的清晰分离，从已识别的目标类中筛选出中毒样本。在各种后门攻击设置下的广泛实验表明，该方法根据样本激活度量标准优于现有的中毒样本检测方法，在检测性能方面表现出优越性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.58] Local-Prompt: Extensible Local Prompts for Few-Shot Out-of-Distribution Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 可扩展本地提示用于少量示例的分布外检测
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Fanhu Zeng;Zhen Cheng;Fei Zhu;Hongxin Wei;Xu-Yao Zhang;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;Hong Kong Institute of Science & Innovation;Southern University of Science and Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30380
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04796
英摘: Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection, aiming to distinguish outliers from known categories, has gained prominence in practical scenarios. Recently, the advent of vision-language models (VLM) has heightened interest in enhancing OOD detection for VLM through few-shot tuning. However, existing methods mainly focus on optimizing global prompts, ignoring refined utilization of local information with regard to outliers. Motivated by this, we freeze global prompts and introduce Local-Prompt, a novel coarse-to-fine tuning paradigm to emphasize regional enhancement with local prompts. Our method comprises two integral components: global prompt guided negative augmentation and local prompt enhanced regional regularization. The former utilizes frozen, coarse global prompts as guiding cues to incorporate negative augmentation, thereby leveraging local outlier knowledge. The latter employs trainable local prompts and a regional regularization to capture local information effectively, aiding in outlier identification. We also propose regional-related metric to empower the enrichment of OOD detection. Moreover, since our approach explores enhancing local prompts only, it can be seamlessly integrated with trained global prompts during inference to boost the performance. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and potential of our method. Notably, our method reduces average FPR95 by 5.17% against state-of-the-art method in 4-shot tuning on challenging ImageNet-1k dataset, even outperforming 16-shot results of previous methods. Code is released at https://github.com/AuroraZengfh/Local-Prompt.
中摘: 分布外（Out-of-Distribution，OOD）检测旨在区分异常值与已知类别，近年来在实际应用场景中引起了广泛关注。最近，视觉语言模型（Vision-Language Models，VLM）的出现进一步激发了通过少样本调优来提升VLM的OOD检测能力的兴趣。然而，现有的方法主要集中在优化全局提示上，忽略了对局部信息尤其是异常值的精细利用。受此启发，我们冻结了全局提示，并引入了Local-Prompt这一新颖的粗细调优范式，以强调基于局部提示的区域增强。我们的方法包括两个核心组件：全局提示引导的负增广和局部提示增强的区域正则化。前者利用冻结的粗略全局提示作为指导线索，纳入负增广，从而利用局部异常知识。后者采用可训练的局部提示和区域正则化来有效捕捉局部信息，有助于异常值的识别。此外，我们还提出了一种与区域相关的度量标准，以增强OOD检测的能力。由于我们的方法仅探索局部提示的增强，因此可以在推理过程中无缝集成训练好的全局提示，从而提高性能。全面的实验展示了我们方法的有效性和潜力。值得注意的是，在具有挑战性的ImageNet-1k数据集上进行的四次调优实验表明，我们的方法将平均FPR95降低了5.17%，甚至超过了先前方法在16次调优实验中的表现。代码发布在https://github.com/AuroraZengfh/Local-Prompt。

[No.62] Detecting Backdoor Samples in Contrastive Language Image Pretraining
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 检测对比语言图像预训练中的后门样本
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Hanxun Huang;Sarah Monazam Erfani;Yige Li;Xingjun Ma;James Bailey;
机构: University of Melbourne;Singapore Management University;Fudan University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30032
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01385
英摘: Contrastive language-image pretraining (CLIP) has been found to be vulnerable to poisoning backdoor attacks where the adversary can achieve an almost perfect attack success rate on CLIP models by poisoning only 0.01\% of the training dataset. This raises security concerns on the current practice of pretraining large-scale models on unscrutinized web data using CLIP. In this work, we analyze the representations of backdoor-poisoned samples learned by CLIP models and find that they exhibit unique characteristics in their local subspace, i.e., their local neighborhoods are far more sparse than that of clean samples. Based on this finding, we conduct a systematic study on detecting CLIP backdoor attacks and show that these attacks can be easily and efficiently detected by traditional density ratio-based local outlier detectors, whereas existing backdoor sample detection methods fail. Our experiments also reveal that an unintentional backdoor already exists in the original CC3M dataset and has been trained into a popular open-source model released by OpenCLIP. Based on our detector, one can clean up a million-scale web dataset (e.g., CC3M) efficiently within 15 minutes using 4 Nvidia A100 GPUs. The code is publicly available in our \href{https://github.com/HanxunH/Detect-CLIP-Backdoor-Samples}{GitHub repository}.
中摘: 对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）模型被发现容易受到后门攻击的毒化，攻击者只需污染训练数据集的0.01%即可几乎实现完美的攻击成功率。这引发了对当前使用CLIP在未经仔细审查的网络数据上预训练大规模模型的安全性担忧。在本研究中，我们分析了CLIP模型学习到的后门污染样本的表示，并发现它们在其局部子空间中表现出独特的特征，即它们的局部邻域比干净样本的局部邻域更为稀疏。基于这一发现，我们系统地研究了检测CLIP后门攻击的方法，并表明传统的基于密度比率的局部离群点检测器可以轻松且高效地检测这些攻击，而现有的后门样本检测方法则失败了。我们的实验还揭示，在原始CC3M数据集中已经存在一个无意中的后门，并已被训练进由OpenCLIP发布的流行开源模型中。利用我们的检测器，可以在15分钟内使用4块Nvidia A100 GPU高效地清理百万级别的网络数据集（例如CC3M）。相关代码已公开发布在我们的GitHub仓库中：[https://github.com/HanxunH/Detect-CLIP-Backdoor-Samples](https://github.com/HanxunH/Detect-CLIP-Backdoor-Samples)。

[No.65] SAGEPhos: Sage Bio-Coupled and Augmented Fusion for Phosphorylation Site Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: SAGEPhos：基于生物耦合和增强融合的磷酸化位点检测方法
类别: applications to physical sciences (physics, chemistry, biology, etc.)
作者: Jingjie Zhang;Hanqun CAO;Zijun Gao;Xiaorui Wang;Chunbin Gu;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;;Zhejiang University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28768
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07384
英摘: Phosphorylation site prediction based on kinase-substrate interaction plays a vital role in understanding cellular signaling pathways and disease mechanisms. Computational methods for this task can be categorized into kinase-family-focused and individual kinase-targeted approaches. Individual kinase-targeted methods have gained prominence for their ability to explore a broader protein space and provide more precise target information for kinase inhibitors. However, most existing individual kinase-based approaches focus solely on sequence inputs, neglecting crucial structural information. To address this limitation, we introduce SAGEPhos (Structure-aware kinAse-substrate bio-coupled and bio-auGmented nEtwork for Phosphorylation site prediction), a novel framework that modifies the semantic space of main protein inputs using auxiliary inputs at two distinct modality levels. At the inter-modality level, SAGEPhos introduces a Bio-Coupled Modal Fusion method, distilling essential kinase sequence information to refine task-oriented local substrate feature space, creating a shared semantic space that captures crucial kinase-substrate interaction patterns. Within the substrate's intra-modality domain, it focuses on Bio-Augmented Fusion, emphasizing 2D local sequence information while selectively incorporating 3D spatial information from predicted structures to complement the sequence space. Moreover, to address the lack of structural information in current datasets, we contribute a new, refined phosphorylation site prediction dataset, which incorporates crucial structural elements and will serve as a new benchmark for the field. Experimental results demonstrate that SAGEPhos significantly outperforms baseline methods. We release the SAGEPhos models and code at https://github.com/ZhangJJ26/SAGEPhos.
中摘: 基于激酶-底物相互作用的磷酸化位点预测在理解细胞信号通路和疾病机制方面发挥着重要作用。针对这一任务的计算方法可以分为激酶家族聚焦型和单个激酶靶向型两大类。单个激酶靶向型方法因其能够探索更广泛的蛋白质空间并为激酶抑制剂提供更精确的目标信息而备受关注。然而，大多数现有的单个激酶基方法仅专注于序列输入，忽视了关键的结构信息。为了解决这一局限性，我们引入了SAGEPhos（结构感知激酶-底物生物耦合和生物增强网络，用于磷酸化位点预测）这一新型框架，该框架通过两个不同的模态层次使用辅助输入来修改主要蛋白质输入的语义空间。在跨模态层面，SAGEPhos引入了一种生物耦合模态融合方法，提取关键的激酶序列信息以细化任务导向的局部底物特征空间，创建了一个共享的语义空间，捕捉到了重要的激酶-底物相互作用模式。在底物的内在模态领域内，它侧重于生物增强融合，强调2D局部序列信息，并选择性地整合预测结构中的3D空间信息以补充序列空间。此外，为了应对当前数据集中缺乏结构信息的问题，我们贡献了一个新的、改进的磷酸化位点预测数据集，该数据集包含了关键的结构元素，并将成为该领域的新的基准数据集。实验结果表明，SAGEPhos显著优于基线方法。我们已在https://github.com/ZhangJJ26/SAGEPhos发布了SAGEPhos模型和代码。

[No.66] 3D-AffordanceLLM: Harnessing Large Language Models for Open-Vocabulary Affordance Detection in 3D Worlds
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 3D-AffordanceLLM：利用大型语言模型在3D世界中进行开放词汇 affordance 检测
类别: applications to robotics, autonomy, planning
作者: Hengshuo Chu;Xiang Deng;Qi Lv;Xiaoyang Chen;Yinchuan Li;Jianye HAO;Liqiang Nie;
机构: ;Harbin Institute of Technology;Huawei;Tianjin University;Shandong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30275
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20041
英摘: 3D Affordance detection is a challenging problem with broad applications on various robotic tasks. Existing methods typically formulate the detection paradigm as a label-based semantic segmentation task. This paradigm relies on predefined labels and lacks the ability to comprehend complex natural language, resulting in limited generalization in open-world scene. To address these limitations, we reformulate the traditional affordance detection paradigm into \textit{Instruction Reasoning Affordance Segmentation} (IRAS) task. This task is designed to output a affordance mask region given a query reasoning text, which avoids fixed categories of input labels. We accordingly propose the \textit{3D-AffordanceLLM} (3D-ADLLM), a framework designed for reasoning affordance detection in 3D open-scene. Specifically, 3D-ADLLM introduces large language models (LLMs) to 3D affordance perception with a custom-designed decoder for generating affordance masks, thus achieving open-world reasoning affordance detection. In addition, given the scarcity of 3D affordance datasets for training large models, we seek to extract knowledge from general segmentation data and transfer it to affordance detection. Thus, we propose a multi-stage training strategy that begins with a novel pre-training task, i.e., \textit{Referring Object Part Segmentation}~(ROPS). This stage is designed to equip the model with general recognition and segmentation capabilities at the object-part level. Then followed by fine-tuning with the IRAS task, 3D-ADLLM obtains the reasoning ability for affordance detection. In summary, 3D-ADLLM leverages the rich world knowledge and human-object interaction reasoning ability of LLMs, achieving approximately an 8\% improvement in mIoU on open-vocabulary affordance detection tasks.
中摘: 三维抓取检测是一个具有广泛应用前景的挑战性问题，特别是在各种机器人任务中。现有方法通常将检测范式形式化为基于标签的语义分割任务。这种范式依赖于预定义的标签，缺乏理解和处理复杂自然语言的能力，导致在开放世界场景中的泛化能力有限。为了应对这些限制，我们将传统的抓取检测范式重新表述为“指令推理抓取分割”（IRAS）任务。该任务旨在根据查询推理文本输出一个抓取掩码区域，从而避免固定类别输入标签。我们相应地提出了“3D-AffordanceLLM”（3D-ADLLM），一个用于三维开放场景中推理抓取检测的框架。具体而言，3D-ADLLM 引入了大型语言模型（LLMs）到三维抓取感知中，并设计了一个自定义解码器来生成抓取掩码，从而实现开放世界推理抓取检测。此外，由于训练大型模型时三维抓取数据集稀缺，我们寻求从通用分割数据中提取知识并将其转移到抓取检测中。因此，我们提出了一种多阶段训练策略，该策略始于一种新颖的预训练任务，即“引用对象部分分割”（ROPS）。这一阶段旨在使模型在对象部分级别具备一般识别和分割能力。随后通过使用 IRAS 任务进行微调，3D-ADLLM 获得了抓取检测的推理能力。总之，3D-ADLLM 利用了大型语言模型丰富的世界知识和人类-物体交互的推理能力，在开放词汇抓取检测任务中实现了约 8% 的 mIoU 提升。

[No.69] IRIS: LLM-Assisted Static Analysis for Detecting Security Vulnerabilities
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: IRIS：基于LLM的静态分析以检测安全漏洞
类别: neurosymbolic & hybrid AI systems (physics-informed, logic & formal reasoning, etc.)
作者: Ziyang Li;Saikat Dutta;Mayur Naik;
机构: University of Pennsylvania;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30698
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17238
英摘: Software is prone to security vulnerabilities. Program analysis tools to detect them have limited effectiveness in practice due to their reliance on human labeled specifications. Large language models (or LLMs) have shown impressive code generation capabilities but they cannot do complex reasoning over code to detect such vulnerabilities especially since this task requires whole-repository analysis. We propose IRIS, a neuro-symbolic approach that systematically combines LLMs with static analysis to perform whole-repository reasoning for security vulnerability detection. Specifically, IRIS leverages LLMs to infer taint specifications and perform contextual analysis, alleviating needs for human specifications and inspection. For evaluation, we curate a new dataset, CWE-Bench-Java, comprising 120 manually validated security vulnerabilities in real-world Java projects. A state-of-the-art static analysis tool CodeQL detects only 27 of these vulnerabilities whereas IRIS with GPT-4 detects 55 (+28) and improves upon CodeQL's average false discovery rate by 5% points. Furthermore, IRIS identifies 4 previously unknown vulnerabilities which cannot be found by existing tools. IRIS is available publicly at https://github.com/iris-sast/iris.
中摘: 软件容易存在安全漏洞。由于程序分析工具依赖于人工标注的规范，它们在实际应用中的检测效果有限。大型语言模型（或LLM）展示了出色的代码生成能力，但它们无法对代码进行复杂的推理以检测此类漏洞，尤其是因为这项任务需要整个仓库级别的分析。我们提出了一种名为IRIS的神经符号方法，该方法系统地结合了LLM与静态分析，以执行整个仓库级别的安全漏洞检测推理。具体而言，IRIS利用LLM推断污点规范并进行上下文分析，从而减少对人工规范和检查的需求。为了评估其性能，我们整理了一个新的数据集CWE-Bench-Java，包含120个经过手动验证的真实世界Java项目的安全漏洞。最先进的静态分析工具CodeQL仅检测出其中27个漏洞，而IRIS使用GPT-4检测出了55个（+28个），并将CodeQL的平均误报率降低了5个百分点。此外，IRIS还识别出4个之前未知的安全漏洞，这些漏洞现有的工具无法找到。IRIS可以在https://github.com/iris-sast/iris公开获取。

[No.70] IMDPrompter: Adapting SAM to Image Manipulation Detection by Cross-View Automated Prompt Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: IMDPrompter: 通过跨视图自动化提示学习适应SAM的图像修改检测
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Quan Zhang;Yuxin Qi;Xi Tang;Jinwei Fang;Xi Lin;Ke Zhang;Chun Yuan;
机构: Tsinghua University;;University of Science and Technology of China;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27891
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.02454
英摘: Using extensive training data from SA-1B, the Segment Anything Model (SAM) has demonstrated exceptional generalization and zero-shot capabilities, attracting widespread attention in areas such as medical image segmentation and remote sensing image segmentation. However, its performance in the field of image manipulation detection remains largely unexplored and unconfirmed. There are two main challenges in applying SAM to image manipulation detection: a) reliance on manual prompts, and b) the difficulty of single-view information in supporting cross-dataset generalization. To address these challenges, we develops a cross-view prompt learning paradigm called IMDPrompter based on SAM. Benefiting from the design of automated prompts, IMDPrompter no longer relies on manual guidance, enabling automated detection and localization. Additionally, we propose components such as Cross-view Feature Perception, Optimal Prompt Selection, and Cross-View Prompt Consistency, which facilitate cross-view perceptual learning and guide SAM to generate accurate masks. Extensive experimental results from five datasets (CASIA, Columbia, Coverage, IMD2020, and NIST16) validate the effectiveness of our proposed method.
中摘: 使用来自SA-1B的大量训练数据，Segment Anything模型（SAM）展示了出色的泛化能力和零样本能力，在医学图像分割和遥感图像分割等领域引起了广泛关注。然而，其在图像操纵检测领域的表现仍然主要未被探索和验证。应用SAM进行图像操纵检测面临两大主要挑战：a) 对手动提示的依赖；b) 单视角信息难以支持跨数据集泛化。为应对这些挑战，我们基于SAM开发了一种跨视角提示学习范式，称为IMDPrompter。得益于自动提示的设计，IMDPrompter不再依赖于手动指导，能够实现自动化检测和定位。此外，我们还提出了跨视角特征感知、最优提示选择以及跨视角提示一致性等组件，这些组件有助于跨视角感知学习，并引导SAM生成准确的掩码。来自五个数据集（CASIA、哥伦比亚大学、Coverage、IMD2020和NIST16）的广泛实验结果验证了我们所提出方法的有效性。

[No.71] TDDBench: A Benchmark for Training data detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: TDDBench：训练数据检测基准
类别: datasets and benchmarks
作者: Zhihao Zhu;Yi Yang;Defu Lian;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28739
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.03363
英摘: Training Data Detection (TDD) is a task aimed at determining whether a specific data instance is used to train a machine learning model. In the computer security literature, TDD is also referred to as Membership Inference Attack (MIA). Given its potential to assess the risks of training data breaches, ensure copyright authentication, and verify model unlearning, TDD has garnered significant attention in recent years, leading to the development of numerous methods. Despite these advancements, there is no comprehensive benchmark to thoroughly evaluate the effectiveness of TDD methods. In this work, we introduce TDDBench, which consists of 13 datasets spanning three data modalities: image, tabular, and text. We benchmark 21 different TDD methods across four detection paradigms and evaluate their performance from five perspectives: average detection performance, best detection performance, memory consumption, and computational efficiency in both time and memory. With TDDBench, researchers can identify bottlenecks and areas for improvement in TDD algorithms, while practitioners can make informed trade-offs between effectiveness and efficiency when selecting TDD algorithms for specific use cases. Our extensive experiments also reveal the generally unsatisfactory performance of TDD algorithms across different datasets. To enhance accessibility and reproducibility, we open-source TDDBench for the research community at https://github.com/zzh9568/TDDBench.
中摘: 训练数据检测（TDD）是一项旨在确定特定数据实例是否用于训练机器学习模型的任务。在计算机安全文献中，TDD 也被称为成员推断攻击（MIA）。鉴于其评估训练数据泄露风险、确保版权认证和验证模型遗忘的有效性潜力，TDD 近年来引起了广泛关注，推动了多种方法的发展。尽管取得了这些进展，但目前还没有全面的基准来充分评估 TDD 方法的效果。在本工作中，我们引入了 TDDBench，它包含13个数据集，涵盖了三种数据模态：图像、表格和文本。我们在四个检测范式下对21种不同的TDD方法进行了基准测试，并从五个方面评估了它们的性能：平均检测性能、最佳检测性能、内存消耗以及时间和内存方面的计算效率。通过TDDBench，研究人员可以识别TDD算法中的瓶颈和改进区域，而实践者则可以在选择适用于特定应用场景的TDD算法时做出知情权衡。我们的大量实验还揭示了不同数据集中TDD算法普遍表现不佳的现象。为了增强可访问性和可重复性，我们已在https://github.com/zzh9568/TDDBench 开源了TDDBench。

[No.73] SpaceGNN: Multi-Space Graph Neural Network for Node Anomaly Detection with Extremely Limited Labels
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: SpaceGNN：基于极少量标签的节点异常检测多空间图神经网络
类别: learning on graphs and other geometries & topologies
作者: Xiangyu Dong;Xingyi Zhang;Lei Chen;Mingxuan Yuan;Sibo Wang;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;Huawei;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29569
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03201
英摘: Node Anomaly Detection (NAD) has gained significant attention in the deep learning community due to its diverse applications in real-world scenarios. Existing NAD methods primarily embed graphs within a single Euclidean space, while overlooking the potential of non-Euclidean spaces. Besides, to address the prevalent issue of limited supervision in real NAD tasks, previous methods tend to leverage synthetic data to collect auxiliary information, which is not an effective solution as shown in our experiments. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel SpaceGNN model designed for NAD tasks with extremely limited labels. Specifically, we provide deeper insights into a task-relevant framework by empirically analyzing the benefits of different spaces for node representations, based on which, we design a Learnable Space Projection function that effectively encodes nodes into suitable spaces. Besides, we introduce the concept of weighted homogeneity, which we empirically and theoretically validate as an effective coefficient during information propagation. This concept inspires the design of the Distance Aware Propagation module. Furthermore, we propose the Multiple Space Ensemble module, which extracts comprehensive information for NAD under conditions of extremely limited supervision. Our findings indicate that this module is more beneficial than data augmentation techniques for NAD. Extensive experiments conducted on 9 real datasets confirm the superiority of SpaceGNN, which outperforms the best rival by an average of 8.55% in AUC and 4.31% in F1 scores. Our code is available at https://github.com/xydong127/SpaceGNN.
中摘: 节点异常检测（NAD）在深度学习社区中因其在实际应用场景中的多样化应用而引起了广泛关注。现有的NAD方法主要将图嵌入单一的欧几里得空间中，而忽视了非欧几里得空间的潜在价值。此外，为了应对真实NAD任务中普遍存在的监督有限问题，先前的方法倾向于利用合成数据来收集辅助信息，但我们的实验表明这并不是一个有效的解决方案。为克服这些挑战，我们提出了一种新型的SpaceGNN模型，专门针对标签极为有限的节点异常检测任务。具体而言，我们通过实证分析不同空间对节点表示的不同益处，提供了更深入的任务相关框架见解，并据此设计了一个可学习的空间投影函数，该函数能够有效将节点编码到合适的空间中。此外，我们引入了加权同质性的概念，在我们的实证和理论验证中，该概念被证明是一个有效的传播信息系数。这一概念启发了距离感知传播模块的设计。进一步地，我们提出了多空间集成模块，在极其有限的监督条件下，该模块能够提取全面的信息用于节点异常检测。我们的研究发现，这一模块比数据增强技术更能提高NAD的效果。在对9个真实数据集进行的广泛实验中，SpaceGNN表现出色，平均在AUC上优于最佳竞争对手8.55%，在F1分数上优于4.31%。我们的代码可在https://github.com/xydong127/SpaceGNN获取。

[No.75] Intent3D: 3D Object Detection in RGB-D Scans Based on Human Intention
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: Intent3D：基于人类意图的RGB-D扫描中三维物体检测
类别: datasets and benchmarks
作者: Weitai Kang;Mengxue Qu;Jyoti Kini;Yunchao Wei;Mubarak Shah;Yan Yan;
机构: University of Illinois at Chicago;Beijing Jiao Tong University;University of Central Florida;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18295
英摘: In real-life scenarios, humans seek out objects in the 3D world to fulfill their daily needs or intentions. This inspires us to introduce 3D intention grounding, a new task in 3D object detection employing RGB-D, based on human intention, such as "I want something to support my back". Closely related, 3D visual grounding focuses on understanding human reference. To achieve detection based on human intention, it relies on humans to observe the scene, reason out the target that aligns with their intention ("pillow" in this case), and finally provide a reference to the AI system, such as "A pillow on the couch". Instead, 3D intention grounding challenges AI agents to automatically observe, reason and detect the desired target solely based on human intention. To tackle this challenge, we introduce the new Intent3D dataset, consisting of 44,990 intention texts associated with 209 fine-grained classes from 1,042 scenes of the ScanNet dataset. We also establish several baselines based on different language-based 3D object detection models on our benchmark. Finally, we propose IntentNet, our unique approach, designed to tackle this intention-based detection problem. It focuses on three key aspects: intention understanding, reasoning to identify object candidates, and cascaded adaptive learning that leverages the intrinsic priority logic of different losses for multiple objective optimization. Project Page: https://weitaikang.github.io/Intent3D-webpage/
中摘: 在实际场景中，人类会在三维（3D）世界中寻找对象以满足日常需求或意图。这启发我们引入基于人类意图的3D意图定位这一新任务，它是基于RGB-D的3D物体检测任务。例如，“我想要一个支撑背部的东西”。与之密切相关的是3D视觉定位，它侧重于理解人类参考。为了基于人类意图进行检测，它依赖人类观察场景，推理出与其意图相匹配的目标（如“枕头”），最后为AI系统提供参考，例如“沙发上有一个枕头”。相比之下，3D意图定位则挑战AI代理仅基于人类意图自动观察、推理和检测所需目标。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了新的Intent3D数据集，该数据集包含与ScanNet数据集中1042个场景相关的44,990条意图文本，并且细分为209个类别。此外，我们在基准测试上建立了几种基于不同语言驱动的3D物体检测模型的基础方法。最后，我们提出了IntentNet，这是我们独特的解决方案，旨在解决这一基于意图的检测问题。IntentNet专注于三个方面：意图理解、推理以识别对象候选以及级联自适应学习，该学习方式利用多种损失函数的内在优先逻辑进行多目标优化。项目页面：https://weitaikang.github.io/Intent3D-webpage/

[No.76] An Auditing Test to Detect Behavioral Shift in Language Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 一种审计测试以检测语言模型的行为变化
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Leo Richter;Xuanli He;Pasquale Minervini;Matt Kusner;
机构: University College London;University of Edinburgh;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28786
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19406
英摘: As language models (LMs) approach human-level performance, a comprehensive understanding of their behavior becomes crucial. This includes evaluating capabilities, biases, task performance, and alignment with societal values. Extensive initial evaluations, including red teaming and diverse benchmarking, can establish a model's behavioral profile. However, subsequent fine-tuning or deployment modifications may alter these behaviors in unintended ways. We present a method for continual Behavioral Shift Auditing (BSA) in LMs. Building on recent work in hypothesis testing, our auditing test detects behavioral shifts solely through model generations. Our test compares model generations from a baseline model to those of the model under scrutiny and provides theoretical guarantees for change detection while controlling false positives. The test features a configurable tolerance parameter that adjusts sensitivity to behavioral changes for different use cases. We evaluate our approach using two case studies: monitoring changes in (a) toxicity and (b) translation performance. We find that the test is able to detect meaningful changes in behavior distributions using just hundreds of examples.
中摘: 随着语言模型（LMs）接近人类水平的表现，对其行为的全面理解变得至关重要。这包括评估其能力、偏见、任务性能以及与社会价值观的对齐。通过广泛的初步评估，包括红队测试和多样化的基准测试，可以建立模型的行为档案。然而，后续的微调或部署修改可能会以意想不到的方式改变这些行为。我们提出了一种在LMs中持续进行行为变化审计的方法。基于最近在假设检验方面的研究工作，我们的审计测试仅通过模型生成来检测行为变化，并提供了在控制假阳性的同时进行变化检测的理论保证。该测试包含一个可配置的容差参数，可以根据不同的应用场景调整对行为变化的敏感度。我们使用两个案例研究评估了这种方法：监控（a）毒性以及（b）翻译性能的变化。我们发现，该测试仅使用几百个例子就能检测到行为分布中的有意义变化。

[No.77] MetaOOD: Automatic Selection of OOD Detection Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: MetaOOD：自动选择异常值检测模型
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Yuehan Qin;Yichi Zhang;Yi Nian;Xueying Ding;Yue Zhao;
机构: University of Southern California;;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30662
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03074
英摘: How can we automatically select an out-of-distribution (OOD) detection model for various underlying tasks? This is crucial for maintaining the reliability of open-world applications by identifying data distribution shifts, particularly in critical domains such as online transactions, autonomous driving, and real-time patient diagnosis. Despite the availability of numerous OOD detection methods, the challenge of selecting an optimal model for diverse tasks remains largely underexplored, especially in scenarios lacking ground truth labels. In this work, we introduce MetaOOD, the first zero-shot, unsupervised framework that utilizes meta-learning to select an OOD detection model automatically. As a meta-learning approach, MetaOOD leverages historical performance data of existing methods across various benchmark OOD detection datasets, enabling the effective selection of a suitable model for new datasets without the need for labeled data at the test time. To quantify task similarities more accurately, we introduce language model-based embeddings that capture the distinctive OOD characteristics of both datasets and detection models. Through extensive experimentation with 24 unique test dataset pairs to choose from among 11 OOD detection models, we demonstrate that MetaOOD significantly outperforms existing methods and only brings marginal time overhead. Our results, validated by Wilcoxon statistical tests, show that MetaOOD surpasses a diverse group of 11 baselines, including established OOD detectors and advanced unsupervised selection methods.
中摘: 我们如何自动选择适用于各种基础任务的异常分布外（OOD）检测模型？这在维护开放世界应用的可靠性方面至关重要，通过识别数据分布的变化，特别是在在线交易、自动驾驶和实时患者诊断等关键领域。尽管存在多种OOD检测方法，但在缺乏真实标签的情况下，为多样化的任务选择最优模型的挑战仍然很大程度上未被探索。在本文中，我们介绍了MetaOOD，这是首个零样本、无监督框架，利用元学习自动选择OOD检测模型。作为元学习的方法，MetaOOD利用了现有方法在多个基准OOD检测数据集上的历史性能数据，在测试时无需标记数据即可有效选择适合新数据集的模型。为了更准确地量化任务相似性，我们引入了基于语言模型的嵌入，捕捉数据集和检测模型的独特OOD特征。通过在11种OOD检测模型中选择24个独特的测试数据集对进行广泛的实验，我们证明了MetaOOD显著优于现有方法，并仅带来轻微的时间开销。我们的结果经由Wilcoxon统计检验验证，显示MetaOOD超越了包括已建立的OOD检测器和高级无监督选择方法在内的多样化基线群体。

[No.78] Gaussian-Det: Learning Closed-Surface Gaussians for 3D Object Detection
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 高斯检测：学习闭合表面高斯分布进行三维物体检测
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Hongru Yan;Yu Zheng;Yueqi Duan;
机构: ;Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30438
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01404
英摘: Skins wrapping around our bodies, leathers covering over the sofa, sheet metal coating the car - it suggests that objects are enclosed by a series of continuous surfaces, which provides us with informative geometry prior for objectness deduction. In this paper, we propose Gaussian-Det which leverages Gaussian Splatting as surface representation for multi-view based 3D object detection. Unlike existing monocular or NeRF-based methods which depict the objects via discrete positional data, Gaussian-Det models the objects in a continuous manner by formulating the input Gaussians as feature descriptors on a mass of partial surfaces. Furthermore, to address the numerous outliers inherently introduced by Gaussian splatting, we accordingly devise a Closure Inferring Module (CIM) for the comprehensive surface-based objectness deduction. CIM firstly estimates the probabilistic feature residuals for partial surfaces given the underdetermined nature of Gaussian Splatting, which are then coalesced into a holistic representation on the overall surface closure of the object proposal. In this way, the surface information Gaussian-Det exploits serves as the prior on the quality and reliability of objectness and the information basis of proposal refinement. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that Gaussian-Det outperforms various existing approaches, in terms of both average precision and recall.
中摘: 皮肤包裹在我们的身体上，皮革覆盖在沙发表面，钢板覆盖在汽车上——这表明物体被一系列连续表面所包围，为我们提供了关于物体性的几何先验信息。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种Gaussian-Det方法，利用高斯点积作为多视图基于3D物体检测的表面表示。与现有的单目或多目或NeRF（神经辐射场）方法不同，这些方法通过离散的位置数据来描绘物体，Gaussian-Det以连续的方式建模物体，通过将输入的高斯函数形式化为大量部分表面的特征描述子来进行。此外，为了应对由高斯点积固有引入的大量异常值，我们相应地设计了一个闭合推理模块（CIM），用于全面的基于表面的物体性推断。CIM首先估计给定高斯点积未确定性质的部分表面的概率特征残差，然后将这些残差整合成物体提议的整体表面闭合的综合表示。这样，Gaussian-Det所利用的表面信息就成为物体性质量与可靠性的先验，并且是提案细化的信息基础。在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，Gaussian-Det在平均精度和召回率方面均优于各种现有方法。

[No.79] Open-Set Graph Anomaly Detection via Normal Structure Regularisation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 开放集图异常检测通过正常结构正则化
类别: learning on graphs and other geometries & topologies
作者: Qizhou Wang;Guansong Pang;Mahsa Salehi;Xiaokun Xia;Christopher Leckie;
机构: ;Singapore Management University;Monash University;University of Melbourne;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28580
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.06835
英摘: This paper considers an important Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) task, namely open-set GAD, which aims to train a detection model using a small number of normal and anomaly nodes (referred to as seen anomalies) to detect both seen anomalies and unseen anomalies (i.e., anomalies that cannot be illustrated the training anomalies). Those labelled training data provide crucial prior knowledge about abnormalities for GAD models, enabling substantially reduced detection errors. However, current supervised GAD methods tend to over-emphasise fitting the seen anomalies, leading to many errors of detecting the unseen anomalies as normal nodes. Further, existing open-set AD models were introduced to handle Euclidean data, failing to effectively capture discriminative features from graph structure and node attributes for GAD. In this work, we propose a novel open-set GAD approach, namely normal structure regularisation (NSReg), to achieve generalised detection ability to unseen anomalies, while maintaining its effectiveness on detecting seen anomalies. The key idea in NSReg is to introduce a regularisation term that enforces the learning of compact, semantically-rich representations of normal nodes based on their structural relations to other nodes. When being optimised with supervised anomaly detection losses, the regularisation term helps incorporate strong normality into the modelling, and thus, it effectively avoids over-fitting the seen anomalies and learns a better normality decision boundary, largely reducing the false negatives of detecting unseen anomalies as normal. Extensive empirical results on seven real-world datasets show that NSReg significantly outperforms state-of-the-art competing methods by at least 14% AUC-ROC on the unseen anomaly classes and by 10% AUC-ROC on all anomaly classes.
中摘: 这篇论文探讨了一个重要的图异常检测（GAD）任务，即开放集GAD，其目标是使用少量正常节点和异常节点（称为已知异常）来训练一个检测模型，以检测已知异常和未知异常（即无法由训练异常表示的异常）。这些标记的训练数据为GAD模型提供了关于异常的关键先验知识，使检测错误大幅减少。然而，当前的监督GAD方法往往会过度强调拟合已知异常，导致许多未知异常被误检为正常节点。此外，现有的开放集异常检测（AD）模型主要针对欧几里得数据设计，未能有效捕捉来自图结构和节点属性的区分性特征。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种新的开放集GAD方法，即正常结构正则化（NSReg），以实现对未知异常的一般检测能力，同时保持其在检测已知异常方面的有效性。NSReg的核心思想是在优化过程中引入一个正则化项，该项基于节点之间的结构关系强制学习紧凑且语义丰富的正常节点表示。当与监督异常检测损失一起优化时，正则化项有助于将强烈的正常性纳入建模，从而有效避免过度拟合已知异常，并学习更好的正常性决策边界，大幅降低将未知异常误检为正常的假阴性率。在七个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验证明，NSReg在未知异常类别的AUC-ROC上至少显著优于最先进的竞争方法14%，在所有异常类别的AUC-ROC上则至少优于10%。

[No.80] Advancing Out-of-Distribution Detection via Local Neuroplasticity
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过局部神经可塑性推进离分布检测
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Alessandro Canevaro;Julian Schmidt;Mohammad Sajad Marvi;Hang Yu;Georg Martius;Julian Jordan;
机构: Eberhard Karls University of Tübingen;;University of Freiburg;Karlsruher Institut für Technologie;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/31215
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15833
英摘: In the domain of machine learning, the assumption that training and test data share the same distribution is often violated in real-world scenarios, requiring effective out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. This paper presents a novel OOD detection method that leverages the unique local neuroplasticity property of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs). Unlike traditional multilayer perceptrons, KANs exhibit local plasticity, allowing them to preserve learned information while adapting to new tasks. Our method compares the activation patterns of a trained KAN against its untrained counterpart to detect OOD samples. We validate our approach on benchmarks from image and medical domains, demonstrating superior performance and robustness compared to state-of-the-art techniques. These results underscore the potential of KANs in enhancing the reliability of machine learning systems in diverse environments.
中摘: 在机器学习领域，训练数据和测试数据共享相同分布的假设在现实场景中经常被违反，这需要有效的异常分布（OOD）检测方法。本文提出了一种新颖的OOD检测方法，该方法利用了柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺尔德网络（KANs）的独特局部神经可塑性特性。与传统的多层感知机不同，KANs表现出局部可塑性，使其能够在适应新任务的同时保留已学习的信息。我们的方法通过将训练好的KAN与其未训练版本的激活模式进行比较来检测OOD样本。我们在图像和医疗领域的基准测试上验证了这种方法，结果显示其性能和鲁棒性均优于最先进的技术。这些结果突显了KANs在提高机器学习系统在各种环境下的可靠性的潜力。

[No.82] Orthogonal Subspace Decomposition for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通用可迁移的AI生成图像检测的正交子空间分解方法
类别: applications->computer vision
作者: Zhiyuan Yan;Jiangming Wang;Peng Jin;Ke-Yue Zhang;Chengchun Liu;Shen Chen;Taiping Yao;Shouhong Ding;Baoyuan Wu;Li Yuan;
机构: Peking University;Tencent;;Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Oral
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45843
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15633
英摘: AI-generated images (AIGIs), such as natural or face images, have become increasingly important yet challenging. In this paper, we start from a new perspective to excavate the reason behind the failure generalization in AIGI detection, named the \textit{asymmetry phenomenon}, where a naively trained detector tends to favor overfitting to the limited and monotonous fake patterns, causing the feature space to become highly constrained and low-ranked, which is proved seriously limiting the expressivity and generalization. One potential remedy is incorporating the pre-trained knowledge within the vision foundation models (higher-ranked) to expand the feature space, alleviating the model's overfitting to fake. To this end, we employ Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) to decompose the original feature space into \textit{two orthogonal subspaces}. By freezing the principal components and adapting only the remained components, we preserve the pre-trained knowledge while learning fake patterns. Compared to existing full-parameters and LoRA-based tuning methods, we explicitly ensure orthogonality, enabling the higher rank of the whole feature space, effectively minimizing overfitting and enhancing generalization. We finally identify a crucial insight: our method implicitly learns \textit{a vital prior that fakes are actually derived from the real}, indicating a hierarchical relationship rather than independence. Modeling this prior, we believe, is essential for achieving superior generalization. Our codes are publicly available at \href{https://github.com/YZY-stack/Effort-AIGI-Detection}{GitHub}.
中摘: AI生成的图像（AIGI）检测中，自然或人脸图像等AI生成的图像变得越来越重要但也更具挑战性。本文从一个新的角度出发，探讨了AI生成图像检测中普遍存在的失败泛化现象的原因，称之为“不对称现象”。这种现象表现为一个简单训练的检测器倾向于过度拟合有限且单调的假图像模式，导致特征空间变得高度受限和低秩，从而严重限制了表达能力和泛化能力。一种潜在的解决方法是，在视觉基础模型中融入预训练知识（高秩），以扩展特征空间，减轻模型对假图像的过度拟合。为此，我们采用奇异值分解（SVD）将原始特征空间分解为两个正交子空间。通过冻结主成分并仅适应剩余成分，我们保留了预训练知识的同时学习假图像模式。与现有的全参数调优和LoRA基于调优方法相比，我们明确地确保了正交性，从而使整个特征空间的秩更高，有效地减少了过拟合并提升了泛化能力。我们最终发现了一个关键见解：我们的方法隐式地学习了一种重要的先验知识，即假图像实际上是源自真实的图像，表明了一种层次关系而非独立关系。建模这一先验知识，我们认为对于实现更优秀的泛化能力至关重要。我们的代码已公开发布在\href{https://github.com/YZY-stack/Effort-AIGI-Detection}{GitHub}上。

[No.83] Adaptive Multi-prompt Contrastive Network for Few-shot Out-of-distribution Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 自适应多提示对比网络在少样本未知分布检测中的应用
类别: applications->computer vision
作者: Xiang Fang;Arvind Easwaran;Blaise Genest;
机构: ;Nanyang Technological University;Centre National de la Recherche Scientifique;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45809
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17633
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection attempts to distinguish outlier samples to prevent models trained on the in-distribution (ID) dataset from producing unavailable outputs. Most OOD detection methods require many IID samples for training, which seriously limits their real-world applications. To this end, we target a challenging setting: few-shot OOD detection, where {Only a few {\em labeled ID} samples are available.} Therefore, few-shot OOD detection is much more challenging than the traditional OOD detection setting. Previous few-shot OOD detection works ignore the distinct diversity between different classes. In this paper, we propose a novel network: Adaptive Multi-prompt Contrastive Network (AMCN), which adapts the ID-OOD separation boundary by learning inter- and intra-class distribution. To compensate for the absence of OOD and scarcity of ID {\em image samples}, we leverage CLIP, connecting text with images, engineering learnable ID and OOD {\em textual prompts}. Specifically, we first generate adaptive prompts (learnable ID prompts, label-fixed OOD prompts and label-adaptive OOD prompts). Then, we generate an adaptive class boundary for each class by introducing a class-wise threshold. Finally, we propose a prompt-guided ID-OOD separation module to control the margin between ID and OOD prompts. Experimental results show that AMCN outperforms other state-of-the-art works.
中摘: 分布外（OOD）检测试图区分异常样本，以防止在分布内（ID）数据集上训练的模型产生不可用的输出。大多数OOD检测方法需要大量的独立同分布（IID）样本进行训练，这严重限制了它们的实际应用。为此，我们针对一个具有挑战性的设置：少量样本的OOD检测，在这种情况下，“只有少量标记的ID样本可用。”因此，少量样本的OOD检测比传统的OOD检测设置更加具有挑战性。之前的少量样本OOD检测工作忽略了不同类别的独特多样性。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的网络：自适应多提示对比网络（AMCN），该网络通过学习类别间的和类别内的分布来适应ID-OOD分离边界。为了弥补OOD样本缺失和ID图像样本稀缺的问题，我们利用CLIP将文本与图像连接起来，工程化可学习的ID和OOD文本提示。具体而言，我们首先生成自适应提示（可学习的ID提示、标签固定OOD提示和标签自适应OOD提示）。然后，通过引入类别特定的阈值为每个类别生成自适应类别边界。最后，我们提出了一个提示引导的ID-OOD分离模块，以控制ID提示和OOD提示之间的差距。实验结果显示，AMCN优于其他现有的先进方法。

[No.86] LLMScan: Causal Scan for LLM Misbehavior Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: LLMScan：因果扫描用于LLM不当行为检测
类别: general machine learning->causality
作者: Mengdi Zhang;Goh Kai Kiat;Peixin Zhang;Jun Sun;Lin Xin Rose;Hongyu Zhang;
机构: Singapore Management University;Chongqing University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45556
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16638
英摘: Despite the success of Large Language Models (LLMs) across various fields, their potential to generate untruthful, biased and harmful responses poses significant risks, particularly in critical applications. This highlights the urgent need for systematic methods to detect and prevent such misbehavior. While existing approaches target specific issues such as harmful responses, this work introduces LLMScan, an innovative LLM monitoring technique based on causality analysis, offering a comprehensive solution. LLMScan systematically monitors the inner workings of an LLM through the lens of causal inference, operating on the premise that the LLM's `brain' behaves differently when misbehaving. By analyzing the causal contributions of the LLM's input tokens and transformer layers, LLMScan effectively detects misbehavior. Extensive experiments across various tasks and models reveal clear distinctions in the causal distributions between normal behavior and misbehavior, enabling the development of accurate, lightweight detectors for a variety of misbehavior detection tasks.
中摘: 尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）在各个领域取得了成功，但它们生成不真实、有偏见和有害响应的潜在风险仍然存在，特别是在关键应用中。这突显了系统方法检测和预防此类不当行为的迫切需求。虽然现有的方法针对特定问题如有害响应进行处理，这项工作引入了LLMScan，这是一种基于因果分析的创新LLM监控技术，提供了一个全面的解决方案。LLMScan通过因果推理的视角系统地监控LLM的内部运作，其假设是当LLM不当行为时，“大脑”的表现会有所不同。通过对LLM输入标记和变换器层的因果贡献进行分析，LLMScan能够有效检测不当行为。广泛的实验表明，在各种任务和模型上，正常行为和不当行为之间的因果分布存在明显差异，从而使得开发适用于多种不当行为检测任务的准确且轻量级的检测器成为可能。

[No.87] Self-Discriminative Modeling for Anomalous Graph Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 自鉴别建模在异常图检测中的应用
类别: general machine learning->unsupervised and semisupervised learning
作者: Jinyu Cai;Yunhe Zhang;Jicong Fan;
机构: National University of Singapore;University of Macau;Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44215
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.06261
英摘: This paper studies the problem of detecting anomalous graphs using a machine learning model trained on only normal graphs, which has many applications in molecule, biology, and social network data analysis. We present a self-discriminative modeling framework for anomalous graph detection. The key idea, mathematically and numerically illustrated, is to learn a discriminator (classifier) from the given normal graphs together with pseudo-anomalous graphs generated by a model jointly trained, where we never use any true anomalous graphs and we hope that the generated pseudo-anomalous graphs interpolate between normal ones and (real) anomalous ones. Under the framework, we provide three algorithms with different computational efficiencies and stabilities for anomalous graph detection. The three algorithms are compared with several state-of-the-art graph-level anomaly detection baselines on nine popular graph datasets (four with small size and five with moderate size) and show significant improvement in terms of AUC. The success of our algorithms stems from the integration of the discriminative classifier and the well-posed pseudo-anomalous graphs, which provide new insights for anomaly detection. Moreover, we investigate our algorithms for large-scale imbalanced graph datasets. Surprisingly, our algorithms, though fully unsupervised, are able to significantly outperform supervised learning algorithms of anomalous graph detection. The corresponding reason is also analyzed.
中摘: 本文研究了仅使用正常图训练机器学习模型来检测异常图的问题，这一方法在分子、生物学和社会网络数据分析中有广泛应用。我们提出了一种自我区分建模框架用于异常图检测。该框架的核心思想，通过数学和数值方法进行了说明，是从给定的正常图以及由同一模型联合训练生成的伪异常图中学习一个区分器（分类器），其中从未使用真实的异常图，并希望通过生成的伪异常图在正常图与真实异常图之间进行插值。在该框架下，我们提供了三种具有不同计算效率和稳定性的算法来进行异常图检测。这三种算法在九个流行的图数据集（四个小规模数据集和五个中等规模数据集）上与几种最先进的图级别异常检测基准进行了比较，并在AUC指标上显示出显著改进。我们算法的成功源于区分分类器与良好定义的伪异常图的集成，为异常检测提供了新的见解。此外，我们还研究了这些算法在大规模不平衡图数据集上的应用。令人惊讶的是，尽管我们的算法完全无监督，但在异常图检测方面仍能显著超越有监督学习算法。同时，我们也分析了背后的原因。

[No.88] Novelty Detection in Reinforcement Learning with World Models
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于世界模型的强化学习中新颖性检测
类别: reinforcement learning->online
作者: Geigh Zollicoffer;Kenneth Eaton;Jonathan C Balloch;Julia Kim;Wei Zhou;Robert Wright;Mark Riedl;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;Georgia Tech Research Institute;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43561
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.08731
英摘: Reinforcement learning (RL) using world models has found significant recent successes. However, when a sudden change to world mechanics or properties occurs then agent performance and reliability can dramatically decline. We refer to the sudden change in visual properties or state transitions as novelties. Implementing novelty detection within generated world model frameworks is a crucial task for protecting the agent when deployed. In this paper, we propose straightforward bounding approaches to incorporate novelty detection into world model RL agents, by utilizing the misalignment of the world model's hallucinated states and the true observed states as an anomaly score. We provide effective approaches to detecting novelties in a distribution of transitions learned by an agent in a world model. Finally, we show the advantage of our work in a novel environment compared to traditional machine learning novelty detection methods as well as currently accepted RL focused novelty detection algorithms.
中摘: 使用世界模型的强化学习（RL）在最近取得了显著的成功。然而，当世界机械规则或属性突然发生变化时，代理性能和可靠性可能会急剧下降。我们将视觉属性或状态转换的突然变化称为新颖性。在生成的世界模型框架内实现新颖性检测是保护部署代理的关键任务。在本文中，我们提出了一种简单的边界方法，通过利用世界模型生成的状态与真实观察状态之间的不一致作为异常分数，将新颖性检测整合到世界模型RL代理中。我们提供了有效的方法来检测代理在一个世界模型中学习到的状态转换分布中的新颖性。最后，我们在一个新型环境中展示了我们工作的优势，与传统的机器学习新颖性检测方法以及当前接受的专注于RL的新颖性检测算法相比。

[No.89] Demystifying Catastrophic Forgetting in Two-Stage Incremental Object Detector
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 揭开两阶段增量目标检测器中灾难性遗忘的面纱
类别: applications->computer vision
作者: Qirui Wu;Shizhou Zhang;De Cheng;Yinghui Xing;Di Xu;PENG WANG;Yanning Zhang;
机构: Northwest Polytechnical University;Xidian University;;Northwestern Polytechnical University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45396
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05540
英摘: Catastrophic forgetting is a critical chanllenge for incremental object detection (IOD). Most existing methods treat the detector monolithically, relying on instance replay or knowledge distillation without analyzing component-specific forgetting. Through dissection of Faster R-CNN, we reveal a key insight: Catastrophic forgetting is predominantly localized to the RoI Head classifier, while regressors retain robustness across incremental stages. This finding challenges conventional assumptions, motivating us to develop a framework termed NSGP-RePRE. Regional Prototype Replay (RePRE) mitigates classifier forgetting via replay of two types of prototypes: coarse prototypes represent class-wise semantic centers of RoI features, while fine-grained prototypes model intra-class variations. Null Space Gradient Projection (NSGP) is further introduced to eliminate prototype-feature misalignment by updating the feature extractor in directions orthogonal to subspace of old inputs via gradient projection, aligning RePRE with incremental learning dynamics. Our simple yet effective design allows NSGP-RePRE to achieve state-of-the-art performance on the Pascal VOC and MS COCO datasets under various settings. Our work not only advances IOD methodology but also provide pivotal insights for catastrophic forgetting mitigation in IOD. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/fanrena/NSGP-RePRE}{https://github.com/fanrena/NSGP-RePRE} .
中摘: 灾难性遗忘是增量对象检测（IOD）中的一个关键挑战。现有大多数方法将检测器视为一个整体，依赖于实例重放或知识蒸馏，而不分析特定组件的遗忘情况。通过对Faster R-CNN进行剖析，我们揭示了一个关键洞察：灾难性遗忘主要集中在RoI头分类器上，而回归器则在增量阶段保持了稳健性。这一发现挑战了传统的假设，促使我们开发了一种名为NSGP-RePRE的框架。区域原型重放（RePRE）通过重放两种类型的原型来缓解分类器的遗忘问题：粗粒度原型代表RoI特征的类别级语义中心，而细粒度原型则模型类内变化。我们进一步引入了空子空间梯度投影（NSGP），通过梯度投影更新特征提取器的方向，使其与增量学习动态相一致，从而消除原型-特征对齐问题。我们的设计简单有效，在各种设置下，NSGP-RePRE在Pascal VOC和MS COCO数据集上达到了最先进的性能。我们的工作不仅推进了增量对象检测的方法论，还为增量对象检测中的灾难性遗忘缓解提供了关键见解。代码可在[https://github.com/fanrena/NSGP-RePRE](https://github.com/fanrena/NSGP-RePRE) 获取。

[No.93] Mahalanobis++: Improving OOD Detection via Feature Normalization
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: Mahalanobis++：通过特征归一化提高OOD检测性能
类别: deep learning->robustness
作者: Maximilian Müller;Matthias Hein;
机构: ;University of Tübingen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43649
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18032
英摘: Detecting out-of-distribution (OOD) examples is an important task for deploying reliable machine learning models in safety-critial applications. While post-hoc methods based on the Mahalanobis distance applied to pre-logit features are among the most effective for ImageNet-scale OOD detection, their performance varies significantly across models. We connect this inconsistency to strong variations in feature norms, indicating severe violations of the Gaussian assumption underlying the Mahalanobis distance estimation. We show that simple $\ell_2$-normalization of the features mitigates this problem effectively, aligning better with the premise of normally distributed data with shared covariance matrix. Extensive experiments on 44 models across diverse architectures and pretraining schemes show that $\ell_2$-normalization improves the conventional Mahalanobis distance-based approaches significantly and consistently, and outperforms other recently proposed OOD detection methods.
中摘: 检测分布外（OOD）样本是为安全关键应用部署可靠的机器学习模型的一项重要任务。虽然基于马哈拉诺比斯距离的应用于预logit特征的后处理方法在ImageNet规模的OOD检测中效果最佳，但它们在不同模型上的性能却差异显著。我们将这种不一致性归因于特征范数的强烈变化，这表明马哈拉诺比斯距离估计所依赖的高斯假设存在严重的违反情况。我们证明了简单的$\ell_2$正则化可以有效缓解这一问题，更好地与共享协方差矩阵的正态分布数据前提相吻合。在涵盖44种不同架构和预训练方案的广泛实验中显示，$\ell_2$正则化显著且一致地改进了传统的基于马哈拉诺比斯距离的方法，并优于其他最近提出的OOD检测方法。

[No.94] KAN-AD: Time Series Anomaly Detection with Kolmogorov–Arnold Networks
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺尔德网络的时间序列异常检测
类别: general machine learning->sequential network and time series modeling
作者: Quan Zhou;Changhua Pei;Fei Sun;HanJing;Zhengwei Gao;Haiming Zhang;Gaogang Xie;Dan Pei;Jianhui li;
机构: ;Chinese Academy of Sciences;Zhongxing Telecommunications Equipment;Chinese National Identity Card Information Center;Tsinghua University;Nanjing University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45584
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00278
英摘: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) underpins real-time monitoring in cloud services and web systems, allowing rapid identification of anomalies to prevent costly failures. Most TSAD methods driven by forecasting models tend to overfit by emphasizing minor fluctuations. Our analysis reveals that effective TSAD should focus on modeling "normal" behavior through smooth local patterns. To achieve this, we reformulate time series modeling as approximating the series with smooth univariate functions. The local smoothness of each univariate function ensures that the fitted time series remains resilient against local disturbances. However, a direct KAN implementation proves susceptible to these disturbances due to the inherently localized characteristics of B-spline functions. We thus propose KAN-AD, replacing B-splines with truncated Fourier expansions and introducing a novel lightweight learning mechanism that emphasizes global patterns while staying robust to local disturbances. On four popular TSAD benchmarks, KAN-AD achieves an average 15% improvement in detection accuracy (with peaks exceeding 27%) over state-of-the-art baselines. Remarkably, it requires fewer than 1,000 trainable parameters, resulting in a 50% faster inference speed compared to the original KAN, demonstrating the approach's efficiency and practical viability.
中摘: 时间序列异常检测（TSAD）是云服务和网络系统实时监控的基础，能够快速识别异常以防止昂贵的故障。大多数由预测模型驱动的TSAD方法倾向于通过强调细微波动来过度拟合。我们的分析表明，有效的TSAD应该专注于通过平滑的局部模式建模“正常”行为。为此，我们将时间序列建模重新表述为用平滑的单变量函数近似时间序列。每个单变量函数的局部平滑性确保拟合的时间序列能够抵御局部干扰。然而，直接实施KAN（可变核平均）方法由于B样条函数固有的局部特性，使其容易受到这些干扰的影响。因此，我们提出了KAN-AD方法，用截断傅里叶展开替换B样条，并引入了一种新的轻量级学习机制，该机制强调全局模式同时保持对局部干扰的鲁棒性。在四个流行的TSAD基准测试中，KAN-AD在检测准确性上平均提高了15%（峰值超过27%），超越了最先进的基线方法。值得注意的是，KAN-AD只需要不到1000个可训练参数，相比原始的KAN，其推理速度提高了50%，这展示了该方法的高效性和实用性。

[No.99] Steer LLM Latents for Hallucination Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 引导LLM潜在特征以进行幻觉检测
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Seongheon Park;Xuefeng Du;Min-Hsuan Yeh;Haobo Wang;Yixuan Li;
机构: University of Wisconsin-Madison;University of Wisconsin;Zhejiang University;Stanford University;Meta;Google;Cornell University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01917
英摘: Hallucinations in LLMs pose a significant concern to their safe deployment in real-world applications. Recent approaches have leveraged the latent space of LLMs for hallucination detection, but their embeddings, optimized for linguistic coherence rather than factual accuracy, often fail to clearly separate truthful and hallucinated content. To this end, we propose the Truthfulness Separator Vector (TSV), a lightweight and flexible steering vector that reshapes the LLM's representation space during inference to enhance the separation between truthful and hallucinated outputs, without altering model parameters. Our two-stage framework first trains TSV on a small set of labeled exemplars to form compact and well-separated clusters. It then augments the exemplar set with unlabeled LLM generations, employing an optimal transport-based algorithm for pseudo-labeling combined with a confidence-based filtering process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TSV achieves state-of-the-art performance with minimal labeled data, exhibiting strong generalization across datasets and providing a practical solution for real-world LLM applications.

[No.107] Conformal Prediction with Cellwise Outliers: A Detect-then-Impute Approach
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于单元离群值的 conformal 预测：一种先检测后填充的方法
类别: general machine learning
作者: Qian Peng;Yajie Bao;Haojie Ren;Zhaojun Wang;Changliang Zou;
机构: Nankai University;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43829
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04986
英摘: Conformal prediction is a powerful tool for constructing prediction intervals for black-box models, providing a finite sample coverage guarantee for exchangeable data. However, this exchangeability is compromised when some entries of the test feature are contaminated, such as in the case of cellwise outliers. To address this issue, this paper introduces a novel framework called detect-then-impute conformal prediction. This framework first employs an outlier detection procedure on the test feature and then utilizes an imputation method to fill in those cells identified as outliers. To quantify the uncertainty in the processed test feature, we adaptively apply the detection and imputation procedures to the calibration set, thereby constructing exchangeable features for the conformal prediction interval of the test label. We develop two practical algorithms, PDI-CP and JDI-CP, and provide a distribution-free coverage analysis under some commonly used detection and imputation procedures. Notably, JDI-CP achieves a finite sample $1-2α$ coverage guarantee. Numerical experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that our proposed algorithms exhibit robust coverage properties and comparable efficiency to the oracle baseline.
中摘: 自适应预测区间是一种强大的工具，用于为黑盒模型构建预测区间，它能提供交换数据的有限样本覆盖保证。然而，当测试特征的一些条目受到污染时，例如单元异常值的情况下，这种交换性就会受损。为了解决这一问题，本文引入了一个名为“检测-然后-填补”的新颖框架，该框架首先在测试特征上应用异常值检测程序，然后使用填补方法填充那些被识别为异常值的单元。为了量化处理后的测试特征中的不确定性，我们通过自适应地将检测和填补程序应用于校准集，从而构建出可用于测试标签的自适应特征，进而构造出自适应的预测区间。我们开发了两种实用算法PDI-CP和JDI-CP，并在一些常用检测和填补程序下提供了无分布假设的覆盖分析。值得注意的是，JDI-CP实现了有限样本下的$1-2\alpha$覆盖保证。我们在合成数据集和真实数据集上的数值实验表明，我们提出的算法具有稳健的覆盖性能，并且与最优基准具有可比的效率。

[No.108] Controlling Neural Collapse Enhances Out-of-Distribution Detection and Transfer Learning
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 控制神经网络坍缩以增强异分布检测和迁移学习
类别: general machine learning->representation learning
作者: Md Yousuf Harun;Jhair Gallardo;Christopher Kanan;
机构: Rochester Institute of Technology;;University of Rochester;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46287
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10691
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection and OOD generalization are widely studied in Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), yet their relationship remains poorly understood. We empirically show that the degree of Neural Collapse (NC) in a network layer is inversely related with these objectives: stronger NC improves OOD detection but degrades generalization, while weaker NC enhances generalization at the cost of detection. This trade-off suggests that a single feature space cannot simultaneously achieve both tasks. To address this, we develop a theoretical framework linking NC to OOD detection and generalization. We show that entropy regularization mitigates NC to improve generalization, while a fixed Simplex Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF) projector enforces NC for better detection. Based on these insights, we propose a method to control NC at different DNN layers. In experiments, our method excels at both tasks across OOD datasets and DNN architectures. Code for our experiments is available at: https://yousuf907.github.io/ncoodg
中摘: 出分布（Out-of-distribution，OOD）检测和OOD泛化在深度神经网络（DNNs）中得到了广泛研究，但它们之间的关系仍然不甚明了。我们通过实验证明，网络层中神经坍缩（Neural Collapse，NC）的程度与这些目标呈反比关系：更强的NC能够提高OOD检测性能，但会损害泛化能力；而较弱的NC则能增强泛化能力，但会牺牲检测性能。这种权衡表明，单一特征空间无法同时完成这两个任务。为了解决这一问题，我们建立了一个理论框架，将NC与OOD检测和泛化联系起来。我们证明，熵正则化可以减轻NC以提高泛化能力，而固定的基本单纯形等角紧框（Simplex Equiangular Tight Frame，ETF）投影器则可以强制执行NC以更好地进行检测。基于这些见解，我们提出了一种方法来控制不同DNN层中的NC。在实验中，我们的方法在多种OOD数据集和DNN架构上同时出色地完成了这两个任务。我们的实验代码可在以下链接获取：https://yousuf907.github.io/ncoodg

[No.109] Sample Complexity of Correlation Detection in the Gaussian Wigner Model
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 高斯威纳模型中相关性检测的学习复杂度
类别: general machine learning->sequential network and time series modeling
作者: Dong Huang;Pengkun Yang;
机构: Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44089
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14138
英摘: Correlation analysis is a fundamental step in uncovering meaningful insights from complex datasets. In this paper, we study the problem of detecting correlations between two random graphs following the Gaussian Wigner model with unlabeled vertices. Specifically, the task is formulated as a hypothesis testing problem: under the null hypothesis, the two graphs are independent, while under the alternative hypothesis, they are edge-correlated through a latent vertex permutation, yet maintain the same marginal distributions as under the null. We focus on the scenario where two induced subgraphs, each with a fixed number of vertices, are sampled. We determine the optimal rate for the sample size required for correlation detection, derived through an analysis of the conditional second moment. Additionally, we propose an efficient approximate algorithm that significantly reduces running time.
中摘: 相关性分析是揭示复杂数据集有意义洞察的基本步骤。在本文中，我们研究了在遵循高斯威纳模型且顶点未标记的情况下检测两个随机图之间相关性的问题。具体而言，该任务被形式化为一个假设检验问题：在零假设下，两个图是独立的；而在备择假设下，它们通过潜在的顶点置换边相关联，但保持相同的边缘分布。我们关注的是每个包含固定数量顶点的诱导子图被采样的情况。我们确定了用于相关性检测所需的样本大小的最佳速率，这是通过对条件二阶矩的分析得出的。此外，我们提出了一种高效的近似算法，显著减少了运行时间。
