[No.111] Robust Conformal Outlier Detection under Contaminated Reference Data
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 稳健同构异常检测在受污染参考数据下的应用
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Meshi Bashari;Matteo Sesia;Yaniv Romano;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;University of Southern California;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43852
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04807
英摘: Conformal prediction is a flexible framework for calibrating machine learning predictions, providing distribution-free statistical guarantees. In outlier detection, this calibration relies on a reference set of labeled inlier data to control the type-I error rate. However, obtaining a perfectly labeled inlier reference set is often unrealistic, and a more practical scenario involves access to a contaminated reference set containing a small fraction of outliers. This paper analyzes the impact of such contamination on the validity of conformal methods. We prove that under realistic, non-adversarial settings, calibration on contaminated data yields conservative type-I error control, shedding light on the inherent robustness of conformal methods. This conservativeness, however, typically results in a loss of power. To alleviate this limitation, we propose a novel, active data-cleaning framework that leverages a limited labeling budget and an outlier detection model to selectively annotate data points in the contaminated reference set that are suspected as outliers. By removing only the annotated outliers in this ``suspicious'' subset, we can effectively enhance power while mitigating the risk of inflating the type-I error rate, as supported by our theoretical analysis. Experiments on real datasets validate the conservative behavior of conformal methods under contamination and show that the proposed data-cleaning strategy improves power without sacrificing validity.
中摘: 自适应预测是一种灵活的框架，用于校准机器学习预测，提供无分布统计保证。在离群值检测中，这种校准依赖于一个带有标签的内点参考数据集来控制第一类错误率。然而，获得一个完美标注的内点参考数据集往往是不现实的，更实际的情况是能够访问包含少量离群值的受污染参考数据集。本文分析了此类受污染对自适应方法有效性的影响。我们证明，在非对抗性的真实场景下，使用受污染数据进行校准可以保守地控制第一类错误率，揭示了自适应方法固有的鲁棒性。然而，这种保守性通常会导致功效的损失。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了一种新颖的主动数据清洗框架，该框架利用有限的标注预算和离群值检测模型，有选择地标注受污染参考数据集中疑似离群值的数据点。通过仅移除这些标注的离群值，我们可以在增强功效的同时，降低放大第一类错误率的风险，这得到了我们的理论分析的支持。实验结果验证了在受污染情况下自适应方法的保守行为，并表明提出的数据清洗策略可以在不牺牲有效性的情况下提高功效。

[No.112] Test-Time Multimodal Backdoor Detection by Contrastive Prompting
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 测试时多模态后门检测通过对比提示
类别: social aspects->safety
作者: Yuwei Niu;Shuo He;Qi Wei;Zongyu Wu;Feng Liu;Lei Feng;
机构: Chongqing University;Nanyang Technological University;Pennsylvania State University;University of Melbourne;Southeast University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46621
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15269
英摘: While multimodal contrastive learning methods (e.g., CLIP) can achieve impressive zero-shot classification performance, recent research has revealed that these methods are vulnerable to backdoor attacks. To defend against backdoor attacks on CLIP, existing defense methods focus on either the pre-training stage or the fine-tuning stage, which would unfortunately cause high computational costs due to numerous parameter updates and are not applicable in black-box settings. In this paper, we provide the first attempt at a computationally efficient backdoor detection method to defend against backdoored CLIP in the \emph{inference} stage. We empirically find that the visual representations of backdoored images are \emph{insensitive} to \emph{benign} and \emph{malignant} changes in class description texts. Motivated by this observation, we propose BDetCLIP, a novel test-time backdoor detection method based on contrastive prompting. Specifically, we first prompt a language model (e.g., GPT-4) to produce class-related description texts (benign) and class-perturbed random texts (malignant) by specially designed instructions. Then, the distribution difference in cosine similarity between images and the two types of class description texts can be used as the criterion to detect backdoor samples. Extensive experiments validate that our proposed BDetCLIP is superior to state-of-the-art backdoor detection methods, in terms of both effectiveness and efficiency. Our codes are publicly available at: https://github.com/Purshow/BDetCLIP.
中摘: 尽管多模态对比学习方法（例如CLIP）可以在零样本分类任务上取得令人印象深刻的性能，但最近的研究表明这些方法容易受到后门攻击的影响。为了防御针对CLIP的后门攻击，现有的防御方法主要集中在预训练阶段或微调阶段，但由于需要进行大量的参数更新，这会导致高昂的计算成本，并且在黑盒设置下不适用。在本文中，我们提供了第一个在推理阶段实现高效后门检测的方法，以防御被植入后门的CLIP。我们通过实验发现，带有后门的图像的视觉表示对于类别描述文本中的良性变化和恶意变化是不敏感的。基于这一观察，我们提出了BDetCLIP，这是一种新颖的基于对比提示的测试时后门检测方法。具体而言，我们首先使用特别设计的指令来提示语言模型（例如GPT-4）生成与类别相关的描述文本（良性）和类扰动的随机文本（恶意）。然后，可以通过计算图像与这两种类型类别描述文本之间余弦相似度分布的差异作为检测后门样本的标准。广泛的经验研究表明，我们提出的BDetCLIP在有效性和效率方面均优于最先进的后门检测方法。我们的代码已在以下地址公开：https://github.com/Purshow/BDetCLIP。

[No.113] Detecting Strategic Deception with Linear Probes
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 使用线性探针检测战略欺骗
类别: social aspects->accountability transparency and interpretability
作者: Nicholas Goldowsky-Dill;Bilal Chughtai;Stefan Heimersheim;Marius Hobbhahn;
机构: Apollo Research;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46082
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03407
英摘: AI models might use deceptive strategies as part of scheming or misaligned behaviour. Monitoring outputs alone is insufficient, since the AI might produce seemingly benign outputs while their internal reasoning is misaligned. We thus evaluate if linear probes can robustly detect deception by monitoring model activations. We test two probe-training datasets, one with contrasting instructions to be honest or deceptive (following Zou et al., 2023) and one of responses to simple roleplaying scenarios. We test whether these probes generalize to realistic settings where Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct behaves deceptively, such as concealing insider trading (Scheurer et al., 2023) and purposely underperforming on safety evaluations (Benton et al., 2024). We find that our probe distinguishes honest and deceptive responses with AUROCs between 0.96 and 0.999 on our evaluation datasets. If we set the decision threshold to have a 1% false positive rate on chat data not related to deception, our probe catches 95-99% of the deceptive responses. Overall we think white-box probes are promising for future monitoring systems, but current performance is insufficient as a robust defence against deception. Our probes' outputs can be viewed at data.apolloresearch.ai/dd and our code at github.com/ApolloResearch/deception-detection.
中摘: AI模型可能会使用欺骗策略作为其诡计或对齐不当行为的一部分。仅监控输出是不够的，因为AI可能在内部推理与实际输出不一致的情况下生成看似无害的输出。因此，我们评估线性探针是否能够稳健地检测欺骗行为，通过监测模型激活情况来实现。我们测试了两个探针训练数据集：一个包含对比性的指令，要求诚实或欺骗（参考Zou等人，2023的研究），另一个则是简单的角色扮演场景的回答。我们测试这些探针是否能在现实场景中有效工作，例如在Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct隐藏内幕交易（Scheurer等人，2023）和故意在安全性评估中表现不佳（Benton等人，2024）的情况下区分诚实和欺骗的回答。我们的探针在评价数据集中将诚实和欺骗的回答区分开来的AUC值范围在0.96到0.999之间。如果我们设定决策阈值，在与欺骗无关的聊天数据中将假阳性率控制在1%，那么我们的探针可以捕捉到95-99%的欺骗回答。总体而言，我们认为白盒探针对未来监控系统的发展前景看好，但当前性能还不足以作为对抗欺骗的稳健防御手段。我们的探针输出可以在data.apolloresearch.ai/dd查看，代码则可在github.com/ApolloResearch/deception-detection获取。

[No.115] Learning Event Completeness for Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 弱监督视频异常检测的学习事件完整性
类别: applications->computer vision
作者: Yu Wang;Shiwei Chen;
机构: Tongji University;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45685
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13095
英摘: Weakly supervised video anomaly detection (WS-VAD) is tasked with pinpointing temporal intervals containing anomalous events within untrimmed videos, utilizing only video-level annotations. However, a significant challenge arises due to the absence of dense frame-level annotations, often leading to incomplete localization in existing WS-VAD methods. To address this issue, we present a novel LEC-VAD, Learning Event Completeness for Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection, which features a dual structure designed to encode both category-aware and category-agnostic semantics between vision and language. Within LEC-VAD, we devise semantic regularities that leverage an anomaly-aware Gaussian mixture to learn precise event boundaries, thereby yielding more complete event instances. Besides, we develop a novel memory bank-based prototype learning mechanism to enrich concise text descriptions associated with anomaly-event categories. This innovation bolsters the text's expressiveness, which is crucial for advancing WS-VAD. Our LEC-VAD demonstrates remarkable advancements over the current state-of-the-art methods on two benchmark datasets XD-Violence and UCF-Crime.
中摘: 弱监督视频异常检测（WS-VAD）的任务是在未剪辑的视频中定位包含异常事件的时间区间，仅利用视频级别的标注。然而，由于缺乏密集的帧级别标注，现有WS-VAD方法常常面临不完整定位的挑战。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新颖的方法——LEC-VAD（Learning Event Completeness for Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection），该方法具有双重结构，旨在在视觉和语言之间编码类别感知和非类别感知的语义。在LEC-VAD中，我们设计了语义规律，利用异常感知的高斯混合模型学习精确的事件边界，从而提供更完整的事件实例。此外，我们开发了一种基于记忆库的新颖原型学习机制，以丰富与异常事件类别相关的简洁文本描述。这种创新增强了文本的表达能力，对于推动弱监督视频异常检测的发展至关重要。我们的LEC-VAD在两个基准数据集XD-Violence和UCF-Crime上展示了相对于当前最先进的方法显著的进步。

[No.116] Open-Det: An Efficient Learning Framework for Open-Ended Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 开放检测：一种高效的开放性学习框架
类别: deep learning
作者: Guiping Cao;Tao Wang;Wenjian Huang;Xiangyuan Lan;Jianguo Zhang;Dongmei Jiang;
机构: Southern University of Science and Technology;Pengcheng Laboratory;;Southern University for Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45000
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20639
英摘: Open-Ended object Detection (OED) is a novel and challenging task that detects objects and generates their category names in a free-form manner, without requiring additional vocabularies during inference. However, the existing OED models, such as GenerateU, require large-scale datasets for training, suffer from slow convergence, and exhibit limited performance. To address these issues, we present a novel and efficient Open-Det framework, consisting of four collaborative parts. Specifically, Open-Det accelerates model training in both the bounding box and object name generation process by reconstructing the Object Detector and the Object Name Generator. To bridge the semantic gap between Vision and Language modalities, we propose a Vision-Language Aligner with V-to-L and L-to-V alignment mechanisms, incorporating with the Prompts Distiller to transfer knowledge from the VLM into VL-prompts, enabling accurate object name generation for the LLM. In addition, we design a Masked Alignment Loss to eliminate contradictory supervision and introduce a Joint Loss to enhance classification, resulting in more efficient training. Compared to GenerateU, Open-Det, using only 1.5% of the training data (0.077M vs. 5.077M), 20.8% of the training epochs (31 vs. 149), and fewer GPU resources (4 V100 vs. 16 A100), achieves even higher performance (+1.0% in APr). The source codes are available at: https://github.com/Med-Process/Open-Det.
中摘: 开放性对象检测（OED）是一项新颖且具有挑战性的任务，能够在无需额外词汇表的情况下检测对象并生成其类别名称。然而，现有的OED模型，如GenerateU，需要大规模的数据集进行训练，收敛速度较慢，并且表现有限。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新颖且高效的Open-Det框架，由四个协作部分组成。具体来说，Open-Det通过重构对象检测器和对象名称生成器，在边界框生成和对象名称生成过程中加速了模型训练。为了弥合视觉和语言模态之间的语义差距，我们提出了一个视觉-语言对齐器（Vision-Language Aligner），其中包括视图到语言（V-to-L）和语言到视图（L-to-V）对齐机制，并结合提示蒸馏器（Prompts Distiller）将VL模型中的知识转移到VL提示中，从而实现更准确的对象名称生成。此外，我们设计了一个掩码对齐损失来消除矛盾的监督，并引入了一个联合损失以增强分类，从而实现更高效的训练。与GenerateU相比，Open-Det仅使用了1.5%的训练数据（0.077M vs. 5.077M）、20.8%的训练周期（31 vs. 149）和更少的GPU资源（4个V100 vs. 16个A100），却实现了更高的性能（+1.0%的APr）。源代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/Med-Process/Open-Det。

[No.117] Towards a Unified Framework of Clustering-based Anomaly Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于聚类的异常检测统一框架
类别: deep learning->generative models and autoencoders
作者: Zeyu Fang;Ming Gu;Sheng Zhou;Jiawei Chen;Qiaoyu Tan;Haishuai Wang;Jiajun Bu;
机构: Zhejiang University;New York University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46624
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00452
英摘: Unsupervised Anomaly Detection (UAD) plays a crucial role in identifying abnormal patterns within data without labeled examples, holding significant practical implications across various domains. Although the individual contributions of representation learning and clustering to anomaly detection are well-established, their interdependencies remain under-explored due to the absence of a unified theoretical framework. Consequently, their collective potential to enhance anomaly detection performance remains largely untapped. To bridge this gap, in this paper, we propose a novel probabilistic mixture model for anomaly detection to establish a theoretical connection among representation learning, clustering, and anomaly detection. By maximizing a novel anomaly-aware data likelihood, representation learning and clustering can effectively reduce the adverse impact of anomalous data and collaboratively benefit anomaly detection. Meanwhile, a theoretically substantiated anomaly score is naturally derived from this framework. Lastly, drawing inspiration from gravitational analysis in physics, we have devised an improved anomaly score that more effectively harnesses the combined power of representation learning and clustering. Extensive experiments, involving 17 baseline methods across 30 diverse datasets, validate the effectiveness and generalization capability of the proposed method, surpassing state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: 无监督异常检测（UAD）在无需标注示例的情况下识别数据中的异常模式方面发挥着至关重要的作用，具有跨各个领域的重大实际意义。尽管表示学习和聚类在异常检测中的个体贡献已经被广泛认可，但由于缺乏统一的理论框架，它们之间的相互依赖关系仍然没有得到充分探索。因此，它们共同提升异常检测性能的潜力尚未被充分利用。为了弥合这一差距，本文提出了一种新颖的概率混合模型，以建立表示学习、聚类和异常检测之间的理论联系。通过最大化一种新的异常感知数据似然性，表示学习和聚类可以有效减少异常数据的负面影响，并且能够协同提升异常检测的效果。同时，该框架自然地导出了一种理论支持的异常评分。最后，受到物理学中引力分析的启发，我们设计了一种改进的异常评分，更有效地利用了表示学习和聚类的联合力量。广泛的实验涉及30个不同领域的17种基线方法，验证了所提出方法的有效性和泛化能力，超越了现有最先进的方法。

[No.118] CurvGAD: Leveraging Curvature for Enhanced Graph Anomaly Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: CurvGAD：利用曲率增强图异常检测
类别: deep learning->graph neural networks
作者: Karish Grover;Geoffrey J. Gordon;Christos Faloutsos;
机构: ;Amazon;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45460
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08605
英摘: Does the intrinsic curvature of complex networks hold the key to unveiling graph anomalies that conventional approaches overlook? Reconstruction-based graph anomaly detection (GAD) methods overlook such geometric outliers, focusing only on structural and attribute-level anomalies. To this end, we propose CurvGAD - a mixed-curvature graph autoencoder that introduces the notion of curvature-based geometric anomalies. CurvGAD introduces two parallel pipelines for enhanced anomaly interpretability: (1) Curvature-equivariant geometry reconstruction, which focuses exclusively on reconstructing the edge curvatures using a mixed-curvature, Riemannian encoder and Gaussian kernel-based decoder; and (2) Curvature-invariant structure and attribute reconstruction, which decouples structural and attribute anomalies from geometric irregularities by regularizing graph curvature under discrete Ollivier-Ricci flow, thereby isolating the non-geometric anomalies. By leveraging curvature, CurvGAD refines the existing anomaly classifications and identifies new curvature-driven anomalies. Extensive experimentation over 10 real-world datasets (both homophilic and heterophilic) demonstrates an improvement of up to 6.5% over state-of-the-art GAD methods. The code is available at: https://github.com/karish-grover/curvgad.
中摘: 复杂网络的固有曲率是否揭示了传统方法忽略的图异常？基于重构的图异常检测（GAD）方法忽略了这种几何异常，仅关注结构和属性级别的异常。为此，我们提出了一种混合曲率图自编码器CurvGAD，引入了基于曲率的几何异常的概念。CurvGAD引入了两个并行管道以增强异常解释性：(1) 曲率等变几何重构，该管道专门使用混合曲率、黎曼编码器和高斯核解码器重构边曲率；(2) 曲率不变结构和属性重构，通过在离散Ollivier-Ricci流下正则化图曲率，将结构和属性异常与几何不规则性分离，从而隔离非几何异常。通过利用曲率，CurvGAD细化了现有的异常分类，并识别出新的由曲率驱动的异常。在10个真实世界数据集（包括同质性和异质性）上的广泛实验表明，CurvGAD相比最先进的GAD方法提高了最高6.5%的性能。代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/karish-grover/curvgad。

[No.119] A Variational Information Theoretic Approach to Out-of-Distribution Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于变分信息论的方法进行分布外检测
类别: general machine learning
作者: Sudeepta Mondal;Zhuolin Jiang;Ganesh Sundaramoorthi;
机构: RTX Technology Research Center;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45839
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14194
英摘: We present a theory for the construction of out-of-distribution (OOD) detection features for neural networks. We introduce random features for OOD through a novel information-theoretic loss functional consisting of two terms, the first based on the KL divergence separates resulting in-distribution (ID) and OOD feature distributions and the second term is the Information Bottleneck, which favors compressed features that retain the OOD information. We formulate a variational procedure to optimize the loss and obtain OOD features. Based on assumptions on OOD distributions, one can recover properties of existing OOD features, i.e., shaping functions. Furthermore, we show that our theory can predict a new shaping function that out-performs existing ones on OOD benchmarks. Our theory provides a general framework for constructing a variety of new features with clear explainability.
中摘: 我们提出了一种理论，用于构建神经网络的分布外（OOD）检测特征。我们通过一个新颖的信息论损失泛函引入了OOD随机特征，该泛函由两部分组成：第一部分基于KL散度，将结果内的分布（ID）和OOD特征分布分开；第二部分是信息瓶颈，它倾向于压缩特征但保留OOD信息。我们制定了一种变分程序来优化损失并获得OOD特征。基于对OOD分布的假设，可以恢复现有OOD特征的属性，即形状函数。此外，我们展示了我们的理论可以预测一个新的形状函数，该函数在OOD基准测试中表现优于现有的形状函数。我们的理论提供了一个通用框架，用于构建具有明确解释性的各种新特征。

英摘: Existing prompt-based approaches have demonstrated impressive performance in continual learning, leveraging pre-trained large-scale models for classification tasks; however, the tight coupling between foreground-background information and the coupled attention between prompts and image-text tokens present significant challenges in incremental medical object detection tasks, due to the conceptual gap between medical and natural domains. To overcome these challenges, we introduce the \method~framework, which comprises two main components: 1) Instance-level Prompt Generation (\ipg), which decouples fine-grained instance-level knowledge from images and generates prompts that focus on dense predictions, and 2) Decoupled Prompt Attention (\dpa), which decouples the original prompt attention, enabling a more direct and efficient transfer of prompt information while reducing memory usage and mitigating catastrophic forgetting. We collect 13 clinical, cross-modal, multi-organ, and multi-category datasets, referred to as \dataset, and experiments demonstrate that \method~outperforms existing SOTA methods, with FAP improvements of 5.44\%, 4.83\%, 12.88\%, and 4.59\% in full data, 1-shot, 10-shot, and 50-shot settings, respectively.

英摘: Large repositories of image-caption pairs are essential for the development of vision-language models. However, these datasets are often extracted from noisy data scraped from the web, and contain many mislabeled instances. In order to improve the reliability of downstream models, it is important to identify and filter images with incorrect captions. However, beyond filtering based on image-caption embedding similarity, no prior works have proposed other methods to filter noisy multimodal data, or concretely assessed the impact of noisy captioning data on downstream training. In this work, we propose, theoretically justify, and empirically validate LEMoN, a method to identify label errors in image-caption datasets. [No.123] Improving Out-of-Distribution Detection via Dynamic Covariance Calibration
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过动态协方差校准提高分布外检测性能
类别: social aspects->safety
作者: Kaiyu Guo;Zijian Wang;Tan Pan;Brian C. Lovell;Mahsa Baktashmotlagh;
机构: University of Queensland;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45102
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09399
英摘: Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection is essential for the trustworthiness of AI systems. Methods using prior information (i.e., subspace-based methods) have shown effective performance by extracting information geometry to detect OOD data with a more appropriate distance metric. However, these methods fail to address the geometry distorted by ill-distributed samples, due to the limitation of statically extracting information geometry from the training distribution. In this paper, we argue that the influence of ill-distributed samples can be corrected by dynamically adjusting the prior geometry in response to new data. Based on this insight, we propose a novel approach that dynamically updates the prior covariance matrix using real-time input features, refining its information. Specifically, we reduce the covariance along the direction of real-time input features and constrain adjustments to the residual space, thus preserving essential data characteristics and avoiding effects on unintended directions in the principal space. We evaluate our method on two pre-trained models for the CIFAR dataset and five pre-trained models for ImageNet-1k, including the self-supervised DINO model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances OOD detection across various models. The code is released at https://github.com/workerbcd/ooddcc.
中摘: 分布外（Out-of-Distribution，OOD）检测对于AI系统的可信度至关重要。利用先验信息（即子空间方法）的方法通过提取信息几何来使用更合适的距离度量检测OOD数据，显示出了有效的性能。然而，这些方法无法解决由不良分布样本引起的几何畸变问题，因为它们受限于从训练分布静态提取信息几何的能力。在本文中，我们提出观点认为，可以通过动态调整先验几何来纠正不良分布样本的影响，以应对新数据。基于这一见解，我们提出了一种新颖的方法，该方法使用实时输入特征动态更新先验协方差矩阵，并对其进行细化。具体来说，我们沿着实时输入特征的方向减少协方差，并将调整限制在残差空间中，从而保留了关键的数据特性，并避免了对主空间中非预期方向的影响。我们在CIFAR数据集上的两个预训练模型和ImageNet-1k上的五个预训练模型上评估了我们的方法，包括自监督学习的DINO模型。广泛的实验表明，我们的方法显著提升了各种模型的OOD检测性能。相关代码已发布在https://github.com/workerbcd/ooddcc。

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01656
英摘: Mixture of Experts (MoE), an ensemble of specialized models equipped with a router that dynamically distributes each input to appropriate experts, has achieved successful results in the field of machine learning. However, theoretical understanding of this architecture is falling behind due to its inherent complexity. In this paper, we theoretically study the sample and runtime complexity of MoE following the stochastic gradient descent (SGD) when learning a regression task with an underlying cluster structure of single index models. On the one hand, we prove that a vanilla neural network fails in detecting such a latent organization as it can only process the problem as a whole. This is intrinsically related to the concept of information exponent which is low for each cluster, but increases when we consider the entire task. On the other hand, we show that a MoE succeeds in dividing this problem into easier subproblems by leveraging the ability of each expert to weakly recover the simpler function corresponding to an individual cluster. To the best of our knowledge, this work is among the first to explore the benefits of the MoE framework by examining its SGD dynamics in the context of nonlinear regression.
中摘: 混合专家模型（MoE），一种配备路由器的特殊模型集合，能够在输入动态分配给合适的专家方面取得成功的成果，在机器学习领域已有广泛应用。然而，由于其固有的复杂性，对这种架构的理论理解相对滞后。在本文中，我们研究了在单指数模型潜在簇结构背景下使用随机梯度下降（SGD）学习回归任务时，MoE的样本和运行时间复杂度。一方面，我们证明了一般的神经网络无法检测到这种潜在的组织结构，因为它只能整体处理问题。这与信息指数的概念密切相关，每个簇的信息指数较低，但在考虑整个任务时则会增加。另一方面，我们展示了MoE能够通过利用每个专家弱恢复对应于单一簇的简单函数的能力，将这个问题分解为更易处理的子问题。据我们所知，这项工作是首次通过分析MoE框架下的SGD动态来探讨其在非线性回归中的优势。

[No.125] CostFilter-AD: Enhancing Anomaly Detection through Matching Cost Filtering
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: CostFilter-AD：通过匹配成本过滤增强异常检测
类别: applications->computer vision
作者: Zhe Zhang;Mingxiu Cai;Hanxiao Wang;Gaochang Wu;Tianyou Chai;Xiatian Zhu;
机构: Northeastern University;University of Surrey;Onfido;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46359
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01476
英摘: Unsupervised anomaly detection (UAD) seeks to localize the anomaly mask of an input image with respect to normal samples. Either by reconstructing normal counterparts (reconstruction-based) or by learning an image feature embedding space (embedding-based), existing approaches fundamentally rely on image-level or feature-level matching to derive anomaly scores. Often, such a matching process is inaccurate yet overlooked, leading to sub-optimal detection. To address this issue, we introduce the concept of cost filtering, borrowed from classical matching tasks, such as depth and flow estimation, into the UAD problem. We call this approach {\em CostFilter-AD}. Specifically, we first construct a matching cost volume between the input and normal samples, comprising two spatial dimensions and one matching dimension that encodes potential matches. To refine this, we propose a cost volume filtering network, guided by the input observation as an attention query across multiple feature layers, which effectively suppresses matching noise while preserving edge structures and capturing subtle anomalies. Designed as a generic post-processing plug-in, CostFilter-AD can be integrated with either reconstruction-based or embedding-based methods. Extensive experiments on MVTec-AD and VisA benchmarks validate the generic benefits of CostFilter-AD for both single- and multi-class UAD tasks. Code and models will be released at https://github.com/ZHE-SAPI/CostFilter-AD.
中摘: 无监督异常检测（UAD）旨在根据正常样本定位输入图像中的异常掩码。现有的方法要么通过重建正常样本（基于重构的方法），要么通过学习图像特征嵌入空间（基于嵌入的方法），本质上依赖于图像级或特征级匹配来计算异常评分。然而，这种匹配过程往往不够准确却被忽视了，导致检测效果不佳。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了成本过滤的概念，借鉴了经典匹配任务如深度和流估计中的方法，应用于UAD问题。我们称之为“CostFilter-AD”方法。具体而言，我们首先在输入图像与正常样本之间构建一个匹配成本体素，包含两个空间维度和一个编码潜在匹配的匹配维度。为了细化这个成本体素，我们提出了一种由输入观察引导的成本体素过滤网络，在多个特征层上作为注意力查询，有效地抑制了匹配噪声，同时保留了边缘结构并捕捉到了细微的异常。作为一种通用的后处理插件，CostFilter-AD可以与基于重构的方法或基于嵌入的方法集成使用。在MVTec-AD和VisA基准上的广泛实验验证了CostFilter-AD在单类和多类UAD任务中的通用优势。代码和模型将在https://github.com/ZHE-SAPI/CostFilter-AD发布。

英摘: Utilizing the complex inter-variable causal relationships within multivariate time-series provides a promising avenue toward more robust and reliable multivariate time-series anomaly detection (MTSAD) but remains an underexplored area of research. This paper proposes Causality-Aware contrastive learning for RObust multivariate Time-Series (CAROTS), a novel MTSAD pipeline that incorporates the notion of causality into contrastive learning. CAROTS employs two data augmentors to obtain causality-preserving and -disturbing samples that serve as a wide range of normal variations and synthetic anomalies, respectively. With causality-preserving and -disturbing samples as positives and negatives, CAROTS performs contrastive learning to train an encoder whose latent space separates normal and abnormal samples based on causality. Moreover, CAROTS introduces a similarity-filtered one-class contrastive loss that encourages the contrastive learning process to gradually incorporate more semantically diverse samples with common causal relationships. Extensive experiments on five real-world and two synthetic datasets validate that the integration of causal relationships endows CAROTS with improved MTSAD capabilities. [No.127] Emoji Attack: Enhancing Jailbreak Attacks Against Judge LLM Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: Emoji攻击：增强对法官LLM检测的逃逸攻击
类别: social aspects->safety
作者: Zhipeng Wei;Yuqi Liu;N. Benjamin Erichson;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;China University of Political Science and Law;Lawrence Berkeley National Laboratory;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45356
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01077
英摘: Jailbreaking techniques trick Large Language Models (LLMs) into producing restricted output, posing a potential threat. One line of defense is to use another LLM as a Judge to evaluate the harmfulness of generated text. However, we reveal that these Judge LLMs are vulnerable to token segmentation bias, an issue that arises when delimiters alter the tokenization process, splitting words into smaller sub-tokens. This alters the embeddings of the entire sequence, reducing detection accuracy and allowing harmful content to be misclassified as safe. In this paper, we introduce Emoji Attack, a novel strategy that amplifies existing jailbreak prompts by exploiting token segmentation bias. Our method leverages in-context learning to systematically insert emojis into text before it is evaluated by a Judge LLM, inducing embedding distortions that significantly lower the likelihood of detecting unsafe content. Unlike traditional delimiters, emojis also introduce semantic ambiguity, making them particularly effective in this attack. Through experiments on state-of-the-art Judge LLMs, we demonstrate that Emoji Attack substantially reduces the unsafe prediction rate, bypassing existing safeguards.
中摘: 越狱技术会诱骗大型语言模型（LLMs）产生受限输出，这可能带来潜在威胁。一种防御措施是使用另一个LLM作为评判者来评估生成文本的危害性。然而，我们揭示这些评判者LLM容易受到标记分割偏差的影响，这是一种当分隔符改变分词过程、将单词分割成更小子词时出现的问题。这种分割改变了整个序列的嵌入表示，降低了检测准确性，并允许有害内容被误分类为安全内容。在本文中，我们介绍了一种名为Emoji Attack的新策略，该策略通过利用标记分割偏差来放大现有的越狱提示。我们的方法利用上下文学习，系统地在文本中插入表情符号，在由评判者LLM评估之前诱导嵌入失真，从而显著降低检测不安全内容的可能性。与传统的分隔符不同，表情符号还引入了语义模糊性，使其在这种攻击中特别有效。通过在最先进的评判者LLM上的实验，我们证明Emoji Attack显著降低了不安全预测率，绕过了现有防护措施。

[No.128] Stay-Positive: A Case for Ignoring Real Image Features in Fake Image Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 保持积极：在假图像检测中忽略真实图像特征的理由
类别: social aspects->security
作者: Anirudh Sundara Rajan;Yong Jae Lee;
机构: University of Wisconsin-Madison;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45069
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07778
英摘: Detecting AI generated images is a challenging yet essential task. A primary difficulty arises from the detectors tendency to rely on spurious patterns, such as compression artifacts, which can influence its decisions. These issues often stem from specific patterns that the detector associates with the real data distribution, making it difficult to isolate the actual generative traces. We argue that an image should be classified as fake if and only if it contains artifacts introduced by the generative model. Based on this premise, we propose Stay Positive, an algorithm designed to constrain the detectors focus to generative artifacts while disregarding those associated with real data. Experimental results demonstrate that detectors trained with Stay Positive exhibit reduced susceptibility to spurious correlations, leading to improved generalization and robustness to post processing. Additionally, unlike detectors that associate artifacts with real images, those that focus purely on fake artifacts are better at detecting inpainted real images.
中摘: 检测AI生成的图像是一项具有挑战性但又必不可少的任务。主要困难在于检测器倾向于依赖虚假模式，如压缩伪影，这些模式会影响其决策。这些问题通常源于检测器与真实数据分布相关的特定模式，使得难以将实际生成的痕迹区分开来。我们认为，如果图像包含由生成模型引入的伪影，则该图像应被分类为假的。基于这一前提，我们提出了一种名为Stay Positive的算法，旨在限制检测器的关注点集中在生成伪影上，而忽略与真实数据相关的伪影。实验结果表明，使用Stay Positive训练的检测器对虚假相关性的敏感度较低，从而提高了泛化能力和对抗后处理的鲁棒性。此外，与将伪影与真实图像关联的检测器不同，专注于假伪影的检测器在检测修补的真实图像方面表现更好。

[No.129] Online Detection of LLM-Generated Texts via Sequential Hypothesis Testing by Betting
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过赌注进行的LLM生成文本的序列假设检验在线检测
类别: general machine learning->everything else
作者: Can Chen;Jun-Kun Wang;
机构: University of California, San Diego;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44240
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22318
英摘: Developing algorithms to differentiate between machine-generated texts and human-written texts has garnered substantial attention in recent years. Existing methods in this direction typically concern an offline setting where a dataset containing a mix of real and machine-generated texts is given upfront, and the task is to determine whether each sample in the dataset is from a large language model (LLM) or a human. However, in many practical scenarios, sources such as news websites, social media accounts, and online forums publish content in a streaming fashion. Therefore, in this online scenario, how to quickly and accurately determine whether the source is an LLM with strong statistical guarantees is crucial for these media or platforms to function effectively and prevent the spread of misinformation and other potential misuse of LLMs. To tackle the problem of online detection, we develop an algorithm based on the techniques of sequential hypothesis testing by betting that not only builds upon and complements existing offline detection techniques but also enjoys statistical guarantees, which include a controlled false positive rate and the expected time to correctly identify a source as an LLM. Experiments were conducted to demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.
中摘: 近年来，开发算法以区分机器生成的文字和人类撰写的文字引起了广泛关注。现有方法通常关注离线设置，在这种设置下，会提前给出一个包含真实文本和机器生成文本混合的语料库，并且任务是确定数据集中每个样本是来自大型语言模型（LLM）还是人类。然而，在许多实际场景中，新闻网站、社交媒体账号和在线论坛等来源以流式的方式发布内容。因此，在这种在线场景中，如何快速而准确地判断来源是否为具有强大统计保证的LLM，对于这些媒体或平台有效运作以及防止虚假信息和其他潜在的LLM滥用至关重要。为了应对在线检测的问题，我们基于顺序假设检验技术开发了一种算法，不仅建立在并补充了现有的离线检测技术之上，还享有统计保证，包括控制的假阳性率和正确识别源为LLM所需的预期时间。实验结果证明了我们方法的有效性。

[No.130] Improving Out-of-Distribution Detection with Markov Logic Networks
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 使用马尔可夫逻辑网络提高分布外检测能力
类别: social aspects->safety
作者: Konstantin Kirchheim;Frank Ortmeier;
机构: Otto-von-Guericke University;Otto-von-Guericke-Universitaet Magdeburg;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46267
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04241
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential for ensuring the reliability of deep learning models operating in open-world scenarios. Current OOD detectors mainly rely on statistical models to identify unusual patterns in the latent representations of a deep neural network. This work proposes to augment existing OOD detectors with probabilistic reasoning, utilizing Markov logic networks (MLNs). MLNs connect first-order logic with probabilistic reasoning to assign probabilities to inputs based on weighted logical constraints defined over human-understandable concepts, which offers improved explainability. Through extensive experiments on multiple datasets, we demonstrate that MLNs can significantly enhance the performance of a wide range of existing OOD detectors while maintaining computational efficiency. Furthermore, we introduce a simple algorithm for learning logical constraints for OOD detection from a dataset and showcase its effectiveness.
中摘: 开放分布（OOD）检测对于确保深度学习模型在开放世界场景中的可靠性至关重要。当前的OOD检测器主要依赖于统计模型来识别深度神经网络潜在表示中的异常模式。本研究提议将概率推理与现有的OOD检测器相结合，利用马尔可夫逻辑网络（MLN）。MLN将一阶逻辑与概率推理结合起来，根据定义在易于理解的概念上的加权逻辑约束为输入分配概率，从而提高解释性。通过在多个数据集上进行广泛的实验，我们证明MLN能够显著增强各种现有OOD检测器的性能，同时保持计算效率。此外，我们介绍了一个简单的算法，用于从数据集中学习OOD检测的一阶逻辑约束，并展示了其有效性。

[No.132] Few-Shot Learner Generalizes Across AI-Generated Image Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 少样本学习者在AI生成图像检测中实现跨域泛化
类别: applications->computer vision
作者: Shiyu Wu;Jing Liu;Jing Li;Yequan Wang;
机构: University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Chinese Academy of Sciences;Harbin Institute of Technology;Beijing Academy of Artificial Intelligence;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43709
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08763
英摘: Current fake image detectors trained on large synthetic image datasets perform satisfactorily on limited studied generative models. However, these detectors suffer a notable performance decline over unseen models. Besides, collecting adequate training data from online generative models is often expensive or infeasible. To overcome these issues, we propose Few-Shot Detector (FSD), a novel AI-generated image detector which learns a specialized metric space for effectively distinguishing unseen fake images using very few samples. Experiments show that FSD achieves state-of-the-art performance by $+11.6\%$ average accuracy on the GenImage dataset with only $10$ additional samples. More importantly, our method is better capable of capturing the intra-category commonality in unseen images without further training. Our code is available at https://github.com/teheperinko541/Few-Shot-AIGI-Detector.
中摘: 当前在大规模合成图像数据集上训练的假图像检测器在研究有限的生成模型上表现良好，但在未见过的模型上性能明显下降。此外，从在线生成模型收集足够的训练数据往往成本高昂或不可行。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新型AI生成图像检测器——Few-Shot Detector（FSD），它能够仅使用极少数量的样本学习一个专门的度量空间，从而有效地区分未见过的假图像。实验结果显示，FSD在GenImage数据集上仅使用额外的10个样本，平均准确率提高了11.6%，达到了最先进的性能。更重要的是，我们的方法能够在未见过的图像中更好地捕捉类别内的共同特征，而无需进一步训练。我们的代码可在https://github.com/teheperinko541/Few-Shot-AIGI-Detector获取。

[No.133] Unlocking the Capabilities of Large Vision-Language Models for Generalizable and Explainable Deepfake Detection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: 利用大型视觉-语言模型解锁通用且可解释的换脸检测能力
类别: applications->computer vision
作者: Peipeng Yu;Jianwei Fei;Hui Gao;Xuan Feng;Zhihua Xia;Chip Hong Chang;
机构: Jinan University;University of Macau;Nanyang Technological University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43687
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14853
英摘: Current Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in understanding multimodal data, but their potential remains underexplored for deepfake detection due to the misalignment of their knowledge and forensics patterns. To this end, we present a novel framework that unlocks LVLMs' potential capabilities for deepfake detection. Our framework includes a Knowledge-guided Forgery Detector (KFD), a Forgery Prompt Learner (FPL), and a Large Language Model (LLM). The KFD is used to calculate correlations between image features and pristine/deepfake image description embeddings, enabling forgery classification and localization. The outputs of the KFD are subsequently processed by the Forgery Prompt Learner to construct fine-grained forgery prompt embeddings. These embeddings, along with visual and question prompt embeddings, are fed into the LLM to generate textual detection responses. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks, including FF++, CDF2, DFD, DFDCP, DFDC, and DF40, demonstrate that our scheme surpasses state-of-the-art methods in generalization performance, while also supporting multi-turn dialogue capabilities.
中摘: 当前的大规模视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）已经在理解多模态数据方面展现了非凡的能力，但由于其知识与取证模式之间的不匹配，它们在深度伪造检测方面的潜力尚未得到充分挖掘。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新的框架，以释放LVLMs在深度伪造检测方面的潜在能力。该框架包括一个知识引导的伪造检测器（KFD）、一个伪造提示学习器（FPL）和一个大规模语言模型（LLM）。KFD用于计算图像特征与原始/深度伪造图像描述嵌入之间的相关性，从而实现伪造分类和定位。KFD的输出随后由伪造提示学习器处理，构建细粒度的伪造提示嵌入。这些嵌入与视觉和问题提示嵌入一起输入到LLM中，生成文本检测响应。我们在多个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，我们的方案在泛化性能上超越了现有最先进的方法，同时支持多轮对话能力。

[No.134] B-score: Detecting biases in large language models using response history
来源: ICML 2025
命中: detect
标题: B分值：通过响应历史检测大型语言模型中的偏见
类别: social aspects->accountability transparency and interpretability
作者: An Vo;Mohammad Reza Taesiri;Daeyoung Kim;Anh Totti Nguyen;
机构: ;University of Alberta;Auburn University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44236
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18545
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) often exhibit strong biases, e.g, against women or in favor of the number 7. We investigate whether LLMs would be able to output less biased answers when allowed to observe their prior answers to the same question in a multi-turn conversation. To understand which types of questions invite more biased answers, we test LLMs on our proposed set of questions that span 9 topics and belong to three types: (1) Subjective; (2) Random; and (3) Objective. Interestingly, LLMs are able to "de-bias" themselves in a multi-turn conversation in response to questions that seek an Random, unbiased answer. Furthermore, we propose B-score, a novel metric that is effective in detecting biases to Subjective, Random, Easy, and Hard questions. On MMLU, HLE, and CSQA, leveraging B-score substantially improves the verification accuracy of LLM answers (i.e, accepting LLM correct answers and rejecting incorrect ones) compared to using verbalized confidence scores or the frequency of single-turn answers alone. Code and data are available at: https://b-score.github.io.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）往往表现出强烈的偏见，例如对女性有偏见或偏好数字7。我们研究了当LLMs被允许观察他们在多轮对话中对同一问题的先前回答时，它们是否能够输出较少偏见的答案。为了了解哪些类型的问题更容易引发偏见的回答，我们在涵盖9个主题、分为三类的提问集上测试了LLMs：（1）主观性问题；（2）随机性问题；（3）客观性问题。有趣的是，LLMs能够在多轮对话中对寻求随机且无偏见答案的问题进行“去偏”。此外，我们提出了B分值，这是一种新颖的指标，能够有效检测主观性、随机性以及容易和困难问题中的偏见。在MMLU、HLE和CSQA数据集上，利用B分值显著提高了对LLMs答案的验证准确性（即接受正确的答案并拒绝错误的答案），这比仅使用口头化的置信度评分或单独依赖单轮回答的频率更为有效。相关代码和数据可在以下网址获取：https://b-score.github.io。

[No.138] Seeing What Matters: Generalizable AI-generated Video Detection with Forensic-Oriented Augmentation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 关注关键信息：面向取证增强的通用人工智能生成视频检测
类别: applications
作者: Riccardo Corvi;Davide Cozzolino;Ekta Prashnani;Shalini De Mello;Koki Nagano;Luisa Verdoliva;
机构: ;University of Naples Federico II;NVIDIA;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=dOGXKBL7IE
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16802
英摘: Synthetic video generation is progressing very rapidly. The latest models can produce very realistic high-resolution videos that are virtually indistinguishable from real ones. Although several video forensic detectors have been recently proposed, they often exhibit poor generalization, which limits their applicability in a real-world scenario. Our key insight to overcome this issue is to guide the detector towards *seeing what really matters*. In fact, a well-designed forensic classifier should focus on identifying intrinsic low-level artifacts introduced by a generative architecture rather than relying on high-level semantic flaws that characterize a specific model. In this work, first, we study different generative architectures, searching and identifying discriminative features that are unbiased, robust to impairments, and shared across models. Then, we introduce a novel forensic-oriented data augmentation strategy based on the wavelet decomposition and replace specific frequency-related bands to drive the model to exploit more relevant forensic cues. Our novel training paradigm improves the generalizability of AI-generated video detectors, without the need for complex algorithms and large datasets that include multiple synthetic generators. To evaluate our approach, we train the detector using data from a single generative model and test it against videos produced by a wide range of other models. Despite its simplicity, our method achieves a significant accuracy improvement over state-of-the-art detectors and obtains excellent results even on very recent generative models, such as NOVA and FLUX.
中摘: 合成视频生成正在迅速进步。最新的模型能够生成非常逼真、高分辨率的视频，几乎与真实视频无法区分。尽管已经提出了几种视频取证检测器，但它们往往表现出较差的一般化能力，这限制了它们在实际场景中的应用。我们克服这一问题的关键洞察是引导检测器“看到真正重要的内容”。实际上，一个设计良好的取证分类器应该专注于识别由生成架构引入的固有低级伪影，而不是依赖于特定模型特有的高级语义错误。在这项工作中，首先，我们研究不同的生成架构，寻找并识别出无偏见、对损伤具有鲁棒性且跨模型共享的判别特征。然后，我们提出了一种基于小波分解的新颖的取证导向数据增强策略，通过替换特定频率相关的频带来驱动模型利用更多相关的取证线索。我们的新颖训练范式提高了AI生成视频检测器的一般化能力，无需使用复杂的算法和包含多种合成生成器的大规模数据集。为了评估我们的方法，我们在单一生成模型的数据上训练检测器，并将其测试应用于由广泛其他模型生成的视频。尽管方法简单，但我们的方法在准确度方面显著优于最先进的检测器，并且即使在非常近期的生成模型（如NOVA和FLUX）上也能取得优异的结果。

[No.139] Spot the Fake: Large Multimodal Model-Based Synthetic Image Detection with Artifact Explanation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 鉴别假图：基于大型多模态模型的合成图像检测及 artifacts 解释
类别: applications
作者: Siwei Wen;Junyan Ye;Peilin Feng;Hengrui Kang;Zichen Wen;Yize Chen;Jiang Wu;wenjun wu;Conghui He;Weijia Li;
机构: Beihang University;Sun Yat-sen University;Shanghai AI Laboratory;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;Chinese University of Hong Kong;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=xLFYd1owiP
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14905
英摘: With the rapid advancement of Artificial Intelligence Generated Content (AIGC) technologies, synthetic images have become increasingly prevalent in everyday life, posing new challenges for authenticity assessment and detection. Despite the effectiveness of existing methods in evaluating image authenticity and locating forgeries, these approaches often lack human interpretability and do not fully address the growing complexity of synthetic data. To tackle these challenges, we introduce FakeVLM, a specialized large multimodal model designed for both general synthetic image and DeepFake detection tasks. FakeVLM not only excels in distinguishing real from fake images but also provides clear, natural language explanations for image artifacts, enhancing interpretability. Additionally, we present FakeClue, a comprehensive dataset containing over 100,000 images across seven categories, annotated with fine-grained artifact clues in natural language. FakeVLM demonstrates performance comparable to expert models while eliminating the need for additional classifiers, making it a robust solution for synthetic data detection. Extensive evaluations across multiple datasets confirm the superiority of FakeVLM in both authenticity classification and artifact explanation tasks, setting a new benchmark for synthetic image detection. The code, model weights, and dataset can be found here: https://github.com/opendatalab/FakeVLM.
中摘: 随着人工智能生成内容（AIGC）技术的迅速发展，合成图像在日常生活中越来越普遍，这为真实性评估和检测带来了新的挑战。尽管现有的方法在评估图像真实性及定位伪造方面效果显著，但这些方法往往缺乏人类可解释性，并未能充分应对合成数据日益增长的复杂性。为应对这些挑战，我们引入了FakeVLM，这是一种专门针对一般合成图像和Deepfake检测任务的大规模多模态模型。FakeVLM不仅能够出色地区分真实与合成图像，还能以清晰的自然语言提供图像伪迹的解释，增强其可解释性。此外，我们还推出了FakeClue，这是一个包含超过100,000张图像的综合数据集，这些图像被划分为七个类别，并用自然语言标注了详细的伪迹线索。FakeVLM的表现与专家模型相当，同时无需额外的分类器，使其成为合成数据检测的稳健解决方案。跨多个数据集的广泛评估证实了FakeVLM在真实性分类和伪迹解释任务中的优越性能，为合成图像检测设立了新的标准。有关代码、模型权重和数据集的信息，请访问：https://github.com/opendatalab/FakeVLM。

[No.140] From Specificity to Generality: Revisiting Generalizable Artifacts in Detecting Face Deepfakes
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 从具体性到一般性：重新审视检测面部Deepfake中的可泛化制品
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Long Ma;Zhiyuan Yan;Jin Xu;Yize Chen;Qinglang Guo;Zhen Bi;Yong Liao;Hui Lin;
机构: ;Peking University;University of Science and Technology of China;Chinese University of Hong Kong;China Academy of Electronics and Information Technology;China Electronics Technology Group Corporation;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=HCSjARBq5T
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04827
英摘: Detecting deepfakes has been an increasingly important topic, especially given the rapid development of AI generation techniques. In this paper, we ask: How can we build a universal detection framework that is effective for most facial deepfakes? One significant challenge is the wide variety of deepfake generators available, resulting in varying forgery artifacts (e.g., lighting inconsistency, color mismatch, etc). But should we ``teach" the detector to learn all these artifacts separately? It is impossible and impractical to elaborate on them all. So the core idea is to pinpoint the more common and general artifacts across different deepfakes. Accordingly, we categorize deepfake artifacts into two distinct yet complementary types: Face Inconsistency Artifacts (FIA) and Up-Sampling Artifacts (USA). FIA arise from the challenge of generating all intricate details, inevitably causing inconsistencies between the complex facial features and relatively uniform surrounding areas. USA, on the other hand, are the inevitable traces left by the generator's decoder during the up-sampling process. This categorization stems from the observation that all existing deepfakes typically exhibit one or both of these artifacts. To achieve this, we propose a new data-level pseudo-fake creation framework that constructs fake samples with only the FIA and USA, without introducing extra less-general artifacts. Specifically, we employ a super-resolution to simulate the USA, while design a Blender module that uses image-level self-blending on diverse facial regions to create the FIA. We surprisingly found that, with this intuitive design, a standard image classifier trained only with our pseudo-fake data can non-trivially generalize well to unseen deepfakes.
中摘: 检测深度假（deepfakes）已成为一个越来越重要的话题，尤其是考虑到AI生成技术的迅速发展。在本文中，我们提出一个问题：如何构建一个适用于大多数面部深度假的有效检测框架？一个主要挑战在于可供选择的深度假生成器种类繁多，导致伪造痕迹各不相同（例如，光照不一致、色彩不符等）。但我们是否应该让检测器分别学习所有这些痕迹呢？这既不可能也不实际。因此，核心思想是指出不同深度假中更为普遍和通用的痕迹。据此，我们将深度假痕迹分为两类：面部不一致性痕迹（FIA）和上采样痕迹（USA）。FIA源于生成所有复杂细节的挑战，不可避免地会导致面部特征与相对均匀的背景区域之间的一致性问题。而USA则是生成器解码器在上采样过程中留下的不可避免的痕迹。这种分类源自观察到现有的所有深度假通常会表现出这两种痕迹之一或两者兼有。为了实现这一点，我们提出了一种新的数据级别伪假样本创建框架，仅使用FIA和USA构建假样本，而不引入额外的不具普遍性的痕迹。具体而言，我们利用超分辨率模拟USA，设计了一个Blender模块，在不同的面部区域使用图像级别的自我融合来生成FIA。我们惊讶地发现，通过这种直观的设计，仅用我们的伪假数据训练的标准图像分类器能够较好地泛化到未见过的深度假。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.141] Among Us: A Sandbox for Measuring and Detecting Agentic Deception
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: Among Us：一个用于测量和检测代理欺骗的沙箱
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Satvik Golechha;Adrià Garriga-Alonso;
机构: Independent;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=XP3v1THxsq
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04072
英摘: Prior studies on deception in language-based AI agents typically assess whether the agent produces a false statement about a topic, or makes a binary choice prompted by a goal, rather than allowing open-ended deceptive behavior to emerge in pursuit of a longer-term goal. To fix this, we introduce Among Us, a sandbox social deception game where LLM-agents exhibit long-term, open-ended deception as a consequence of the game objectives. While most benchmarks saturate quickly, Among Us can be expected to last much longer, because it is a multi-player game far from equilibrium. Using the sandbox, we evaluate 18 proprietary and open-weight LLMs and uncover a general trend: models trained with RL are comparatively much better at producing deception than detecting it. We evaluate the effectiveness of methods to detect lying and deception: logistic regression on the activations and sparse autoencoders (SAEs). We find that probes trained on a dataset of "pretend you're a dishonest model:.." generalize extremely well out-of-distribution, consistently obtaining AUROCs over 95% even when evaluated just on the deceptive statement, without the chain of thought. We also find two SAE features that work well at deception detection but are unable to steer the model to lie less. We hope our open-sourced sandbox, game logs, and probes serve to anticipate and mitigate deceptive behavior and capabilities in language-based agents.
中摘: 以往对语言基础AI代理中的欺骗行为的研究通常评估这些代理是否关于某个话题产生了虚假陈述，或者是否在目标驱动下做出二元选择，而不是允许代理通过追求长期目标而表现出开放式的欺骗行为。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了《Among Us》这一沙盒社交欺骗游戏，在游戏中，LLM代理会展现出由于游戏目标而导致的长期、开放式欺骗行为。虽然大多数基准测试很快达到饱和状态，但《Among Us》可以预期会持续更长时间，因为它是一个远离平衡状态的多人游戏。利用沙盒环境，我们评估了18个专有和开源权重的LLM，并发现一个普遍趋势：使用强化学习（RL）训练的模型在产生欺骗行为方面明显优于检测欺骗行为。我们还评估了检测说谎和欺骗的方法：逻辑回归在激活上的应用和稀疏自编码器（SAE）。我们发现，针对“假装你是不诚实的模型…”的数据集训练的探针在分布外的表现极其出色，即使仅在评估欺骗性陈述时也能获得超过95%的AUROC值，而不需要考虑推理过程。我们还发现两种SAE特征在欺骗检测方面表现良好，但无法引导模型减少说谎。我们希望我们的开源沙盒、游戏日志和探针能够帮助预测并减轻语言基础代理中的欺骗行为及其能力。

[No.142] Scalable, Explainable and Provably Robust Anomaly Detection with One-Step Flow Matching
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 一气呵成流匹配：实现可扩展、可解释且可证明鲁棒的异常检测
类别: general machine learning
作者: Zhong Li;Qi Huang;Yuxuan Zhu;Lincen Yang;Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri;Niki van Stein;Matthijs van Leeuwen;
机构: Leiden University;Rensselaer Polytechnic Institute;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=jDYuadVajk
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.18328
英摘: We introduce Time-Conditioned Contraction Matching (TCCM), a novel method for semi-supervised anomaly detection in tabular data. TCCM is inspired by flow matching, a recent generative modeling framework that learns velocity fields between probability distributions and has shown strong performance compared to diffusion models and generative adversarial networks. Instead of directly applying flow matching as originally formulated, TCCM builds on its core idea -- learning velocity fields between distributions -- but simplifies the framework by predicting a time-conditioned contraction vector toward a fixed target (the origin) at each sampled time step. This design offers three key advantages: (1) a lightweight and scalable training objective that removes the need for solving ordinary differential equations during training and inference; (2) an efficient scoring strategy called one time-step deviation, which quantifies deviation from expected contraction behavior in a single forward pass, addressing the inference bottleneck of existing continuous-time models such as DTE (a diffusion-based model with leading anomaly detection accuracy but heavy inference cost); and (3) explainability and provable robustness, as the learned velocity field operates directly in input space, making the anomaly score inherently feature-wise attributable; moreover, the score function is Lipschitz-continuous with respect to the input, providing theoretical guarantees under small perturbations. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark show that TCCM strikes a favorable balance between detection accuracy and inference cost, outperforming state-of-the-art methods -- especially on high-dimensional and large-scale datasets. The source code is available at our GitHub repository.
中摘: 我们介绍了时间条件化收缩匹配（TCCM），这是一种用于表格数据半监督异常检测的新方法。TCCM受到流匹配的启发，这是一种最近的生成建模框架，能够学习概率分布之间的速度场，并且在与扩散模型和生成对抗网络相比时表现出强大的性能。与原始的流匹配方法不同，TCCM在其核心思想——学习分布之间的速度场——的基础上进行改进，通过在每个采样时间步预测一个指向固定目标（原点）的时间条件化收缩向量来简化框架。这种设计提供了三个关键优势：（1）一种轻量级且可扩展的训练目标，消除了训练和推理过程中求解常微分方程的需求；（2）一种高效的评分策略，称为“单时间步偏差”，该策略在一个前向传递过程中量化了与预期收缩行为的偏差，解决了现有连续时间模型（如DTE，一种基于扩散的模型，在异常检测准确性方面表现突出但推理成本高昂）的推理瓶颈问题；以及（3）解释性和可证明的鲁棒性，因为学习到的速度场直接作用于输入空间，使得异常分数本质上具有特征级别的可归因性；此外，评分函数相对于输入是Lipschitz连续的，这在小扰动下提供了理论上的保证。在ADBench基准上的广泛实验表明，TCCM在检测准确性和推理成本之间取得了良好的平衡，特别是在高维和大规模数据集上优于最先进的方法。源代码可在我们的GitHub仓库中获得。

[No.143] ScatterAD: Temporal-Topological Scattering Mechanism for Time Series Anomaly Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 散射AD：时间-拓扑散射机制在时间序列异常检测中的应用
类别: deep learning
作者: Tao Yin;Shaochen Fu;Zhibin Zhang;Li Huang;Xiaohong Zhang;Yiyuan Yang;Kaixiang Yang;Meng Yan;
机构: Chongqing University;University of Oxford;South China University of Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=UB5u3KvOS1
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24414
英摘: One main challenge in time series anomaly detection for industrial IoT lies in the complex spatio-temporal couplings within multivariate data. However, traditional anomaly detection methods focus on modeling spatial or temporal dependencies independently, resulting in suboptimal representation learning and limited sensitivity to anomalous dispersion in high-dimensional spaces. In this work, we conduct an empirical analysis showing that both normal and anomalous samples tend to scatter in high-dimensional space, especially anomalous samples are markedly more dispersed. We formalize this dispersion phenomenon as scattering, quantified by the mean pairwise distance among sample representations, and leverage it as an inductive signal to enhance spatio-temporal anomaly detection. Technically, we propose ScatterAD to model representation scattering across temporal and topological dimensions. ScatterAD incorporates a topological encoder for capturing graph-structured scattering and a temporal encoder for constraining over-scattering through mean squared error minimization between neighboring time steps. We introduce a contrastive fusion mechanism to ensure the complementarity of the learned temporal and topological representations. Additionally, we theoretically show that maximizing the conditional mutual information between temporal and topological views improves cross-view consistency and enhances more discriminative representations. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks show that ScatterAD achieves state-of-the-art performance on multivariate time series anomaly detection. Code is available at this repository: https://github.com/jk-sounds/ScatterAD.
中摘: 工业物联网中的时间序列异常检测面临的主要挑战在于多变量数据内的复杂时空耦合。然而，传统的异常检测方法侧重于独立建模时空依赖性，这导致了次优的表示学习，并且在高维空间中对异常分散的敏感性有限。在这项工作中，我们进行了一项实证分析，表明正常样本和异常样本都会在高维空间中分散，尤其是异常样本更为分散。我们将这种分散现象形式化为散射，并通过量化样本表示之间的平均成对距离来衡量它，将其作为一种诱导信号来增强时空异常检测。技术上，我们提出了ScatterAD方法，以跨时间和拓扑维度建模表示散射。ScatterAD结合了一个拓扑编码器用于捕获基于图的散射，并通过相邻时间步的均方误差最小化来限制过度散射。我们引入了一种对比融合机制，以确保学习到的时间和拓扑表示的互补性。此外，我们从理论上证明，最大化时间和拓扑视图之间的条件互信息可以提高跨视图一致性并生成更具区分性的表示。在多个公开基准上的广泛实验表明，ScatterAD在多变量时间序列异常检测方面达到了最先进的性能。代码可在以下仓库获取：https://github.com/jk-sounds/ScatterAD。

英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is crucial for ensuring the reliability and safety of machine learning models in real-world applications, where they frequently face data distributions unseen during training. Despite progress, existing methods are often vulnerable to spurious correlations that mislead models and compromise robustness. To address this, we propose SPROD, a novel prototype-based OOD detection approach that explicitly addresses the challenge posed by unknown spurious correlations. Our post-hoc method refines class prototypes to mitigate bias from spurious features without additional data or hyperparameter tuning, and is broadly applicable across diverse backbones and OOD detection settings. However, recent advances in generative language modeling have resulted in significant overlap between the feature distributions of human-written and AI-generated text, blurring classification boundaries and making accurate detection increasingly challenging. To address the above challenges, we propose a DNA-inspired perspective, leveraging a repair-based process to directly and interpretably capture the intrinsic differences between human-written and AI-generated text. Building on this perspective, we introduce DNA-DetectLLM, a zero-shot detection method for distinguishing AI-generated and human-written text. 中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的迅速发展模糊了AI生成文本和人类撰写的文本之间的界限。这一进展带来了诸如误导信息、作者身份模糊和知识产权担忧等社会风险，突显了可靠AI生成文本检测方法的迫切需求。然而，近期生成语言模型的进步导致人类撰写的文本和AI生成的文本特征分布之间出现了显著重叠，这模糊了分类边界，并使得准确检测越来越具有挑战性。为了解决上述挑战，我们提出了一种DNA启发式的视角，利用基于修复的过程直接且可解释地捕捉人类撰写的文本与AI生成的文本之间的内在差异。在此基础上，我们引入了DNA-DetectLLM，这是一种零样本检测方法，用于区分AI生成的文本和人类撰写的文本。该方法为每个输入构建一个理想的AI生成序列，迭代修复非最优的标记，并量化累积的修复努力作为可解释的检测信号。实证评估表明，我们的方法在多个公开基准数据集上实现了最先进的检测性能，并且表现出强大的鲁棒性，能够抵抗各种对抗攻击和输入长度的变化。具体而言，DNA-DetectLLM在多个公共基准数据集上的AUROC提高了5.55%，F1分数提高了2.08%。代码和数据可在https://github.com/Xiaoweizhu57/DNA-DetectLLM获取。

[No.147] ReCon: Region-Controllable Data Augmentation with Rectification and Alignment for Object Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: ReCon: 区域可控的数据增强与校正对齐方法在目标检测中的应用

英摘: The scale and quality of datasets are crucial for training robust perception models. However, obtaining large-scale annotated data is both costly and time-consuming. Generative models have emerged as a powerful tool for data augmentation by synthesizing samples that adhere to desired distributions. However, current generative approaches often rely on complex post-processing or extensive fine-tuning on massive datasets to achieve satisfactory results, and they remain prone to content-position mismatches and semantic leakage. To overcome these limitations, we introduce ReCon, a novel augmentation framework that enhances the capacity of structure-controllable generative models for object detection. ReCon integrates region-guided rectification into the diffusion sampling process, using feedback from a pre-trained perception model to rectify misgenerated regions within diffusion sampling process. We further propose region-aligned cross-attention to enforce spatial-semantic alignment between image regions and their textual cues, thereby improving both semantic consistency and overall image fidelity. 中摘: 数据集的规模和质量对于训练稳健的感知模型至关重要。然而，获取大规模标注数据既昂贵又耗时。生成模型已成为通过合成符合期望分布的样本进行数据增强的一种强大工具。然而，当前的生成方法往往依赖于复杂的后处理或在大规模数据集上进行广泛的微调才能达到满意的效果，并且仍然容易出现内容位置匹配不当和语义泄露的问题。为克服这些局限性，我们引入了ReCon，这是一种新颖的数据增强框架，旨在增强结构可控生成模型在物体检测中的能力。ReCon 将区域引导校正整合到扩散采样过程中，利用预训练感知模型的反馈来校正扩散采样过程中的错误生成区域。我们还提出了区域对齐交叉注意力，以确保图像区域与其文本提示之间的空间语义对齐，从而提高语义一致性和整体图像保真度。大量实验表明，ReCon 显著提高了生成数据的质量和可训练性，在各种数据集、骨干网络架构和数据规模下均实现了性能提升。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/haoweiz23/ReCon 获取。

[No.148] On the Empirical Power of Goodness-of-Fit Tests in Watermark Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 水印检测中拟合优度检验的实际功效研究
类别: evaluation

英摘: Large language models (LLMs) raise concerns about content authenticity and integrity because they can generate human-like text at scale. Text watermarks, which embed detectable statistical signals into generated text, offer a provable way to verify content origin. Many detection methods rely on pivotal statistics that are i.i.d. under human-written text, making goodness-of-fit (GoF) tests a natural tool for watermark detection. However, GoF tests remain largely underexplored in this setting. In this paper, we systematically evaluate eight GoF tests across three popular watermarking schemes, using three open-source LLMs, two datasets, various generation temperatures, and multiple post-editing methods. Our results highlight that classic GoF tests are a simple yet powerful and underused tool for watermark detection in LLMs.

[No.150] Truth over Tricks: Measuring and Mitigating Shortcut Learning in Misinformation Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 真相胜于技巧：在虚假信息检测中的衡量与减轻捷径学习方法
类别: applications
作者: Herun Wan;Jiaying Wu;Minnan Luo;Zhi Zeng;Zhixiong Su;
机构: Xi'an Jiao Tong University;National University of Singapore;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ngxGNQE1M2
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02350
英摘: Misinformation detection models often rely on superficial cues (i.e., \emph{shortcuts}) that correlate with misinformation in training data but fail to generalize to the diverse and evolving nature of real-world misinformation. This issue is exacerbated by large language models (LLMs), which can easily generate convincing misinformation through simple prompts. We introduce TruthOverTricks, a unified evaluation paradigm for measuring shortcut learning in misinformation detection. TruthOverTricks categorizes shortcut behaviors into intrinsic shortcut induction and extrinsic shortcut injection, and evaluates seven representative detectors across 14 popular benchmarks, along with two new factual misinformation datasets, NQ-Misinfo and Streaming-Misinfo. Empirical results reveal that existing detectors suffer severe performance degradation when exposed to both naturally occurring and adversarially crafted shortcuts. To address this, we propose SMF, an LLM-augmented data augmentation framework that mitigates shortcut reliance through paraphrasing, factual summarization, and sentiment normalization. SMF consistently enhances robustness across 16 benchmarks, encouraging models to rely on deeper semantic understanding rather than shortcut cues. To promote the development of misinformation detectors, we have published the resources publicly at https://github.com/whr000001/TruthOverTricks.
中摘: 误信检测模型通常依赖于训练数据中与误信相关的表面线索（即，捷径），但在面对现实世界中多样且不断演变的误信时，这些模型往往无法泛化。这一问题在大型语言模型（LLMs）中尤为突出，因为它们可以通过简单的提示轻易生成令人信服的误信。我们引入了“TruthOverTricks”这一统一评估框架，用于衡量误信检测中的捷径学习。TruthOverTricks将捷径行为分为内在捷径诱导和外在捷径注入，并对七种代表性检测器进行了评估，涵盖14个流行基准测试，以及两个新的事实误信数据集NQ-Misinfo和Streaming-Misinfo。实证结果显示，现有检测器在遇到自然出现和对抗性构造的捷径时会遭受严重的性能下降。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了SMF（捷径缓解框架），这是一种增强型数据增强框架，通过改写、事实总结和情感规范化来减轻对捷径线索的依赖。SMF在16个基准测试中一致增强了鲁棒性，促使模型更多地依赖于深层次的语义理解而非表面线索。为了促进误信检测器的发展，我们已将这些资源公开发布在https://github.com/whr000001/TruthOverTricks。

[No.152] Advancing Machine-Generated Text Detection from an Easy to Hard Supervision Perspective
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 从易监督到难监督的角度推进机器生成文本检测
类别: general machine learning
作者: Chenwang Wu;Yiu-ming Cheung;Bo Han;Defu Lian;
机构: Hong Kong Baptist University;;University of Science and Technology of China;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=BUkXhMb7ml
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00988
英摘: Existing machine-generated text (MGT) detection methods implicitly assume labels as the "golden standard". However, we reveal boundary ambiguity in MGT detection, implying that traditional training paradigms are inexact. Moreover, limitations of human cognition and the superintelligence of detectors make inexact learning widespread and inevitable. To this end, we propose an easy-to-hard enhancement framework to provide reliable supervision under such inexact conditions. Distinct from knowledge distillation, our framework employs an easy supervisor targeting relatively simple longer-text detection tasks (despite weaker capabilities), to enhance the more challenging target detector. Firstly, longer texts targeted by supervisors theoretically alleviate the impact of inexact labels, laying the foundation for reliable supervision. Secondly, by structurally incorporating the detector into the supervisor, we theoretically model the supervisor as a lower performance bound for the detector. Thus, optimizing the supervisor indirectly optimizes the detector, ultimately approximating the underlying "golden" labels. Extensive experiments across diverse practical scenarios, including cross-LLM, cross-domain, mixed text, and paraphrase attacks, demonstrate the framework's significant detection effectiveness. The code is available at: https://github.com/tmlr-group/Easy2Hard.
中摘: 现有的机器生成文本（MGT）检测方法隐含地假设标签为“金标准”。然而，我们揭示了MGT检测中的边界模糊性，这表明传统的训练范式是不准确的。此外，人类认知的局限性和检测器的超级智能使得不准确的学习在广泛和不可避免的情况下发生。为此，我们提出了一种从易到难的增强框架，以在这些不准确条件下提供可靠的监督。与知识蒸馏不同，我们的框架采用了一个简单的监督者来针对相对简单的较长文本检测任务（尽管其能力较弱），以增强更具挑战性的目标检测器。首先，由监督者针对的较长文本理论上可以缓解不准确标签的影响，从而为可靠监督奠定基础。其次，通过结构化地将检测器纳入监督者中，我们理论上将监督者建模为检测器的较低性能下限。因此，优化监督者间接优化了检测器，最终逼近潜在的“金标准”标签。在包括跨大语言模型、跨领域、混合文本以及同义词攻击在内的多种实际场景下的广泛实验中，证明了该框架显著的检测效果。代码可在以下地址获取：https://github.com/tmlr-group/Easy2Hard。

[No.153] Preference Learning with Lie Detectors can Induce Honesty or Evasion
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 使用说谎检测器进行偏好学习可能会诱导诚实或规避行为
类别: deep learning
作者: Chris Cundy;Adam Gleave;
机构: FAR AI;FAR.AI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ibLGUkBWlz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13787
英摘: As AI systems become more capable, deceptive behaviors can undermine evaluation and mislead users at deployment. Recent work has shown that lie detectors can accurately classify deceptive behavior, but they are not typically used in the training pipeline due to concerns around contamination and objective hacking. We examine these concerns by incorporating a lie detector into the labelling step of LLM post-training and evaluating whether the learned policy is genuinely more honest, or instead learns to fool the lie detector while remaining deceptive. Using DolusChat, a novel 65k-example dataset with paired truthful/deceptive responses, we identify three key factors that determine the honesty of learned policies: amount of exploration during preference learning, lie detector accuracy, and KL regularization strength. We find that preference learning with lie detectors and GRPO can lead to policies which evade lie detectors, with deception rates of over 85\%. However, if the lie detector true positive rate (TPR) or KL regularization is sufficiently high, GRPO learns honest policies. In contrast, off-policy algorithms (DPO) consistently lead to deception rates under 25\% for realistic TPRs. Our results illustrate a more complex picture than previously assumed: depending on the context, lie-detector-enhanced training can be a powerful tool for scalable oversight, or a counterproductive method encouraging undetectable misalignment.
中摘: 随着人工智能系统的能力不断增强，欺骗行为可能会削弱评估并误导部署中的用户。近期的研究表明，谎言检测器可以准确地分类欺骗行为，但它们通常不用于训练管道中，因为存在污染和客观黑客攻击的担忧。我们通过将谎言检测器纳入LLM（大型语言模型）后训练阶段的标注步骤来研究这些担忧，并评估学习到的策略是否真的更诚实，还是反而学会欺骗谎言检测器但仍保持欺骗性。使用DolusChat这一新颖的数据集，该数据集包含65000个配对的真实/欺骗响应，我们确定了决定学习策略诚实性的三个关键因素：偏好学习期间的探索量、谎言检测器的准确性以及KL正则化强度。我们发现，在使用谎言检测器和GRPO（一种强化学习算法）进行偏好学习的情况下，可以导致策略逃避谎言检测器，欺骗率超过85%。然而，如果谎言检测器的真正阳性率（TPR）或KL正则化足够高，GRPO会学习到诚实的策略。相比之下，离策算法（DPO）在现实的TPR下始终会导致欺骗率低于25%。我们的研究结果表明，谎言检测器增强的训练比之前假设的要复杂得多：根据具体情境，谎言检测器增强的训练可以成为强大的可扩展监督工具，或者反而会鼓励不可检测的不对齐。

[No.154] Aha! - Predicting What Matters Next: Online Highlight Detection Without Looking Ahead
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: aha！——预测下一步重要事项：无需提前查看的在线亮点检测

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16421
英摘: Real-time understanding of continuous video streams is essential for intelligent agents operating in high-stakes environments, including autonomous vehicles, surveillance drones, and disaster response robots. Yet, most existing video understanding and highlight detection methods assume access to the entire video during inference, making them unsuitable for online or streaming scenarios. In particular, current models optimize for offline summarization, failing to support step-by-step reasoning needed for real-time decision-making. We introduce Aha, an autoregressive highlight detection framework that predicts the relevance of each video frame against a task described in natural language. Without accessing future video frames, Aha utilizes a multimodal vision-language model and lightweight, decoupled heads trained on a large, curated dataset of human-centric video labels. To enable scalability, we introduce the Dynamic SinkCache mechanism that achieves constant memory usage across infinite-length streams without degrading performance on standard benchmarks. This encourages the hidden representation to capture high-level task objectives, enabling effective frame-level rankings for informativeness, relevance, and uncertainty with respect to the natural language task. Aha achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on highlight detection benchmarks, surpassing even prior offline, full-context approaches and video-language models by +5.9% on TVSum and +8.3% on Mr. Hisum in mAP (mean Average Precision). We explore Aha's potential for real-world robotics applications given a task-oriented natural language input and a continuous, robot-centric video. Both experiments demonstrate Aha's potential effectiveness as a real-time reasoning module for downstream planning and long-horizon understanding.
中摘: 实时理解连续视频流对于在高风险环境中运作的智能代理至关重要，包括自动驾驶车辆、监控无人机和灾难响应机器人。然而，大多数现有的视频理解和亮点检测方法在推理时假设可以访问整个视频，这使得它们不适合在线或流式传输场景。特别是，当前模型优化的是离线总结，无法支持实时决策所需的逐步推理。我们提出了Aha，这是一种自回归亮点检测框架，能够根据自然语言描述的任务预测每个视频帧的相关性。Aha无需访问未来的视频帧，利用多模态的视觉-语言模型以及轻量级且解耦的头部，并在一个人类为中心的大规模标注数据集上进行训练。为了实现可扩展性，我们引入了动态SinkCache机制，在处理无限长度的视频流时实现了恒定的内存使用，同时在标准基准测试上的性能不降反增。这促使隐藏表示捕捉高层次的任务目标，从而实现与自然语言任务相关的有用性、相关性和不确定性方面的有效帧级别排名。Aha在亮点检测基准测试中达到了最先进的（SOTA）性能，在TVSum上超越了之前的离线全上下文方法和视频-语言模型5.9%，在Mr. Hisum上则超越了8.3%（以mAP，平均精度均值衡量）。鉴于任务导向的自然语言输入和连续的机器人中心视频，我们探索了Aha在真实世界机器人应用中的潜力。实验表明，Aha作为实时推理模块，对下游规划和长时理解具有潜在的有效性。

[No.155] RODS: Robust Optimization Inspired Diffusion Sampling for Detecting and Reducing Hallucination in Generative Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: RODS：基于稳健优化启发式的扩散采样方法，用于检测和减少生成模型中的幻觉
类别: deep learning
作者: Yiqi Tian;Pengfei Jin;Mingze Yuan;Na Li;Bo Zeng;Quanzheng Li;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12201
英摘: Diffusion models have achieved state-of-the-art performance in generative modeling, yet their sampling procedures remain vulnerable to hallucinations-often stemming from inaccuracies in score approximation. In this work, we reinterpret diffusion sampling through the lens of optimization and introduce RODS (Robust Optimization-inspired Diffusion Sampler), a novel method that detects and corrects high-risk sampling steps using geometric cues from the loss landscape. RODS enforces smoother sampling trajectories and adaptively adjusts perturbations, reducing hallucinations without retraining and at minimal additional inference cost. Experiments on AFHQv2, FFHQ, and 11k-hands demonstrate that RODS maintains comparable image quality and preserves generation diversity. More importantly, it improves both sampling fidelity and robustness, detecting over 70% of hallucinated samples and correcting more than 25%, all while avoiding the introduction of new artifacts. We release our code at https://github.com/Yiqi-Verna-Tian/RODS.
中摘: 扩散模型在生成建模方面已达到最先进的性能，但在采样过程中仍然容易出现幻觉——这些问题通常源于分数近似的不准确性。在这项工作中，我们从优化的角度重新诠释了扩散采样，并引入了一种名为RODS（鲁棒优化启发的扩散采样）的新方法。RODS利用损失景观中的几何线索来检测和纠正高风险的采样步骤，从而确保更平滑的采样轨迹，并自适应调整扰动，减少幻觉现象而不需重新训练，并且几乎不会增加额外的推理成本。在AFHQv2、FFHQ和11k-hands数据集上的实验表明，RODS能够保持与之相当的图像质量并保留生成多样性。更重要的是，它提高了采样的准确性和鲁棒性，在幻觉样本中检测出超过70%，并纠正了超过25%的幻觉样本，同时避免引入新的伪影。我们已在https://github.com/Yiqi-Verna-Tian/RODS发布了我们的代码。

[No.156] An Evidence-Based Post-Hoc Adjustment Framework for Anomaly Detection Under Data Contamination
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于证据的后验调整框架：在数据污染下的异常检测
类别: general machine learning
作者: Sukanya Patra;Souhaib Ben Taieb;
机构: University of Mons;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=NgLFQTBPRR
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21296
英摘: Unsupervised anomaly detection (AD) methods typically assume clean training data, yet real-world datasets often contain undetected or mislabeled anomalies, leading to significant performance degradation. Existing solutions require access to the training pipelines, data or prior knowledge of the proportions of anomalies in the data, limiting their real-world applicability. To address this challenge, we propose EPHAD, a simple yet effective test-time adaptation framework that updates the outputs of AD models trained on contaminated datasets using evidence gathered at test time. Our approach integrates the prior knowledge captured by the AD model trained on contaminated datasets with evidence derived from multimodal foundation models like Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP), classical AD methods like the Local Outlier Factor or domain-specific knowledge. We illustrate the intuition behind EPHAD using a synthetic toy example and validate its effectiveness through comprehensive experiments across eight visual AD datasets, twenty-six tabular AD datasets, and a real-world industrial AD dataset. Additionally, we conduct an ablation study to analyse hyperparameter influence and robustness to varying contamination levels, demonstrating the versatility and robustness of EPHAD across diverse AD models and evidence pairs. To ensure reproducibility, our code is publicly available at https://github.com/sukanyapatra1997/EPHAD.
中摘: 无监督异常检测（AD）方法通常假设训练数据干净，但实际上，现实世界的数据集往往包含未被检测到或错误标记的异常，这会导致显著的性能下降。现有的解决方案需要访问训练管道、数据或了解数据中异常的比例，这限制了它们在实际应用中的适用性。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出了EPHAD，这是一种简单而有效的测试时适应框架，在受污染的数据集上训练的AD模型的输出会在测试时使用收集到的证据进行更新。我们的方法将受污染数据集上训练的AD模型捕获的先验知识与来自多模态基础模型（如对比语言-图像预训练CLIP）、经典AD方法（如局部异常因子）或领域特定知识的证据结合起来。我们通过一个合成玩具示例来说明EPHAD背后的直觉，并通过在八个视觉AD数据集、二十六个表格AD数据集以及一个实际工业AD数据集上的全面实验验证其有效性。此外，我们还进行了消融研究以分析超参数的影响和对不同污染水平的鲁棒性，展示了EPHAD在多种AD模型和证据对中的多样性和鲁棒性。为了确保可重复性，我们的代码已在https://github.com/sukanyapatra1997/EPHAD公开。

标题: 神经元作为感官动态中一致集合的检测器
类别: neuroscience and cognitive science
作者: Joshua L. Pughe-Sanford;Xuehao Ding;Jason J Moore;Anirvan M. Sengupta;Charles Epstein;Philip Greengard;Dmitri Chklovskii;
机构: Flatiron Institute;New York University;;Simons Foundation;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=XTHI90js2e
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26955
英摘: We model sensory streams as observations from high-dimensional stochastic dynamical systems and conceptualize sensory neurons as self-supervised learners of compact representations of such dynamics. From prior experience, neurons learn coherent sets-regions of stimulus state space whose trajectories evolve cohesively over finite times-and assign membership indices to new stimuli. Coherent sets are identified via spectral clustering of the stochastic Koopman operator (SKO), where the sign pattern of a subdominant singular function partitions the state space into minimally coupled regions. For multivariate Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes, this singular function reduces to a linear projection onto the dominant singular vector of the whitened state-transition matrix. Encoding this singular vector as a receptive field enables neurons to compute membership indices via the projection sign in a biologically plausible manner. Each neuron detects either a predictive coherent set (stimuli with common futures) or a retrospective coherent set (stimuli with common pasts), suggesting a functional dichotomy among neurons. Since neurons lack access to explicit dynamical equations, the requisite singular vectors must be estimated directly from data, for example, via past-future canonical correlation analysis on lag-vector representations-an approach that naturally extends to nonlinear dynamics. This framework provides a novel account of neuronal temporal filtering, the ubiquity of rectification in neural responses, and known functional dichotomies. Coherent-set clustering thus emerges as a fundamental computation underlying sensory processing and transferable to bio-inspired artificial systems.
中摘: 我们将感觉流视为高维随机动力系统的观察，并将感觉神经元视为学习此类动力系统紧凑表示的自我监督学习者。基于先前的经验，神经元学会识别一致集——刺激状态空间中的区域，其轨迹在有限时间内协同演化——并为新刺激分配成员身份索引。通过谱聚类随机Koopman算子（SKO）中的次主导奇异函数来识别这些一致集，该奇异函数的符号模式将状态空间划分为最小耦合区域。对于多元Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，该奇异函数简化为白化状态转移矩阵的主导奇异向量的线性投影。将这个奇异向量编码为感受野，使神经元能够以生物上合理的途径通过投影符号计算成员身份索引。每个神经元检测的是预测一致集（具有共同未来特征的刺激）或回顾一致集（具有共同过去特征的刺激），这表明神经元之间存在功能上的二分性。由于神经元无法访问显式的动力学方程，因此所需的奇异向量必须直接从数据中估计，例如通过滞后向量表示的过去-未来典型相关分析——这种方法自然可以扩展到非线性动力学。该框架为神经元的时间滤波、神经响应中的普遍整流现象以及已知的功能二分性提供了新的解释。因此，一致集聚类作为感觉处理的基础计算而出现，并且可以应用于仿生启发的人工系统。

[No.158] Delving into Large Language Models for Effective Time-Series Anomaly Detection

[No.159] DETree: DEtecting Human-AI Collaborative Texts via Tree-Structured Hierarchical Representation Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: DETree: 基于树结构层次表示学习的人机协作文本检测
类别: evaluation
作者: Yongxin He;Shan Zhang;Yixuan Cao;Lei Ma;Ping Luo;
机构: University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=vm84b0Gksj
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17489
英摘: Detecting AI-involved text is essential for combating misinformation, plagiarism, and academic misconduct. However, AI text generation includes diverse collaborative processes (AI-written text edited by humans, human-written text edited by AI, and AI-generated text refined by other AI), where various or even new LLMs could be involved. Texts generated through these varied processes exhibit complex characteristics, presenting significant challenges for detection. Current methods model these processes rather crudely, primarily employing binary classification (purely human vs. AI-involved) or multi-classification (treating human-AI collaboration as a new class). We observe that representations of texts generated through different processes exhibit inherent clustering relationships. Therefore, we propose DETree, a novel approach that models the relationships among different processes as a Hierarchical Affinity Tree structure, and introduces a specialized loss function that aligns text representations with this tree. To facilitate this learning, we developed RealBench, a comprehensive benchmark dataset that automatically incorporates a wide spectrum of hybrid texts produced through various human-AI collaboration processes. Our method improves performance in hybrid text detection tasks and significantly enhances robustness and generalization in out-of-distribution scenarios, particularly in few-shot learning conditions, further demonstrating the promise of training-based approaches in OOD settings. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/heyongxin233/DETree.
中摘: 检测涉及AI的文字对于打击虚假信息、剽窃和学术不端至关重要。然而，AI文本生成包括多种协作过程（人类编辑AI撰写的文字、AI编辑人类撰写的文字以及AI生成的文字由其他AI进一步完善），其中可能涉及各种不同的甚至全新的语言模型。这些不同过程生成的文本表现出复杂的特性，给检测带来了重大挑战。当前的方法对这些过程的建模相当粗糙，主要采用二元分类（纯粹的人类撰写 vs. 涉及AI）或多元分类（将人类与AI的合作视为一个新的类别）。我们观察到，通过不同过程生成的文本在表示上具有内在的聚类关系。因此，我们提出了DETree这一新颖的方法，将其不同过程之间的关系建模为层次亲和树结构，并引入了一个专门的损失函数，使文本表示与该树对齐。为了促进这种学习，我们开发了RealBench，这是一个全面的基准数据集，能够自动包含通过各种人类与AI合作过程产生的广泛混合文本。我们的方法在混合文本检测任务中的性能得到了提升，并显著增强了在分布外场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，特别是在少量样本学习条件下，进一步证明了基于训练的方法在分布外设置中的潜力。我们的代码和数据集可在https://github.com/heyongxin233/DETree获取。
