[No.160] IPAD: Inverse Prompt for AI Detection - A Robust and Interpretable LLM-Generated Text Detector
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: IPAD：逆向提示词AI检测——一种稳健且可解释的LLM生成文本检测器
类别: applications
作者: Zheng CHEN;Yushi Feng;Jisheng Dang;Changyang He;Yue Deng;Hongxi Pu;Haoxuan Li;Bo Li;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;;Lanzhou University;Sun Yat-sen University;Max Planck Institute;Meta;Peking University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=3JoQTGhUzz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15902
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) have attained human-level fluency in text generation, which complicates the distinguishing between human-written and LLM-generated texts. This increases the risk of misuse and highlights the need for reliable detectors. Yet, existing detectors exhibit poor robustness on out-of-distribution (OOD) data and attacked data, which is critical for real-world scenarios. Also, they struggle to provide interpretable evidence to support their decisions, thus undermining the reliability. In light of these challenges, we propose IPAD (Inverse Prompt for AI Detection), a novel framework consisting of a Prompt Inverter that identifies predicted prompts that could have generated the input text, and two Distinguishers that examine the probability that the input texts align with the predicted prompts. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that IPAD outperforms the strongest baselines by 9.05% (Average Recall) on in-distribution data, 12.93% (AUROC) on out-of-distribution data, and 5.48% (AUROC) on attacked data. IPAD also performs robustly on structured datasets. Furthermore, an interpretability assessment is conducted to illustrate that IPAD enhances the AI detection trustworthiness by allowing users to directly examine the decision-making evidence, which provides interpretable support for its state-of-the-art detection results.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在文本生成方面达到了与人类相当的流畅度，这使得区分由人类编写和由LLM生成的文本变得更加复杂。这增加了滥用的风险，并突显了需要可靠检测器的需求。然而，现有的检测器在处理分布外（OOD）数据和攻击数据时表现出较差的鲁棒性，这对于实际应用场景至关重要。此外，它们难以提供可解释的证据来支持其决策，从而削弱了可靠性。鉴于这些挑战，我们提出了一种名为IPAD（逆提示用于AI检测）的新框架，该框架包括一个提示反转器，它可以识别出能够生成输入文本的预测提示，以及两个辨别器，用来检查输入文本与预测提示一致的概率。实证评估表明，在分布内数据上，IPAD 的平均召回率比最强基线高出 9.05%，在分布外数据上，AUCROC 高出 12.93%，在受攻击数据上，AUCROC 高出 5.48%。此外，IPAD 在结构化数据集上也表现出了良好的鲁棒性。进一步的可解释性评估显示，IPAD 通过让用户直接检查决策证据来增强AI检测的信任度，从而为最先进的检测结果提供了可解释的支持。

[No.161] Mysteries of the Deep: Role of Intermediate Representations in Out of Distribution Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 深海之谜：中间表示在分布外检测中的作用
类别: deep learning
作者: Imezadelajara;Cristian Rodriguez-Opazo;Damien Teney;Damith Ranasinghe;Ehsan Abbasnejad;
机构: University of Adelaide;;Idiap Research Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=HUJXOQkLex
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05782
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential for reliably deploying machine learning models in the wild. Yet, most methods treat large pre-trained models as monolithic encoders and rely solely on their final-layer representations for detection. We challenge this wisdom. We reveal the \textit{intermediate layers} of pre-trained models, shaped by residual connections that subtly transform input projections, \textit{can} encode \textit{surprisingly rich and diverse signals} for detecting distributional shifts. Importantly, to exploit latent representation diversity across layers, we introduce an entropy-based criterion to \textit{automatically} identify layers offering the most complementary information in a training-free setting -- \textit{without access to OOD data}. We show that selectively incorporating these intermediate representations can increase the accuracy of OOD detection by up to \textbf{$10\%$} in far-OOD and over \textbf{$7\%$} in near-OOD benchmarks compared to state-of-the-art training-free methods across various model architectures and training objectives. Our findings reveal a new avenue for OOD detection research and uncover the impact of various training objectives and model architectures on confidence-based OOD detection methods.
中摘: 分布外（OOD）检测对于可靠地在实际环境中部署机器学习模型至关重要。然而，大多数方法将大型预训练模型视为单一的整体编码器，并仅依赖其最终层表示来进行检测。我们挑战这一智慧。我们揭示了预训练模型的中间层，这些层受到残差连接的影响，微妙地变换输入投影，能够编码出令人惊讶的丰富多样的信号，用于检测分布变化。重要的是，为了利用各层中潜在表示的多样性，我们引入了一个基于熵的标准来自动识别在无训练环境下提供最多互补信息的层——无需访问分布外数据。我们展示了有选择性地整合这些中间层表示可以提高远分布外和近分布外基准上OOD检测的准确性，分别达到最高**10%**和超过**7%**，这在各种模型架构和训练目标下相比最先进的无训练方法有所提升。我们的研究发现为OOD检测研究开辟了一条新途径，并揭示了不同训练目标和模型架构对基于置信度的OOD检测方法的影响。

[No.162] Dual Data Alignment Makes AI-Generated Image Detector Easier Generalizable
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 双重数据对齐使AI生成图像检测器更具泛化能力
类别: general machine learning
作者: Ruoxin Chen;Junwei Xi;Zhiyuan Yan;Ke-Yue Zhang;Shuang Wu;Jingyi Xie;Xu Chen;Lei Xu;Isabel Guan;Taiping Yao;Shouhong Ding;
机构: Tencent;East China University of Science and Technology;Peking University;;Renmin University of China;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=C39ShJwtD5
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14359
英摘: Existing detectors are often trained on biased datasets, leading to the possibility of overfitting on non-causal image attributes that are spuriously correlated with real/synthetic labels. While these biased features enhance performance on the training data, they result in substantial performance degradation when applied to unbiased datasets. One common solution is to perform dataset alignment through generative reconstruction, matching the semantic content between real and synthetic images. However, we revisit this approach and show that pixel-level alignment alone is insufficient. The reconstructed images still suffer from frequency-level misalignment, which can perpetuate spurious correlations. To illustrate, we observe that reconstruction models tend to restore the high-frequency details lost in real images (possibly due to JPEG compression), inadvertently creating a frequency-level misalignment, where synthetic images appear to have richer high-frequency content than real ones. This misalignment leads to models associating high-frequency features with synthetic labels, further reinforcing biased cues. To resolve this, we propose Dual Data Alignment (DDA), which aligns both the pixel and frequency domains. Moreover, we introduce two new test sets: DDA-COCO, containing DDA-aligned synthetic images for testing detector performance on the most aligned dataset, and EvalGEN, featuring the latest generative models for assessing detectors under new generative architectures such as visual auto-regressive generators. Finally, our extensive evaluations demonstrate that a detector trained exclusively on DDA-aligned MSCOCO could improve across 8 diverse benchmarks by a non-trivial margin, showing a +7.2% on in-the-wild benchmarks, highlighting the improved generalizability of unbiased detectors. Our code is available at: https://github.com/roy-ch/Dual-Data-Alignment.
中摘: 现有的检测器通常是在带有偏差的数据集上训练的，这可能导致它们在非因果图像属性上过度拟合，而这些属性与真实或合成标签之间存在虚假的相关性。虽然这些偏差特征可以在训练数据上提升性能，但在应用到无偏差数据集时会导致显著的性能下降。一种常见的解决方案是通过生成重建来进行数据集对齐，匹配真实和合成图像的语义内容。然而，我们重新审视了这一方法，并表明仅在像素级别进行对齐是不够的。重建后的图像仍然存在频域级别的错位，这种错位可以延续虚假的相关性。举例来说，我们观察到重建模型倾向于恢复由于JPEG压缩而在真实图像中丢失的高频细节，无意中在频域级别造成了错位，使得合成图像看起来具有比真实图像更丰富的高频内容。这种错位导致模型将高频特征与合成标签关联起来，进一步强化了偏差线索。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了双重数据对齐（DDA），它同时对齐像素和频域。此外，我们引入了两个新的测试集：DDA-COCO，包含经过DDA对齐的合成图像，用于测试检测器在最对齐的数据集上的性能；EvalGEN，则展示了最新的生成模型，用于在包括视觉自回归生成器在内的新型生成架构下评估检测器的表现。最后，我们的广泛评估表明，仅在DDA对齐的MSCOCO数据集上训练的检测器可以在8个不同的基准测试中取得明显的改进，特别是在野外场景基准测试中提升了7.2%，突显了无偏差检测器的更好泛化能力。我们的代码可在以下地址获取：https://github.com/roy-ch/Dual-Data-Alignment。

[No.164] Fact-R1: Towards Explainable Video Misinformation Detection with Deep Reasoning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 可解释的视频 misinformation 检测与深度推理
类别: applications
作者: Fanrui Zhang;Dian Li;Qiang Zhang;Chenjun;sinbadliu;Junxiong Lin;Jiahong Yan;Jiawei Liu;Zheng-Jun Zha;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Tencent;;PCG;Community Products Department;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=EeyvDitalf
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16836
英摘: The rapid spread of multimodal misinformation on social media has raised growing concerns, while research on video misinformation detection remains limited due to the lack of large-scale, diverse datasets. Existing methods often overfit to rigid templates and lack deep reasoning over deceptive content. To address these challenges, we introduce FakeVV, a large-scale benchmark comprising over 100,000 video-text pairs with fine-grained, interpretable annotations. In addition, we further propose Fact-R1, a novel framework that integrates deep reasoning with collaborative rule-based reinforcement learning. Fact-R1 is trained through a three-stage process: (1) misinformation long-Chain-of-Thought (CoT) instruction tuning, (2) preference alignment via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), and (3) Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) using a novel verifiable reward function. This enables Fact-R1 to exhibit emergent reasoning behaviors comparable to those observed in advanced text-based reinforcement learning systems, but in the more complex multimodal misinformation setting. Our work establishes a new paradigm for misinformation detection, bridging large-scale video understanding, reasoning-guided alignment, and interpretable verification.
中摘: 社交媒体上多模态虚假信息的迅速传播引发了越来越多的关注，但由于缺乏大规模和多样化的数据集，现有的视频虚假信息检测研究仍然有限。现有方法往往过度拟合于固定的模板，缺乏对欺骗性内容进行深入推理的能力。为应对这些挑战，我们引入了FakeVV，这是一个包含超过100,000个视频-文本对的大规模基准数据集，具有精细、可解释的注释。此外，我们还提出了Fact-R1这一创新框架，该框架结合了深度推理与协作基于规则的强化学习。Fact-R1通过三阶段训练过程进行训练：（1）虚假信息长链推理指令调优，（2）通过直接偏好优化（DPO）进行偏好对齐，（3）使用新型可验证奖励函数进行组相对策略优化（GRPO）。这使得Fact-R1能够在更复杂的多模态虚假信息环境中展现出与先进文本基强化学习系统相当的新兴推理行为。我们的工作建立了一个新的虚假信息检测范式，融合了大规模视频理解、推理引导的对齐以及可解释的验证。

[No.165] FrameShield: Adversarially Robust Video Anomaly Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: FrameShield：对抗性鲁棒视频异常检测
类别: deep learning
作者: Mojtaba Nafez;Mobina Poulaei;Nikan Vasei;Bardia soltani moakhar;Mohammad Sabokrou;Mohammad Hossein Rohban;
机构: Sharif University of Technology;Istanbul Technical University;Okinawa Institute of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=7FLKzOqsKd
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21532
英摘: Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection (WSVAD) has achieved notable advancements, yet existing models remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, limiting their reliability. Due to the inherent constraints of weak supervision, where only video-level labels are provided despite the need for frame-level predictions, traditional adversarial defense mechanisms, such as adversarial training, are not effective since video-level adversarial perturbations are typically weak and inadequate. To address this limitation, pseudo-labels generated directly from the model can enable frame-level adversarial training; however, these pseudo-labels are inherently noisy, significantly degrading performance. We therefore introduce a novel Pseudo-Anomaly Generation method called Spatiotemporal Region Distortion (SRD), which creates synthetic anomalies by applying severe augmentations to localized regions in normal videos while preserving temporal consistency. Integrating these precisely annotated synthetic anomalies with the noisy pseudo-labels substantially reduces label noise, enabling effective adversarial training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly enhances the robustness of WSVAD models against adversarial attacks, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by an average of 71.0\% in overall AUROC performance across multiple benchmarks. The implementation and code are publicly available at https://github.com/rohban-lab/FrameShield.
中摘: 弱监督视频异常检测（WSVAD）已经取得了显著的进步，但现有模型仍然容易受到对抗攻击的影响，这限制了它们的可靠性。由于弱监督固有的约束条件，在仅提供视频级标签的情况下需要进行帧级预测，传统的对抗防御机制，如对抗训练，通常无效，因为视频级的对抗扰动往往较弱且不足。为了解决这一局限性，直接从模型生成的伪标签可以实现帧级的对抗训练；然而，这些伪标签本身是噪声较大的，严重影响了性能。因此，我们提出了一种名为时空区域失真（SRD）的新型伪异常生成方法，该方法通过在正常视频中对局部区域应用严重的增强操作来生成合成异常，同时保持时间一致性。将这些精确标注的合成异常与噪声伪标签结合，显著减少了标签噪声，从而实现了有效的对抗训练。大量实验表明，我们的方法显著增强了WSVAD模型对对抗攻击的鲁棒性，在多个基准测试的整体AUROC性能上平均优于最先进的方法71.0%。该方法的实现和代码已在https://github.com/rohban-lab/FrameShield公开可用。

[No.167] MoniTor: Exploiting Large Language Models with Instruction for Online Video Anomaly Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: MoniTor：利用指令驱动的大语言模型进行在线视频异常检测
类别: applications
作者: Shengtian Yang;Yue Feng;Yingshi Liu;Jingrou Zhang;Jie Qin;
机构: Nanjing University of Aeronautics and Astronautics;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=6Had86RHix
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21449
英摘: Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) aims to locate unusual activities or behaviors within videos. Recently, offline VAD has garnered substantial research attention, which has been invigorated by the progress in large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs), offering the potential for a more nuanced understanding of anomalies. However, online VAD has seldom received attention due to real-time constraints and computational intensity. In this paper, we introduce a novel Memory-based online scoring queue scheme for Training-free VAD (MoniTor), to address the inherent complexities in online VAD. Specifically, MoniTor applies a streaming input to VLMs, leveraging the capabilities of pre-trained large-scale models. To capture temporal dependencies more effectively, we incorporate a novel prediction mechanism inspired by Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. This ensures the model can effectively model past states and leverage previous predictions to identify anomalous behaviors. Thereby, it better understands the current frame. Moreover, we design a scoring queue and an anomaly prior to dynamically store recent scores and cover all anomalies in the monitoring scenario, providing guidance for LLMs to distinguish between normal and abnormal behaviors over time. We evaluate MoniTor on two large datasets (i.e., UCF-Crime and XD-Violence) containing various surveillance and real-world scenarios. The results demonstrate that MoniTor outperforms state-of-the-art methods and is competitive with weakly supervised methods without training. Code is available at https://github.com/YsTvT/MoniTor.
中摘: 视频异常检测（VAD）旨在定位视频中的不寻常活动或行为。近年来，离线VAD获得了大量的研究关注，这得益于大型语言模型（LLMs）和视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的进步，为更深入地理解异常提供了可能性。然而，由于实时约束和计算强度，线上VAD很少受到关注。在本文中，我们介绍了一种新的基于记忆的在线评分队列方案——无需训练的VAD（MoniTor），以解决线上VAD固有的复杂性问题。具体而言，MoniTor采用流式输入应用于VLMs，利用预训练大规模模型的能力。为了更有效地捕捉时间依赖性，我们引入了一种受长短期记忆（LSTM）网络启发的新预测机制。这确保了模型能够有效建模过去状态，并利用之前的预测来识别异常行为，从而更好地理解当前帧。此外，我们设计了一个评分队列和一个异常先验，以动态存储最近的分数并覆盖监控场景中的所有异常，为LLMs提供指导，使其能够在时间上区分正常和异常行为。我们在两个大型数据集（即UCF-Crime和XD-Violence）上评估了MoniTor，这两个数据集包含各种监控和真实世界场景。结果显示，MoniTor优于现有最先进的方法，并且在无需训练的情况下与弱监督方法竞争。代码可在https://github.com/YsTvT/MoniTor获取。

[No.169] Rooms from Motion: Un-posed Indoor 3D Object Detection as Localization and Mapping
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 动捕空间：无摆Pose室内3D物体检测作为定位与建图
类别: applications
作者: Justin Lazarow;Kai Kang;Afshin Dehghan;
机构: Apple;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=0NexR6LDiG
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23756
英摘: We revisit scene-level 3D object detection as the output of an object-centric framework capable of both localization and mapping using 3D oriented boxes as the underlying geometric primitive. While existing 3D object detection approaches operate globally and implicitly rely on the a priori existence of metric camera poses, our method, Rooms from Motion (RfM) operates on a collection of un-posed images. By replacing the standard 2D keypoint-based matcher of structure-from-motion with an object-centric matcher based on image-derived 3D boxes, we estimate metric camera poses, object tracks, and finally produce a global, semantic 3D object map. When a priori pose is available, we can significantly improve map quality through optimization of global 3D boxes against individual observations. RfM shows strong localization performance and subsequently produces maps of higher quality than leading point-based and multi-view 3D object detection methods on CA-1M and ScanNet++, despite these global methods relying on overparameterization through point clouds or dense volumes. Rooms from Motion achieves a general, object-centric representation which not only extends the work of Cubify Anything to full scenes but also allows for inherently sparse localization and parametric mapping proportional to the number of objects in a scene.
中摘: 我们重新审视场景级别的三维物体检测，将其作为基于对象中心框架的输出，该框架能够同时进行定位和建图，并使用三维定向框作为基础几何原语。尽管现有的三维物体检测方法全局操作且隐含依赖于先验存在的度量相机姿态，我们的方法——来自运动的房间（Rooms from Motion, RfM）——则在未标定的图像集合上进行操作。通过用图像衍生的三维盒子替代结构从运动的标准二维关键点匹配器，我们估计出度量相机姿态、对象轨迹，最终生成一个全局的、语义化的三维物体地图。当先验姿态可用时，我们可以通过优化全局三维盒子来显著提高地图质量，针对个体观测进行优化。在CA-1M和ScanNet++上，RfM展示了强大的定位性能，并且生成了比领先的关键点基三维物体检测和多视图三维物体检测方法更高的质量地图，尽管这些全局方法通过点云或密集体积进行过度参数化。来自运动的房间实现了通用的对象中心表示，不仅将Cubify Anything的工作扩展到完整场景，还允许与场景中对象数量成比例的固有稀疏定位和参数化建图。

[No.173] VADTree: Explainable Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection via Hierarchical Granularity-Aware Tree
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: VADTree：基于分层粒度感知树的无训练可解释视频异常检测
类别: applications
作者: Wenlong Li;Yifei Xu;Yuan Rao;Zhenhua Wang;Shuiguang Deng;
机构: Xi'an Jiao Tong University;;China Railway Xi'an Bureau Group Co., Ltd;Zhejiang University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=fVgnP5WHXX
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22693
英摘: Video anomaly detection (VAD) focuses on identifying anomalies in videos. Supervised methods demand substantial in-domain training data and fail to deliver clear explanations for anomalies. In contrast, training-free methods leverage the knowledge reserves and language interactivity of large pre-trained models to detect anomalies. However, the current fixed-length temporal window sampling approaches struggle to accurately capture anomalies with varying temporal spans. Therefore, we propose VADTree that utilizes a Hierarchical Granularityaware Tree (HGTree) structure for flexible sampling in VAD. VADTree leverages the knowledge embedded in a pre-trained Generic Event Boundary Detection (GEBD) model to characterize potential anomaly event boundaries. Specifically, VADTree decomposes the video into generic event nodes based on boundary confidence, and performs adaptive coarse-fine hierarchical structuring and redundancy removal to construct the HGTree. Then, the multi-dimensional priors are injected into the visual language models (VLMs) to enhance the node-wise anomaly perception, and anomaly reasoning for generic event nodes is achieved via large language models (LLMs). Finally, an inter-cluster node correlation method is used to integrate the multi-granularity anomaly scores. Extensive experiments on three challenging datasets demonstrate that VADTree achieves state-of-the-art performance in training-free settings while drastically reducing the number of sampled video segments. The code will be available at https://github.com/wenlongli10/VADTree.
中摘: 视频异常检测（VAD）专注于识别视频中的异常。监督方法需要大量的领域内训练数据，并且无法为异常提供清晰的解释。相比之下，无需训练的方法利用了大型预训练模型的知识储备和语言交互性来进行异常检测。然而，当前固定长度的时间窗口采样方法难以准确捕捉具有不同时间跨度的异常。因此，我们提出了VADTree，它利用层次粒度感知树（HGTree）结构进行灵活的采样。VADTree通过一个预先训练的一般事件边界检测（GEBD）模型嵌入的知识来表征潜在的异常事件边界。具体来说，VADTree基于边界置信度将视频分解为一般事件节点，并通过自适应粗细层次结构化和冗余去除构建HGTree。然后，将多维度先验注入视觉语言模型（VLMs），以增强节点级别的异常感知，并通过大型语言模型（LLMs）实现对一般事件节点的异常推理。最后，使用跨簇节点相关方法整合多粒度异常评分。在三个具有挑战性的数据集上的广泛实验表明，VADTree在无需训练的情况下实现了最先进的性能，同时大大减少了采样的视频片段数量。代码将在https://github.com/wenlongli10/VADTree上提供。

[No.174] Any Large Language Model Can Be a Reliable Judge: Debiasing with a Reasoning-based Bias Detector
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 任何大型语言模型都可以成为一个可靠的裁判：基于推理的偏见检测方法
类别: deep learning
作者: Haoyan Yang;Runxue Bao;Cao Xiao;Jun Ma;Parminder Bhatia;Shangqian Gao;Taha Kass-Hout;
机构: State University of New York at Stony Brook;New York University;Google;GE Healthcare;General Electric Healthcare;Florida State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=sLNz60fJFF
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17100
英摘: LLM-as-a-Judge has emerged as a promising tool for automatically evaluating generated outputs, but its reliability is often undermined by potential biases in judgment. Existing efforts to mitigate these biases face key limitations: in-context learning-based methods fail to address rooted biases due to the evaluator's limited capacity for self-reflection, whereas fine-tuning is not applicable to all evaluator types, especially closed-source models. To address this challenge, we introduce the Reasoning-based Bias Detector (RBD), which is a plug-in module that identifies biased evaluations and generates structured reasoning to guide evaluator self-correction. Rather than modifying the evaluator itself, RBD operates externally and engages in an iterative process of bias detection and feedback-driven revision. To support its development, we design a complete pipeline consisting of biased dataset construction, supervision collection, distilled reasoning-based fine-tuning of RBD, and integration with LLM evaluators. We fine-tune four sizes of RBD models, ranging from 1.5B to 14B, and observe consistent performance improvements across all scales. Experimental results on 4 bias types--verbosity, position, bandwagon, and sentiment--evaluated using 8 LLM evaluators demonstrate RBD's strong effectiveness. For example, the RBD-8B model improves evaluation accuracy by an average of 18.5% and consistency by 10.9%, and surpasses prompting-based baselines and fine-tuned judges by 12.8% and 17.2%, respectively. These results highlight RBD's effectiveness and scalability. Additional experiments further demonstrate its strong generalization across biases and domains, as well as its efficiency.
中摘: LLM-as-a-Judge作为一种自动评估生成输出的有前途的工具已经出现，但其可靠性常常受到评判者潜在偏见的影响。现有的减轻这些偏见的努力面临关键限制：基于上下文学习的方法由于评估者缺乏自我反思的能力而无法解决根深蒂固的偏见问题，而微调方法并不适用于所有类型的评估者，尤其是封闭源模型。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了基于推理的偏见检测器（RBD），这是一种插件模块，能够识别偏见评价并生成结构化的推理以指导评估者的自我纠正。与直接修改评估者不同，RBD外部操作，并通过迭代的偏见检测和反馈驱动的修订过程进行工作。为了支持其开发，我们设计了一个完整的管道，包括偏见数据集构建、监督收集、蒸馏推理为基础的RBD微调以及将其与LLM评估者集成。我们对四个大小的RBD模型进行了微调，从1.5B到14B，并观察到在所有规模上都有一致的性能提升。使用8个LLM评估者对四种偏见类型——冗长性、位置、随大流和情感——进行评估的结果显示，RBD具有很强的效果。例如，RBD-8B模型在评估准确性上平均提高了18.5%，在一致性上提高了10.9%，分别超越了基于提示的基础模型和微调的评判者12.8%和17.2%。这些结果突显了RBD的有效性和可扩展性。进一步的实验还表明，它在偏见和领域上的泛化能力强，且效率高。

[No.175] CrossAD: Time Series Anomaly Detection with Cross-scale Associations and Cross-window Modeling
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: CrossAD：跨尺度关联与跨窗口建模的时间序列异常检测
类别: deep learning
作者: Beibu Li;Qichao Shentu;Yang Shu;Hui Zhang;Ming Li;Ning Jin;Bin Yang;Chenjuan Guo;
机构: ;East China Normal University;Shandong Inspur Database Technology Co., Ltd;University of Jinan;Aalborg University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=fkYFDxiLpi
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12489
英摘: Time series anomaly detection plays a crucial role in a wide range of real-world applications. Given that time series data can exhibit different patterns at different sampling granularities, multi-scale modeling has proven beneficial for uncovering latent anomaly patterns that may not be apparent at a single scale. However, existing methods often model multi-scale information independently or rely on simple feature fusion strategies, neglecting the dynamic changes in cross-scale associations that occur during anomalies. Moreover, most approaches perform multi-scale modeling based on fixed sliding windows, which limits their ability to capture comprehensive contextual information. In this work, we propose CrossAD, a novel framework for time series Anomaly Detection that takes Cross-scale associations and Cross-window modeling into account. We propose a cross-scale reconstruction that reconstructs fine-grained series from coarser series, explicitly capturing cross-scale associations. Furthermore, we design a query library and incorporate global multi-scale context to overcome the limitations imposed by fixed window sizes. Extensive experiments conducted on multiple real-world datasets using nine evaluation metrics validate the effectiveness of CrossAD, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance in anomaly detection.
中摘: 时间序列异常检测在众多实际应用中发挥着关键作用。由于时间序列数据在不同的采样粒度下可以表现出不同的模式，多尺度建模已被证明有助于揭示可能在单一尺度上不明显的潜在异常模式。然而，现有的方法往往独立地建模多尺度信息或依赖于简单的特征融合策略，忽视了在异常期间跨尺度关联发生的动态变化。此外，大多数方法基于固定滑动窗口进行多尺度建模，这限制了它们捕捉全面上下文信息的能力。在本工作中，我们提出了一种名为CrossAD的新框架，用于考虑跨尺度关联和跨窗口建模的时间序列异常检测。我们提出了跨尺度重构，从粗粒度序列重构细粒度序列，明确捕获跨尺度关联。此外，我们设计了一个查询库，并结合全局多尺度上下文来克服固定窗口大小带来的局限性。在多个真实世界数据集上进行的广泛实验使用了九个评估指标，验证了CrossAD的有效性，展示了其在异常检测方面的先进性能。

[No.177] PolyJuice Makes It Real: Black-Box, Universal Red Teaming for Synthetic Image Detectors
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: PolyJuice 实现真相：黑盒、通用红队测试对合成图像检测器的研究
类别: deep learning
作者: Sepehr Dehdashtian;Mashrur M. Morshed;Jacob H Seidman;Gaurav Bharaj;Vishnu Boddeti;
机构: Michigan State University;;Reality Defender;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=rldWzb0E7G
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15551
英摘: Synthetic image detectors (SIDs) are a key defense against the risks posed by the growing realism of images from text-to-image (T2I) models. Red teaming improves SID's effectiveness by identifying and exploiting their failure modes via misclassified synthetic images. However, existing red-teaming solutions (i) require white-box access to SIDs, which is infeasible for proprietary state-of-the-art detectors, and (ii) generate image-specific attacks through expensive online optimization. To address these limitations, we propose PolyJuice, the first black-box, image-agnostic red-teaming method for SIDs, based on an observed distribution shift in the T2I latent space between samples correctly and incorrectly classified by the SID. PolyJuice generates attacks by (i) identifying the direction of this shift through a lightweight offline process that only requires black-box access to the SID, and (ii) exploiting this direction by universally steering all generated images towards the SID's failure modes. PolyJuice-steered T2I models are significantly more effective at deceiving SIDs (up to 84%) compared to their unsteered counterparts. We also show that the steering directions can be estimated efficiently at lower resolutions and transferred to higher resolutions using simple interpolation, reducing computational overhead. Finally, tuning SID models on PolyJuice-augmented datasets notably enhances the performance of the detectors (up to 30%).
中摘: 合成图像检测器（SIDs）是应对来自文本转图像（T2I）模型的日益逼真图像风险的关键防御手段。红队测试通过识别并利用SID的失败模式来提高其有效性，特别是通过对误分类的合成图像进行操纵。然而，现有的红队测试解决方案存在以下局限性：（i）需要对SID有白盒访问权限，这在处理专有最先进的检测器时是不可行的；（ii）通过昂贵的在线优化生成针对特定图像的攻击。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了PolyJuice，这是一种基于观察到的T2I潜在空间中样本被正确和错误分类之间分布偏移现象的首个黑盒、图像无关的红队测试方法。PolyJuice通过以下方式生成攻击：（i）利用一个轻量级的离线过程，仅需对SID进行黑盒访问，以识别这种偏移的方向；（ii）通过普遍引导所有生成的图像朝向SID的失败模式来利用这一方向。经过PolyJuice引导的T2I模型在欺骗SID方面显著更有效（最高可达84%），相比之下，未引导的模型则不然。我们还展示了可以在较低分辨率下高效估计引导方向，并使用简单的插值将其转移到更高分辨率，从而减少计算开销。最后，将SID模型在PolyJuice增强的数据集上进行调整，显著提升了检测器的表现（最高可达30%）。

[No.178] Refining Norms: A Post-hoc Framework for OOD Detection in Graph Neural Networks

[No.179] Semi-supervised Graph Anomaly Detection via Robust Homophily Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于稳健同质性学习的半监督图异常检测
类别: deep learning
作者: GuoguoAi;Hezhe Qiao;Hui Yan;Guansong Pang;
机构: Nanjing University of Science and Technology;Singapore Management University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=hTOWYQNsrb
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15448
英摘: Semi-supervised graph anomaly detection (GAD) utilizes a small set of labeled normal nodes to identify abnormal nodes from a large set of unlabeled nodes in a graph. Current methods in this line posit that 1) normal nodes share a similar level of homophily and 2) the labeled normal nodes can well represent the homophily patterns in the normal class. However, this assumption often does not hold well since normal nodes in a graph can exhibit diverse homophily in real-world GAD datasets. In this paper, we propose RHO, namely Robust Homophily Learning, to adaptively learn such homophily patterns. RHO consists of two novel modules, adaptive frequency response filters (AdaFreq) and graph normality alignment (GNA). AdaFreq learns a set of adaptive spectral filters that capture different frequency components of the labeled normal nodes with varying homophily in the channel-wise and cross-channel views of node attributes. GNA is introduced to enforce consistency between the channel-wise and cross-channel homophily representations to robustify the normality learned by the filters in the two views. Experiments on eight real-world GAD datasets show that RHO can effectively learn varying, often under-represented, homophily in the small normal node set and substantially outperforms state-of-the-art competing methods. Code is available at https://github.com/mala-lab/RHO.
中摘: 半监督图异常检测（GAD）利用一小部分标记的正常节点来识别大量未标记节点中的异常节点。当前该领域的研究方法假设：1）正常节点具有相似水平的同质性；2）标记的正常节点能够很好地代表正常类中的同质性模式。然而，在实际世界的GAD数据集中，图中的正常节点可能会表现出多样化的同质性，这一假设往往并不成立。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为RHO（Robust Homophily Learning）的方法，以适应性地学习这些同质性模式。RHO由两个新颖模块组成：自适应频率响应滤波器（AdaFreq）和图正常性对齐（GNA）。AdaFreq学习一组自适应谱滤波器，以在节点属性的通道内和跨通道视图中捕捉标记正常节点的不同频率成分，这些成分具有不同的同质性水平。GNA旨在在两个视图中增强通道内和跨通道同质性表示的一致性，从而增强滤波器在两种视图下学习的正常性。在八个实际世界的GAD数据集上的实验表明，RHO能够有效地学习小正常节点集中的变化且常被低估的同质性，并且显著优于最先进的竞争方法。代码可在https://github.com/mala-lab/RHO获取。

[No.181] Training-free Detection of AI-generated images via Cropping Robustness
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 无训练检测AI生成图像的方法：基于裁剪稳健性
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Sungik Choi;Hankook Lee;Moontae Lee;
机构: ;Sungkyunkwan University;LG;University of Illinois at Chicago;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=UPxyTg7dTL
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14030
英摘: AI-generated image detection has become crucial with the rapid advancement of vision-generative models. Instead of training detectors tailored to specific datasets, we study a training-free approach leveraging self-supervised models without requiring prior data knowledge. These models, pre-trained with augmentations like RandomResizedCrop, learn to produce consistent representations across varying resolutions. Motivated by this, we propose WaRPAD, a training-free AI-generated image detection algorithm based on self-supervised models. Since neighborhood pixel differences in images are highly sensitive to resizing operations, WaRPAD first defines a base score function that quantifies the sensitivity of image embeddings to perturbations along high-frequency directions extracted via Haar wavelet decomposition. To simulate robustness against cropping augmentation, we rescale each image to a multiple of the models input size, divide it into smaller patches, and compute the base score for each patch. The final detection score is then obtained by averaging the scores across all patches. We validate WaRPAD on real datasets of diverse resolutions and domains, and images generated by 23 different generative models. Our method consistently achieves competitive performance and demonstrates strong robustness to test-time corruptions. Furthermore, as invariance to RandomResizedCrop is a common training scheme across self-supervised models, we show that WaRPAD is applicable across self-supervised models.
中摘: 随着视觉生成模型的快速发展，AI生成图像检测已成为关键。我们不再针对特定数据集训练专门的检测器，而是研究一种无需训练的方法，利用不需要先验数据知识的自我监督模型。这些模型通过使用如随机缩放裁剪（RandomResizedCrop）等增强技术进行预训练，能够在不同分辨率下产生一致的表示。受此启发，我们提出了一种基于自我监督模型的无需训练的AI生成图像检测算法——WaRPAD。由于图像中的邻域像素差异对缩放操作高度敏感，WaRPAD首先定义了一个基础评分函数，该函数通过haar小波分解提取的高频方向上的扰动来量化图像嵌入对这些扰动的敏感性。为了模拟对抗裁剪增强的鲁棒性，我们将每张图像按模型输入大小的整数倍进行缩放，然后将其划分为更小的patches，并为每个patch计算基础评分。最终的检测评分则是通过所有patches评分的平均值获得。我们在多样分辨率和领域的实际数据集以及由23种不同的生成模型生成的图像上验证了WaRPAD，我们的方法在性能上表现出色，并且能够很好地抵抗测试时的损坏。此外，由于随机缩放裁剪的不变性是自我监督模型中常见的训练方案，我们展示了WaRPAD适用于各种自我监督模型。

[No.184] AI-Generated Video Detection via Perceptual Straightening
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: AI生成视频的知觉校正检测
类别: neuroscience and cognitive science
作者: Christian Internò;Robert Geirhos;Markus Olhofer;Sunny Liu;Barbara Hammer;David Klindt;
机构: Bielefeld University;Google;Honda Research Institute;Cold Spring Harbor Laboratory;Universität Bielefeld;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=LsmUgStXby
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00583
英摘: The rapid advancement of generative AI enables highly realistic synthetic videos, posing significant challenges for content authentication and raising urgent concerns about misuse. Existing detection methods often struggle with generalization and capturing subtle temporal inconsistencies. We propose ReStraV(Representation Straightening Video), a novel approach to distinguish natural from AI-generated videos. Inspired by the "perceptual straightening" hypothesis -- which suggests real-world video trajectories become more straight in neural representation domain -- we analyze deviations from this expected geometric property. Using a pre-trained self-supervised vision transformer (DINOv2), we quantify the temporal curvature and stepwise distance in the model's representation domain. We aggregate statistics of these measures for each video and train a classifier. Our analysis shows that AI-generated videos exhibit significantly different curvature and distance patterns compared to real videos. A lightweight classifier achieves state-of-the-art detection performance (e.g., 97.17% accuracy and 98.63% AUROC on the VidProM benchmark), substantially outperforming existing image- and video-based methods. ReStraV is computationally efficient, it is offering a low-cost and effective detection solution. This work provides new insights into using neural representation geometry for AI-generated video detection.
中摘: 生成式AI的快速发展使得合成视频变得极其逼真，这为内容认证带来了重大挑战，并引发了对其潜在滥用的紧迫担忧。现有的检测方法往往在泛化能力和捕捉细微的时间不一致性方面存在困难。我们提出了一种名为ReStraV（Representation Straightening Video）的新颖方法，用于区分自然视频与AI生成的视频。该方法受到“感知直化”假设的启发——即假设现实世界的视频轨迹在神经表示域内会变得更加直线——我们分析了这些视频偏离预期几何特性的程度。利用预训练的自监督视觉变换器（DINOv2），我们在模型的表示域中量化了时间曲率和逐步距离。我们为每个视频聚合这些度量的统计信息并训练分类器。我们的分析表明，AI生成的视频在曲率和距离模式上与真实视频存在显著差异。一个轻量级的分类器在VidProM基准测试中实现了最先进的检测性能（例如，准确率为97.17%，AUROC为98.63%），显著优于现有的基于图像和视频的方法。ReStraV计算效率高，提供了一种低成本且有效的检测解决方案。这项工作为使用神经表示几何学进行AI生成视频检测提供了新的见解。

[No.186] Extremely Simple Multimodal Outlier Synthesis for Out-of-Distribution Detection and Segmentation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 极简多模态离群点合成用于分布外检测与分割
类别: applications
作者: Moru Liu;Hao Dong;Jessica Ivy Kelly;Olga Fink;Mario Trapp;

英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection and segmentation are crucial for deploying machine learning models in safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving and robot-assisted surgery. While prior research has primarily focused on unimodal image data, real-world applications are inherently multimodal, requiring the integration of multiple modalities for improved OOD detection. A key challenge is the lack of supervision signals from unknown data, leading to overconfident predictions on OOD samples. To address this challenge, we propose Feature Mixing, an extremely simple and fast method for multimodal outlier synthesis with theoretical support, which can be further optimized to help the model better distinguish between in-distribution (ID) and OOD data. Feature Mixing is modality-agnostic and applicable to various modality combinations. Additionally, we introduce CARLA-OOD, a novel multimodal dataset for OOD segmentation, featuring synthetic OOD objects across diverse scenes and weather conditions. Extensive experiments on SemanticKITTI, nuScenes, CARLA-OOD datasets, and the MultiOOD benchmark demonstrate that Feature Mixing achieves state-of-the-art performance with a $10 \times$ to $370 \times$ speedup. Our source code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/mona4399/FeatureMixing.
中摘: 分布外（OOD）检测和分割对于在自动驾驶和机器人辅助手术等安全关键应用中部署机器学习模型至关重要。尽管先前的研究主要集中在单模态图像数据上，但现实世界的应用本身是多模态的，需要结合多种模态以提高OOD检测效果。一个主要挑战是没有来自未知数据的监督信号，这导致模型对OOD样本做出过于自信的预测。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种名为特征混合（Feature Mixing）的方法，这是一种理论支持下的极其简单且快速的多模态异常合成方法，可以进一步优化以帮助模型更好地区分内分布（ID）数据和OOD数据。特征混合方法不依赖于特定模态，并适用于各种模态组合。此外，我们还引入了CARLA-OOD，这是一个用于OOD分割的新颖多模态数据集，包含各种场景和天气条件下合成的OOD对象。在SemanticKITTI、nuScenes、CARLA-OOD数据集以及MultiOOD基准上的广泛实验表明，特征混合方法在性能上达到了最先进的水平，并实现了10至370倍的速度提升。我们的源代码和数据集将在https://github.com/mona4399/FeatureMixing 获取。

英摘: We study the problem of determining whether a piece of text has been authored by a human or by a large language model (LLM). [No.188] Beyond Token Probes: Hallucination Detection via Activation Tensors with ACT-ViT

英摘: Detecting hallucinations in Large Language Model-generated text is crucial for their safe deployment. [No.189] Delving into Cascaded Instability: A Lipschitz Continuity View on Image Restoration and Object Detection Synergy
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 深入探讨级联不稳定性：从Lipschitz连续性的视角看图像恢复与目标检测的协同作用
类别: deep learning
作者: Qing Zhao;Weijian Deng;Pengxu Wei;ZiYi Dong;Hannan Lu;Xiangyang Ji;Liang Lin;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;Australian National University;;Harbin Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=zPgPDHupcE
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24232
英摘: To improve detection robustness in adverse conditions (e.g., haze and low light), image restoration is commonly applied as a pre-processing step to enhance image quality for the detector. However, the functional mismatch between restoration and detection networks can introduce instability and hinder effective integration -- an issue that remains underexplored. We revisit this limitation through the lens of Lipschitz continuity, analyzing the functional differences between restoration and detection networks in both the input space and the parameter space. Our analysis shows that restoration networks perform smooth, continuous transformations, while object detectors operate with discontinuous decision boundaries, making them highly sensitive to minor perturbations. This mismatch introduces instability in traditional cascade frameworks, where even imperceptible noise from restoration is amplified during detection, disrupting gradient flow and hindering optimization. To address this, we propose Lipschitz-regularized object detection (LROD), a simple yet effective framework that integrates image restoration directly into the detector's feature learning, harmonizing the Lipschitz continuity of both tasks during training. We implement this framework as Lipschitz-regularized YOLO (LR-YOLO), extending seamlessly to existing YOLO detectors. Extensive experiments on haze and low-light benchmarks demonstrate that LR-YOLO consistently improves detection stability, optimization smoothness, and overall accuracy.
中摘: 为了在恶劣条件下（如雾霾和低光照）提高检测的鲁棒性，通常会在检测之前应用图像恢复作为预处理步骤来提升图像质量。然而，恢复网络和检测网络之间的功能不匹配可能会引入不稳定性和妨碍有效的集成——这个问题目前仍较少被探讨。我们通过Lipschitz连续性的视角重新审视这一限制，分析了恢复网络和检测网络在输入空间和参数空间的功能差异。我们的分析表明，恢复网络执行平滑、连续的变换，而目标检测器则具有不连续的决策边界，使其对细微扰动极为敏感。这种不匹配在传统的级联框架中引入了不稳定性，即使来自恢复过程的微不足道的噪声也会在检测过程中被放大，扰乱梯度流动并妨碍优化。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种Lipschitz正则化目标检测（LROD）框架，这是一种简单而有效的框架，将图像恢复直接整合到检测器的特征学习中，在训练过程中协调两个任务的Lipschitz连续性。我们将此框架实现为Lipschitz正则化的YOLO（LR-YOLO），无缝扩展到现有的YOLO检测器。在雾霾和低光照基准上的大量实验表明，LR-YOLO能够一致地提高检测的稳定性、优化的平滑性和整体准确性。

[No.190] Optimal Online Change Detection via Random Fourier Features
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于随机傅里叶特征的最优在线变化检测
类别: general machine learning
作者: Florian Kalinke;Shakeel A O B Gavioli-Akilagun;
机构: Karlsruher Institut für Technologie;Karlsruhe Institute of Technology;City University;London School of Economics and Political Science;

英摘: This article studies the problem of online non-parametric change point detection in multivariate data streams. We approach the problem through the lens of kernel-based two-sample testing and introduce a sequential testing procedure based on random Fourier features, running with logarithmic time complexity per observation and with overall logarithmic space complexity. The algorithm has two advantages compared to the state of the art. First, our approach is genuinely online, and no access to training data known to be from the pre-change distribution is necessary. Second, the algorithm does not require the user to specify a window parameter over which local tests are to be calculated. We prove strong theoretical guarantees on the algorithm's performance, including information-theoretic bounds demonstrating that the detection delay is optimal in the minimax sense. Numerical studies on real and synthetic data show that our algorithm is competitive with respect to the state of the art.
中摘: 这篇文章研究了多变量数据流中在线非参数变化点检测的问题。我们通过核基于两样本检验的角度来解决这个问题，并引入了一种基于随机傅里叶特征的序列性检验程序，该程序每次观测的时间复杂度为对数级别，整体空间复杂度也为对数级别。与现有方法相比，我们的算法具有两个优势。首先，我们的方法真正实现了在线处理，不需要访问已知来自变更前分布的训练数据。其次，算法不需要用户指定一个局部测试窗口参数。我们证明了该算法在理论上具有强大的保证，包括信息论界，表明检测延迟在最小最大意义上是最佳的。在真实和合成数据上的数值研究表明，我们的算法在与现有方法的竞争中表现良好。

类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=HiBoJLCyEo
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08073
英摘: AI-generated videos have achieved near-perfect visual realism (e.g., Sora), urgently necessitating reliable detection mechanisms. However, detecting such videos faces significant challenges in modeling high-dimensional spatiotemporal dynamics and identifying subtle anomalies that violate physical laws. In this paper, we propose a physics-driven AI-generated video detection paradigm based on probability flow conservation principles. Specifically, we propose a statistic called Normalized Spatiotemporal Gradient (NSG), which quantifies the ratio of spatial probability gradients to temporal density changes, explicitly capturing deviations from natural video dynamics. Leveraging pre-trained diffusion models, we develop an NSG estimator through spatial gradients approximation and motion-aware temporal modeling without complex motion decomposition while preserving physical constraints. Building on this, we propose an NSG-based video detection method (NSG-VD) that computes the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) between NSG features of the test and real videos as a detection metric. Last, we derive an upper bound of NSG feature distances between real and generated videos, proving that generated videos exhibit amplified discrepancies due to distributional shifts. Extensive experiments confirm that NSG-VD outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by 16.00% in Recall and 10.75% in F1-Score, validating the superior performance of NSG-VD. The source code is available at https://github.com/ZSHsh98/NSG-VD.
中摘: AI生成的视频已经实现了近乎完美的视觉真实性（例如Sora），迫切需要可靠的检测机制。然而，检测这类视频面临着在建模高维时空动态和识别细微违反物理定律的异常方面的重要挑战。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于概率流守恒原理的物理驱动型AI生成视频检测范式。具体而言，我们提出了一种名为归一化时空梯度（NSG）的统计量，该统计量量化了空间概率梯度与时间密度变化的比例，明确捕捉了与自然视频动态的偏差。利用预训练的扩散模型，我们通过空间梯度近似和运动感知的时间建模开发了一个NSG估计器，同时避免了复杂的运动分解，但仍保持了物理约束。在此基础上，我们提出了一种基于NSG特征的视频检测方法（NSG-VD），该方法计算测试视频和真实视频之间的最大均值离散性（MMD）作为检测指标。最后，我们推导了真实视频和生成视频之间NSG特征距离的上界，证明了生成视频由于分布转移表现出放大的差异性。大量实验表明，NSG-VD在召回率上比最先进的基线高出16.00%，在F1分数上高出10.75%，验证了NSG-VD的优越性能。源代码可在https://github.com/ZSHsh98/NSG-VD获取。

[No.192] Quantifying Statistical Significance of Deep Nearest Neighbor Anomaly Detection via Selective Inference
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过选择性推断量化深度最近邻异常检测的统计显著性
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Mizuki Niihori;Shuichi Nishino;Teruyuki Katsuoka;Tomohiro Shiraishi;Kouichi Taji;Ichiro Takeuchi;
机构: Nagoya University;RIKEN;Hitachi, Ltd.;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=pKg5zKIEuV
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12978
英摘: In real-world applications, anomaly detection (AD) often operates without access to anomalous data, necessitating semi-supervised methods that rely solely on normal data. Among these methods, deep k-nearest neighbor (deep kNN) AD stands out for its interpretability and flexibility, leveraging distance-based scoring in deep latent spaces.Despite its strong performance, deep kNN lacks a mechanism to quantify uncertainty-an essential feature for critical applications such as industrial inspection. To address this limitation, we propose a statistical framework that quantifies the significance of detected anomalies in the form of p-values, thereby enabling control over false positive rates at a user-specified significance level (e.g.,0.05). A central challenge lies in managing selection bias, which we tackle using Selective Inference-a principled method for conducting inference conditioned on data-driven selections. We evaluate our method on diverse datasets and demonstrate that it provides reliable AD well-suited for industrial use cases.
中摘: 在实际应用中，异常检测（AD）通常无法访问异常数据，因此需要依赖正常数据的半监督方法。在这类方法中，基于深度k最近邻（deep kNN）的异常检测因其可解释性和灵活性脱颖而出，它通过在深度潜在空间中的距离评分来实现异常检测。尽管其性能强大，但深kNN缺乏量化不确定性的能力——这对于工业检查等关键应用来说是一个必不可少的功能。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了一种统计框架，以p值的形式量化检测到的异常的重要性，从而能够在用户指定的显著性水平（例如0.05）下控制假阳性率。一个核心挑战在于管理选择偏差，我们通过选择推断（Selective Inference）来应对这一问题——这是一种基于数据驱动选择进行推理的方法。我们在多种数据集上评估了我们的方法，并证明了它能够提供可靠的异常检测，适用于工业应用场景。

[No.193] Optimal community detection in dense bipartite graphs
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 稠密二部图中的最优社区检测
类别: theory
作者: Julien Chhor;Parker Knight;
机构: Toulouse School of Economics;Harvard University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=e0Pq8EZx37
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18372
英摘: We consider the problem of detecting a community of densely connected vertices in a high-dimensional bipartite graph of size $n_1 \times n_2$. Under the null hypothesis, the observed graph is drawn from a bipartite Erdős-Renyi distribution with connection probability $p_0$. Under the alternative hypothesis, there exists an unknown bipartite subgraph of size $k_1 \times k_2$ in which edges appear with probability $p_1 = p_0 + δ$ for some $δ> 0$, while all other edges outside the subgraph appear with probability $p_0$. Specifically, we provide non-asymptotic upper and lower bounds on the smallest signal strength $δ^*$ that is both necessary and sufficient to ensure the existence of a test with small enough type one and type two errors. We also derive novel minimax-optimal tests achieving these fundamental limits when the underlying graph is sufficiently dense. Our proposed tests involve a combination of hard-thresholded nonlinear statistics of the adjacency matrix, the analysis of which may be of independent interest. In contrast with previous work, our non-asymptotic upper and lower bounds match for any configuration of $n_1,n_2, k_1,k_2$.
中摘: 我们考虑在一个大小为 \(n_1 \times n_2\) 的高维二分图中检测密集连接顶点社区的问题。在零假设下，观察到的图是从连接概率为 \(p_0\) 的二分Erdős-Renyi分布中抽取出来的。在备择假设下，存在一个未知的大小为 \(k_1 \times k_2\) 的二分子图，在该子图中边出现的概率为 \(p_1 = p_0 + \delta\)，其中 \(\delta > 0\)，而子图之外的所有其他边出现的概率均为 \(p_0\)。具体而言，我们提供了最小信号强度 \(\delta^*\) 的非渐近上界和下界，该信号强度既是必要的又是充分的，以确保存在一种具有足够小的第一类错误和第二类错误的检验方法。我们还推导出了在底层图足够密集时达到这些基本限制的新型最小最大最优检验方法。我们提出的检验方法涉及邻接矩阵的硬阈值非线性统计量的组合，这种分析可能具有独立的研究价值。与以往的工作不同，我们的非渐近上界和下界对于任何 \(n_1, n_2, k_1, k_2\) 的配置都是匹配的。

[No.194] Fairness-aware Anomaly Detection via Fair Projection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 公平意识异常检测通过公平投影实现
类别: general machine learning
作者: Feng Xiao;Xiaoying Tang;Jicong Fan;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong, Shenzhen;Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=gjjsDmlQRW
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11132
英摘: Unsupervised anomaly detection is a critical task in many high-social-impact applications such as finance, healthcare, social media, and cybersecurity, where demographics involving age, gender, race, disease, etc, are used frequently. In these scenarios, possible bias from anomaly detection systems can lead to unfair treatment for different groups and even exacerbate social bias. In this work, first, we thoroughly analyze the feasibility and necessary assumptions for ensuring group fairness in unsupervised anomaly detection. Second, we propose a novel fairness-aware anomaly detection method FairAD. From the normal training data, FairAD learns a projection to map data of different demographic groups to a common target distribution that is simple and compact, and hence provides a reliable base to estimate the density of the data. The density can be directly used to identify anomalies while the common target distribution ensures fairness between different groups. Furthermore, we propose a threshold-free fairness metric that provides a global view for model's fairness, eliminating dependence on manual threshold selection. Experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves an improved trade-off between detection accuracy and fairness under both balanced and skewed data across different groups.
中摘: 无监督异常检测在金融、医疗保健、社交媒体和网络安全等高社会影响的应用中是一项关键任务，其中经常涉及年龄、性别、种族、疾病等因素。在这些场景中，异常检测系统的潜在偏差可能导致不同群体受到不公平对待，甚至加剧社会偏见。在此项工作中，首先，我们全面分析了确保无监督异常检测中的群体公平性的可行性和必要假设。其次，我们提出了一种新的公平意识异常检测方法FairAD。FairAD从正常训练数据中学习一个投影，将不同人口统计学群体的数据映射到一个简单且紧凑的共同目标分布上，从而为数据密度估计提供可靠的基础。密度可以直接用于识别异常，而共同的目标分布则确保了不同群体之间的公平性。此外，我们还提出了一种无需阈值的公平性度量方法，该方法可以为模型的整体公平性提供全局视角，消除手动选择阈值的依赖性。实验结果显示，在不同群体的平衡和不平衡数据上，我们的方法能够在检测准确性和公平性之间取得更好的权衡。

[No.195] Double Descent Meets Out-of-Distribution Detection: Theoretical Insights and Empirical Analysis on the Role of Model Complexity
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 双下降现象与异常分布检测：模型复杂度的作用及其理论洞察与实证分析
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Mouin Ben Ammar;David Brellmann;Arturo Mendoza;Antoine Manzanera;Gianni Franchi;
机构: ;Safran;Ecole Nationale Supérieure de Techniques Avancées;École Nationale Supérieure de Techniques Avancées;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Y5xuVoVz7g
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02184
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential for ensuring the reliability and safety of machine learning systems. In recent years, it has received increasing attention, particularly through post-hoc detection and training-based methods. In this paper, we focus on post-hoc OOD detection, which enables identifying OOD samples without altering the model's training procedure or objective. Our primary goal is to investigate the relationship between model capacity and its OOD detection performance. Specifically, we aim to answer the following question: Does the Double Descent phenomenon manifest in post-hoc OOD detection? This question is crucial, as it can reveal whether overparameterization, which is already known to benefit generalization, can also enhance OOD detection. Despite the growing interest in these topics by the classic supervised machine learning community, this intersection remains unexplored for OOD detection. We empirically demonstrate that the Double Descent effect does indeed appear in post-hoc OOD detection. Furthermore, we provide theoretical insights to explain why this phenomenon emerges in such setting. Finally, we show that the overparameterized regime does not yield superior results consistently, and we propose a method to identify the optimal regime for OOD detection based on our observations.
中摘: 分布外（OOD）检测对于确保机器学习系统的可靠性和安全性至关重要。近年来，它受到了越来越多的关注，特别是在事后检测和基于训练的方法方面。在本文中，我们专注于事后OOD检测，这种检测方法可以在不改变模型训练过程或目标的情况下识别OOD样本。我们的主要目标是研究模型容量与其OOD检测性能之间的关系。具体来说，我们旨在回答以下问题：双下降现象是否在事后OOD检测中表现出来？这个问题非常重要，因为它可以揭示过度参数化是否不仅能改善泛化能力，还能增强OOD检测。尽管经典监督机器学习社区对这些主题越来越感兴趣，但这一交叉领域在OOD检测方面的研究仍处于空白状态。我们通过实验证明，双下降效应确实出现在事后OOD检测中。此外，我们提供了理论见解来解释为什么在这种设置中会出现这一现象。最后，我们表明过度参数化的阶段并不总是能取得更优的结果，并根据我们的观察提出了一种基于此来识别OOD检测最优阶段的方法。

[No.196] Test-Time Adaptive Object Detection with Foundation Model
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于基础模型的测试时自适应目标检测
类别: applications
作者: Yingjie Gao;Yanan Zhang;Zhi Cai;Di Huang;
机构: Beihang University;Hefei University of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=MO4U4mg0oT
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25175
英摘: In recent years, test-time adaptive object detection has attracted increasing attention due to its unique advantages in online domain adaptation, which aligns more closely with real-world application scenarios. However, existing approaches heavily rely on source-derived statistical characteristics while making the strong assumption that the source and target domains share an identical category space. In this paper, we propose the first foundation model-powered test-time adaptive object detection method that eliminates the need for source data entirely and overcomes traditional closed-set limitations. Specifically, we design a Multi-modal Prompt-based Mean-Teacher framework for vision-language detector-driven test-time adaptation, which incorporates text and visual prompt tuning to adapt both language and vision representation spaces on the test data in a parameter-efficient manner. Correspondingly, we propose a Test-time Warm-start strategy tailored for the visual prompts to effectively preserve the representation capability of the vision branch. Furthermore, to guarantee high-quality pseudo-labels in every test batch, we maintain an Instance Dynamic Memory (IDM) module that stores high-quality pseudo-labels from previous test samples, and propose two novel strategies-Memory Enhancement and Memory Hallucination-to leverage IDM's high-quality instances for enhancing original predictions and hallucinating images without available pseudo-labels, respectively. Extensive experiments on cross-corruption and cross-dataset benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods, and can adapt to arbitrary cross-domain and cross-category target data. Code is available at https://github.com/gaoyingjay/ttaod_foundation.
中摘: 近年来，测试时自适应目标检测由于其在在线领域适应性方面的独特优势而引起了越来越多的关注。然而，现有的方法严重依赖于源数据衍生的统计特性，并且假设源域和目标域共享相同类别空间。本文中，我们提出了一种基于基础模型的首个测试时自适应目标检测方法，完全消除了对源数据的需求并克服了传统的封闭集限制。具体而言，我们设计了一个多模态提示驱动的Mean-Teacher框架，用于视觉语言检测器驱动的测试时自适应，该框架通过文本和视觉提示调优来高效地适应测试数据的语言和视觉表示空间。此外，我们提出了一个针对视觉提示的测试时预热策略，以有效保留视觉分支的表示能力。为进一步确保每个测试批次中的高质量伪标签，我们维护了一个实例动态记忆（IDM）模块，用于存储之前测试样本中的高质量伪标签，并提出了两种新的策略——记忆增强和记忆幻觉，以利用IDM中的高质量实例来增强原始预测和生成没有可用伪标签的图像。在跨污染和跨数据集基准上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法在所有测试中均优于先前的最佳方法，并能够适应任意跨域和跨类的目标数据。代码可在https://github.com/gaoyingjay/ttaod_foundation获取。

[No.198] Self-Supervised Learning of Graph Representations for Network Intrusion Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 自监督学习在网络入侵检测中的图表示学习
类别: applications
作者: Lorenzo Guerra;Thomas Chapuis;Guillaume Duc;Pavlo Mozharovskyi;Van-Tam Nguyen;
机构: Télécom Paris;Ampere Software Technology;Ampere;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=5bu1IOOvf0
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16625
英摘: Detecting intrusions in network traffic is a challenging task, particularly under limited supervision and constantly evolving attack patterns. While recent works have leveraged graph neural networks for network intrusion detection, they often decouple representation learning from anomaly detection, limiting the utility of the embeddings for identifying attacks. We propose GraphIDS, a self-supervised intrusion detection model that unifies these two stages by learning local graph representations of normal communication patterns through a masked autoencoder. An inductive graph neural network embeds each flow with its local topological context to capture typical network behavior, while a Transformer-based encoder-decoder reconstructs these embeddings, implicitly learning global co-occurrence patterns via self-attention without requiring explicit positional information. During inference, flows with unusually high reconstruction errors are flagged as potential intrusions. This end-to-end framework ensures that embeddings are directly optimized for the downstream task, facilitating the recognition of malicious traffic. On diverse NetFlow benchmarks, GraphIDS achieves up to 99.98% PR-AUC and 99.61% macro F1-score, outperforming baselines by 5-25 percentage points.
中摘: 在网络流量中检测入侵是一项具有挑战性的任务，特别是在监督有限且不断演变的攻击模式下。尽管最近的研究利用了图神经网络进行网络入侵检测，但它们通常将表示学习与异常检测分离开来，限制了嵌入在识别攻击方面的实用性。我们提出了一种名为GraphIDS的自监督入侵检测模型，该模型通过掩码自编码器统一了这两个阶段，学习正常通信模式的局部图表示。一种归纳性的图神经网络将每个数据流与其局部拓扑上下文嵌入在一起以捕捉典型的网络行为，而基于Transformer的编码器-解码器则重构这些嵌入，通过自注意力机制隐式学习全局共现模式，无需显式的位置信息。在推理过程中，重建误差异常高的数据流会被标记为潜在的入侵行为。这种端到端框架确保嵌入直接优化了下游任务，从而促进了恶意流量的识别。在多种NetFlow基准测试上，GraphIDS实现了高达99.98%的PR-AUC和99.61%的宏F1分数，并在基线模型上提高了5-25个百分点。

[No.199] DIsoN: Decentralized Isolation Networks for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Medical Imaging
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: DIsoN：分布式隔离网络在医学影像领域中的分布外检测
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Felix Wagner;Pramit Saha;Harry Anthony;Alison Noble;Konstantinos Kamnitsas;
机构: University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=wbifKziqd2
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09024
英摘: Safe deployment of machine learning (ML) models in safety-critical domains such as medical imaging requires detecting inputs with characteristics not seen during training, known as out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, to prevent unreliable predictions. Effective OOD detection after deployment could benefit from access to the training data, enabling direct comparison between test samples and the training data distribution to identify differences. State-of-the-art OOD detection methods, however, either discard the training data after deployment or assume that test samples and training data are centrally stored together, an assumption that rarely holds in real-world settings. This is because shipping the training data with the deployed model is usually impossible due to the size of training databases, as well as proprietary or privacy constraints. We introduce the Isolation Network, an OOD detection framework that quantifies the difficulty of separating a target test sample from the training data by solving a binary classification task. We then propose Decentralized Isolation Networks (DIsoN), which enables the comparison of training and test data when data-sharing is impossible, by exchanging only model parameters between the remote computational nodes of training and deployment. We further extend DIsoN with class-conditioning, comparing a target sample solely with training data of its predicted class. We evaluate DIsoN on four medical imaging datasets (dermatology, chest X-ray, breast ultrasound, histopathology) across 12 OOD detection tasks. DIsoN performs favorably against existing methods while respecting data-privacy. This decentralized OOD detection framework opens the way for a new type of service that ML developers could provide along with their models: providing remote, secure utilization of their training data for OOD detection services. Code: https://github.com/FelixWag/DIsoN
中摘: 在医疗影像等安全关键领域安全部署机器学习（ML）模型需要检测训练期间未见过特征的输入，这被称为离分布（OOD）检测，以防止不可靠的预测。部署后的有效OOD检测可以通过访问训练数据直接比较测试样本和训练数据分布来识别差异，从而受益。然而，最先进的OOD检测方法要么在部署后丢弃训练数据，要么假设测试样本和训练数据集中存储在一起，而在实际应用场景中这种假设很少成立。这是因为由于训练数据库的大小以及专有或隐私限制，通常不可能将训练数据与部署模型一起运输。我们引入了隔离网络（Isolation Network），这是一种通过解决二元分类任务来量化将目标测试样本从训练数据中分离出来的难度的OOD检测框架。随后，我们提出了去中心化隔离网络（Decentralized Isolation Networks, DIsoN），当无法共享数据时，它可以在远程计算节点之间仅交换模型参数，从而实现训练数据和测试数据的比较。进一步地，我们扩展了DIsoN，加入了类别条件处理，仅将目标样本与其预测类别的训练数据进行比较。我们在四个医疗影像数据集（皮肤科、胸部X光、乳腺超声、组织病理学）上对DIsoN进行了12项OOD检测任务的评估，结果显示其表现优于现有方法，同时尊重数据隐私。这一去中心化的OOD检测框架为机器学习开发者提供了一种新的服务模式：除了提供模型外，还可以远程、安全地利用其训练数据来提供OOD检测服务。代码：https://github.com/FelixWag/DIsoN

[No.201] SAFE: Multitask Failure Detection for Vision-Language-Action Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 安全：针对视觉-语言-行动模型的多任务故障检测
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Qiao Gu;Yuanliang Ju;Shengxiang Sun;Igor Gilitschenski;Haruki Nishimura;Masha Itkina;Florian Shkurti;
机构: University of Toronto;;Toyota Research Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=XPyAukgsFf
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09937
英摘: While vision-language-action models (VLAs) have shown promising robotic behaviors across a diverse set of manipulation tasks, they achieve limited success rates when deployed on novel tasks out of the box. To allow these policies to safely interact with their environments, we need a failure detector that gives a timely alert such that the robot can stop, backtrack, or ask for help. However, existing failure detectors are trained and tested only on one or a few specific tasks, while generalist VLAs require the detector to generalize and detect failures also in unseen tasks and novel environments. In this paper, we introduce the multitask failure detection problem and propose SAFE, a failure detector for generalist robot policies such as VLAs. We analyze the VLA feature space and find that VLAs have sufficient high-level knowledge about task success and failure, which is generic across different tasks. Based on this insight, we design SAFE to learn from VLA internal features and predict a single scalar indicating the likelihood of task failure. SAFE is trained on both successful and failed rollouts and is evaluated on unseen tasks. SAFE is compatible with different policy architectures. We test it on OpenVLA, $π_0$, and $π_0$-FAST in both simulated and real-world environments extensively. We compare SAFE with diverse baselines and show that SAFE achieves state-of-the-art failure detection performance and the best trade-off between accuracy and detection time using conformal prediction. More qualitative results and code can be found at the project webpage: https://vla-safe.github.io/
中摘: 尽管视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）在多种操作任务中展示了有前景的机器人行为表现，但在部署到全新的任务时，它们的成功率却有限。为了使这些策略能够安全地与环境互动，我们需要一个故障检测器，能够及时发出警报，从而使机器人可以停止、回溯或寻求帮助。然而，现有的故障检测器仅针对一两个特定任务进行训练和测试，而通用型VLAs则需要故障检测器具备泛化能力，在未见过的任务和新型环境中也能检测故障。在本文中，我们提出了多任务故障检测问题，并提出了一种名为SAFE的故障检测器，用于通用型机器人策略，如VLAs。我们分析了VLAs的功能空间，发现VLAs具有足够的高层次知识来判断任务的成功与失败，这种知识在不同任务中是通用的。基于这一洞察，我们设计了SAFE，使其能够从VLAs内部特征中学习，并预测一个表示任务失败可能性的单一标量值。SAFE在成功的和失败的演示中进行了训练，并在未见过的任务上进行了评估。SAFE兼容不同的策略架构。我们在模拟和真实环境中广泛测试了它在OpenVLA、$π_0$和$π_0$-FAST上的性能。我们将SAFE与多种基线进行比较，并展示了SAFE在故障检测性能上达到了最先进的水平，同时在准确性和检测时间之间取得了最佳平衡，使用的是符合性预测方法。更多定性的结果和代码可以在项目网页上找到：https://vla-safe.github.io/

[No.202] DitHub: A Modular Framework for Incremental Open-Vocabulary Object Detection

英摘: Open-Vocabulary object detectors can generalize to an unrestricted set of categories through simple textual prompting. However, adapting these models to rare classes or reinforcing their abilities on multiple specialized domains remains essential. While recent methods rely on monolithic adaptation strategies with a single set of weights, we embrace modular deep learning. We introduce DitHub, a framework designed to build and maintain a library of efficient adaptation modules. Inspired by Version Control Systems, DitHub manages expert modules as branches that can be fetched and merged as needed. [No.203] Redundancy-Aware Test-Time Graph Out-of-Distribution Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 冗余意识下的测试时图分布外检测
类别: general machine learning
作者: Yue Hou;He Zhu;Ruomei Liu;Yingke Su;Junran Wu;Ke Xu;
机构: Beihang University;OPPO Mobile Telecommunications Corp.,Ltd.;;National University of Singapore;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=CQBiYvXLgz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14562
英摘: Distributional discrepancy between training and test data can lead models to make inaccurate predictions when encountering out-of-distribution (OOD) samples in real-world applications. Although existing graph OOD detection methods leverage data-centric techniques to extract effective representations, their performance remains compromised by structural redundancy that induces semantic shifts. To address this dilemma, we propose RedOUT, an unsupervised framework that integrates structural entropy into test-time OOD detection for graph classification. Concretely, we introduce the Redundancy-aware Graph Information Bottleneck (ReGIB) and decompose the objective into essential information and irrelevant redundancy. By minimizing structural entropy, the decoupled redundancy is reduced, and theoretically grounded upper and lower bounds are proposed for optimization. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the superior performance of RedOUT on OOD detection. Specifically, our method achieves an average improvement of 6.7%, significantly surpassing the best competitor by 17.3% on the ClinTox/LIPO dataset pair.
中摘: 训练数据和测试数据之间的分布差异可能导致模型在遇到真实世界应用中的新型分布样本（OOD样本）时产生不准确的预测。尽管现有的图OOD检测方法利用以数据为中心的技术提取有效的表示，但它们的表现仍然受到结构冗余的影响，这种结构冗余会引起语义变化。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为RedOUT的无监督框架，该框架将结构熵集成到图分类的测试时OOD检测中。具体而言，我们引入了冗余感知的图信息瓶颈（ReGIB），并将其目标分解为必要的信息和无关的冗余。通过最小化结构熵，解耦的冗余被减少，并且提出了理论依据的上界和下界来优化。在实际数据集上的广泛实验表明，RedOUT在OOD检测方面表现出优越性能。具体来说，我们的方法在ClinTox/LIPO数据集对上平均提高了6.7%，显著优于最佳竞争对手17.3%。

[No.204] Normal-Abnormal Guided Generalist Anomaly Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 正常-异常引导通用异常检测
类别: applications
作者: Yuexin Wang;Xiaolei Wang;Yizheng Gong;Jimin XIAO;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00495
英摘: Generalist Anomaly Detection (GAD) aims to train a unified model on an original domain that can detect anomalies in new target domains. Previous GAD methods primarily use only normal samples as references, overlooking the valuable information contained in anomalous samples that are often available in real-world scenarios. To address this limitation, we propose a more practical approach: normal-abnormal-guided generalist anomaly detection, which leverages both normal and anomalous samples as references to guide anomaly detection across diverse domains. We introduce the Normal-Abnormal Generalist Learning (NAGL) framework, consisting of two key components: Residual Mining (RM) and Anomaly Feature Learning (AFL). RM extracts abnormal patterns from normal-abnormal reference residuals to establish transferable anomaly representations, while AFL adaptively learns anomaly features in query images through residual mapping to identify instance-aware anomalies. Our approach effectively utilizes both normal and anomalous references for more accurate and efficient cross-domain anomaly detection. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing GAD approaches. This work represents the first to adopt a mixture of normal and abnormal samples as references in generalist anomaly detection. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/JasonKyng/NAGL.
中摘: 通用异常检测（GAD）旨在在一个原始领域训练一个统一模型，以检测新目标领域的异常。以往的GAD方法主要仅使用正常样本作为参考，忽视了其中包含的重要异常信息，这些异常信息在现实场景中往往是可以获得的。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了一种更为实际的方法：正常-异常引导的通用异常检测，该方法利用正常和异常样本作为参考来指导跨不同领域的异常检测。我们引入了Normal-Abnormal Generalist Learning（NAGL）框架，该框架由两个关键组件组成：残差挖掘（RM）和异常特征学习（AFL）。RM从正常-异常参考残差中提取异常模式，建立可转移的异常表示；而AFL通过残差映射在查询图像中自适应地学习异常特征，以识别实例感知的异常。我们的方法有效地利用了正常和异常两种参考样本，从而实现更准确、更高效的跨域异常检测。在多个基准上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法显著优于现有的GAD方法。这项工作是首次在通用异常检测中采用正常和异常样本混合作为参考的方法。代码和数据集可在https://github.com/JasonKyng/NAGL获取。

[No.205] Visual Diversity and Region-aware Prompt Learning for Zero-shot HOI Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 视觉多样性和区域意识提示学习在零样本HOI检测中的应用
类别: applications
作者: Chanhyeong Yang;Taehoon song;Jihwan Park;Hyunwoo J. Kim;
机构: ;Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Korea University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=KN2UMEvUOb
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25094
英摘: Zero-shot Human-Object Interaction detection aims to localize humans and objects in an image and recognize their interaction, even when specific verb-object pairs are unseen during training. Recent works have shown promising results using prompt learning with pretrained vision-language models such as CLIP, which align natural language prompts with visual features in a shared embedding space. However, existing approaches still fail to handle the visual complexity of interaction, including (1) intra-class visual diversity, where instances of the same verb appear in diverse poses and contexts, and (2) inter-class visual entanglement, where distinct verbs yield visually similar patterns. To address these challenges, we propose VDRP, a framework for Visual Diversity and Region-aware Prompt learning. First, we introduce a visual diversity-aware prompt learning strategy that injects group-wise visual variance into the context embedding. We further apply Gaussian perturbation to encourage the prompts to capture diverse visual variations of a verb. Second, we retrieve region-specific concepts from the human, object, and union regions. These are used to augment the diversity-aware prompt embeddings, yielding region-aware prompts that enhance verb-level discrimination. Experiments on the HICO-DET benchmark demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance under four zero-shot evaluation settings, effectively addressing both intra-class diversity and inter-class visual entanglement. Code is available at https://github.com/mlvlab/VDRP.
中摘: 零样本人类对象交互检测旨在在一个图像中定位人类和对象，并识别它们之间的交互，即使在训练过程中未见过特定的动词-对象配对。近期的研究表明，使用预训练的视觉语言模型（如CLIP）进行提示学习可以取得令人鼓舞的结果，这些模型能够在共享嵌入空间中将自然语言提示与视觉特征对齐。然而，现有的方法仍然无法处理交互的视觉复杂性，包括（1）同一类别的视觉多样性，即同一个动词在不同的姿态和上下文中出现；（2）类别间的视觉纠缠，即不同的动词会产生视觉上相似的模式。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了VDRP框架，这是一种视觉多样性和区域感知提示学习的方法。首先，我们引入了一种视觉多样性感知的提示学习策略，在上下文嵌入中注入组内视觉变异。进一步地，我们应用高斯扰动来鼓励提示捕捉动词的多种视觉变化。其次，我们从人体、对象以及联合区域检索出特定的区域概念，并将其用于增强视觉多样性感知的提示嵌入，生成区域感知的提示以提高动词级别的区分能力。在HICO-DET基准测试上的实验表明，我们的方法在四种零样本评估设置下达到了最先进的性能，有效地解决了同一类别内的视觉多样性以及不同类别间的视觉纠缠问题。代码可在https://github.com/mlvlab/VDRP获取。

[No.206] Harnessing Feature Resonance under Arbitrary Target Alignment for Out-of-Distribution Node Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 在任意目标对齐下利用特征共振进行分布外节点检测
类别: general machine learning
作者: Shenzhi Yang;Junbo Zhao;Sharon Li;Shouqing Yang;Dingyu Yang;Xiaofang Zhang;Haobo Wang;
机构: Zhejiang University;Soochow University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=JWmfbzw8ur
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16076
英摘: Detecting out-of-distribution (OOD) nodes in the graph-based machine-learning field is challenging, particularly when in-distribution (ID) node multi-category labels are unavailable. Thus, we focus on feature space rather than label space and find that, ideally, during the optimization of known ID samples, unknown ID samples undergo more significant representation changes than OOD samples, even if the model is trained to fit random targets, which we called the Feature Resonance phenomenon. The rationale behind it is that even without gold labels, the local manifold may still exhibit smooth resonance. Based on this, we further develop a novel graph OOD framework, dubbed Resonance-based Separation and Learning (RSL), which comprises two core modules: (i) a more practical micro-level proxy of feature resonance that measures the movement of feature vectors in one training step. (ii) integrate with synthetic OOD nodes strategy to train an effective OOD classifier. Theoretically, we derive an error bound showing the superior separability of OOD nodes during the resonance period. Extensive experiments on a total of thirteen real-world graph datasets empirically demonstrate that RSL achieves state-of-the-art performance.
中摘: 在基于图的机器学习领域检测分布外（OOD）节点具有挑战性，尤其是在无法获得分布内（ID）节点多类别标签的情况下。因此，我们专注于特征空间而非标签空间，并发现理想情况下，在优化已知ID样本的过程中，未知ID样本会在特征表示上经历更为显著的变化，即使模型被训练以拟合随机目标，我们将其称为特征共振现象。其背后的原理是，即使没有黄金标签，局部流形仍可能表现出平滑的共振。基于此，我们进一步开发了一种新颖的图分布外（OOD）框架，称之为基于共振分离与学习（RSL）。该框架包括两个核心模块：（i）一个更实用的微观层面的特征共振代理，用于衡量单步训练中特征向量的移动情况。（ii）结合合成OOD节点策略来训练有效的OOD分类器。理论上，我们推导出一个误差界，展示了共振期间OOD节点的优越可分性。在总共十三个真实世界的图数据集上的广泛实验中，RSL实现了最先进的性能。

[No.207] CSPCL: Category Semantic Prior Contrastive Learning for Deformable DETR-Based Prohibited Item Detectors
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: CSPCL：类别语义先验对比学习在可变形DETR基禁止物品检测器中的应用
类别: applications
作者: Mingyuan Li;Tong Jia;Hao Wang;Bowen Ma;Luhui;Shiyi Guo;Da Cai;Dongyue Chen;
机构: Northeastern University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=bzjlMvUKDv
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.16665
英摘: Prohibited item detection based on X-ray images is one of the most effective security inspection methods. However, the foreground-background feature coupling caused by the overlapping phenomenon specific to X-ray images makes general detectors designed for natural images perform poorly. To address this issue, we propose a Category Semantic Prior Contrastive Learning (CSPCL) mechanism, which aligns the class prototypes perceived by the classifier with the content queries to correct and supplement the missing semantic information responsible for classification, thereby enhancing the model sensitivity to foreground features. To achieve this alignment, we design a specific contrastive loss, CSP loss, which comprises the Intra-Class Truncated Attraction (ITA) loss and the Inter-Class Adaptive Repulsion (IAR) loss, and outperforms classic contrastive losses. Specifically, the ITA loss leverages class prototypes to attract intra-class content queries and preserves essential intra-class diversity via a gradient truncation function. The IAR loss employs class prototypes to adaptively repel inter-class content queries, with the repulsion strength scaled by prototype-prototype similarity, thereby improving inter-class discriminability, especially among similar categories. CSPCL is general and can be easily integrated into Deformable DETR-based models. Extensive experiments on the PIXray, OPIXray, PIDray, and CLCXray datasets demonstrate that CSPCL significantly enhances the performance of various state-of-the-art models without increasing inference complexity. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Limingyuan001/CSPCL.
中摘: 基于X射线图像的禁止物品检测是安全检查中最有效的方法之一。然而，由X射线图像特有的重叠现象引起的前景背景特征耦合使得设计用于自然图像的一般检测器表现不佳。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种类别语义先验对比学习（CSPCL）机制，该机制将分类器感知的类原型与内容查询对齐，以纠正和补充负责分类的缺失语义信息，从而增强模型对前景特征的敏感性。为了实现这种对齐，我们设计了一种特定的对比损失函数——CSP损失，它包括内部类截断吸引（ITA）损失和跨类别自适应排斥（IAR）损失，并且优于经典的对比损失函数。具体来说，ITA损失利用类原型吸引同一类的内容查询，并通过梯度截断函数保留关键的同一类多样性。IAR损失则利用类原型自适应地排斥不同类的内容查询，排斥强度根据类原型之间的相似度进行缩放，从而提高跨类别的可区分性，特别是在相似类别之间。CSPCL是一种通用机制，可以轻松集成到Deformable DETR基础上的模型中。在PIXray、OPIXray、PIDray和CLCXray数据集上的广泛实验表明，CSPCL显著提升了各种先进模型的性能，而无需增加推理复杂度。代码已公开发布在https://github.com/Limingyuan001/CSPCL。

[No.208] Denoising Trajectory Biases for Zero-Shot AI-Generated Image Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 去除轨迹偏差以提高零样本AI生成图像检测效果
类别: applications
作者: Yachao Liang;Min Yu;Gang Li;Jianguo Jiang;Fuqiang Du;Li Jingyuan;Lanchi Xie;Zhen Xu;Weiqing Huang;

[No.209] YOLOv12: Attention-Centric Real-Time Object Detectors
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: YOLOv12：以注意力为中心的实时目标检测器
类别: deep learning
作者: Yunjie Tian;Qixiang Ye;David Doermann;
机构: State University of New York at Buffalo;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=gCvByDI4FN
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12524
英摘: Enhancing the network architecture of the YOLO framework has been crucial for a long time, but has focused on CNN-based improvements despite the proven superiority of attention mechanisms in modeling capabilities. This is because attention-based models cannot match the speed of CNN-based models. This paper proposes an attention-centric YOLO framework, namely YOLOv12, that matches the speed of previous CNN-based ones while harnessing the performance benefits of attention mechanisms. YOLOv12 surpasses all popular real-time object detectors in accuracy with competitive speed. For example, YOLOv12-N achieves 40.6% mAP with an inference latency of 1.64 ms on a T4 GPU, outperforming advanced YOLOv10-N / YOLOv11-N by 2.1%/1.2% mAP with a comparable speed. This advantage extends to other model scales. YOLOv12 also surpasses end-to-end real-time detectors that improve DETR, such as RT-DETR / RT-DETRv2: YOLOv12-S beats RT-DETR-R18 / RT-DETRv2-R18 while running 42% faster, using only 36% of the computation and 45% of the parameters. More comparisons are shown in Figure 1.
中摘: 长久以来，增强YOLO框架的网络架构至关重要，但这些改进主要集中在基于CNN的方法上，尽管注意力机制在建模能力方面已被证明具有优越性。这是因为基于注意力的模型无法匹配基于CNN的模型的速度。本文提出了一种以注意力为中心的YOLO框架，即YOLOv12，它在速度上与之前的基于CNN的模型相当，同时利用了注意力机制带来的性能优势。YOLOv12在准确性上超越了所有流行的实时目标检测器，并且具有竞争力的速度。例如，YOLOv12-N在T4 GPU上的推理延迟为1.64毫秒时达到了40.6%的mAP，分别比先进的YOLOv10-N / YOLOv11-N高出2.1%和1.2%，并且具有相近的速度。这种优势也扩展到了其他模型规模。YOLOv12还超越了改进DETR的端到端实时检测器，如RT-DETR / RT-DETRv2：YOLOv12-S运行速度快42%，仅使用36%的计算量和45%的参数就击败了RT-DETR-R18 / RT-DETRv2-R18。更多的比较见图1。

[No.211] Lie Detector: Unified Backdoor Detection via Cross-Examination Framework
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 谎言检测：通过交叉审问框架实现统一后门检测
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Xuan Wang;Siyuan Liang;Dongping Liao;Han Fang;Aishan Liu;Xiaochun Cao;Yu-liang Lu;Ee-Chien Chang;Xitong Gao;
机构: National University of Defense Technology;Nanyang Technological University;National University of Singapore;University of Macau;Beihang University;Sun Yat-sen University;Shenzhen University of Advanced Technology;Shenzhen Institute of Advanced Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=yBWRrqPwyN
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16872
英摘: Institutions with limited data and computing resources often outsource model training to third-party providers in a semi-honest setting, assuming adherence to prescribed training protocols with pre-defined learning paradigm (e.g., supervised or semi-supervised learning). However, this practice can introduce severe security risks, as adversaries may poison the training data to embed backdoors into the resulting model. Existing detection approaches predominantly rely on statistical analyses, which often fail to maintain universally accurate detection accuracy across different learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose a unified backdoor detection framework in the semi-honest setting that exploits cross-examination of model inconsistencies between two independent service providers. Specifically, we integrate central kernel alignment to enable robust feature similarity measurements across different model architectures and learning paradigms, thereby facilitating precise recovery and identification of backdoor triggers. We further introduce backdoor fine-tuning sensitivity analysis to distinguish backdoor triggers from adversarial perturbations, substantially reducing false positives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior detection performance, improving accuracy by 5.4%, 1.6%, and 11.9% over SoTA baselines across supervised, semi-supervised, and autoregressive learning tasks, respectively. Notably, it is the first to effectively detect backdoors in multimodal large language models, further highlighting its broad applicability and advancing secure deep learning.
中摘: 资源有限的机构通常会在半诚实设置下将模型训练外包给第三方提供商，前提是这些提供商遵守预先规定的训练协议，并采用预定义的学习范式（例如监督学习或半监督学习）。然而，这种做法可能会引入严重的安全风险，因为对手可以通过污染训练数据来在最终模型中植入后门。现有的检测方法主要依赖于统计分析，但这种方法往往难以在不同的学习范式之间保持普遍准确的检测精度。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种在半诚实设置下的统一后门检测框架，该框架通过两个独立服务提供商之间的模型不一致性交叉审查来利用模型不一致性的检测。具体来说，我们整合了中心核对齐技术，以实现不同模型架构和学习范式之间的稳健特征相似性测量，从而促进后门触发器的精确恢复和识别。此外，我们引入了后门微调敏感性分析，以区分后门触发器和对抗性扰动，显著减少了误报。广泛的实验表明，我们的方法在检测性能上取得了优越的表现，分别在监督学习、半监督学习和自回归学习任务中提高了5.4%、1.6%和11.9%的准确性。值得注意的是，这是首次有效检测多模态大型语言模型中的后门，进一步突显了其广泛适用性和推动了安全深度学习的发展。

[No.213] Mind-the-Glitch: Visual Correspondence for Detecting Inconsistencies in Subject-Driven Generation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: Mind-the-Glitch: 视觉对应检测在主题驱动生成中的一致性问题识别
类别: deep learning
作者: Abdelrahman Eldesokey;Aleksandar Cvejić;Bernard Ghanem;Peter Wonka;
机构: King Abdullah University of Science and Technology;Snap Inc.;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=4FyNdd2b5S
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21989
英摘: We propose a novel approach for disentangling visual and semantic features from the backbones of pre-trained diffusion models, enabling visual correspondence in a manner analogous to the well-established semantic correspondence. While diffusion model backbones are known to encode semantically rich features, they must also contain visual features to support their image synthesis capabilities. However, isolating these visual features is challenging due to the absence of annotated datasets. To address this, we introduce an automated pipeline that constructs image pairs with annotated semantic and visual correspondences based on existing subject-driven image generation datasets, and design a contrastive architecture to separate the two feature types. Leveraging the disentangled representations, we propose a new metric, Visual Semantic Matching (VSM), that quantifies visual inconsistencies in subject-driven image generation. Empirical results show that our approach outperforms global feature-based metrics such as CLIP, DINO, and vision--language models in quantifying visual inconsistencies while also enabling spatial localization of inconsistent regions. To our knowledge, this is the first method that supports both quantification and localization of inconsistencies in subject-driven generation, offering a valuable tool for advancing this task. Project Page:https://abdo-eldesokey.github.io/mind-the-glitch/
中摘: 我们提出了一种新颖的方法，从预训练扩散模型的骨干网络中分离视觉和语义特征，使视觉对应关系类似于已建立的语义对应关系。虽然扩散模型的骨干网络已知能够编码丰富的语义特征，但它们也必须包含视觉特征以支持其图像合成能力。然而，由于缺乏标注数据集，隔离这些视觉特征具有挑战性。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了一个自动化的管道，该管道基于现有的主题驱动图像生成数据集构建带有标注语义和视觉对应关系的图像对，并设计了一种对比架构来分离这两种特征类型。利用分离出的表示形式，我们提出了一种新的度量标准——视觉语义匹配（VSM），用于量化主题驱动图像生成中的视觉不一致性。实验结果表明，我们的方法在量化视觉不一致性方面优于基于全局特征的度量标准，如CLIP、DINO和视觉-语言模型，并且还能够定位不一致区域的空间位置。据我们所知，这是首个同时支持不一致性量化和定位的主题驱动生成方法，为推进这一任务提供了有价值的工具。项目页面：https://abdo-eldesokey.github.io/mind-the-glitch/
