[No.214] GOOD: Training-Free Guided Diffusion Sampling for Out-of-Distribution Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 训练-free 引导扩散采样用于异常分布检测
类别: deep learning
作者: Xin Gao;Jiyao Liu;Guanghao Li;Yueming Lyu;Jianxiong Gao;Weichen Yu;Ningsheng Xu;Liang Wang;Caifeng Shan;Ziwei Liu;Chenyang Si;
机构: Fudan University;;Nanjing University;Shanghai AI Laboratory;Carnegie Mellon University;Chinese Academy of Sciences;Nanyang Technological University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=lRkeVFL85X
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17131
英摘: Recent advancements have explored text-to-image diffusion models for synthesizing out-of-distribution (OOD) samples, substantially enhancing the performance of OOD detection. However, existing approaches typically rely on perturbing text-conditioned embeddings, resulting in semantic instability and insufficient shift diversity, which limit generalization to realistic OOD. To address these challenges, we propose GOOD, a novel and flexible framework that directly guides diffusion sampling trajectories towards OOD regions using off-the-shelf in-distribution (ID) classifiers. GOOD incorporates dual-level guidance: (1) Image-level guidance based on the gradient of log partition to reduce input likelihood, drives samples toward low-density regions in pixel space. (2) Feature-level guidance, derived from k-NN distance in the classifier's latent space, promotes sampling in feature-sparse regions. Hence, this dual-guidance design enables more controllable and diverse OOD sample generation. Additionally, we introduce a unified OOD score that adaptively combines image and feature discrepancies, enhancing detection robustness. We perform thorough quantitative and qualitative analyses to evaluate the effectiveness of GOOD, demonstrating that training with samples generated by GOOD can notably enhance OOD detection performance.
中摘: 最近的研究探索了用于生成离分布（OOD）样本的文本到图像扩散模型，显著提升了OOD检测的表现。然而，现有方法通常依赖于扰动文本条件嵌入，这导致语义不稳定性和特征变化多样性不足，从而限制了对现实世界OOD的泛化能力。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种名为GOOD的新颖且灵活的框架，该框架直接使用现成的在分布（ID）分类器来引导扩散采样轨迹进入OOD区域。GOOD结合了双层指导：（1）基于对数分区梯度的图像级指导，降低输入似然性，促使样本向像素空间中的低密度区域移动。（2）基于分类器潜在空间中k-NN距离的功能级指导，促进特征稀疏区域的采样。因此，这种双层指导设计能够实现更可控和多样化的OOD样本生成。此外，我们引入了一个统一的OOD评分，该评分能自适应地结合图像和特征差异，增强检测鲁棒性。我们进行了详尽的定量和定性分析，以评估GOOD的有效性，并证明使用GOOD生成的样本进行训练可以显著提升OOD检测性能。

[No.221] FerretNet: Efficient Synthetic Image Detection via Local Pixel Dependencies
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: FerretNet：通过局部像素依赖高效检测合成图像
类别: deep learning
作者: Shuqiao Liang;Jian Liu;Chen Renzhang;Quanlong Guan;
机构: ;Jinan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=JScOknY0Su
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20890
英摘: The increasing realism of synthetic images generated by advanced models such as VAEs, GANs, and LDMs poses significant challenges for synthetic image detection. To address this issue, we explore two artifact types introduced during the generation process: (1) latent distribution deviations and (2) decoding-induced smoothing effects, which manifest as inconsistencies in local textures, edges, and color transitions. Leveraging local pixel dependencies (LPD) properties rooted in Markov Random Fields, we reconstruct synthetic images using neighboring pixel information to expose disruptions in texture continuity and edge coherence. Building upon LPD, we propose FerretNet, a lightweight neural network with only 1.1M parameters that delivers efficient and robust synthetic image detection. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FerretNet, trained exclusively on the 4-class ProGAN dataset, achieves an average accuracy of 97.1% on an open-world benchmark comprising 22 generative models. Our code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/xigua7105/FerretNet.
中摘: 高级模型如VAEs、GANs和LDMs生成的合成图像日益逼真，这为合成图像检测带来了重大挑战。为应对这一问题，我们探索了生成过程中引入的两种缺陷类型：（1）潜在分布偏差和（2）解码引起的平滑效应，这些缺陷表现为局部纹理、边缘和颜色过渡的一致性问题。利用马尔可夫随机场（Markov Random Fields）中的局部像素依赖性（Local Pixel Dependencies, LPD）特性，我们通过利用邻近像素信息重构合成图像，以揭示纹理连续性和边缘一致性中断。基于LPD，我们提出了一种轻量级神经网络FerretNet，该网络仅有1.1M个参数，能够实现高效的稳健合成图像检测。广泛实验表明，仅在4类ProGAN数据集上训练的FerretNet，在包含22个生成模型的开放世界基准测试中，平均准确率达到97.1%。我们的代码和数据集已公开发布在https://github.com/xigua7105/FerretNet。

[No.222] Robust Hallucination Detection in LLMs via Adaptive Token Selection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过自适应词元选择在大语言模型中实现稳健的幻觉检测
类别: deep learning
作者: Mengjia Niu;Hamed Haddadi;Guansong Pang;
机构: Imperial College London;brave;Singapore Management University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=gOwqPdBlRB
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07863
英摘: Hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) pose significant safety concerns that impede their broader deployment. Recent research in hallucination detection has demonstrated that LLMs' internal representations contain truthfulness hints, which can be harnessed for detector training. However, the performance of these detectors is heavily dependent on the internal representations of predetermined tokens, fluctuating considerably when working on free-form generations with varying lengths and sparse distributions of hallucinated entities. To address this, we propose HaMI, a novel approach that enables robust detection of hallucinations through adaptive selection and learning of critical tokens that are most indicative of hallucinations. We achieve this robustness by an innovative formulation of the Hallucination detection task as Multiple Instance (HaMI) learning over token-level representations within a sequence, thereby facilitating a joint optimisation of token selection and hallucination detection on generation sequences of diverse forms. Comprehensive experimental results on four hallucination benchmarks show that HaMI significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）中的幻觉引发了重要的安全问题，阻碍了它们的广泛部署。近期在幻觉检测方面的研究显示，LLMs内部表示包含可信度提示，这些提示可以被用于训练检测器。然而，这些检测器的表现高度依赖于预设令牌的内部表示，在处理不同长度和幻觉实体稀疏分布的自由形式生成时，性能波动较大。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为HaMI的新方法，该方法能够通过自适应选择和学习最能指示幻觉的关键令牌来实现稳健的幻觉检测。我们通过将幻觉检测任务创新地表述为序列中令牌级表示的多重实例（HaMI）学习，从而实现了对各种形式生成序列的联合优化，以选择关键令牌并检测幻觉。在四个幻觉基准测试上的全面实验结果显示，HaMI显著优于现有的先进方法。

[No.224] Detecting Generated Images by Fitting Natural Image Distributions
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过拟合自然图像分布检测生成的图像
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Yonggang Zhang;Jun Nie;Xinmei Tian;Mingming Gong;Kun Zhang;Bo Han;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Hong Kong Baptist University;University of Science and Technology of China;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;University of Melbourne;Carnegie Mellon University;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=27xTIAFbc6
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01293
英摘: The increasing realism of generated images has raised significant concerns about their potential misuse, necessitating robust detection methods. Current approaches mainly rely on training binary classifiers, which depend heavily on the quantity and quality of available generated images. In this work, we propose a novel framework that exploits geometric differences between the data manifolds of natural and generated images. To exploit this difference, we employ a pair of functions engineered to yield consistent outputs for natural images but divergent outputs for generated ones, leveraging the property that their gradients reside in mutually orthogonal subspaces. This design enables a simple yet effective detection method: an image is identified as generated if a transformation along its data manifold induces a significant change in the loss value of a self-supervised model pre-trained on natural images. Further more, to address diminishing manifold disparities in advanced generative models, we leverage normalizing flows to amplify detectable differences by extruding generated images away from the natural image manifold. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficacy of this method. Code is available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/ConV.
中摘: 生成图像日益逼真的特点引发了对其潜在滥用的关注，这需要我们采用稳健的检测方法。当前的方法主要依赖于训练二元分类器，这些分类器对可用生成图像的数量和质量有着很高的依赖性。在本工作中，我们提出了一种新颖的框架，该框架利用自然图像和生成图像数据流形之间的几何差异。为了利用这种差异，我们采用了一对函数，这些函数能够为自然图像提供一致的输出，但对生成图像则产生不同的输出，利用其梯度位于彼此正交子空间中的特性。这种设计使得一种简单而有效的检测方法得以实现：如果对图像在其数据流形上的变换导致自监督模型（该模型已在自然图像上预训练）的损失值发生显著变化，则该图像被识别为生成图像。此外，为了应对高级生成模型中数据流形差异逐渐减小的问题，我们利用标准化流动来通过将生成图像从自然图像流形中拉出，增强可检测的差异。大量实验表明了该方法的有效性。相关代码可在https://github.com/tmlr-group/ConV获取。

[No.226] TopoPoint: Enhance Topology Reasoning via Endpoint Detection in Autonomous Driving
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 拓扑点：通过自主驾驶中的端点检测增强拓扑推理
类别: deep learning
作者: Yanping Fu;Xinyuan Liu;Tianyu Li;Yike Ma;Yucheng Zhang;Feng Dai;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;Fudan University;Shanghai AI Laboratory;Institute of Computing Technology, Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=C2fJE8t0lH
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17771
英摘: Topology reasoning, which unifies perception and structured reasoning, plays a vital role in understanding intersections for autonomous driving. However, its performance heavily relies on the accuracy of lane detection, particularly at connected lane endpoints. Existing methods often suffer from lane endpoints deviation, leading to incorrect topology construction. To address this issue, we propose TopoPoint, a novel framework that explicitly detects lane endpoints and jointly reasons over endpoints and lanes for robust topology reasoning. During training, we independently initialize point and lane query, and proposed Point-Lane Merge Self-Attention to enhance global context sharing through incorporating geometric distances between points and lanes as an attention mask . We further design Point-Lane Graph Convolutional Network to enable mutual feature aggregation between point and lane query. During inference, we introduce Point-Lane Geometry Matching algorithm that computes distances between detected points and lanes to refine lane endpoints, effectively mitigating endpoint deviation. Extensive experiments on the OpenLane-V2 benchmark demonstrate that TopoPoint achieves state-of-the-art performance in topology reasoning (48.8 on OLS). Additionally, we propose DET$_p$ to evaluate endpoint detection, under which our method significantly outperforms existing approaches (52.6 v.s. 45.2 on DET$_p$). The code is released at https://github.com/Franpin/TopoPoint.
中摘: 拓扑推理，它将感知和结构化推理统一起来，在自动驾驶中理解交叉口方面发挥着至关重要的作用。然而，其性能高度依赖于车道检测的准确性，尤其是在连接车道的端点处。现有方法往往在车道端点检测方面存在偏差，导致拓扑结构构建不准确。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为TopoPoint的新框架，该框架明确检测车道端点，并通过结合点与车道之间的几何距离作为注意力掩码来增强端点和车道之间的联合推理，从而实现稳健的拓扑推理。在训练过程中，我们独立初始化点查询和车道查询，并提出了“点-车道合并自注意力”机制，以增强全局上下文共享。此外，我们设计了点-车道图卷积网络，使点查询和车道查询之间能够相互聚合特征。在推理阶段，我们引入了点-车道几何匹配算法，该算法计算检测到的点与车道之间的距离，以精炼车道端点，有效减轻端点偏差。在OpenLane-V2基准上的广泛实验表明，TopoPoint在拓扑推理方面达到了最先进的性能（OLS得分为48.8）。此外，我们还提出了一种DET$_p$方法来评估端点检测，我们的方法在DET$_p$上显著优于现有方法（DET$_p$得分为52.6，而现有方法得分为45.2）。代码已发布在https://github.com/Franpin/TopoPoint。

[No.227] Automated Detection of Visual Attribute Reliance with a Self-Reflective Agent
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 自动检测视觉属性依赖性的一种自我反思代理方法
类别: evaluation
作者: Christy Li;Josep Lopez Camuñas;Jake Thomas Touchet;Jacob Andreas;Agata Lapedriza;Antonio Torralba;Tamar Rott Shaham;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;;Louisiana Tech University;Northeastern University;Universitat Oberta de Catalunya;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=LyH2ISbOV8
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21704
英摘: When a vision model performs image recognition, which visual attributes drive its predictions? Detecting unintended reliance on specific visual features is critical for ensuring model robustness, preventing overfitting, and avoiding spurious correlations. We introduce an automated framework for detecting such dependencies in trained vision models. At the core of our method is a self-reflective agent that systematically generates and tests hypotheses about visual attributes that a model may rely on. This process is iterative: the agent refines its hypotheses based on experimental outcomes and uses a self-evaluation protocol to assess whether its findings accurately explain model behavior. When inconsistencies arise, the agent self-reflects over its findings and triggers a new cycle of experimentation. We evaluate our approach on a novel benchmark of 130 models designed to exhibit diverse visual attribute dependencies across 18 categories. Our results show that the agent's performance consistently improves with self-reflection, with a significant performance increase over non-reflective baselines. We further demonstrate that the agent identifies real-world visual attribute dependencies in state-of-the-art models, including CLIP's vision encoder and the YOLOv8 object detector.
中摘: 当视觉模型进行图像识别时，哪些视觉属性驱动其预测？检测模型对特定视觉特征的非预期依赖对于确保模型的稳健性、防止过拟合以及避免虚假相关性至关重要。我们介绍了一种自动化的框架来检测训练好的视觉模型中的此类依赖关系。我们的方法的核心是一个自我反思代理，它系统地生成并测试模型可能依赖的视觉属性假设。这一过程是迭代的：代理根据实验结果不断细化其假设，并使用自我评估协议来评估其发现是否准确解释了模型行为。当出现不一致时，代理会对其发现进行自我反思，并触发新一轮的实验。我们在一个包含130个模型的新颖基准上评估了我们的方法，这些模型旨在展示在18个类别中的多样化视觉属性依赖关系。结果显示，随着自我反思的进行，代理的表现持续提升，与无自我反思的基础模型相比有显著的性能增强。此外，我们还证明了代理能够识别最先进的模型中的真实世界视觉属性依赖关系，包括CLIP的视觉编码器和YOLOv8目标检测器。

[No.228] Reliably detecting model failures in deployment without labels
来源: NeurIPS 2025

作者: Viet Nguyen;Changjian Shui;Vijay Giri;Siddharth Arya;Amol Verma;Fahad Razak;Rahul Krishnan;

英摘: The distribution of data changes over time; models operating in dynamic environments need retraining. This paper formalizes and addresses the problem of post-deployment deterioration (PDD) monitoring. We propose D3M, a practical and efficient monitoring algorithm based on the disagreement of predictive models, achieving low false positive rates under non-deteriorating shifts and provides sample complexity bounds for high true positive rates under deteriorating shifts. 中摘: 数据分布会随时间变化；在动态环境中运行的模型需要重新训练。但是，在没有标签的情况下确定何时重新训练是一个开放性的挑战，因为并非所有变化都会降低模型性能。本文正式化并解决了部署后性能退化（PDD）监控的问题。我们提出了一种名为D3M的实用且高效的监控算法，该算法基于预测模型之间的分歧，能够在非退化变化下实现低误报率，并为退化变化下高真阳性率提供了样本复杂度界。在标准基准数据集和一个实际的大规模内部医学数据集上的实证结果证明了该框架的有效性，并强调了其作为高风险机器学习管道警报机制的可行性。

[No.229] Fair Deepfake Detectors Can Generalize
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 公平的深度假信息检测器可以泛化
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Harry Cheng;Ming-Hui Liu;Yangyang Guo;Tianyi Wang;Liqiang Nie;Mohan Kankanhalli;
机构: National University of Singapore;Shandong University;;Harbin Institute of Technology;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=p27bSdc3FS
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02645
英摘: Deepfake detection models face two critical challenges: generalization to unseen manipulations and demographic fairness among population groups. However, existing approaches often demonstrate that these two objectives are inherently conflicting, revealing a trade-off between them. In this paper, we, for the first time, uncover and formally define a causal relationship between fairness and generalization. Building on the back-door adjustment, we show that controlling for confounders (data distribution and model capacity) enables improved generalization via fairness interventions. Motivated by this insight, we propose Demographic Attribute-insensitive Intervention Detection (DAID), a plug-and-play framework composed of: i) Demographic-aware data rebalancing, which employs inverse-propensity weighting and subgroup-wise feature normalization to neutralize distributional biases; and ii) Demographic-agnostic feature aggregation, which uses a novel alignment loss to suppress sensitive-attribute signals. Across three cross-domain benchmarks, DAID consistently achieves superior performance in both fairness and generalization compared to several state-of-the-art detectors, validating both its theoretical foundation and practical effectiveness.
中摘: 深度伪造检测模型面临两大关键挑战：对未见过的篡改行为的泛化能力和不同人群组之间的公平性。然而，现有方法往往表明这两个目标是固有冲突的，揭示了它们之间的权衡关系。在本文中，我们首次揭示并正式定义了公平性和泛化能力之间的因果关系。基于后门调整的方法，我们证明通过控制混杂因素（数据分布和模型容量），可以借助公平性干预措施提高泛化能力。受这一洞察的启发，我们提出了Demographic Attribute-insensitive Intervention Detection（DAID）框架，该框架由两部分组成：i) 民族属性感知的数据重平衡，采用逆倾向加权和子组特征归一化来消除分布偏差；ii) 民族属性无关的特征聚合，使用一种新型对齐损失来抑制敏感属性信号。在三个跨领域基准测试中，DAID在公平性和泛化能力方面均表现出优于多种先进检测器的性能，验证了其理论基础和实际有效性。

[No.231] Epistemic Uncertainty for Generated Image Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 生成图像检测中的知识不确定性
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Jun Nie;Yonggang Zhang;Tongliang Liu;Yiu-ming Cheung;Bo Han;Xinmei Tian;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Hong Kong Baptist University;University of Sydney;RIKEN;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=5SqbLPaBww
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05897
英摘: We introduce a novel framework for AI-generated image detection through epistemic uncertainty, aiming to address critical security concerns in the era of generative models. Our key insight stems from the observation that distributional discrepancies between training and testing data manifest distinctively in the epistemic uncertainty space of machine learning models. In this context, the distribution shift between natural and generated images leads to elevated epistemic uncertainty in models trained on natural images when evaluating generated ones. Hence, we exploit this phenomenon by using epistemic uncertainty as a proxy for detecting generated images. This converts the challenge of generated image detection into the problem of uncertainty estimation, underscoring the generalization performance of the model used for uncertainty estimation. Fortunately, advanced large-scale vision models pre-trained on extensive natural images have shown excellent generalization performance for various scenarios. Thus, we utilize these pre-trained models to estimate the epistemic uncertainty of images and flag those with high uncertainty as generated. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficacy of our method. Code is available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/WePe.
中摘: 我们提出了一种新的框架，通过表征不确定性来检测AI生成的图像，旨在解决生成模型时代的关键安全问题。我们的核心洞察来自于这样一个观察：训练数据和测试数据之间的分布差异在机器学习模型的表征不确定性空间中表现得尤为明显。在这种情况下，自然图像和生成图像之间的分布偏移导致了在使用自然图像训练的模型中评估生成图像时表征不确定性增加。因此，我们利用这一现象，通过将表征不确定性作为检测生成图像的代理来进行这种探索。这将生成图像检测的挑战转化为不确定性估计的问题，并突显了用于不确定性估计的模型的一般化性能。幸运的是，预先在大量自然图像上训练的先进大规模视觉模型在各种场景下都表现出色。因此，我们利用这些预训练模型来估计图像的表征不确定性，并将具有高不确定性的图像标记为生成图像。大量的实验表明了我们方法的有效性。代码可在https://github.com/tmlr-group/WePe获取。

英摘: We propose the task of knowledge distillation detection, which aims to determine whether a student model has been distilled from a given teacher, under a practical setting where only the student's weights and the teacher's API are available. This problem is motivated by growing concerns about model provenance and unauthorized replication through distillation. To address this task, we introduce a model-agnostic framework that combines data-free input synthesis and statistical score computation for detecting distillation. [No.233] Looking Beyond the Known: Towards a Data Discovery Guided Open-World Object Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 超越已知领域：面向数据发现的开放世界目标检测
类别: applications
作者: Anay Majee;Amitesh Gangrade;Rishabh K Iyer;
机构: University of Texas at Dallas;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=2tqcROH3iO
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00303
英摘: Open-World Object Detection (OWOD) enriches traditional object detectors by enabling continual discovery and integration of unknown objects via human guidance. However, existing OWOD approaches frequently suffer from semantic confusion between known and unknown classes, alongside catastrophic forgetting, leading to diminished unknown recall and degraded known-class accuracy. To overcome these challenges, we propose Combinatorial Open-World Detection (CROWD), a unified framework reformulating unknown object discovery and adaptation as an interwoven combinatorial (set-based) data-discovery (CROWD-Discover) and representation learning (CROWD-Learn) task. CROWD-Discover strategically mines unknown instances by maximizing Submodular Conditional Gain (SCG) functions, selecting representative examples distinctly dissimilar from known objects. Subsequently, CROWD-Learn employs novel combinatorial objectives that jointly disentangle known and unknown representations while maintaining discriminative coherence among known classes, thus mitigating confusion and forgetting. Extensive evaluations on OWOD benchmarks illustrate that CROWD achieves improvements of 2.83% and 2.05% in known-class accuracy on M-OWODB and S-OWODB, respectively, and nearly 2.4x unknown recall compared to leading baselines.
中摘: 开放世界对象检测（OWOD）通过在传统对象检测器中引入人类指导下的持续发现和整合未知对象的能力，丰富了传统的对象检测方法。然而，现有的OWOD方法经常面临已知类和未知类之间的语义混淆，以及灾难性遗忘的问题，导致未知对象召回率降低和已知类准确性下降。为克服这些挑战，我们提出了一种统一框架——组合开放世界检测（CROWD），将未知对象的发现和适应重新定义为交织的组合（集基）数据发现任务（CROWD-Discover）和表示学习任务（CROWD-Learn）。CROWD-Discover通过最大化子模态条件收益（SCG）函数，战略性地挖掘与已知对象明显不同的代表性样本。随后，CROWD-Learn采用新的组合目标，同时解耦已知和未知的表示，保持已知类之间的判别一致性，从而减轻了混淆和遗忘的问题。在OWOD基准测试上的广泛评估表明，CROWD分别在M-OWODB和S-OWODB上将已知类准确性提高了2.83%和2.05%，并且其未知对象召回率几乎提高了2.4倍，相比领先的基线方法取得了显著提升。

英摘: Monitoring is an important aspect of safely deploying Large Language Models (LLMs). [No.235] PANDA: Towards Generalist Video Anomaly Detection via Agentic AI Engineer

作者: Zhiwei Yang;Chen Gao;Mike Zheng Shou;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26386
英摘: Video anomaly detection (VAD) is a critical yet challenging task due to the complex and diverse nature of real-world scenarios. Previous methods typically rely on domain-specific training data and manual adjustments when applying to new scenarios and unseen anomaly types, suffering from high labor costs and limited generalization. Therefore, we aim to achieve generalist VAD, \ie, automatically handle any scene and any anomaly types without training data or human involvement. In this work, we propose PANDA, an agentic AI engineer based on MLLMs. Specifically, we achieve PANDA by comprehensively devising four key capabilities: (1) self-adaptive scene-aware strategy planning, (2) goal-driven heuristic reasoning, (3) tool-augmented self-reflection, and (4) self-improving chain-of-memory. Concretely, we develop a self-adaptive scene-aware RAG mechanism, enabling PANDA to retrieve anomaly-specific knowledge for anomaly detection strategy planning. Next, we introduce a latent anomaly-guided heuristic prompt strategy to enhance reasoning precision. Furthermore, PANDA employs a progressive reflection mechanism alongside a suite of context-aware tools to iteratively refine decision-making in complex scenarios. Finally, a chain-of-memory mechanism enables PANDA to leverage historical experiences for continual performance improvement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PANDA achieves state-of-the-art performance in multi-scenario, open-set, and complex scenario settings without training and manual involvement, validating its generalizable and robust anomaly detection capability. Code is released at https://github.com/showlab/PANDA.
中摘: 视频异常检测（VAD）是一项关键但具有挑战性的任务，原因在于现实世界场景的复杂多样。以往的方法通常依赖于特定领域的训练数据，并在应用于新场景和未见过的异常类型时需要手动调整，这导致了高劳动成本和有限的泛化能力。因此，我们旨在实现通用型VAD，即无需训练数据或人工干预即可自动处理任何场景和任何异常类型的任务。在本文中，我们提出了PANDA，这是一种基于MLLMs的代理AI工程师。具体而言，我们通过全面设计四种关键能力来实现PANDA：（1）自适应场景感知策略规划，（2）目标驱动的经验启发式推理，（3）工具增强的自我反思，以及（4）自我提升的记忆链机制。具体来说，我们开发了一种自适应场景感知的RAG机制，使PANDA能够检索异常特定的知识以进行异常检测策略规划。接下来，我们引入了一种潜在异常引导的经验启发式提示策略，以提高推理精度。此外，PANDA采用了一种渐进式的自我反思机制，并结合了一系列上下文感知的工具，以在复杂场景中逐步细化决策过程。最后，记忆链机制使PANDA能够利用历史经验持续提升性能。广泛的实验表明，PANDA在多场景、开放集和复杂场景设置中无需训练和人工干预即可达到最先进的性能，验证了其可泛化且稳健的异常检测能力。代码已发布在https://github.com/showlab/PANDA。

[No.238] Revisiting Logit Distributions for Reliable Out-of-Distribution Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 重新审视逻辑分布以实现可靠的分布外检测
类别: general machine learning
作者: Jiachen Liang;RuiBing Hou;Minyang Hu;Hong Chang;Shiguang Shan;Xilin Chen;
机构: ;Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=FLdLPUqnsP
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20134
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is critical for ensuring the reliability of deep learning models in open-world applications. While post-hoc methods are favored for their efficiency and ease of deployment, existing approaches often underexploit the rich information embedded in the model's logits space. In this paper, we propose LogitGap, a novel post-hoc OOD detection method that explicitly exploits the relationship between the maximum logit and the remaining logits to enhance the separability between in-distribution (ID) and OOD samples. To further improve its effectiveness, we refine LogitGap by focusing on a more compact and informative subset of the logit space. Specifically, we introduce a training-free strategy that automatically identifies the most informative logits for scoring. We provide both theoretical analysis and empirical evidence to validate the effectiveness of our approach. Extensive experiments on both vision-language and vision-only models demonstrate that LogitGap consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse OOD detection scenarios and benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/GIT-LJc/LogitGap.
中摘: 开放世界应用中确保深度学习模型可靠性的关键在于离分布（Out-of-Distribution, OOD）检测。尽管事后方法因其高效性和易于部署而受到青睐，现有的方法往往未能充分利用模型logits空间中丰富的信息。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为LogitGap的新颖事后OOD检测方法，该方法明确地利用了最大logits与剩余logits之间的关系，以增强分布内（In-Distribution, ID）样本和OOD样本之间的可分性。为了进一步提高其有效性，我们通过关注logits空间的一个更紧凑且更具信息量的子集来细化LogitGap。具体而言，我们引入了一种无需训练的策略，该策略能够自动识别用于评分的最具信息量的logits。我们提供了理论分析和实验证据来验证我们方法的有效性。广泛的实验表明，LogitGap在多种OOD检测场景和基准测试中均能实现最先进的性能。相关代码可在https://github.com/GIT-LJc/LogitGap获取。

[No.239] ADPretrain: Advancing Industrial Anomaly Detection via Anomaly Representation Pretraining
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: ADPretrain: 通过异常表示预训练推进工业异常检测
类别: applications
作者: Xincheng Yao;Yan Luo;Zefeng Qian;Chongyang Zhang;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;Nanjing Agricultural University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=mHfpziOtTW
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05245
英摘: The current mainstream and state-of-the-art anomaly detection (AD) methods are substantially established on pretrained feature networks yielded by ImageNet pretraining. However, regardless of supervised or self-supervised pretraining, the pretraining process on ImageNet does not match the goal of anomaly detection (i.e., pretraining in natural images doesn't aim to distinguish between normal and abnormal). Moreover, natural images and industrial image data in AD scenarios typically have the distribution shift. The two issues can cause ImageNet-pretrained features to be suboptimal for AD tasks. To further promote the development of the AD field, pretrained representations specially for AD tasks are eager and very valuable. To this end, we propose a novel AD representation learning framework specially designed for learning robust and discriminative pretrained representations for industrial anomaly detection. Specifically, closely surrounding the goal of anomaly detection (i.e., focus on discrepancies between normals and anomalies), we propose angle- and norm-oriented contrastive losses to maximize the angle size and norm difference between normal and abnormal features simultaneously. To avoid the distribution shift from natural images to AD images, our pretraining is performed on a large-scale AD dataset, RealIAD. To further alleviate the potential shift between pretraining data and downstream AD datasets, we learn the pretrained AD representations based on the class-generalizable representation, residual features. For evaluation, based on five embedding-based AD methods, we simply replace their original features with our pretrained representations. Extensive experiments on five AD datasets and five backbones consistently show the superiority of our pretrained features. The code is available at https://github.com/xcyao00/ADPretrain.
中摘: 当前主流和最先进的异常检测（AD）方法主要建立在通过ImageNet预训练得到的特征网络之上。然而，无论是在监督学习还是自我监督学习下进行的预训练过程，都不符合异常检测的目标（即，自然图像的预训练并不旨在区分正常与异常）。此外，在工业场景下的异常检测中，自然图像和工业图像数据通常具有分布偏移。这两个问题可能导致基于ImageNet的预训练特征在异常检测任务中表现不佳。为了进一步促进异常检测领域的进步，专门针对异常检测任务的预训练表示变得尤为迫切且宝贵。为此，我们提出了一种新的AD表示学习框架，专门设计用于学习鲁棒且区分度高的预训练表示，以适应工业异常检测需求。具体而言，我们的框架紧密围绕异常检测的目标（即，专注于正常样本与异常样本之间的差异），提出了角度和范数导向的对比损失，同时最大化正常和异常特征之间的角度差和范数差。为避免从自然图像到异常检测图像的分布偏移，我们在一个大规模的AD数据集RealIAD上进行了预训练。为了进一步缓解预训练数据与下游AD数据集之间的潜在分布偏移，我们基于可泛化的类表示和残差特征来学习预训练的AD表示。在评估中，我们基于五种嵌入式AD方法，简单地将它们的原始特征替换为我们预训练的表示。在五个AD数据集和五个骨干网络上的广泛实验一致显示了我们预训练特征的优越性。代码可在https://github.com/xcyao00/ADPretrain获取。

[No.240] CQ-DINO: Mitigating Gradient Dilution via Category Queries for Vast Vocabulary Object Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: CQ-DINO：通过类别查询减轻梯度稀释的大词汇量目标检测
类别: applications
作者: Zhichao Sun;Huazhang Hu;Yidong Ma;Gang Liu;Nemo Chen;Xu Tang;Yao Hu;Yongchao Xu;
机构: Wuhan University;ShanghaiTech University;Xiaohongshu;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=evTXNxaH0f
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18430
英摘: With the exponential growth of data, traditional object detection methods are increasingly struggling to handle vast vocabulary object detection tasks effectively. We analyze two key limitations of classification-based detectors: positive gradient dilution, where rare positive categories receive insufficient learning signals, and hard negative gradient dilution, where discriminative gradients are overwhelmed by numerous easy negatives. To address these challenges, we propose CQ-DINO, a category query-based object detection framework that reformulates classification as a contrastive task between object queries and learnable category queries. Our method introduces image-guided query selection, which reduces the negative space by adaptively retrieving top-K relevant categories per image via cross-attention, thereby rebalancing gradient distributions and facilitating implicit hard example mining. Furthermore, CQ-DINO flexibly integrates explicit hierarchical category relationships in structured datasets (e.g., V3Det) or learns implicit category correlations via self-attention in generic datasets (e.g., COCO). Experiments demonstrate that CQ-DINO achieves superior performance on the challenging V3Det benchmark (surpassing previous methods by 2.1% AP) while maintaining competitiveness in COCO. Our work provides a scalable solution for real-world detection systems requiring wide category coverage. The code is publicly at https://github.com/FireRedTeam/CQ-DINO.
中摘: 随着数据呈指数级增长，传统的对象检测方法在处理庞大的词汇量对象检测任务时越来越力不从心。我们分析了基于分类的对象检测器的两个关键局限性：正梯度稀释，其中罕见的正类别接收到的学习信号不足；以及难以梯度稀释，其中区分性梯度被大量的简单负类所淹没。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了CQ-DINO，一种基于类别查询的对象检测框架，将分类重新定义为对象查询与可学习类别查询之间的对比任务。我们的方法引入了图像引导的查询选择，通过交叉注意力机制适当地检索每个图像的前K个相关类别来减少负空间，从而重新平衡梯度分布并促进隐式的困难样本挖掘。此外，CQ-DINO可以根据结构化数据集（例如V3Det）中的显式层次类别关系灵活集成，或通过自我注意力机制在通用数据集（例如COCO）中学习隐式的类别关联。实验结果显示，CQ-DINO在具有挑战性的V3Det基准测试中实现了卓越的性能（超越了之前的方法2.1%的AP），同时在COCO上保持了竞争力。我们的工作提供了一种针对需要广泛类别覆盖的实际检测系统的大规模解决方案。代码已公开发布在https://github.com/FireRedTeam/CQ-DINO。

来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: Text-to-SQL systems translate natural language (NL) questions into SQL queries, enabling non-technical users to interact with structured data. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promising results on the text-to-SQL task, they often produce semantically incorrect yet syntactically valid queries, with limited insight into their reliability. We propose SQLens, an end-to-end framework for fine-grained detection and correction of semantic errors in LLM-generated SQL. SQLens integrates error signals from both the underlying database and the LLM to identify potential semantic errors within SQL clauses. It further leverages these signals to guide query correction. [No.242] Towards Single-Source Domain Generalized Object Detection via Causal Visual Prompts
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过因果视觉提示实现单一来源领域泛化物体检测
类别: applications
作者: Chen Li;Huiying Xu;Changxin Gao;Zeyu Wang;Yun Liu;Xinzhong Zhu;
机构: ;Zhejiang Normal University;Huazhong University of Science and Technology;Northwest Polytechnical University;Nankai University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19487
英摘: Single-source Domain Generalized Object Detection (SDGOD), as a cutting-edge research topic in computer vision, aims to enhance model generalization capability in unseen target domains through single-source domain training. Current mainstream approaches attempt to mitigate domain discrepancies via data augmentation techniques. However, due to domain shift and limited domain-specific knowledge, models tend to fall into the pitfall of spurious correlations. This manifests as the model's over-reliance on simplistic classification features (e.g., color) rather than essential domain-invariant representations like object contours. To address this critical challenge, we propose the Cauvis (Causal Visual Prompts) method. First, we introduce a Cross-Attention Prompts module that mitigates bias from spurious features by integrating visual prompts with cross-attention. To address the inadequate domain knowledge coverage and spurious feature entanglement in visual prompts for single-domain generalization, we propose a dual-branch adapter that disentangles causal-spurious features while achieving domain adaptation via high-frequency feature extraction. Cauvis achieves state-of-the-art performance with 15.9-31.4% gains over existing domain generalization methods on SDGOD datasets, while exhibiting significant robustness advantages in complex interference environments.
中摘: 单源领域泛化对象检测（SDGOD），作为计算机视觉领域的前沿研究课题，旨在通过单一来源领域训练来增强模型在未见过的目标领域中的泛化能力。当前主流方法试图通过数据增强技术减轻领域差异。然而，由于领域偏移和有限的领域特定知识，模型往往陷入虚假相关性的陷阱。这表现为模型过度依赖简单的视觉分类特征（例如颜色）而非关键的领域不变表示（如物体轮廓）。为应对这一关键挑战，我们提出了因果视觉提示（Cauvis）方法。首先，我们引入了一个交叉注意力提示模块，通过将视觉提示与交叉注意力相结合来减轻来自虚假特征的偏差。为了应对单一领域泛化中视觉提示不足的领域知识覆盖范围以及虚假特征纠缠的问题，我们提出了一种双分支适配器，在实现高频率特征提取的同时，分离因果性虚假特征并完成领域适应。Cauvis 在 SDGOD 数据集上实现了最先进的性能，相比现有领域泛化方法取得了 15.9% 至 31.4% 的提升，并且在复杂干扰环境中表现出显著的鲁棒性优势。

[No.243] VLForgery Face Triad: Detection, Localization and Attribution via Multimodal Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: VLF造谣人脸三联体：通过多模态大语言模型进行检测、定位和溯源
类别: applications
作者: Xinan He;Yue Zhou;Bing Fan;Bin Li;Guopu Zhu;Feng Ding;
机构: Nanchang University;Shenzhen University;University of North Texas;Harbin Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06142
英摘: Faces synthesized by diffusion models (DMs) with high-quality and controllable attributes pose a significant challenge for Deepfake detection. Most state-of-the-art detectors only yield a binary decision, incapable of forgery localization, attribution of forgery methods, and providing analysis on the cause of forgeries. In this work, we integrate Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) within DM-based face forensics, and propose a fine-grained analysis triad framework called VLForgery, that can 1) predict falsified facial images; 2) locate the falsified face regions subjected to partial synthesis; and 3) attribute the synthesis with specific generators. To achieve the above goals, we introduce VLF (Visual Language Forensics), a novel and diverse synthesis face dataset designed to facilitate rich interactions between Visual and Language modalities in MLLMs. Additionally, we propose an extrinsic knowledge-guided description method, termed EkCot, which leverages knowledge from the image generation pipeline to enable MLLMs to quickly capture image content. Furthermore, we introduce a low-level vision comparison pipeline designed to identify differential features between real and fake that MLLMs can inherently understand. These features are then incorporated into EkCot, enhancing its ability to analyze forgeries in a structured manner, following the sequence of detection, localization, and attribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VLForgery outperforms other state-of-the-art forensic approaches in detection accuracy, with additional potential for falsified region localization and attribution analysis.
中摘: 由基于扩散模型（DMs）合成的高质量且可控属性人脸图像对Deepfake检测构成了重大挑战。现有的大多数先进检测器只能提供二元决策，无法进行伪造定位、伪造方法归因以及分析伪造的原因。在本工作中，我们将多模态大型语言模型（MLLMs）整合到基于DM的人脸forensic中，并提出了一种细粒度分析三部曲框架VLForgery，该框架能够1）预测虚假的人脸图像；2）定位部分合成的人脸区域；3）通过特定生成器归因合成。为了实现上述目标，我们引入了VLF（视觉语言forensics），这是一个新颖且多样化的合成人脸数据集，旨在促进MLLMs在视觉和语言模态之间的丰富交互。此外，我们提出了一个外在知识引导的描述方法，称为EkCot，该方法利用图像生成管道的知识，使MLLMs能够快速捕捉图像内容。同时，我们引入了一个低级视觉比较流水线，旨在识别真实和虚假图像之间的差异特征，这些特征MLLMs可以自然理解。然后将这些特征整合到EkCot中，增强其结构化分析伪造的能力，遵循检测、定位和归因的顺序。大量实验表明，VLForgery在检测准确性方面优于其他最先进的forensic方法，并且具有额外的潜在能力，用于虚假区域的定位和归因分析。

[No.244] UniSite: The First Cross-Structure Dataset and Learning Framework for End-to-End Ligand Binding Site Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: UniSite：首个端到端配体结合位点检测跨结构数据集及学习框架
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Jigang Fan;QuanLin Wu;Shengjie Luo;Liwei Wang;
机构: Stanford University;Princeton University;Peking University;ByteDance;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=APXcX7z1Bi
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03237
英摘: The detection of ligand binding sites for proteins is a fundamental step in Structure-Based Drug Design. Despite notable advances in recent years, existing methods, datasets, and evaluation metrics are confronted with several key challenges: (1) current datasets and methods are centered on individual protein-ligand complexes and neglect that diverse binding sites may exist across multiple complexes of the same protein, introducing significant statistical bias; (2) ligand binding site detection is typically modeled as a discontinuous workflow, employing binary segmentation and subsequent clustering algorithms; (3) traditional evaluation metrics do not adequately reflect the actual performance of different binding site prediction methods. To address these issues, we first introduce UniSite-DS, the first UniProt (Unique Protein)-centric ligand binding site dataset, which contains 4.81 times more multi-site data and 2.08 times more overall data compared to the previously most widely used datasets. We then propose UniSite, the first end-to-end ligand binding site detection framework supervised by set prediction loss with bijective matching. In addition, we introduce Average Precision based on Intersection over Union (IoU) as a more accurate evaluation metric for ligand binding site prediction. Extensive experiments on UniSite-DS and several representative benchmark datasets demonstrate that IoU-based Average Precision provides a more accurate reflection of prediction quality, and that UniSite outperforms current state-of-the-art methods in ligand binding site detection. The dataset and codes will be made publicly available at https://github.com/quanlin-wu/unisite.
中摘: 蛋白质配体结合位点的检测是基于结构的药物设计中的一个基本步骤。尽管近年来取得了显著进展，现有的方法、数据集和评估指标仍然面临几个关键挑战：（1）当前的数据集和方法主要集中在单个蛋白质-配体复合物上，忽略了同一蛋白质的不同复合物可能存在多种不同的结合位点，从而引入了显著的统计偏差；（2）配体结合位点的检测通常被视为一个不连续的工作流程，采用二元分割和后续聚类算法；（3）传统的评估指标未能充分反映不同结合位点预测方法的实际性能。为了解决这些问题，我们首先引入了UniSite-DS，这是首个以UniProt（唯一蛋白质）为中心的配体结合位点数据集，其包含的数据量是之前最广泛使用的数据集的4.81倍，并且总体数据量增加了2.08倍。然后，我们提出了UniSite，这是首个通过集合预测损失和双射匹配监督的端到端配体结合位点检测框架。此外，我们还引入了基于交并比（Intersection over Union，IoU）的平均精度作为更准确的配体结合位点预测评估指标。在UniSite-DS以及多个代表性基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，基于IoU的平均精度能更准确地反映预测质量，而UniSite在配体结合位点检测方面优于当前最先进的方法。该数据集和代码将在https://github.com/quanlin-wu/unisite公开提供。

[No.246] Registration is a Powerful Rotation-Invariance Learner for 3D Anomaly Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: 3D anomaly detection in point-cloud data is critical for industrial quality control, aiming to identify structural defects with high reliability. These limitations become more pronounced when registration fails, leading to unreliable detection results. We argue that point-cloud registration plays an essential role not only in aligning geometric structures but also in guiding feature extraction toward rotation-invariant and locally discriminative representations. Our key insight is that both tasks rely on modeling local geometric structures and leveraging feature similarity across samples. 中摘: 点云数据中的3D异常检测对于工业质量控制至关重要，旨在以高可靠性识别结构性缺陷。然而，当前基于记忆库的方法常常受到不一致特征变换和有限的判别能力的困扰，尤其是在捕捉局部几何细节和实现旋转不变性方面。当注册失败时，这些限制变得更加明显，导致检测结果不可靠。我们认为，点云注册不仅在对齐几何结构方面发挥着重要作用，也在指导特征提取向旋转不变性和局部判别性表示方向发展方面扮演关键角色。为此，我们提出了一种注册诱导的、旋转不变的特征提取框架，将点云注册和基于记忆库的异常检测目标结合起来。我们的核心见解是，这两个任务都依赖于建模局部几何结构并利用样本间的特征相似性。通过将特征提取嵌入到注册学习过程中，我们的框架可以同时优化对齐和表示学习。这种整合使得网络能够获得既对旋转具有鲁棒性又高度有效的异常检测特征。在Anomaly-ShapeNet和Real3D-AD数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法在有效性和泛化能力方面始终优于现有方法。

[No.251] Part-Aware Bottom-Up Group Reasoning for Fine-Grained Social Interaction Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于部分意识的自底向上群体推理用于细粒度社会互动检测

作者: Dongkeun Kim;Minsu Cho;Suha Kwak;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03666
英摘: Social interactions often emerge from subtle, fine-grained cues such as facial expressions, gaze, and gestures. However, existing methods for social interaction detection overlook such nuanced cues and primarily rely on holistic representations of individuals. Moreover, they directly detect social groups without explicitly modeling the underlying interactions between individuals. These drawbacks limit their ability to capture localized social signals and introduce ambiguity when group configurations should be inferred from social interactions grounded in nuanced cues. In this work, we propose a part-aware bottom-up group reasoning framework for fine-grained social interaction detection. The proposed method infers social groups and their interactions using body part features and their interpersonal relations. Our model first detects individuals and enhances their features using part-aware cues, and then infers group configuration by associating individuals via similarity-based reasoning, which considers not only spatial relations but also subtle social cues that signal interactions, leading to more accurate group inference. Experiments on the NVI dataset demonstrate that our method outperforms prior methods, achieving the new state of the art, while additional results on the Café dataset further validate its generalizability to group activity understanding.

[No.253] Breaking Latent Prior Bias in Detectors for Generalizable AIGC Image Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 破除检测器中的潜在先验偏见以实现可泛化的AIGC图像检测
类别: applications
作者: Yue Zhou;Xinan He;Kaiqing Lin;Bing Fan;Feng Ding;Bin Li;
机构: Shenzhen University;Nanchang University;University of North Texas;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=sVSVgWIkb0
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00874
英摘: Current AIGC detectors often achieve near-perfect accuracy on images produced by the same generator used for training but struggle to generalize to outputs from unseen generators. We trace this failure in part to latent prior bias: detectors learn shortcuts tied to patterns stemming from the initial noise vector rather than learning robust generative artifacts. To address this, we propose On-Manifold Adversarial Training (OMAT): by optimizing the initial latent noise of diffusion models under fixed conditioning, we generate on-manifold adversarial examples that remain on the generator's output manifold-unlike pixel-space attacks, which introduce off-manifold perturbations that the generator itself cannot reproduce and that can obscure the true discriminative artifacts. To test against state-of-the-art generative models, we introduce GenImage++, a test-only benchmark of outputs from advanced generators (Flux.1, SD3) with extended prompts and diverse styles. We apply our adversarial-training paradigm to ResNet50 and CLIP baselines and evaluate across existing AIGC forensic benchmarks and recent challenge datasets. Extensive experiments show that adversarially trained detectors significantly improve cross-generator performance without any network redesign. Our findings on latent-prior bias offer valuable insights for future dataset construction and detector evaluation, guiding the development of more robust and generalizable AIGC forensic methodologies.
中摘: 当前的AIGC检测器在由训练时使用的相同生成器产生的图像上往往能够达到近乎完美的准确性，但在处理来自未见过的生成器的输出时却难以泛化。我们部分归因于潜在先验偏差的原因：检测器学会了一些与初始噪声向量衍生出的模式相关的捷径，而不是学习稳健的生成特征。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新的方法——流形对抗训练（On-Manifold Adversarial Training, OMAT）：通过在固定条件下的优化扩散模型的初始潜在噪声，我们生成了流形上的对抗样本，这些样本仍然保持在生成器的输出流形上——不同于像素空间攻击，后者引入了生成器本身无法再现的离流形扰动，这些扰动可能会掩盖真正的判别特征。

为了测试最先进的生成模型，我们引入了GenImage++基准测试，这是一个仅用于测试高级生成器（Flux.1, SD3）输出的基准，这些输出具有扩展提示和多样化的风格。我们将我们的对抗训练范式应用于ResNet50和CLIP基线，并在现有的AIGC法医取证基准和最近的挑战数据集中进行了评估。广泛的实验表明，对抗训练的检测器可以在不进行任何网络设计更改的情况下显著提高跨生成器的表现。我们关于潜在先验偏差的研究发现提供了对未来数据集构建和检测器评估的重要见解，指导了更稳健和通用的AIGC法医取证方法的发展。

[No.254] Domain-RAG: Retrieval-Guided Compositional Image Generation for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 域-RAG：检索引导的跨域少量样本物体检测合成图像生成
类别: applications
作者: Yu Li;Xingyu Qiu;Yuqian Fu;Jie Chen;Tianwen Qian;Xu Zheng;Danda Pani Paudel;Yanwei Fu;Xuanjing Huang;Luc Van Gool;Yu-Gang Jiang;
机构: Fudan University;;East China Normal University;Institute for Computer Science, Artificial Intelligence and Technology;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Sofia Un. St. Kliment Ohridski;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=p2EycV4XOa
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05872
英摘: Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection (CD-FSOD) aims to detect novel objects with only a handful of labeled samples from previously unseen domains. While data augmentation and generative methods have shown promise in few-shot learning, their effectiveness for CD-FSOD remains unclear due to the need for both visual realism and domain alignment. Existing strategies, such as copy-paste augmentation and text-to-image generation, often fail to preserve the correct object category or produce backgrounds coherent with the target domain, making them non-trivial to apply directly to CD-FSOD. To address these challenges, we propose Domain-RAG, a training-free, retrieval-guided compositional image generation framework tailored for CD-FSOD. Domain-RAG consists of three stages: domain-aware background retrieval, domain-guided background generation, and foreground-background composition. Specifically, the input image is first decomposed into foreground and background regions. We then retrieve semantically and stylistically similar images to guide a generative model in synthesizing a new background, conditioned on both the original and retrieved contexts. Finally, the preserved foreground is composed with the newly generated domain-aligned background to form the generated image. Without requiring any additional supervision or training, Domain-RAG produces high-quality, domain-consistent samples across diverse tasks, including CD-FSOD, remote sensing FSOD, and camouflaged FSOD. Extensive experiments show consistent improvements over strong baselines and establish new state-of-the-art results. Codes will be released upon acceptance.
中摘: 跨域少样本对象检测（CD-FSOD）旨在仅使用以前未见过领域的少量标注样本来检测新颖的对象。虽然数据增强和生成方法在少样本学习中显示出潜力，但它们在CD-FSOD中的有效性仍然不清楚，因为需要同时保持视觉真实性和领域对齐。现有的策略，如复制粘贴增强和文本到图像生成，往往无法保留正确的对象类别或产生与目标领域一致的背景，使得它们直接应用于CD-FSOD并不容易。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种名为Domain-RAG的训练免费、检索引导的组合图像生成框架，专门针对CD-FSOD。Domain-RAG包括三个阶段：领域感知背景检索、领域引导背景生成以及前景背景合成。具体来说，输入图像首先被分解为前景和背景区域。然后，检索出语义和风格相似的图像以引导生成模型根据原始上下文和检索到的上下文合成新的背景。最后，保留的前景与新生成的领域对齐背景组合形成生成图像。无需任何额外的监督或训练，Domain-RAG能够在各种任务中生成高质量且领域一致的样本，包括CD-FSOD、遥感少样本对象检测（FSOD）和伪装少样本对象检测（FSOD）。广泛实验显示，Domain-RAG在多个基准上均表现出一致的改进，并建立了新的最先进的结果。在接受后，代码将被发布。

[No.255] VL-SAM-V2: Open-World Object Detection with General and Specific Query Fusion
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: VL-SAM-V2：具有通用查询和特定查询融合的开放世界目标检测
类别: applications
作者: Zhiwei Lin;Yongtao Wang;
机构: Peking University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=KoCytqC9gx
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18986
英摘: Current perception models have achieved remarkable success by leveraging large-scale labeled datasets, but still face challenges in open-world environments with novel objects. To address this limitation, researchers introduce open-set perception models to detect or segment arbitrary test-time user-input categories. However, open-set models rely on human involvement to provide predefined object categories as input during inference. More recently, researchers have framed a more realistic and challenging task known as open-ended perception that aims to discover unseen objects without requiring any category-level input from humans at inference time. Nevertheless, open-ended models suffer from low performance compared to open-set models. In this paper, we present VL-SAM-V2, an open-world object detection framework that is capable of discovering unseen objects while achieving favorable performance. To achieve this, we combine queries from open-set and open-ended models and propose a general and specific query fusion module to allow different queries to interact. By adjusting queries from open-set models, we enable VL-SAM-V2 to be evaluated in the open-set or open-ended mode. In addition, to learn more diverse queries, we introduce ranked learnable queries to match queries with proposals from open-ended models by sorting. Moreover, we design a denoising point training strategy to facilitate the training process. Experimental results on LVIS show that our method surpasses the previous open-set and open-ended methods, especially on rare objects.
中摘: 当前的感知模型通过利用大规模标注数据集取得了显著的成功，但在开放世界环境中面对新颖对象时仍面临挑战。为解决这一限制，研究人员引入了开放集感知模型来检测或分割任意测试时间用户输入的类别。然而，开放集模型在推理过程中依赖于人类提供预定义的对象类别作为输入。最近，研究人员将一个更为现实和具有挑战性的任务——开放生成感知——构架出来，其目标是在无需人类在推理时提供任何类别级别的输入的情况下发现未见过的对象。尽管如此，开放生成模型的性能仍然低于开放集模型。在本文中，我们提出了VL-SAM-V2，这是一种能够在发现未见过的对象的同时实现良好性能的开放世界物体检测框架。为了实现这一点，我们将开放集和开放生成模型的查询结合起来，并提出了一种通用和特定查询融合模块，以允许不同查询之间的交互。通过调整开放集模型的查询，我们使VL-SAM-V2能够在开放集或开放生成模式下进行评估。此外，为了学习更多样化的查询，我们引入了排序可学习查询，通过排序匹配开放生成模型的提议查询。同时，我们设计了一种去噪点训练策略来促进训练过程。在LVIS上的实验结果显示，我们的方法在稀有对象方面尤其超过了之前的开放集和开放生成方法。

来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: We propose BlockScan, a customized Transformer for anomaly detection in blockchain transactions. Unlike existing methods that rely on rule-based systems or directly apply off-the-shelf large language models (LLMs), BlockScan introduces a series of customized designs to effectively model the unique data structure of blockchain transactions. First, a blockchain transaction is multi-modal, containing blockchain-specific tokens, texts, and numbers. We design a novel modularized tokenizer to handle these multi-modal inputs, balancing the information across different modalities. Second, we design a customized masked language modeling mechanism for pretraining the Transformer architecture, incorporating RoPE embedding and FlashAttention for handling longer sequences. Finally, we design a novel anomaly detection method based on the model outputs. We further provide theoretical analysis for the detection method of our system. Extensive evaluations on Ethereum and Solana transactions demonstrate BlockScan's exceptional capability in anomaly detection while maintaining a low false positive rate. Remarkably, BlockScan is the only method that successfully detects anomalous transactions on Solana with high accuracy, whereas all other approaches achieved very low or zero detection recall scores. This work sets a new benchmark for applying Transformer-based approaches in blockchain data analysis.
中摘: 我们提出了一种针对区块链交易异常检测的定制Transformer——BlockScan。与依赖于规则系统或直接应用现成的大语言模型（LLMs）的方法不同，BlockScan 引入了一系列定制设计来有效建模区块链交易的独特数据结构。首先，一个区块链交易是多模态的，包含区块链特定的标记、文本和数字。我们设计了一种新颖的模块化分词器来处理这些多模态输入，平衡不同模态的信息。其次，我们为Transformer架构设计了一种定制化的掩码语言模型机制，结合了RoPE嵌入和FlashAttention来处理更长的序列。最后，我们基于模型输出设计了一种新颖的异常检测方法，并对系统的检测方法进行了理论分析。在以太坊和Solana交易上的广泛评估表明，BlockScan 在异常检测方面表现出色，同时保持了较低的误报率。特别地，BlockScan 是唯一一种在Solana上能够以高精度成功检测异常交易的方法，而其他所有方法的检测召回率都非常低或为零。这项工作为基于Transformer的方法在区块链数据分析中的应用设定了新的基准。

[No.262] Structured Temporal Causality for Interpretable Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 结构化时间因果关系在可解释的多变量时间序列异常检测中的应用
类别: deep learning
作者: Dongchan Cho;Jiho Han;Keumyeong Kang;Minsang Kim;Honggyu Ryu;Namsoon Jung;
机构: Simplatform Co. Ltd. Affiliate Research Institute;;SimPlatform Co. Ltd.;simplatform;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=V5kzCSeaXF
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16511
英摘: Real-world multivariate time series anomalies are rare and often unlabeled. Additionally, prevailing methods rely on increasingly complex architectures tuned to benchmarks, detecting only fragments of anomalous segments and overstating performance. In this paper, we introduce OracleAD, a simple and interpretable unsupervised framework for multivariate time series anomaly detection. OracleAD encodes each variable's past sequence into a single causal embedding to jointly predict the present time point and reconstruct the input window, effectively modeling temporal dynamics. These embeddings then undergo a self-attention mechanism to project them into a shared latent space and capture spatial relationships. These relationships are not static, since they are modeled by a property that emerges from each variable's temporal dynamics. The projected embeddings are aligned to a Stable Latent Structure (SLS) representing normal-state relationships. Anomalies are identified using a dual scoring mechanism based on prediction error and deviation from the SLS, enabling fine-grained anomaly diagnosis at each time point and across individual variables. Since any noticeable SLS deviation originates from embeddings that violate the learned temporal causality of normal data, OracleAD directly pinpoints the root-cause variables at the embedding level. OracleAD achieves state-of-the-art results across multiple real-world datasets and evaluation protocols, while remaining interpretable through SLS.
中摘: 现实世界中的多变量时间序列异常较为罕见且往往未被标记。此外，现有方法依赖于越来越复杂的架构，这些架构是为了基准而调整的，只能检测异常段落的碎片，并高估性能。在本文中，我们介绍了OracleAD，这是一种简单且可解释的无监督框架，用于多变量时间序列异常检测。OracleAD 将每个变量的过去序列编码为单一的因果嵌入，以联合预测当前时间点并重构输入窗口，有效地建模了时间动态。这些嵌入随后通过一个自我注意机制投影到共享的潜在空间中，以捕捉空间关系。这些关系并非静态的，因为它们是由每个变量的时间动态所体现的特性来建模的。投影后的嵌入与稳定潜在结构（SLS）对齐，后者代表正常状态下的关系。异常是通过基于预测误差和SLS偏差的双重评分机制来识别的，这使得OracleAD能够在每个时间点和各个变量上进行精细的异常诊断。由于任何显著的SLS偏差都源于违反了学习到的正常数据时间因果性的嵌入，OracleAD直接在嵌入级别指出了异常的根本原因变量。OracleAD在多个实际数据集和评估协议上取得了最先进的结果，同时通过SLS保持了可解释性。

[No.265] Multi-Agent Debate for LLM Judges with Adaptive Stability Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 面向LLM法官的多智能体辩论与自适应稳定性检测
类别: applications
作者: Tianyu Hu;Zhen Tan;Song Wang;Huaizhi Qu;Tianlong Chen;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Arizona State University;University of Central Florida;University of Virginia;University of North Carolina at Chapel Hill;University of North Carolina;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Vusd1Hw2D9
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12697
英摘: With advancements in reasoning capabilities, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly employed for automated judgment tasks. While LLMs-as-Judges offer promise in automating evaluations, current approaches often rely on simplistic aggregation methods (e.g., majority voting), which can fail even when individual agents provide correct answers. To address this, we propose a multi-agent debate judge framework where agents collaboratively reason and iteratively refine their responses. We formalize the debate process mathematically, analyzing agent interactions and proving that debate amplifies correctness compared to static ensembles. To enhance efficiency, we introduce a stability detection mechanism that models judge consensus dynamics via a time-varying Beta-Binomial mixture, with adaptive stopping based on distributional similarity (Kolmogorov-Smirnov test). This mechanism models the judges' collective correct rate dynamics using a time-varying mixture of Beta-Binomial distributions and employs an adaptive stopping criterion based on distributional similarity (Kolmogorov-Smirnov statistic). Experiments across multiple benchmarks and models demonstrate that our framework improves judgment accuracy over majority voting while maintaining computational efficiency.
中摘: 随着推理能力的进步，大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用于自动化判断任务。虽然LLMs-as-Judges在自动化评估方面具有潜力，但当前的方法往往依赖于简单的聚合方法（例如多数投票），即使个体代理提供的答案正确，这种方法也可能失效。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种多代理辩论裁判框架，其中代理们协作进行推理并迭代改进其响应。我们从数学上形式化了辩论过程，分析了代理之间的交互，并证明了辩论相较于静态集成能够增强正确性。为了提高效率，我们引入了一种稳定性检测机制，该机制通过时间变化的Beta-Binomial混合模型来建模裁判共识的动力学，并基于分布相似性（Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验）进行自适应停止。这种机制使用时间变化的Beta-Binomial分布混合模型来建模裁判集体正确率的动力学，并基于分布相似性（Kolmogorov-Smirnov统计量）采用自适应停止标准。我们在多个基准和模型上的实验表明，我们的框架在保持计算效率的同时，提高了判断准确性。

[No.267] S$^2$M-Former: Spiking Symmetric Mixing Branchformer for Brain Auditory Attention Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: S$^2$M-Former：基于脉冲对称混合分支器的脑听觉注意力检测模型
类别: neuroscience and cognitive science
作者: Jiaqi Wang;Zhengyu Ma;Xiongri Shen;Chenlin Zhou;Leilei Zhao;Han Zhang;Yi Zhong;Siqi Cai;Zhenxi Song;Zhiguo Zhang;
机构: Harbin Institute of Technology;;Peking University;Pengcheng Laboratory;Lanzhou University;National University of Singapore;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=WtMuGdHvh6
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05164
英摘: Auditory attention detection (AAD) aims to decode listeners' focus in complex auditory environments from electroencephalography (EEG) recordings, which is crucial for developing neuro-steered hearing devices. Despite recent advancements, EEG-based AAD remains hindered by the absence of synergistic frameworks that can fully leverage complementary EEG features under energy-efficiency constraints. We propose S$^2$M-Former, a novel spiking symmetric mixing framework to address this limitation through two key innovations: i) Presenting a spike-driven symmetric architecture composed of parallel spatial and frequency branches with mirrored modular design, leveraging biologically plausible token-channel mixers to enhance complementary learning across branches; ii) Introducing lightweight 1D token sequences to replace conventional 3D operations, reducing parameters by 14.7$\times$. The brain-inspired spiking architecture further reduces power consumption, achieving a 5.8$\times$ energy reduction compared to recent ANN methods, while also surpassing existing SNN baselines in terms of parameter efficiency and performance. Comprehensive experiments on three AAD benchmarks (KUL, DTU and AV-GC-AAD) across three settings (within-trial, cross-trial and cross-subject) demonstrate that S$^2$M-Former achieves comparable state-of-the-art (SOTA) decoding accuracy, making it a promising low-power, high-performance solution for AAD tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/JackieWang9811/S2M-Former.
中摘: 听觉注意检测（AAD）旨在从脑电图（EEG）记录中解码听众在复杂听觉环境中的注意力焦点，这对于开发神经控制的助听设备至关重要。尽管最近取得了进展，但基于EEG的AAD仍然受到缺乏能够充分利用在能量效率约束下的互补EEG特征的协同框架的限制。我们提出了一种新颖的脉冲对称混合框架S$^2$M-Former，通过两项关键创新来解决这一局限：i）采用一种由并行的空间和频率分支组成的脉冲驱动对称架构，这些分支具有镜像模块设计，利用生物上合理的令牌-通道混频器增强分支间的互补学习；ii）引入轻量级的一维令牌序列以替代传统的三维操作，参数减少了14.7倍。这种受大脑启发的脉冲架构进一步降低了功耗，与最近的神经网络方法相比，实现了5.8倍的能量减少，同时在参数效率和性能方面也超越了现有的SNN基线。在三个AAD基准测试（KUL、DTU和AV-GC-AAD）的三种设置（单次试验内、跨试验和跨被试者）下进行全面实验表明，S$^2$M-Former在解码准确性方面达到了可比的最新技术水平（SOTA），使其成为AAD任务中一个有前景的低功耗、高性能解决方案。代码可在https://github.com/JackieWang9811/S2M-Former获取。

[No.270] Structural Entropy Guided Agent for Detecting and Repairing Knowledge Deficiencies in LLMs
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: 结构熵引导代理用于检测和修复LLMs中的知识缺陷
类别: deep learning
作者: Yifan Wei;Xiaoyan Yu;Tengfei Pan;Angsheng Li;Li Du;
机构: ;Beijing Institute of Technology;Beijing Academy of Artificial Intelligence;Beihang University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=hTGqC1h8Ig
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07184
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved unprecedented performance by leveraging vast pretraining corpora, yet their performance remains suboptimal in knowledge-intensive domains such as medicine and scientific research, where high factual precision is required. While synthetic data provides a promising avenue for augmenting domain knowledge, existing methods frequently generate redundant samples that do not align with the model's true knowledge gaps. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel Structural Entropy-guided Knowledge Navigator (SENATOR) framework that addresses the intrinsic knowledge deficiencies of LLMs. Our approach employs the Structure Entropy (SE) metric to quantify uncertainty along knowledge graph paths and leverages Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to selectively explore regions where the model lacks domain-specific knowledge. Guided by these insights, the framework generates targeted synthetic data for supervised fine-tuning, enabling continuous self-improvement. Experimental results on LLaMA-3 and Qwen2 across multiple domain-specific benchmarks show that SENATOR effectively detects and repairs knowledge deficiencies, achieving notable performance improvements. The code and data for our methods and experiments are available at https://github.com/weiyifan1023/senator.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）通过利用庞大的预训练语料库实现了前所未有的性能，但在医学和科学研究等需要高事实精确度的知识密集型领域，其性能仍然不尽如人意。尽管合成数据为增强领域知识提供了前景广阔的途径，但现有方法经常生成与模型真实知识缺口不匹配的冗余样本。为了克服这一局限，我们提出了一种新的结构熵导向的知识导航器（SENATOR）框架，该框架旨在解决LLMs固有的知识缺陷。我们的方法采用结构熵（SE）指标来量化沿知识图谱路径的知识不确定性，并利用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）有选择地探索模型缺乏特定领域知识的区域。根据这些洞察，该框架生成针对性的合成数据以进行监督微调，从而实现持续的自我改进。在LLaMA-3和Qwen2上跨多个特定领域的基准测试中的实验结果表明，SENATOR能够有效检测并修复知识缺陷，实现显著的性能提升。我们的方法和实验代码可在https://github.com/weiyifan1023/senator获取。

[No.271] DevFD : Developmental Face Forgery Detection by Learning Shared and Orthogonal LoRA Subspaces
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: DevFD：通过学习共享和正交LoRA子空间进行发展性面部伪造检测
类别: applications
作者: Tianshuo Zhang;Li Gao;Siran Peng;Xiangyu Zhu;Zhen Lei;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;China Mobile Financial Technology Co., Ltd., China;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Xpuci4SV06
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19230
英摘: The rise of realistic digital face generation and manipulation poses significant social risks. The primary challenge lies in the rapid and diverse evolution of generation techniques, which often outstrip the detection capabilities of existing models. To defend against the ever-evolving new types of forgery, we need to enable our model to quickly adapt to new domains with limited computation and data while avoiding forgetting previously learned forgery types. In this work, we posit that genuine facial samples are abundant and relatively stable in acquisition methods, while forgery faces continuously evolve with the iteration of manipulation techniques. Given the practical infeasibility of exhaustively collecting all forgery variants, we frame face forgery detection as a continual learning problem and allow the model to develop as new forgery types emerge. Specifically, we employ a Developmental Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture that uses LoRA models as its individual experts. These experts are organized into two groups: a Real-LoRA to learn and refine knowledge of real faces, and multiple Fake-LoRAs to capture incremental information from different forgery types. To prevent catastrophic forgetting, we ensure that the learning direction of Fake-LoRAs is orthogonal to the established subspace. Moreover, we integrate orthogonal gradients into the orthogonal loss of Fake-LoRAs, preventing gradient interference throughout the training process of each task. Experimental results under both the datasets and manipulation types incremental protocols demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.
中摘: 真实的数字人脸生成和操控的兴起带来了重大的社会风险。主要挑战在于生成技术的快速和多样化演变，这些技术往往超越了现有检测模型的能力。为了防御不断演变的新类型伪造，我们需要让我们的模型在有限的计算能力和数据下迅速适应新的领域，同时避免忘记之前学习的伪造类型。在这项工作中，我们认为真实的人脸样本丰富且相对稳定，在获取方法上较为一致，而伪造的人脸则随着操控技术的迭代而持续演变。鉴于全面收集所有伪造变体的实际不可行性，我们将人脸伪造检测视为一个持续学习问题，并允许模型随着新伪造类型的出现而发展。具体而言，我们采用了Developmental Mixture of Experts（MoE）架构，其中使用LoRA模型作为个体专家。这些专家被分为两组：Real-LoRA用于学习和精炼真实人脸的知识，而多个Fake-LoRA则用于捕捉不同伪造类型中的增量信息。为了防止灾难性遗忘，我们确保Fake-LoRA的学习方向与已建立的子空间正交。此外，我们在Fake-LoRA的正交损失中整合了正交梯度，防止在每个任务的训练过程中梯度干扰。在数据集和操控类型增量协议下的实验结果表明了我们方法的有效性。

[No.272] Human Texts Are Outliers: Detecting LLM-generated Texts via Out-of-distribution Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: 人类文本是异常值：通过离群检测识别大语言模型生成的文本
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Cong Zeng;Shengkun Tang;Yuanzhou Chen;Zhiqiang Shen;Wenchao Yu;Xujiang Zhao;Haifeng Chen;Wei Cheng;zhiqiang xu;
机构: Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;;University of California, Los Angeles;NEC Labs America;NEC Laboratories;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=0XKZFK4hQt
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08602
英摘: The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT, DeepSeek, and Claude has significantly increased the presence of AI-generated text in digital communication. This trend has heightened the need for reliable detection methods to distinguish between human-authored and machine-generated content. Existing approaches both zero-shot methods and supervised classifiers largely conceptualize this task as a binary classification problem, often leading to poor generalization across domains and models. In this paper, we argue that such a binary formulation fundamentally mischaracterizes the detection task by assuming a coherent representation of human-written texts. In reality, human texts do not constitute a unified distribution, and their diversity cannot be effectively captured through limited sampling. This causes previous classifiers to memorize observed OOD characteristics rather than learn the essence of `non-ID' behavior, limiting generalization to unseen human-authored inputs. Based on this observation, we propose reframing the detection task as an out-of-distribution (OOD) detection problem, treating human-written texts as distributional outliers while machine-generated texts are in-distribution (ID) samples. To this end, we develop a detection framework using one-class learning method including DeepSVDD and HRN, and score-based learning techniques such as energy-based method, enabling robust and generalizable performance. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets validate the effectiveness of our OOD-based approach. Specifically, the OOD-based method achieves 98.3% AUROC and AUPR with only 8.9% FPR95 on DeepFake dataset. Moreover, we test our detection framework on multilingual, attacked, and unseen-model and -domain text settings, demonstrating the robustness and generalizability of our framework. Code, pretrained weights, and demo will be released.
中摘: 大型语言模型（如ChatGPT、DeepSeek和Claude）的迅速发展显著增加了AI生成文本在数字通信中的存在。这一趋势加剧了对可靠检测方法的需求，以区分人类创作的内容和机器生成的内容。现有方法，无论是零样本方法还是监督分类器，大多将此任务视为二元分类问题，这往往导致这些方法在不同领域和模型上的泛化能力较差。在这篇论文中，我们argue这样的二元表述本质上错误地表征了检测任务，因为它假设人类撰写的文本具有连贯的表示形式。实际上，人类文本并不构成统一的分布，其多样性无法通过有限采样有效捕捉。这使得先前的分类器记住观察到的OOD特征，而不是学习“非ID”行为的本质，从而限制了它们对未见过的人类创作输入的泛化能力。基于这一观察，我们提出将检测任务重新定义为一种离群值检测问题，将人类撰写的文本视为分布外（OOD）的异常值，而机器生成的文本则被视为分布内（ID）样本。为此，我们开发了一种检测框架，包括使用单类学习方法（如DeepSVDD和HRN），以及基于评分的学习技术（如能量基方法），以实现稳健且可泛化的性能。在多个数据集上的广泛实验验证了我们基于OOD的方法的有效性。具体而言，基于OOD的方法在DeepFake数据集上达到了98.3%的AUROC和AUPR，同时FPR95仅为8.9%。此外，我们还在多语言、受攻击及未见过的语言和领域文本设置中测试了我们的检测框架，展示了该框架的稳健性和可泛化性。代码、预训练权重和演示将在后续发布。

[No.273] FlexEvent: Towards Flexible Event-Frame Object Detection at Varying Operational Frequencies
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: detect
标题: FlexEvent：面向不同运行频率的灵活事件框目标检测
类别: applications
作者: Dongyue Lu;Lingdong Kong;Gim Hee Lee;Camille Simon Chane;Wei Tsang Ooi;
机构: National University of Singapore;CNRS;Ecole Nationale Supérieure de l'Electronique et de ses Applications;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=diyDUH4NtD
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06708
英摘: Event cameras offer unparalleled advantages for real-time perception in dynamic environments, thanks to the microsecond-level temporal resolution and asynchronous operation. Existing event detectors, however, are limited by fixed-frequency paradigms and fail to fully exploit the high-temporal resolution and adaptability of event data. To address these limitations, we propose FlexEvent, a novel framework that enables detection at varying frequencies. Our approach consists of two key components: FlexFuse, an adaptive event-frame fusion module that integrates high-frequency event data with rich semantic information from RGB frames, and FlexTune, a frequency-adaptive fine-tuning mechanism that generates frequency-adjusted labels to enhance model generalization across varying operational frequencies. This combination allows our method to detect objects with high accuracy in both fast-moving and static scenarios, while adapting to dynamic environments. Extensive experiments on large-scale event camera datasets demonstrate that our approach surpasses state-of-the-art methods, achieving significant improvements in both standard and high-frequency settings. Notably, our method maintains robust performance when scaling from 20 Hz to 90 Hz and delivers accurate detection up to 180 Hz, proving its effectiveness in extreme conditions. Our framework sets a new benchmark for event-based object detection and paves the way for more adaptable, real-time vision systems.
中摘: 事件摄像头因其微秒级别的时间分辨率和异步操作，在动态环境中提供了前所未有的实时感知优势。然而，现有的事件检测器受限于固定频率的范式，未能充分利用事件数据的时间高分辨率和适应性。为了解决这些限制，我们提出了一种名为FlexEvent的新框架，能够实现可变频率的检测。我们的方法包含两个关键组件：FlexFuse，一种自适应事件帧融合模块，将高频事件数据与RGB帧中的丰富语义信息集成；FlexTune，一种频率自适应微调机制，生成频率调整后的标签以增强模型在不同操作频率下的泛化能力。这种组合使我们的方法能够在快速移动和静态场景中以高精度检测物体，并适应动态环境。在大规模事件摄像头数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法超越了现有最先进的方法，在标准和高频设置下均实现了显著性能提升。值得注意的是，当从20 Hz扩展到90 Hz时，我们的方法仍能保持稳健的性能，并在高达180 Hz的情况下提供准确检测，证明其在极端条件下的有效性。我们的框架为基于事件的目标检测设定了新的基准，并为更适应性和实时的视觉系统铺平了道路。

[No.275] MIA-Tuner: Adapting Large Language Models as Pre-training Text Detector
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: MIA-Tuner：将大型语言模型适应为预训练文本检测器
类别: ai alignment
作者: Wenjie Fu;Huandong Wang;Chen Gao;Guanghua Liu;Yong Li;Tao Jiang;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;Tsinghua University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34939
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08661
英摘: The increasing parameters and expansive dataset of large language models (LLMs) highlight the urgent demand for a technical solution to audit the underlying privacy risks and copyright issues associated with LLMs. Existing studies have partially addressed this need through an exploration of the pre-training data detection problem, which is an instance of a membership inference attack (MIA). This problem involves determining whether a given piece of text has been used during the pre-training phase of the target LLM. Although existing methods have designed various sophisticated MIA score functions to achieve considerable detection performance in pre-trained LLMs, how to achieve high-confidence detection and how to perform MIA on aligned LLMs remain challenging. In this paper, we propose MIA-Tuner, a novel instruction-based MIA method, which instructs LLMs themselves to serve as a more precise pre-training data detector internally, rather than design an external MIA score function. Furthermore, we design two instruction-based safeguards to respectively mitigate the privacy risks brought by the existing methods and MIA-Tuner. To comprehensively evaluate the most recent state-of-the-art LLMs, we collect a more up-to-date MIA benchmark dataset, named WIKIMIA-24, to replace the widely adopted benchmark WIKIMIA. We conduct extensive experiments across various aligned and unaligned LLMs over the two benchmark datasets. The results demonstrate that MIA-Tuner increases the AUC of MIAs from 0.7 to a significantly high level of 0.9.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）参数不断增加和数据集日益扩大，突显了迫切需要一种技术解决方案来审计与LLMs相关的潜在隐私风险和版权问题。现有研究通过探索预训练数据检测问题，部分解决了这一需求，该问题属于成员推理攻击（MIA）的一种实例。这个问题涉及确定给定的文本片段是否在目标LLM的预训练阶段被使用过。尽管现有方法设计了各种复杂的MIA评分函数以在预训练LLMs中实现显著的检测性能，但如何实现高置信度的检测以及如何对齐LLMs进行MIA仍然是具有挑战性的。在本文中，我们提出了MIA-Tuner，这是一种新颖的基于指令的MIA方法，它指示LLMs本身在内部充当更精确的预训练数据检测器，而不是设计外部MIA评分函数。此外，我们设计了两种基于指令的安全措施，分别减轻现有方法和MIA-Tuner带来的隐私风险。为了全面评估最新的最先进的LLMs，我们收集了一个更新的MIA基准数据集，命名为WIKIMIA-24，以替代广泛采用的基准WIKIMIA。我们在两个基准数据集上对各种对齐和未对齐的LLMs进行了广泛的实验。结果显示，MIA-Tuner将MIAs的AUC从0.7显著提高到了0.9。

[No.280] Generalizable Disaster Damage Assessment via Change Detection with Vision Foundation Model
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于视觉基础模型的通用灾害损失评估方法通过变化检测实现
类别: ai for social impact track
作者: Kyeongjin Ahn;Sungwon Han;Sungwon Park;Jihee Kim;Sangyoon Park;Meeyoung Cha;

英摘: The increasing frequency and intensity of natural disasters call for rapid and accurate damage assessment. In response, disaster benchmark datasets from high-resolution satellite imagery have been constructed to develop methods for detecting damaged areas. However, these methods face significant challenges when applied to previously unseen regions due to the limited geographical and disaster-type diversity in the existing datasets. We introduce DAVI (Disaster Assessment with VIsion foundation model), a novel approach that addresses domain disparities and detects structural damage at the building level without requiring ground-truth labels for target regions. DAVI combines task-specific knowledge from a model trained on source regions with task-agnostic knowledge from an image segmentation model to generate pseudo labels indicating potential damage in target regions. It then utilizes a two-stage refinement process, which operate at both pixel and image levels, to accurately identify changes in disaster-affected areas. Our evaluation, including a case study on the 2023 Türkiye earthquake, demonstrates that our model achieves exceptional performance across diverse terrains (e.g., North America, Asia, and the Middle East) and disaster types (e.g., wildfires, hurricanes, and tsunamis). This confirms its robustness in disaster assessment without dependence on ground-truth labels and highlights its practical applicability.

[No.285] Towards Efficient and Intelligent Laser Weeding: Method and Dataset for Weed Stem Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 高效智能激光除草：杂草茎检测方法与数据集
类别: ai for social impact track
作者: Dingning Liu;Jinzhe Li;Haoyang Su;Bei Cui;Zhihui Wang;Qingbo Yuan;Wanli Ouyang;Nanqing Dong;
机构: Shanghai Artificial Intelligence Laboratory;Yazhouwan National Laboratory;Dalian University of Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/35040
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06255
英摘: Weed control is a critical challenge in modern agriculture, as weeds compete with crops for essential nutrient resources, significantly reducing crop yield and quality. Traditional weed control methods, including chemical and mechanical approaches, have real-life limitations such as associated environmental impact and efficiency. An emerging yet effective approach is laser weeding, which uses a laser beam as the stem cutter. Although there have been studies that use deep learning in weed recognition, its application in intelligent laser weeding still requires a comprehensive understanding. Thus, this study represents the first empirical investigation of weed recognition for laser weeding. To increase the efficiency of laser beam cut and avoid damaging the crops of interest, the laser beam shall be directly aimed at the weed root. Yet, weed stem detection remains an under-explored problem. We integrate the detection of crop and weed with the localization of weed stem into one end-to-end system. To train and validate the proposed system in a real-life scenario, we curate and construct a high-quality weed stem detection dataset with human annotations. The dataset consists of 7,161 high-resolution pictures collected in the field with annotations of 11,151 instances of weed. Experimental results show that the proposed system improves weeding accuracy by 6.7% and reduces energy cost by 32.3% compared to existing weed recognition systems.
中摘: 杂草控制是现代农业中的一个关键挑战，因为杂草会与农作物竞争必需的营养资源，显著降低作物产量和质量。传统的杂草控制方法，包括化学和机械手段，在实际应用中存在诸如环境影响和效率等方面的局限性。一种新兴且有效的办法是激光除草，它利用激光束作为茎切割工具。尽管已有研究使用深度学习进行杂草识别，但在智能激光除草中的应用仍需要全面的理解。因此，本研究代表了对激光除草杂草识别的第一个实证调查。为了提高激光束切割的效率并避免损伤目标农作物，激光束应直接瞄准杂草根部。然而，杂草茎检测仍然是一个未充分探索的问题。我们整合了作物和杂草的检测以及杂草茎的定位，构建了一个端到端系统。为了在真实场景下训练和验证所提出系统，我们人工标注了一个高质量的杂草茎检测数据集，包含7,161张高分辨率图片和11,151个杂草实例的标注信息。实验结果显示，所提出的系统相比现有的杂草识别系统，杂草清除的准确率提高了6.7%，能源成本降低了32.3%。

[No.286] VidSole: A Multimodal Dataset for Joint Kinetics Quantification and Disease Detection with Deep Learning
来源: AAAI 2025

标题: VidSole：一种用于深度学习联合运动学量化和疾病检测的多模态数据集

作者: Archit Kambhamettu;Samantha Snyder;Maliheh Fakhar;Samuel Audia;Ross Miller;Jae Kun Shim;Aniket Bera;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.17890
英摘: Understanding internal joint loading is critical for diagnosing gait-related diseases such as knee osteoarthritis; however, current methods of measuring joint risk factors are time-consuming, expensive, and restricted to lab settings. In this paper, we enable the large-scale, cost-effective biomechanical analysis of joint loading via three key contributions: the development and deployment of novel instrumented insoles, the creation of a large multimodal biomechanics dataset (VidSole), and a baseline deep learning pipeline to predict internal joint loading factors. Our novel instrumented insole measures the tri-axial forces and moments across five high-pressure points under the foot. VidSole consists of the forces and moments measured by these insoles along with corresponding RGB video from two viewpoints, 3D body motion capture, and force plate data for over 2,600 trials of 52 diverse participants performing four fundamental activities of daily living (sit-to-stand, stand-to-sit, walking, and running). We feed the insole data and kinematic parameters extractable from video (i.e., pose, knee angle) into a deep learning pipeline consisting of an ensemble Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU) activity classifier followed by activity-specific Long Short Term Memory (LSTM) regression networks to estimate knee adduction moment (KAM), a biomechanical risk factor for knee osteoarthritis. [No.297] Is LLMs Hallucination Usable? LLM-based Negative Reasoning for Fake News Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于LLM的反向推理在虚假新闻检测中的可用性：LLM的幻觉是否可利用？

英摘: The questionable responses caused by knowledge hallucination may lead to LLMs' unstable ability in decision-making. However, it has never been investigated whether the LLMs' hallucination is possibly usable to generate negative reasoning for facilitating the detection of fake news. This study proposes a novel supervised self-reinforced reasoning rectification approach - SR$^3$ that yields both common reasonable reasoning and wrong understandings (negative reasoning) for news via LLMs reflection for semantic consistency learning. Upon that, we construct a negative reasoning-based news learning model called - \emph{NRFE}, which leverages positive or negative news-reasoning pairs for learning the semantic consistency between them. To avoid the impact of label-implicated reasoning, we deploy a student model - \emph{NRFE-D} that only takes news content as input to inspect the performance of our method by distilling the knowledge from \emph{NRFE}. The experimental results verified on three popular fake news datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method compared with three kinds of baselines including prompting on LLMs, fine-tuning on pre-trained SLMs, and other representative fake news detection methods.
中摘: 知识幻觉引发的可疑回答可能会导致LLMs在决策能力上的不稳定。然而，从未有人调查过，LLMs的知识幻觉是否有可能用于生成负面推理，以促进假新闻检测。本研究提出了一种新颖的监督自我强化推理校正方法——SR$^3$，通过LLMs的反思来实现语义一致性学习，从而生成新闻的常见合理推理和错误理解（负面推理）。在此基础上，我们构建了一个基于负面推理的新闻学习模型——NRFE，该模型利用正向或负向新闻推理对来学习它们之间的语义一致性。为了避免标签相关推理的影响，我们部署了一个学生模型——NRFE-D，该模型仅将新闻内容作为输入，通过从NRFE中提取知识来检验我们的方法性能。在三个流行的假新闻数据集上的实验结果表明，与基于LLMs提示、预训练SLMs微调以及其它代表性假新闻检测方法的三种基线相比，我们的方法具有明显的优势。
