[No.358] UN-DETR: Promoting Objectness Learning via Joint Supervision for Unknown Object Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过联合监督促进未知物体检测的对象性学习
类别: computer vision iv
作者: HaoMiao Liu;Hao Xu;Chuhuai Yue;Bo Ma;
机构: Beijing Institute of Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32579
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10176
英摘: Unknown Object Detection (UOD) aims to identify objects of unseen categories, differing from the traditional detection paradigm limited by the closed-world assumption. A key component of UOD is learning a generalized representation, i.e. objectness for both known and unknown categories to distinguish and localize objects from the background in a class-agnostic manner. However, previous methods obtain supervision signals for learning objectness in isolation from either localization or classification information, leading to poor performance for UOD. To address this issue, we propose a transformer-based UOD framework, UN-DETR. Based on this, we craft Instance Presence Score (IPS) to represent the probability of an object's presence. For the purpose of information complementarity, IPS employs a strategy of joint supervised learning, integrating attributes representing general objectness from the positional and the categorical latent space as supervision signals. To enhance IPS learning, we introduce a one-to-many assignment strategy to incorporate more supervision. Then, we propose Unbiased Query Selection to provide premium initial query vectors for the decoder. Additionally, we propose an IPS-guided post-process strategy to filter redundant boxes and correct classification predictions for known and unknown objects. Finally, we pretrain the entire UN-DETR in an unsupervised manner, in order to obtain objectness prior. Our UN-DETR is comprehensively evaluated on multiple UOD and known detection benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness and achieving state-of-the-art performance.
中摘: 未知对象检测（UOD）旨在识别未见过类别的对象，不同于传统检测范式受限于封闭世界假设。UOD的关键组件是学习一种通用表示，即对已知和未知类别都进行对象性的学习，以在无类别偏见的情况下区分和定位对象与背景。然而，先前的方法在孤立地学习对象性时，从定位或分类信息中获取监督信号，导致UOD性能不佳。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种基于变压器的UOD框架——UN-DETR。在此基础上，我们构建了实例存在得分（IPS）来表示对象存在的概率。为了实现信息互补，IPS采用联合监督学习策略，将位置和类别潜在空间中的代表通用对象性的属性作为监督信号进行整合。为了增强IPS的学习，我们引入了一对多分配策略，以纳入更多的监督信号。此外，我们提出了无偏查询选择，为解码器提供高质量的初始查询向量。最后，我们以无监督方式预训练整个UN-DETR，以获得对象性的先验知识。我们的UN-DETR在多个UOD和已知检测基准上进行了全面评估，证明了其有效性和达到了最先进的性能。

类别: computer vision ix

英摘: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) rely on LiDAR sensors for environmental perception and decision-making in driving scenarios. In this paper, we introduce a novel adversarial perspective by proposing an attack framework that utilizes water mist and smoke to simulate environmental interference. Specifically, we propose a point cloud sequence generation method using a motion and content decomposition generative adversarial network named PCS-GAN to simulate the distribution of random objects. [No.363] Hierarchical Cross-Modal Alignment for Open-Vocabulary 3D Object Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于层次跨模态对齐的开放词汇表三维物体检测
类别: computer vision ix

英摘: Open-vocabulary 3D object detection (OV-3DOD) aims at localizing and classifying novel objects beyond closed sets. Existing works that leverage VLMs for 3D object detection (3DOD) generally resort to representations that lose the rich scene context required for 3D perception. To address this problem, we propose in this paper a hierarchical framework, named HCMA, to simultaneously learn local object and global scene information for OV-3DOD. Specifically, we first design a Hierarchical Data Integration (HDI) approach to obtain coarse-to-fine 3D-image-text data, which is fed into a VLM to extract object-centric knowledge. To facilitate the association of feature hierarchies, we then propose an Interactive Cross-Modal Alignment (ICMA) strategy to establish effective intra-level and inter-level feature connections. To better align features across different levels, we further propose an Object-Focusing Context Adjustment (OFCA) module to refine multi-level features by emphasizing object-related features. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms SOTA methods on the existing OV-3DOD benchmarks. It also achieves promising OV-3DOD results even without any 3D annotations.
中摘: 开放词汇3D物体检测（OV-3DOD）旨在定位和分类超出封闭词汇集的新颖物体。近期视觉语言模型（VLMs）的成功表明了它们在理解开放词汇方面的能力。现有的利用VLMs进行3D物体检测（3DOD）的工作通常依赖于丧失了所需丰富场景上下文的表示方法，这对于3D感知是必不可少的。为了解决这一问题，本文提出了一种分层框架HCMA，同时学习局部物体和全局场景信息以用于OV-3DOD。具体来说，我们首先设计了一种分层数据集成（HDI）方法来获得从粗到细的3D图像-文本数据，并将其输入到VLM中提取以物体为中心的知识。为了促进特征层次之间的关联，我们随后提出了交互式跨模态对齐（ICMA）策略，以建立有效的同一层次内和不同层次间的特征连接。为了更好地在不同层次之间对齐特征，我们进一步提出了一种物体聚焦上下文调整（OFCA）模块，通过强调与物体相关的特征来细化多级特征。广泛的实验表明，所提出的方法在现有OV-3DOD基准测试中优于当前最先进的方法。即使没有任何3D注释，该方法也取得了令人鼓舞的OV-3DOD结果。

[No.366] Seg2Box: 3D Object Detection by Point-Wise Semantics Supervision
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: Seg2Box：基于点wise语义监督的3D物体检测
类别: computer vision ix
作者: Maoji Zheng;Ziyu Xu;Qiming Xia;Hai Wu;Chenglu Wen;Cheng Wang;
机构: Xiamen University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33150
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16811
英摘: LiDAR-based 3D object detection and semantic segmentation are critical tasks in 3D scene understanding. Traditional detection and segmentation methods supervise their models through bounding box labels and semantic mask labels. However, these two independent labels inherently contain significant redundancy. This paper aims to eliminate the redundancy by supervising 3D object detection using only semantic labels. However, the challenge arises due to the incomplete geometry structure and boundary ambiguity of point-cloud instances, leading to inaccurate pseudo labels and poor detection results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel method, named Seg2Box. We first introduce a Multi-Frame Multi-Scale Clustering (MFMS-C) module, which leverages the spatio-temporal consistency of point clouds to generate accurate box-level pseudo-labels. Additionally, the Semantic?Guiding Iterative-Mining Self-Training (SGIM-ST) module is proposed to enhance the performance by progressively refining the pseudo-labels and mining the instances without generating pseudo-labels. Experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset and nuScenes Dataset show that our method significantly outperforms other competitive methods by 23.7\% and 10.3\% in mAP, respectively. The results demonstrate the great label-efficient potential and advancement of our method.
中摘: 基于LiDAR的3D物体检测和语义分割是三维场景理解中的关键任务。传统的检测和分割方法通过边界框标签和语义掩码标签来监督其模型。然而，这两种独立的标签本质上包含了大量的冗余信息。本文旨在通过仅使用语义标签来监督3D物体检测，消除这种冗余。但由于点云实例中存在的不完整的几何结构和边界模糊性，导致生成的伪标签不准确，从而影响了检测结果的精度。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的方法，称为Seg2Box。首先，我们引入了一个多帧多尺度聚类（MFMS-C）模块，该模块利用点云的空间-时间一致性来生成准确的盒状伪标签。此外，我们还提出了一个语义引导的迭代挖掘自训练（SGIM-ST）模块，通过逐步细化伪标签并挖掘实例来提升性能，而无需生成新的伪标签。在Waymo Open Dataset和nuScenes Dataset上的实验表明，我们的方法分别在mAP指标上比其他竞争方法高出23.7%和10.3%，证明了我们的方法具有显著的标签高效潜力和进步。

[No.368] Universal Domain Adaptive Object Detection via Dual Probabilistic Alignment
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通用领域自适应对象检测通过双概率对齐
类别: computer vision ix
作者: Yuanfan Zheng;Jinlin Wu;Wuyang Li;Zhen Chen;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences, Institute of Automation;Chinese Academy of Sciences;Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33156
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11443
英摘: Domain Adaptive Object Detection (DAOD) transfers knowledge from a labeled source domain to an unannotated target domain under closed-set assumption. Universal DAOD (UniDAOD) extends DAOD to handle open-set, partial-set, and closed-set domain adaptation. In this paper, we first unveil two issues: domain-private category alignment is crucial for global-level features, and the domain probability heterogeneity of features across different levels. To address these issues, we propose a novel Dual Probabilistic Alignment (DPA) framework to model domain probability as Gaussian distribution, enabling the heterogeneity domain distribution sampling and measurement. The DPA consists of three tailored modules: the Global-level Domain Private Alignment (GDPA), the Instance-level Domain Shared Alignment (IDSA), and the Private Class Constraint (PCC). GDPA utilizes the global-level sampling to mine domain-private category samples and calculate alignment weight through a cumulative distribution function to address the global-level private category alignment. IDSA utilizes instance-level sampling to mine domain-shared category samples and calculates alignment weight through Gaussian distribution to conduct the domain-shared category domain alignment to address the feature heterogeneity. The PCC aggregates domain-private category centroids between feature and probability spaces to mitigate negative transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our DPA outperforms state-of-the-art UniDAOD and DAOD methods across various datasets and scenarios, including open, partial, and closed sets. Codes are available at \url{https://github.com/zyfone/DPA}.
中摘: 领域自适应对象检测（Domain Adaptive Object Detection，DAOD）在闭集假设下，将标记的源领域知识转移到未标注的目标领域。通用领域自适应对象检测（Universal DAOD，UniDAOD）扩展了DAOD，以处理开放领域、部分领域和闭合领域的自适应问题。在本文中，我们首先揭示了两个问题：全局级别的特征需要领域私有类别的对齐，以及特征在不同级别上的领域概率异质性。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新颖的双重概率对齐（Dual Probabilistic Alignment，DPA）框架，将领域概率建模为高斯分布，从而实现异质领域分布采样和测量。DPA由三个定制模块组成：全局级别领域私有对齐（Global-level Domain Private Alignment，GDPA）、实例级别领域共享对齐（Instance-level Domain Shared Alignment，IDSA）和私有类别约束（Private Class Constraint，PCC）。GDPA利用全局级别的采样挖掘领域私有类别的样本，并通过累积分布函数计算对齐权重，以解决全局级别的领域私有类别对齐问题。IDSA利用实例级别的采样挖掘领域共享类别的样本，并通过高斯分布计算对齐权重，以执行领域共享类别的领域对齐，解决特征异质性问题。PCC在特征空间和概率空间之间聚合领域私有类别的中心点，以减轻负迁移的影响。广泛的经验表明，我们的DPA在各种数据集和场景（包括开放、部分和闭合集）中优于最先进的UniDAOD和DAOD方法。代码可在<https://github.com/zyfone/DPA> 获取。

[No.369] Video Anomaly Detection with Motion and Appearance Guided Patch Diffusion Model
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于运动和外观引导的块扩散模型的视频异常检测
类别: computer vision ix
作者: Hang Zhou;Jiale Cai;Yuteng Ye;Yonghui Feng;Chenxing Gao;Junqing Yu;Zikai Song;Wei Yang;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33169
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09026
英摘: A recent endeavor in one class of video anomaly detection is to leverage diffusion models and posit the task as a generation problem, where the diffusion model is trained to recover normal patterns exclusively, thus reporting abnormal patterns as outliers. Yet, existing attempts neglect the various formations of anomaly and predict normal samples at the feature level regardless that abnormal objects in surveillance videos are often relatively small. To address this, a novel patch-based diffusion model is proposed, specifically engineered to capture fine-grained local information. We further observe that anomalies in videos manifest themselves as deviations in both appearance and motion. Therefore, we argue that a comprehensive solution must consider both of these aspects simultaneously to achieve accurate frame prediction. To address this, we introduce innovative motion and appearance conditions that are seamlessly integrated into our patch diffusion model. These conditions are designed to guide the model in generating coherent and contextually appropriate predictions for both semantic content and motion relations. Experimental results in four challenging video anomaly detection datasets empirically substantiate the efficacy of our proposed approach, demonstrating that it consistently outperforms most existing methods in detecting abnormal behaviors.
中摘: 最近在视频异常检测的一个类别中的一项努力是利用扩散模型，并将任务视为生成问题，其中扩散模型被训练为仅恢复正常模式，因此将异常模式报告为离群值。然而，现有的尝试忽视了异常的各种形式，并在特征级别上预测正常样本，尽管在监控视频中异常对象往往相对较小。为了解决这一问题，提出了一种新颖的基于补丁的扩散模型，专门设计用于捕捉细粒度的局部信息。我们进一步观察到，视频中的异常表现为外观和运动上的偏差。因此，我们认为一个全面的解决方案必须同时考虑这两个方面，以实现准确的帧预测。为此，我们引入了创新的运动和外观条件，这些条件无缝地集成到了我们的补丁扩散模型中。这些条件旨在引导模型生成对于语义内容和运动关系都具有连贯性和上下文适宜性的预测。在四个具有挑战性的视频异常检测数据集上的实验结果实证证明了我们所提出方法的有效性，展示了它在检测异常行为方面始终优于大多数现有方法。

[No.373] CREST: An Efficient Conjointly-trained Spike-driven Framework for Event-based Object Detection Exploiting Spatiotemporal Dynamics
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: CREST：一种高效的联合训练尖峰驱动框架，利用时空动态进行事件触发对象检测
类别: computer vision v
作者: Ruixin Mao;Aoyu Shen;Lin Tang;Jun Zhou;
机构: University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32649
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12525
英摘: Event-based cameras feature high temporal resolution, wide dynamic range, and low power consumption, which is ideal for high-speed and low-light object detection. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) are promising for event-based object recognition and detection due to their spiking nature but lack efficient training methods, leading to gradient vanishing and high computational complexity, especially in deep SNNs. Additionally, existing SNN frameworks often fail to effectively handle multi-scale spatiotemporal features, leading to increased data redundancy and reduced accuracy. To address these issues, we propose CREST, a novel conjointly-trained spike-driven framework to exploit spatiotemporal dynamics in event-based object detection. We introduce the conjoint learning rule to accelerate SNN learning and alleviate gradient vanishing. It also supports dual operation modes for efficient and flexible implementation on different hardware types. Additionally, CREST features a fully spike-driven framework with a multi-scale spatiotemporal event integrator (MESTOR) and a spatiotemporal-IoU (ST-IoU) loss. Our approach achieves superior object recognition & detection performance and up to 100X energy efficiency compared with state-of-the-art SNN algorithms on three datasets, providing an efficient solution for event-based object detection algorithms suitable for SNN hardware implementation.
中摘: 事件驱动相机具有高时间分辨率、宽动态范围和低功耗，这使其非常适合高速和低光条件下的物体检测。由于其脉冲特性，脉冲神经网络（SNNs）在事件驱动的物体识别和检测方面颇具前景，但缺乏有效的训练方法，导致梯度消失和高计算复杂性，尤其是在深度SNNs中。此外，现有的SNN框架往往难以有效处理多尺度时空特征，导致数据冗余增加并降低了准确性。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种名为CREST的新颖联合训练脉冲驱动框架，以利用事件驱动物体检测中的时空动态特性。我们引入了联合学习规则来加速SNN的学习过程，并缓解梯度消失问题。CREST还支持双操作模式，以便在不同硬件类型上实现高效灵活的操作。此外，CREST包含一个完全由脉冲驱动的框架，该框架包括一个多尺度时空事件积分器（MESTOR）和一个时空交并比损失（ST-IoU）。我们的方法在三个数据集上的物体识别与检测性能优于最先进的SNN算法，并且在能耗效率上最高可提升100倍，提供了一种适用于SNN硬件实现的高效解决方案。

[No.377] Locate Anything on Earth: Advancing Open-Vocabulary Object Detection for Remote Sensing Community
来源: AAAI 2025

标题: 地球上的任何物体：推进遥感社区的开放词汇对象检测技术

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09110
英摘: Object detection, particularly open-vocabulary object detection, plays a crucial role in Earth sciences, such as environmental monitoring, natural disaster assessment, and land-use planning. However, existing open-vocabulary detectors, primarily trained on natural-world images, struggle to generalize to remote sensing images due to a significant data domain gap. Thus, this paper aims to advance the development of open-vocabulary object detection in remote sensing community. To achieve this, we first reformulate the task as Locate Anything on Earth (LAE) with the goal of detecting any novel concepts on Earth. We then developed the LAE-Label Engine which collects, auto-annotates, and unifies up to 10 remote sensing datasets creating the LAE-1M - the first large-scale remote sensing object detection dataset with broad category coverage. Using the LAE-1M, we further propose and train the novel LAE-DINO Model, the first open-vocabulary foundation object detector for the LAE task, featuring Dynamic Vocabulary Construction (DVC) and Visual-Guided Text Prompt Learning (VisGT) modules. DVC dynamically constructs vocabulary for each training batch, while VisGT maps visual features to semantic space, enhancing text features. We comprehensively conduct experiments on established remote sensing benchmark DIOR, DOTAv2.0, as well as our newly introduced 80-class LAE-80C benchmark. Results demonstrate the advantages of the LAE-1M dataset and the effectiveness of the LAE-DINO method.
中摘: 物体检测，特别是开放词汇物体检测，在地球科学领域中扮演着至关重要的角色，如环境监测、自然灾害评估和土地利用规划。然而，现有的开放词汇检测器主要在自然世界的图像上进行训练，难以泛化到遥感图像中，因为存在显著的数据域差距。因此，本文旨在推动开放词汇物体检测在遥感领域的研究与发展。为了实现这一目标，我们首先将任务重新定义为“地球上的任何物体定位”（Locate Anything on Earth, LAE），旨在检测地球上任何新颖的概念。然后，我们开发了LAE-Label Engine，该工具收集、自动标注并统一多达10个遥感数据集，创建了LAE-1M——第一个具有广泛类别覆盖的大规模遥感物体检测数据集。利用LAE-1M，我们进一步提出了并训练了新的LAE-DINO模型，这是首个针对LAE任务的开放词汇基础物体检测器，其特点是动态词汇构造（Dynamic Vocabulary Construction, DVC）模块和视觉引导文本提示学习（Visual-Guided Text Prompt Learning, VisGT）模块。DVC能够动态地为每个训练批次构建词汇表，而VisGT则将视觉特征映射到语义空间，增强文本特征。我们在已建立的遥感基准DIOR以及DOTAv2.0上，以及我们新引入的80类LAE-80C基准上进行了全面的实验。结果表明，LAE-1M数据集的优势以及LAE-DINO方法的有效性。

[No.378] SAUGE: Taming SAM for Uncertainty-Aligned Multi-Granularity Edge Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: SAUGE: 驯化SAM以实现不确定性对齐的多粒度边缘检测
类别: computer vision v
作者: Xing Liufu;Chaolei Tan;Xiaotong Lin;Yonggang Qi;Jinxuan Li;Jian-Fang Hu;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;Key Laboratory of Machine Intelligence and Advanced Computing;Guangdong Province Key Laboratory of Information Security Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32615
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12892
英摘: Edge labels are typically at various granularity levels owing to the varying preferences of annotators, thus handling the subjectivity of per-pixel labels has been a focal point for edge detection. Previous methods often employ a simple voting strategy to diminish such label uncertainty or impose a strong assumption of labels with a pre-defined distribution, e.g., Gaussian. In this work, we unveil that the segment anything model (SAM) provides strong prior knowledge to model the uncertainty in edge labels. Our key insight is that the intermediate SAM features inherently correspond to object edges at various granularities, which reflects different edge options due to uncertainty. Therefore, we attempt to align uncertainty with granularity by regressing intermediate SAM features from different layers to object edges at multi-granularity levels. In doing so, the model can fully and explicitly explore diverse ``uncertainties'' in a data-driven fashion. Specifically, we inject a lightweight module (~ 1.5% additional parameters) into the frozen SAM to progressively fuse and adapt its intermediate features to estimate edges from coarse to fine. It is crucial to normalize the granularity level of human edge labels to match their innate uncertainty. For this, we simply perform linear blending to the real edge labels at hand to create pseudo labels with varying granularities. Consequently, our uncertainty-aligned edge detector can flexibly produce edges at any desired granularity (including an optimal one). Thanks to SAM, our model uniquely demonstrates strong generalizability for cross-dataset edge detection. Extensive experimental results on BSDS500, Muticue and NYUDv2 validate our model's superiority.
中摘: 边缘标签通常具有不同的粒度级别，这归因于注释员的不同偏好。因此，处理像素级标签的主观性一直是边缘检测的一个重点。以往的方法往往采用简单的投票策略来减轻这种标签不确定性，或者对标签施加一个强假设并预定义其分布，例如高斯分布。在本工作中，我们揭示了分割一切模型（SAM）为建模边缘标签中的不确定性提供了强大的先验知识。我们的核心洞察是，SAM的中间特征本质上对应于不同粒度级别的对象边缘，这反映了由于不确定性带来的多种边缘选项。因此，我们尝试通过从不同层回归SAM的中间特征来将不确定性与粒度对齐，以在多粒度水平上估计对象边缘。这样，模型可以通过数据驱动的方式全面且明确地探索各种“不确定性”。具体而言，我们在冻结的SAM中注入了一个轻量级模块（约增加1.5%的参数），逐步融合和适应其中间特征，以从粗略到精细地估计边缘。为了使人类边缘标签的粒度水平与其固有的不确定性匹配，我们简单地对手头的真实边缘标签进行线性混合，创建具有不同粒度级别的伪标签。由此，我们的不确定性对齐边缘检测器可以灵活地在任何期望的粒度级别（包括最优级别）生成边缘。得益于SAM，我们的模型独特地展示了跨数据集边缘检测的强大泛化能力。在BSDS500、Muticue和NYUDv2上的广泛实验结果验证了我们模型的优势。

[No.379] TGBFormer: Transformer-GraphFormer Blender Network for Video Object Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: TGBFormer：Transformer-GraphFormer融合网络在视频对象检测中的应用
类别: computer vision v
作者: Qiang Qi;Xiao Wang;
机构: Qingdao University of Science and Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32703
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13903
英摘: Video object detection has made significant progress in recent years thanks to convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and vision transformers (ViTs). Typically, CNNs excel at capturing local features but struggle to model global representations. Conversely, ViTs are adept at capturing long-range global features but face challenges in representing local feature details. Off-the-shelf video object detection methods solely rely on CNNs or ViTs to conduct feature aggregation, which hampers their capability to simultaneously leverage global and local information, thereby resulting in limited detection performance. In this paper, we propose a Transformer-GraphFormer Blender Network (TGBFormer) for video object detection, with three key technical improvements to fully exploit the advantages of transformers and graph convolutional networks while compensating for their limitations. First, we develop a spatial-temporal transformer module to aggregate global contextual information, constituting global representations with long-range feature dependencies. Second, we introduce a spatial-temporal GraphFormer module that utilizes local spatial and temporal relationships to aggregate features, generating new local representations that are complementary to the transformer outputs. Third, we design a global-local feature blender module to adaptively couple transformer-based global representations and GraphFormer-based local representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our TGBFormer establishes new state-of-the-art results on the ImageNet VID dataset. Particularly, our TGBFormer achieves 86.5% mAP while running at around 41.0 FPS on a single Tesla A100 GPU.
中摘: 近年来，视频对象检测取得了显著进展，这得益于卷积神经网络（CNNs）和视觉变换器（ViTs）。通常，CNNs擅长捕捉局部特征，但在建模全局表示方面存在困难。相反，ViTs在捕捉长程全局特征方面表现出色，但在表示局部特征细节方面面临挑战。现成的视频对象检测方法仅依赖于CNNs或ViTs进行特征聚合，这限制了它们同时利用全局和局部信息的能力，从而导致检测性能有限。在本文中，我们提出了一种Transformer-GraphFormer Blender Network（TGBFormer）用于视频对象检测，并通过三种关键技术改进充分利用变换器和图卷积网络的优势，同时弥补它们的不足。首先，我们开发了一个空间-时间变换器模块来聚合全局上下文信息，构建具有长程特征依赖性的全局表示。其次，我们引入了一个空间-时间GraphFormer模块，利用局部空间和时间关系进行特征聚合，生成与变换器输出互补的新局部表示。第三，我们设计了一个全局-局部特征混合模块，以自适应方式耦合基于变换器的全局表示和基于GraphFormer的局部表示。广泛的实验表明，我们的TGBFormer在ImageNet VID数据集上建立了新的最先进的检测结果。特别是，我们的TGBFormer在单个Tesla A100 GPU上运行时实现了约41.0 FPS的速度，并且达到了86.5%的mAP。

英摘: Video anomaly detection plays a significant role in intelligent surveillance systems. [No.382] Unlocking the Potential of Reverse Distillation for Anomaly Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 解锁逆蒸馏在异常检测中的潜力
类别: computer vision v
作者: Xinyue Liu;Jianyuan Wang;Biao Leng;Shuo Zhang;
机构: Beihang University;University of Science and Technology Beijing;Beijing Jiao Tong University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32601
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07579
英摘: Knowledge Distillation (KD) is a promising approach for unsupervised Anomaly Detection (AD). However, the student network's over-generalization often diminishes the crucial representation differences between teacher and student in anomalous regions, leading to detection failures. To addresses this problem, the widely accepted Reverse Distillation (RD) paradigm designs the asymmetry teacher and student, using an encoder as teacher and a decoder as student. Yet, the design of RD does not ensure that the teacher encoder effectively distinguishes between normal and abnormal features or that the student decoder generates anomaly-free features. Additionally, the absence of skip connections results in a loss of fine details during feature reconstruction. To address these issues, we propose RD with Expert, which introduces a novel Expert-Teacher-Student network for simultaneous distillation of both the teacher encoder and student decoder. The added expert network enhances the student's ability to generate normal features and optimizes the teacher's differentiation between normal and abnormal features, reducing missed detections. Additionally, Guided Information Injection is designed to filter and transfer features from teacher to student, improving detail reconstruction and minimizing false positives. Experiments on several benchmarks prove that our method outperforms existing unsupervised AD methods under RD paradigm, fully unlocking RD's potential.
中摘: 知识蒸馏（KD）是一种有前景的无监督异常检测（AD）方法。然而，学生网络的过度泛化往往削弱了教师和学生在网络异常区域中的关键表示差异，导致检测失败。为了解决这个问题，广泛接受的反向蒸馏（RD）范式设计了不对称的教师和学生网络，使用编码器作为教师，解码器作为学生。然而，RD的设计并不能保证教师编码器能够有效地区分正常和异常特征，也不能确保学生解码器生成无异常特征。此外，缺乏跳跃连接会导致在特征重建过程中丢失细节点。为解决这些问题，我们提出了带有专家的反向蒸馏（RD with Expert），引入了一种新颖的专家-教师-学生网络，用于同时蒸馏教师编码器和学生解码器。添加的专家网络增强了学生生成正常特征的能力，并优化了教师对正常和异常特征的区分，从而减少漏检。另外，引导信息注入被设计用来筛选并传输教师到学生的特征，提高细节重建能力并最小化误报。在多个基准上的实验表明，在RD范式下，我们的方法优于现有的无监督AD方法，完全释放了RD的潜力。

[No.384] Alignment-Free RGB-T Salient Object Detection: A Large-Scale Dataset and Progressive Correlation Network
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 无对齐RGB-T显著目标检测：一个大规模数据集和逐步相关网络
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Kunpeng Wang;Keke Chen;Chenglong Li;Zhengzheng Tu;Bin Luo;
机构: Anhui University;
类型: Technical

英摘: Alignment-free RGB-Thermal (RGB-T) salient object detection (SOD) aims to achieve robust performance in complex scenes by directly leveraging the complementary information from unaligned visible-thermal image pairs, without requiring manual alignment. However, the labor-intensive process of collecting and annotating image pairs limits the scale of existing benchmarks, hindering the advancement of alignment-free RGB-T SOD. In this paper, we construct a large-scale and high-diversity unaligned RGB-T SOD dataset named UVT20K, comprising 20,000 image pairs, 407 scenes, and 1256 object categories. All samples are collected from real-world scenarios with various challenges, such as low illumination, image clutter, complex salient objects, and so on. To support the exploration for further research, each sample in UVT20K is annotated with a comprehensive set of ground truths, including saliency masks, scribbles, boundaries, and challenge attributes. In addition, we propose a Progressive Correlation Network (PCNet), which models inter- and intra-modal correlations on the basis of explicit alignment to achieve accurate predictions in unaligned image pairs. Extensive experiments conducted on unaligned and aligned datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Angknpng/PCNet.
中摘: 无对齐的RGB-热红外（RGB-T）显著对象检测（SOD）旨在通过直接利用未对齐的可见光-热红外图像对中的互补信息，在复杂场景中实现稳健的性能，而无需手动对齐。然而，收集和标注图像对的劳动密集型过程限制了现有基准的规模，阻碍了无对齐RGB-T SOD的发展。在本文中，我们构建了一个名为UVT20K的大规模、高多样性的未对齐RGB-T SOD数据集，包含20,000个图像对、407个场景和1256个对象类别。所有样本均来自具有各种挑战（如低光照、图像杂乱、复杂的显著对象等）的真实世界场景。为了支持进一步研究的探索，UVT20K中的每个样本都附带了一整套全面的ground truth标注，包括显著性掩码、涂鸦、边界和挑战属性。此外，我们提出了一种渐进相关网络（PCNet），该网络基于显式的对齐来建模跨模态和同模态的相关性，从而在未对齐的图像对中实现准确的预测。我们在未对齐和对齐的数据集上进行的大量实验表明，我们的方法是有效的。代码和数据集可在https://github.com/Angknpng/PCNet获取。

[No.385] C2P-CLIP: Injecting Category Common Prompt in CLIP to Enhance Generalization in Deepfake Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: C2P-CLIP：在CLIP中注入类别通用提示以增强深度假脸检测的泛化能力
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Chuangchuang Tan;Renshuai Tao;Huan Liu;Guanghua Gu;Baoyuan Wu;Yao Zhao;Yunchao Wei;
机构: Beijing Jiao Tong University;Visual Intellgence;X International Cooperation Joint Laboratory;Pengcheng Laboratory;Yanshan University;Hebei Key Laboratory of Information Transmission and Signal Processing;Chinese University of Hong Kong, Shenzhen;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32772
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09647
英摘: This work focuses on AIGC detection to develop universal detectors capable of identifying various types of forgery images. Recent studies have found large pre-trained models, such as CLIP, are effective for generalizable deepfake detection along with linear classifiers. However, two critical issues remain unresolved: 1) understanding why CLIP features are effective on deepfake detection through a linear classifier; and 2) exploring the detection potential of CLIP. In this study, we delve into the underlying mechanisms of CLIP's detection capabilities by decoding its detection features into text and performing word frequency analysis. Our finding indicates that CLIP detects deepfakes by recognizing similar concepts (Fig. \ref{fig:fig1} a). Building on this insight, we introduce Category Common Prompt CLIP, called C2P-CLIP, which integrates the category common prompt into the text encoder to inject category-related concepts into the image encoder, thereby enhancing detection performance (Fig. \ref{fig:fig1} b). Our method achieves a 12.41\% improvement in detection accuracy compared to the original CLIP, without introducing additional parameters during testing. Comprehensive experiments conducted on two widely-used datasets, encompassing 20 generation models, validate the efficacy of the proposed method, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance. The code is available at \url{https://github.com/chuangchuangtan/C2P-CLIP-DeepfakeDetection}
中摘: 这项工作专注于AIGC检测，旨在开发能够识别各种伪造图像类型的通用检测器。最近的研究发现，大型预训练模型（如CLIP）与线性分类器结合使用时，对于通用的深度假信息（deepfake）检测非常有效。然而，仍存在两个关键问题未解决：1）通过线性分类器理解为什么CLIP特征在深度假信息检测中有效；2）探索CLIP的检测潜力。在这项研究中，我们通过将CLIP的检测特征解码为文本并进行词频分析，深入探讨了CLIP检测机制的底层原理。我们的研究发现表明，CLIP通过识别相似的概念来检测深度假信息（图\ref{fig:fig1}a）。基于这一洞察，我们引入了一种名为C2P-CLIP的类别通用提示CLIP方法，该方法将类别通用提示集成到文本编码器中，以将类别相关概念注入图像编码器，从而提高检测性能（图\ref{fig:fig1}b）。与原始CLIP相比，我们的方法在测试过程中未引入额外参数，并实现了12.41%的检测准确性提升。在两个广泛使用的数据集上进行全面实验，涵盖了20个生成模型，验证了所提方法的有效性，展示了最先进的性能。代码可在https://github.com/chuangchuangtan/C2P-CLIP-DeepfakeDetection 获取。

[No.386] Dual Conditioned Motion Diffusion for Pose-Based Video Anomaly Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于姿态的视频异常检测的双重条件运动扩散
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Hongsong Wang;Andi Xu;Pinle Ding;Jie Gui;
机构: Southeast University;Purple Mountain Laboratories;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32829
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17210
英摘: Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) is essential for computer vision research. Existing VAD methods utilize either reconstruction-based or prediction-based frameworks. The former excels at detecting irregular patterns or structures, whereas the latter is capable of spotting abnormal deviations or trends. We address pose-based video anomaly detection and introduce a novel framework called Dual Conditioned Motion Diffusion (DCMD), which enjoys the advantages of both approaches. The DCMD integrates conditioned motion and conditioned embedding to comprehensively utilize the pose characteristics and latent semantics of observed movements, respectively. In the reverse diffusion process, a motion transformer is proposed to capture potential correlations from multi-layered characteristics within the spectrum space of human motion. To enhance the discriminability between normal and abnormal instances, we design a novel United Association Discrepancy (UAD) regularization that primarily relies on a Gaussian kernel-based time association and a self-attention-based global association. Finally, a mask completion strategy is introduced during the inference stage of the reverse diffusion process to enhance the utilization of conditioned motion for the prediction branch of anomaly detection. Extensive experiments on four datasets demonstrate that our method dramatically outperforms state-of-the-art methods and exhibits superior generalization performance.
中摘: 视频异常检测（VAD）对于计算机视觉研究至关重要。现有的VAD方法要么基于重建，要么基于预测框架。前者擅长检测不规则模式或结构，而后者则能够识别异常偏差或趋势。我们针对基于姿态的视频异常检测问题，提出了一种名为Dual Conditioned Motion Diffusion（DCMD）的新框架，该框架结合了两种方法的优点。DCMD将条件运动和条件嵌入相结合，分别全面利用观察到的运动的姿态特征和潜在语义。在逆向扩散过程中，我们提出了一个运动变换器来捕捉人类运动频谱空间中多层次特征之间的潜在关联。为了增强正常和异常实例之间的可区分性，我们设计了一种新颖的联合关联正则化（United Association Discrepancy, UAD），主要依赖于高斯核基于的时间关联和自注意力机制的全局关联。最后，在逆向扩散过程的推理阶段引入了一个掩码填充策略，以增强条件运动在异常检测预测分支中的利用。在四个数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法显著优于现有最先进的方法，并且表现出优越的泛化性能。

[No.387] Explicit Relational Reasoning Network for Scene Text Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 显式关系推理网络在场景文本检测中的应用
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Yuchen Su;Zhineng Chen;Yongkun Du;Zhilong Ji;Kai Hu;Jinfeng Bai;Xieping Gao;
机构: Fudan University;Tomorrow Advancing Life;Xiangtan University;Hunan Normal University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32759
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14692
英摘: Connected component (CC) is a proper text shape representation that aligns with human reading intuition. However, CC-based text detection methods have recently faced a developmental bottleneck that their time-consuming post-processing is difficult to eliminate. To address this issue, we introduce an explicit relational reasoning network (ERRNet) to elegantly model the component relationships without post-processing. Concretely, we first represent each text instance as multiple ordered text components, and then treat these components as objects in sequential movement. In this way, scene text detection can be innovatively viewed as a tracking problem. From this perspective, we design an end-to-end tracking decoder to achieve a CC-based method dispensing with post-processing entirely. Additionally, we observe that there is an inconsistency between classification confidence and localization quality, so we propose a Polygon Monte-Carlo method to quickly and accurately evaluate the localization quality. Based on this, we introduce a position-supervised classification loss to guide the task-aligned learning of ERRNet. Experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our ERRNet. It consistently achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while holding highly competitive inference speed.
中摘: 连通组件（CC）是一种符合人类阅读直觉的适当文本形状表示。然而，基于连通组件的文字检测方法最近遇到了一个发展瓶颈，即耗时的后处理步骤难以消除。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了一个显式的关系推理网络（ERRNet），以优雅地建模组件之间的关系，而不进行后处理。具体而言，我们首先将每个文字实例表示为多个有序的文字组件，然后将这些组件视为在序列移动中的对象。这样一来，场景文字检测可以创新地被视为一个跟踪问题。从这个角度来看，我们设计了一个端到端的跟踪解码器，以实现完全无需后处理的基于连通组件的方法。此外，我们观察到分类置信度与定位质量之间存在不一致，因此提出了一种多边形蒙特卡洛方法来快速而准确地评估定位质量。在此基础上，我们引入了一个位置监督分类损失，以指导ERRNet的任务对齐学习。在具有挑战性的基准测试上的实验表明，我们的ERRNet非常有效。它在保持高度竞争的推理速度的同时，始终能够达到最先进的准确性。

[No.389] Geometry-Aware 3D Salient Object Detection Network
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 几何感知三维突出对象检测网络
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Chen Wang;Liyuan Zhang;Le Hui;Qi Liu;Yuchao Dai;
机构: Northwestern Polytechnical University;Nanjing University of Science and Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32813
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16488
英摘: Point cloud salient object detection has attracted the attention of researchers in recent years. Since existing works do not fully utilize the geometry context of 3D objects, blurry boundaries are generated when segmenting objects with complex backgrounds. In this paper, we propose a geometry-aware 3D salient object detection network that explicitly clusters points into superpoints to enhance the geometric boundaries of objects, thereby segmenting complete objects with clear boundaries. Specifically, we first propose a simple yet effective superpoint partition module to cluster points into superpoints. In order to improve the quality of superpoints, we present a point cloud class-agnostic loss to learn discriminative point features for clustering superpoints from the object. After obtaining superpoints, we then propose a geometry enhancement module that utilizes superpoint-point attention to aggregate geometric information into point features for predicting the salient map of the object with clear boundaries. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves new state-of-the-art performance on the PCSOD dataset.
中摘: 点云显著物体检测近年来引起了研究人员的关注。由于现有工作未能充分利用3D对象的几何上下文，在对具有复杂背景的对象进行分割时会产生模糊的边界。在本文中，我们提出了一种几何感知的3D显著物体检测网络，该网络明确地将点聚类为超点，以增强对象的几何边界，从而实现具有清晰边界的完整对象分割。具体来说，我们首先提出一个简单而有效的超点分区模块，将点聚类为超点。为了提高超点的质量，我们提出了一个点云类无感知损失，用于从对象学习区分性的点特征以聚类超点。获取到超点后，我们随后提出了一种几何增强模块，利用超点-点注意力机制将几何信息聚合到点特征中，以预测具有清晰边界的对象显著图。广泛实验表明，我们的方法在PCSOD数据集上达到了新的最佳性能。

[No.390] HS-FPN: High Frequency and Spatial Perception FPN for Tiny Object Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: HS-FPN：高频率和空间感知FPN在微小物体检测中的应用
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Zican Shi;Jing Hu;Jie Ren;Hengkang Ye;Xuyang Yuan;Yan Ouyang;Jia He;Bo Ji;Junyu Guo;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;National Key Laboratory of Science and Technology on Multi-spectral Information Processing;Air Force Early Warning Academy;Chinese People’s Liberation Army;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32740
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10116
英摘: The introduction of Feature Pyramid Network (FPN) has significantly improved object detection performance. However, substantial challenges remain in detecting tiny objects, as their features occupy only a very small proportion of the feature maps. Although FPN integrates multi-scale features, it does not directly enhance or enrich the features of tiny objects. Furthermore, FPN lacks spatial perception ability. To address these issues, we propose a novel High Frequency and Spatial Perception Feature Pyramid Network (HS-FPN) with two innovative modules. First, we designed a high frequency perception module (HFP) that generates high frequency responses through high pass filters. These high frequency responses are used as mask weights from both spatial and channel perspectives to enrich and highlight the features of tiny objects in the original feature maps. Second, we developed a spatial dependency perception module (SDP) to capture the spatial dependencies that FPN lacks. Our experiments demonstrate that detectors based on HS-FPN exhibit competitive advantages over state-of-the-art models on the AI-TOD dataset for tiny object detection.
中摘: 特征金字塔网络（FPN）的引入显著提高了目标检测性能。然而，在检测微小对象时仍面临重大挑战，因为微小对象的特征在特征图中所占的比例非常小。尽管FPN整合了多尺度特征，但它并未直接增强或丰富微小对象的特征。此外，FPN缺乏空间感知能力。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新颖的高频率和空间感知特征金字塔网络（HS-FPN），并设计了两个创新模块。首先，我们设计了一个高频率感知模块（HFP），通过高通滤波器生成高频率响应。这些高频率响应从空间和通道两个角度出发，用作掩码权重，以丰富和突出原始特征图中微小对象的特征。其次，我们开发了一个空间依赖感知模块（SDP），用于捕捉FPN所缺乏的空间依赖关系。我们的实验表明，基于HS-FPN的目标检测器在AI-TOD数据集上的微小对象检测任务中表现出与最先进的模型相媲美的优势。

[No.391] Improving Transformer Based Line Segment Detection with Matched Predicting and Re-ranking
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于匹配预测和重排序改进的变压器基线线段检测
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Xin Tong;Shi Peng;Baojie Tian;Yufei Guo;Xuhui Huang;Zhe Ma;
机构: China Aerospace Science and Industry Corporation;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32799
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17766
英摘: Classical Transformer-based line segment detection methods have delivered impressive results. However, we observe that some accurately detected line segments are assigned low confidence scores during prediction, causing them to be ranked lower and potentially suppressed. Additionally, these models often require prolonged training periods to achieve strong performance, largely due to the necessity of bipartite matching. In this paper, we introduce RANK-LETR, a novel Transformer-based line segment detection method. Our approach leverages learnable geometric information to refine the ranking of predicted line segments by enhancing the confidence scores of high-quality predictions in a posterior verification step. We also propose a new line segment proposal method, wherein the feature point nearest to the centroid of the line segment directly predicts the location, significantly improving training efficiency and stability. Moreover, we introduce a line segment ranking loss to stabilize rankings during training, thereby enhancing the generalization capability of the model. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms other Transformer-based and CNN-based approaches in prediction accuracy while requiring fewer training epochs than previous Transformer-based models.
中摘: 基于经典Transformer的方法在直线段检测方面取得了令人印象深刻的成果。然而，我们观察到一些准确检测出的直线段在预测过程中被赋予了较低的置信度分数，导致它们在排名中较低甚至可能被抑制。此外，这些模型往往需要较长的训练周期才能达到较强的性能，主要是因为需要进行双部分匹配。在本文中，我们引入了一种名为RANK-LETR的新颖Transformer基线直线段检测方法。我们的方法利用可学习的几何信息，在后验验证步骤中通过增强高质量预测的置信度分数来细化预测直线段的排名。我们还提出了一种新的直线段提案方法，在该方法中，与直线段质心最近的特征点直接预测位置，显著提高了训练效率和稳定性。此外，我们引入了一种直线段排名损失，以在训练过程中稳定排名，从而增强模型的一般化能力。实验结果表明，我们的方法在预测准确性上优于其他基于Transformer和CNN的方法，并且所需的训练周期少于之前的基于Transformer的模型。

[No.392] JoVALE: Detecting Human Actions in Video Using Audiovisual and Language Contexts
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: JoVALE：利用音频视觉和语言上下文检测视频中的人类动作
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Taein Son;Soo Won Seo;Jisong Kim;Seok Hwan Lee;Jun Won Choi;
机构: Hanyang University;Seoul National University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32745
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13708
英摘: Video Action Detection (VAD) entails localizing and categorizing action instances within videos, which inherently consist of diverse information sources such as audio, visual cues, and surrounding scene contexts. Leveraging this multi-modal information effectively for VAD poses a significant challenge, as the model must identify action-relevant cues with precision. In this study, we introduce a novel multi-modal VAD architecture, referred to as the Joint Actor-centric Visual, Audio, Language Encoder (JoVALE). JoVALE is the first VAD method to integrate audio and visual features with scene descriptive context sourced from large-capacity image captioning models. At the heart of JoVALE is the actor-centric aggregation of audio, visual, and scene descriptive information, enabling adaptive integration of crucial features for recognizing each actor's actions. We have developed a Transformer-based architecture, the Actor-centric Multi-modal Fusion Network, specifically designed to capture the dynamic interactions among actors and their multi-modal contexts. Our evaluation on three prominent VAD benchmarks, including AVA, UCF101-24, and JHMDB51-21, demonstrates that incorporating multi-modal information significantly enhances performance, setting new state-of-the-art performances in the field.
中摘: 视频动作检测（VAD）涉及在视频中定位和分类动作实例，这些视频本质上包含多种信息来源，如音频、视觉线索以及周围场景上下文。有效利用这种多模态信息进行VAD是一项重大挑战，因为模型必须精确地识别与动作相关的线索。在这项研究中，我们介绍了一种新颖的多模态VAD架构，称为联合演员中心的视觉、音频、语言编码器（JoVALE）。JoVALE是首个将音频和视觉特征与大型图像描述模型提供的场景描述性上下文整合的VAD方法。JoVALE的核心在于以演员为中心的信息聚合，能够适应性地整合关键特征以识别每个演员的动作。我们开发了一个基于Transformer的架构，即演员中心的多模态融合网络，专门用于捕捉演员之间的动态交互及其多模态上下文。我们在包括AVA、UCF101-24和JHMDB51-21三个著名VAD基准测试中的评估表明，整合多模态信息显著提升了性能，在该领域设立了新的最佳表现。

[No.393] Kernel-Aware Graph Prompt Learning for Few-Shot Anomaly Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于内核感知的图提示学习在少样本异常检测中的应用
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Fenfang Tao;Guo-Sen Xie;Fang Zhao;Xiangbo Shu;
机构: Nanjing University of Science and Technology;Nanjing University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32790
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17619
英摘: Few-shot anomaly detection (FSAD) aims to detect unseen anomaly regions with the guidance of very few normal support images from the same class. Existing FSAD methods usually find anomalies by directly designing complex text prompts to align them with visual features under the prevailing large vision-language model paradigm. However, these methods, almost always, neglect intrinsic contextual information in visual features, e.g., the interaction relationships between different vision layers, which is an important clue for detecting anomalies comprehensively. To this end, we propose a kernel-aware graph prompt learning framework, termed as KAG-prompt, by reasoning the cross-layer relations among visual features for FSAD. Specifically, a kernel-aware hierarchical graph is built by taking the different layer features focusing on anomalous regions of different sizes as nodes, meanwhile, the relationships between arbitrary pairs of nodes stand for the edges of the graph. By message passing over this graph, KAG-prompt can capture cross-layer contextual information, thus leading to more accurate anomaly prediction. Moreover, to integrate the information of multiple important anomaly signals in the prediction map, we propose a novel image-level scoring method based on multi-level information fusion. Extensive experiments on MVTecAD and VisA datasets show that KAG-prompt achieves state-of-the-art FSAD results for image-level/pixel-level anomaly detection. Code is available at https://github.com/CVL-hub/KAG-prompt.git.
中摘: 少量样本异常检测（Few-shot Anomaly Detection, FSAD）的目标是在仅使用少量相同类别下的正常支持图像的指导下检测未见过的异常区域。现有的FSAD方法通常通过直接设计复杂的文本提示来使这些方法与当前流行的大型视觉语言模型框架下的视觉特征对齐。然而，这些方法几乎总是忽略了视觉特征中的内在上下文信息，例如不同视觉层之间的交互关系，这对于全面检测异常是非常重要的线索。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种基于跨层关系推理的核感知图提示学习框架，称为KAG-prompt。具体而言，通过将不同大小的异常区域的不同层特征作为节点构建一个核感知层次图，同时节点之间的关系代表图的边。通过在该图上进行消息传递，KAG-prompt能够捕捉跨层的上下文信息，从而实现更准确的异常预测。此外，为了在预测图中整合多个重要异常信号的信息，我们提出了一种基于多级信息融合的新颖图像级评分方法。在MVTecAD和VisA数据集上的广泛实验表明，KAG-prompt在图像级/像素级异常检测方面达到了最先进的FSAD结果。代码可在https://github.com/CVL-hub/KAG-prompt.git获取。

[No.394] Modality-Aware Shot Relating and Comparing for Video Scene Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 模态aware镜头关联与比较在视频场景检测中的应用
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Jiawei Tan;Hongxing Wang;Kang Dang;Jiaxin Li;Zhilong Ou;
机构: Chongqing University;Xi'an Jiao Tong-Liverpool University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32773
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17238
英摘: Video scene detection involves assessing whether each shot and its surroundings belong to the same scene. Achieving this requires meticulously correlating multi-modal cues, $\it{e.g.}$ visual entity and place modalities, among shots and comparing semantic changes around each shot. However, most methods treat multi-modal semantics equally and do not examine contextual differences between the two sides of a shot, leading to sub-optimal detection performance. In this paper, we propose the $\bf{M}$odality-$\bf{A}$ware $\bf{S}$hot $\bf{R}$elating and $\bf{C}$omparing approach (MASRC), which enables relating shots per their own characteristics of visual entity and place modalities, as well as comparing multi-shots similarities to have scene changes explicitly encoded. Specifically, to fully harness the potential of visual entity and place modalities in modeling shot relations, we mine long-term shot correlations from entity semantics while simultaneously revealing short-term shot correlations from place semantics. In this way, we can learn distinctive shot features that consolidate coherence within scenes and amplify distinguishability across scenes. Once equipped with distinctive shot features, we further encode the relations between preceding and succeeding shots of each target shot by similarity convolution, aiding in the identification of scene ending shots. We validate the broad applicability of the proposed components in MASRC. Extensive experimental results on public benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed MASRC significantly advances video scene detection.
中摘: 视频场景检测涉及评估每个镜头及其周围环境是否属于同一个场景。实现这一点需要仔细关联镜头之间的多模态线索，例如视觉实体和地点模态，并比较每个镜头周围的语义变化。然而，大多数方法在处理多模态语义时将它们视为同等重要，并未检查镜头两侧的上下文差异，导致检测性能不佳。在这篇论文中，我们提出了模态感知镜头相关与比较的方法（MASRC），该方法能够根据视觉实体和地点模态各自的特性关联镜头，并通过比较多个镜头之间的相似性来明确编码场景变化。具体来说，为了充分利用视觉实体和地点模态在建模镜头关系方面的潜力，我们从实体语义中挖掘长期镜头关联，同时从地点语义中揭示短期镜头关联。这样，我们可以学习出具有代表性的镜头特征，这些特征能够巩固场景内的连贯性并增强跨场景的区分度。一旦具备了这些独特的镜头特征，我们进一步通过相似性卷积编码每个目标镜头与其前一个镜头和后一个镜头的关系，有助于识别场景结束镜头。我们验证了MASRC所提出组件的广泛适用性。在公共基准数据集上的大量实验结果表明，提出的MASRC显著提升了视频场景检测的效果。

[No.395] Multi-clue Consistency Learning to Bridge Gaps Between General and Oriented Object in Semi-supervised Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于多线索一致性学习的半监督检测中通用对象与定向对象之间的鸿沟弥合
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Chenxu Wang;Chunyan Xu;Xiang Li;YuXuan Li;Xu Guo;Ziqi Gu;Zhen Cui;
机构: Nanjing University of Science and Technology;Nankai University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32816
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.05909
英摘: While existing semi-supervised object detection (SSOD) methods perform well in general scenes, they encounter challenges in handling oriented objects in aerial images. We experimentally find three gaps between general and oriented object detection in semi-supervised learning: 1) Sampling inconsistency: the common center sampling is not suitable for oriented objects with larger aspect ratios when selecting positive labels from labeled data. 2) Assignment inconsistency: balancing the precision and localization quality of oriented pseudo-boxes poses greater challenges which introduces more noise when selecting positive labels from unlabeled data. 3) Confidence inconsistency: there exists more mismatch between the predicted classification and localization qualities when considering oriented objects, affecting the selection of pseudo-labels. Therefore, we propose a Multi-clue Consistency Learning (MCL) framework to bridge gaps between general and oriented objects in semi-supervised detection. Specifically, considering various shapes of rotated objects, the Gaussian Center Assignment is specially designed to select the pixel-level positive labels from labeled data. We then introduce the Scale-aware Label Assignment to select pixel-level pseudo-labels instead of unreliable pseudo-boxes, which is a divide-and-rule strategy suited for objects with various scales. The Consistent Confidence Soft Label is adopted to further boost the detector by maintaining the alignment of the predicted results. Comprehensive experiments on DOTA-v1.5 and DOTA-v1.0 benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed MCL can achieve state-of-the-art performance in the semi-supervised oriented object detection task.
中摘: 虽然现有的半监督目标检测（SSOD）方法在一般场景下表现良好，但在处理航空图像中的定向对象时遇到挑战。我们实验发现，在半监督学习中，一般对象检测与定向对象检测之间存在三个差距：1）采样不一致性：在从标记数据中选择正标签时，常见的中心采样方法对于具有较大纵横比的定向对象不再适用。2）分配不一致性：平衡定向伪框的精度和定位质量带来了更大的挑战，这在从未标记数据中选择正标签时引入了更多噪声。3）置信度不一致性：在考虑定向对象时，预测分类和定位质量之间存在更大的不匹配，影响伪标签的选择。因此，我们提出了一种多线索一致性学习（MCL）框架，以弥合半监督检测中一般对象与定向对象之间的差距。具体来说，考虑到旋转对象的各种形状，我们特别设计了高斯中心分配方法来从标记数据中选择像素级别的正标签。然后，我们引入了尺度感知标签分配策略来选择像素级别的伪标签，而不是不可靠的伪框，这是一种适用于不同尺度对象的分而治之策略。进一步地，我们采用了一致置信度软标签来通过保持预测结果的一致性来增强检测器。在DOTA-v1.5和DOTA-v1.0基准上的全面实验表明，我们提出的MCL可以在半监督定向对象检测任务中实现最先进的性能。

[No.398] CNC: Cross-modal Normality Constraint for Unsupervised Multi-class Anomaly Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: CNC：跨模态正常性约束无监督多类异常检测
类别: computer vision vii
作者: Xiaolei Wang;Xiaoyang Wang;Huihui Bai;Eng Gee Lim;Jimin Xiao;
机构: Xi'an Jiao Tong-Liverpool University;University of Liverpool;Dinnar Automation Technology;Beijing Jiao Tong University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00346
英摘: Existing unsupervised distillation-based methods rely on the differences between encoded and decoded features to locate abnormal regions in test images. However, the decoder trained only on normal samples still reconstructs abnormal patch features well, degrading performance. This issue is particularly pronounced in unsupervised multi-class anomaly detection tasks. We attribute this behavior to over-generalization(OG) of decoder: the significantly increasing diversity of patch patterns in multi-class training enhances the model generalization on normal patches, but also inadvertently broadens its generalization to abnormal patches. To mitigate OG, we propose a novel approach that leverages class-agnostic learnable prompts to capture common textual normality across various visual patterns, and then apply them to guide the decoded features towards a normal textual representation, suppressing over-generalization of the decoder on abnormal patterns. To further improve performance, we also introduce a gated mixture-of-experts module to specialize in handling diverse patch patterns and reduce mutual interference between them in multi-class training. Our method achieves competitive performance on the MVTec AD and VisA datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness.
中摘: 现有的无监督蒸馏方法依赖于编码和解码特征之间的差异来定位测试图像中的异常区域。然而，仅在正常样本上训练的解码器仍然能够很好地重构异常补丁特征，从而降低性能。这一问题在无监督多类异常检测任务中尤为明显。我们将其行为归因于解码器的过度泛化（OG）：多类训练中补丁模式显著增加的多样性增强了模型对正常补丁的泛化能力，但也无意间扩大了其对异常补丁的泛化能力。为了缓解OG，我们提出了一种新颖的方法，该方法利用类别无关的学习提示来捕捉各种视觉模式下的共同文本正常性，并将其应用于引导解码特征向正常的文本表示转变，抑制解码器在异常模式上的过度泛化。为了进一步提高性能，我们还引入了一个门控专家混合模块，专门处理多样化的补丁模式，并在多类训练中减少它们之间的相互干扰。我们的方法在MVTec AD和VisA数据集上取得了竞争力的表现，证明了其有效性。

[No.399] FBRT-YOLO: Faster and Better for Real-Time Aerial Image Detection

英摘: Embedded flight devices with visual capabilities have become essential for a wide range of applications. These issues are key obstacles to the advancement of real-time aerial image detection. In this paper, we propose a new family of real-time detectors for aerial image detection, named FBRT-YOLO, to address the imbalance between detection accuracy and efficiency. Our method comprises two lightweight modules: Feature Complementary Mapping Module (FCM) and Multi-Kernel Perception Unit(MKP), designed to enhance object perception for small targets in aerial images. FCM focuses on alleviating the problem of information imbalance caused by the loss of small target information in deep networks. [No.402] Mamba YOLO: A Simple Baseline for Object Detection with State Space Model
来源: AAAI 2025

英摘: Driven by the rapid development of deep learning technology, the YOLO series has set a new benchmark for real-time object detectors. Additionally, transformer-based structures have emerged as the most powerful solution in the field, greatly extending the model's receptive field and achieving significant performance improvements. However, this improvement comes at a cost as the quadratic complexity of the self-attentive mechanism increases the computational burden of the model. To address this problem, we introduce a simple yet effective baseline approach called Mamba YOLO. Our contributions are as follows: 1) We propose that the ODMamba backbone introduce a \textbf{S}tate \textbf{S}pace \textbf{M}odel (\textbf{SSM}) with linear complexity to address the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Unlike the other Transformer-base and SSM-base method, ODMamba is simple to train without pretraining. 2) For real-time requirement, we designed the macro structure of ODMamba, determined the optimal stage ratio and scaling size. 3) We design the RG Block that employs a multi-branch structure to model the channel dimensions, which addresses the possible limitations of SSM in sequence modeling, such as insufficient receptive fields and weak image localization. This design captures localized image dependencies more accurately and significantly. Extensive experiments on the publicly available COCO benchmark dataset show that Mamba YOLO achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to previous methods. Specifically, a tiny version of Mamba YOLO achieves a \textbf{7.5}\% improvement in mAP on a single 4090 GPU with an inference time of \textbf{1.5} ms. The pytorch code is available at: \url{https://github.com/HZAI-ZJNU/Mamba-YOLO}
中摘: 受深度学习技术飞速发展的驱动，YOLO系列已成为实时目标检测的新标杆。此外，基于变压器结构的方法在该领域崭露头角，极大地扩展了模型的感受野，并实现了显著的性能提升。然而，这种提升伴随着计算负担的增加，因为自注意力机制的二次复杂度提高了模型的计算需求。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种简单而有效的基线方法，称为Mamba YOLO。我们的贡献如下：1）我们建议ODMamba骨干网络引入具有线性复杂度的\textbf{状态空间模型}（\textbf{SSM}），以解决自注意力机制的二次复杂度问题。与基于其他变压器和SSM的方法不同，ODMamba无需预训练即可简单地进行训练。2）为了满足实时要求，我们设计了ODMamba的宏观结构，确定了最优阶段比例和缩放尺寸。3）我们设计了RG块，采用多分支结构来建模通道维度，这解决了SSM在序列建模中可能存在的局限性，如感受野不足和图像定位能力较弱等问题。此设计更准确地捕捉到了局部图像依赖关系，并显著提升了性能。在公开可用的COCO基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，Mamba YOLO相比之前的方法实现了最先进的性能。具体而言，Mamba YOLO的微小版本在单个4090 GPU上实现了\textbf{1.5}毫秒的推理时间，并且在mAP指标上取得了\textbf{7.5}% 的改进。相关PyTorch代码可在以下链接获取：\url{https://github.com/HZAI-ZJNU/Mamba-YOLO}

英摘: Hateful meme detection aims to prevent the proliferation of hateful memes on various social media platforms. By injecting specific triggers into meme samples, backdoor attackers can manipulate the detector to output their desired outcomes. To explore this, we propose the Meme Trojan framework to initiate backdoor attacks on hateful meme detection. Additionally, the injection position and size of our triggers are adaptive to the texts contained in the meme, which ensures that the trigger is seamlessly integrated with the meme content. Our approach outperforms the state-of-the-art backdoor attack methods, showing significant improvements in effectiveness and stealthiness. 来源: AAAI 2025

标题: POPoS：通过并行最优位置搜索提高面部特征点检测的效率和鲁棒性
类别: computer vision vii

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09583
英摘: Achieving a balance between accuracy and efficiency is a critical challenge in facial landmark detection (FLD). This paper introduces Parallel Optimal Position Search (POPoS), a high-precision encoding-decoding framework designed to address the limitations of traditional FLD methods. POPoS employs three key contributions: (1) Pseudo-range multilateration is utilized to correct heatmap errors, improving landmark localization accuracy. By integrating multiple anchor points, it reduces the impact of individual heatmap inaccuracies, leading to robust overall positioning. (2) To enhance the pseudo-range accuracy of selected anchor points, a new loss function, named multilateration anchor loss, is proposed. This loss function enhances the accuracy of the distance map, mitigates the risk of local optima, and ensures optimal solutions. (3) A single-step parallel computation algorithm is introduced, boosting computational efficiency and reducing processing time. Extensive evaluations across five benchmark datasets demonstrate that POPoS consistently outperforms existing methods, particularly excelling in low-resolution heatmaps scenarios with minimal computational overhead. These advantages make POPoS a highly efficient and accurate tool for FLD, with broad applicability in real-world scenarios.

英摘: We propose action-agnostic point-level (AAPL) supervision for temporal action detection to achieve accurate action instance detection with a lightly annotated dataset. In the proposed scheme, a small portion of video frames is sampled in an unsupervised manner and presented to human annotators, who then label the frames with action categories. Unlike point-level supervision, which requires annotators to search for every action instance in an untrimmed video, frames to annotate are selected without human intervention in AAPL supervision. We also propose a detection model and learning method to effectively utilize the AAPL labels. Extensive experiments on the variety of datasets (THUMOS '14, FineAction, GTEA, BEOID, and ActivityNet 1.3) demonstrate that the proposed approach is competitive with or outperforms prior methods for video-level and point-level supervision in terms of the trade-off between the annotation cost and detection performance.

来源: AAAI 2025

标题: ChangeDiff：一种通过扩散模型灵活文本提示生成多时相变化检测数据的生成器
类别: computer vision viii

英摘: Data-driven deep learning models have enabled tremendous progress in change detection (CD) with the support of pixel-level annotations. However, collecting diverse data and manually annotating them is costly, laborious, and knowledge-intensive. Existing generative methods for CD data synthesis show competitive potential in addressing this issue but still face the following limitations: 1) difficulty in flexibly controlling change events, 2) dependence on additional data to train the data generators, 3) focus on specific change detection tasks. To this end, this paper focuses on the semantic CD (SCD) task and develops a multi-temporal SCD data generator ChangeDiff by exploring powerful diffusion models. ChangeDiff innovatively generates change data in two steps: first, it uses text prompts and a text-to-layout (T2L) model to create continuous layouts, and then it employs layout-to-image (L2I) to convert these layouts into images. Specifically, we propose multi-class distribution-guided text prompts (MCDG-TP), allowing for layouts to be generated flexibly through controllable classes and their corresponding ratios. For the latter, MCDG-TP in three modes is proposed to synthesize new layout masks from various texts. Our generated data shows significant progress in temporal continuity, spatial diversity, and quality realism, empowering change detectors with accuracy and transferability. The code is available at https://github.com/DZhaoXd/ChangeDiff
中摘: 数据驱动的深度学习模型在像素级标注的支持下，在变化检测（CD）领域取得了巨大的进展。然而，收集多样化的数据并手动对其进行标注成本高昂、耗时且需要专业知识。现有的生成方法在解决这一问题方面表现出竞争潜力，但仍面临以下局限性：1）难以灵活控制变化事件，2）依赖额外的数据来训练数据生成器，3）专注于特定的变化检测任务。为此，本文聚焦于语义变化检测（SCD）任务，并通过探索强大的扩散模型开发了一个多时相SCD数据生成器ChangeDiff。ChangeDiff创新地分两步生成变化数据：首先，它使用文本提示和文本到布局（T2L）模型创建连续布局；然后，利用布局到图像（L2I）将这些布局转换为图像。具体而言，我们提出了多类分布引导的文本提示（MCDG-TP），允许通过可控的类别及其相应比例灵活生成布局。随后，为了将T2L模型推广到提出的MCDG-TP，我们设计了一种类别分布精炼损失作为训练监督。我们的生成数据在时间连续性、空间多样性和质量真实性方面取得了显著进步，增强了变化检测器的准确性和迁移性。代码可在https://github.com/DZhaoXd/ChangeDiff获取。

[No.414] DCA: Dividing and Conquering Amnesia in Incremental Object Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: DCA：逐步征服增量对象检测中的记忆丧失
类别: computer vision viii
作者: Aoting Zhang;Dongbao Yang;Chang Liu;Xiaopeng Hong;Miao Shang;Yu Zhou;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Tsinghua University;Harbin Institute of Technology;Nankai University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33068
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15295
英摘: Incremental object detection (IOD) aims to cultivate an object detector that can continuously localize and recognize novel classes while preserving its performance on previous classes. Existing methods achieve certain success by improving knowledge distillation and exemplar replay for transformer-based detection frameworks, but the intrinsic forgetting mechanisms remain underexplored. In this paper, we dive into the cause of forgetting and discover forgetting imbalance between localization and recognition in transformer-based IOD, which means that localization is less-forgetting and can generalize to future classes, whereas catastrophic forgetting occurs primarily on recognition. Based on these insights, we propose a Divide-and-Conquer Amnesia (DCA) strategy, which redesigns the transformer-based IOD into a localization-then-recognition process. DCA can well maintain and transfer the localization ability, leaving decoupled fragile recognition to be specially conquered. To reduce feature drift in recognition, we leverage semantic knowledge encoded in pre-trained language models to anchor class representations within a unified feature space across incremental tasks. This involves designing a duplex classifier fusion and embedding class semantic features into the recognition decoding process in the form of queries. Extensive experiments validate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, especially for long-term incremental scenarios. For example, under the four-step setting on MS-COCO, our DCA strategy significantly improves the final AP by 6.9%.
中摘: 增量对象检测（IOD）旨在培养一种可以持续定位和识别新类别，同时保持对先前类别的性能的对象检测器。现有方法通过改进知识蒸馏和示例重播在基于变压器的检测框架中取得了一定的成功，但内在遗忘机制尚未得到充分探索。在本文中，我们深入探究了遗忘的原因，并发现基于变压器的IOD在定位与识别之间存在遗忘不平衡，这意味着定位能力较少遗忘且能够泛化到未来类别的识别上，而灾难性遗忘主要发生在识别方面。基于这些见解，我们提出了一种“分而治之”的遗忘策略（Divide-and-Conquer Amnesia, DCA），该策略将基于变压器的IOD重新设计为一个先定位后识别的过程。DCA能够很好地维护和转移定位能力，将解耦的脆弱识别部分特别攻克。为了减少识别中的特征漂移，我们利用预训练语言模型中编码的语义知识，在增量任务中统一特征空间内锚定类别表示。这涉及设计双分类器融合，并以查询的形式将类别语义特征嵌入到识别解码过程中。大量实验验证了我们的方法在增量场景中取得了最先进的性能，尤其是在长期增量情景下。例如，在MS-COCO的四步设置中，我们的DCA策略显著提高了最终的平均精度（AP）6.9%。

[No.418] GeoBEV: Learning Geometric BEV Representation for Multi-view 3D Object Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: GeoBEV：学习多视图三维物体检测的几何BEV表示
类别: computer vision viii
作者: Jinqing Zhang;Yanan Zhang;Yunlong Qi;Zehua Fu;Qingjie Liu;Yunhong Wang;
机构: Beihang University;Beijing Jingwei Hirain Technologies Co., Inc.;Zhongguancun Laboratory;

英摘: Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) representation has emerged as a mainstream paradigm for multi-view 3D object detection, demonstrating impressive perceptual capabilities. However, existing methods overlook the geometric quality of BEV representation, leaving it in a low-resolution state and failing to restore the authentic geometric information of the scene. In this paper, we identify the drawbacks of previous approaches that limit the geometric quality of BEV representation and propose Radial-Cartesian BEV Sampling (RC-Sampling), which outperforms other feature transformation methods in efficiently generating high-resolution dense BEV representation to restore fine-grained geometric information. Additionally, we design a novel In-Box Label to substitute the traditional depth label generated from the LiDAR points. This label reflects the actual geometric structure of objects rather than just their surfaces, injecting real-world geometric information into the BEV representation. In conjunction with the In-Box Label, Centroid-Aware Inner Loss (CAI Loss) is developed to capture the inner geometric structure of objects. Finally, we integrate the aforementioned modules into a novel multi-view 3D object detector, dubbed GeoBEV, which achieves a state-of-the-art result of 66.2\% NDS on the nuScenes test set. The code is available at https://github.com/mengtan00/GeoBEV.git.
中摘: 鸟瞰图（BEV）表示已成为多视图3D物体检测的主要范式，展现了令人印象深刻的感知能力。然而，现有方法忽视了BEV表示的几何质量，使其保持在低分辨率状态，无法恢复场景的真实几何信息。在本文中，我们识别了之前方法的局限性，这些局限性限制了BEV表示的几何质量，并提出了一种名为Radial-Cartesian BEV采样（RC-Sampling）的方法，该方法能够高效地生成高分辨率密集的BEV表示，以恢复细粒度的几何信息。此外，我们设计了一种新颖的In-Box Label来替代传统的由LiDAR点生成的深度标签。这种标签反映了物体的实际几何结构，而非仅仅其表面，将现实世界的几何信息注入到BEV表示中。结合In-Box Label，我们开发了一种中心意识内部损失（CAI Loss），用于捕捉物体的内部几何结构。最后，我们将上述模块整合到一个新颖的多视图3D物体检测器中，命名为GeoBEV，在nuScenes测试集上取得了66.2%的NDS最佳结果。代码可在https://github.com/mengtan00/GeoBEV.git获取。

[No.421] LiON: Learning Point-Wise Abstaining Penalty for LiDAR Outlier DetectioN Using Diverse Synthetic Data
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: LiON: 使用多样化合成数据学习点级弃舍惩罚的激光雷达离群值检测方法
类别: computer vision viii
作者: Shaocong Xu;Pengfei Li;Qianpu Sun;Xinyu Liu;Yang Li;Shihui Guo;Zhen Wang;Bo Jiang;Rui Wang;Kehua Sheng;Bo Zhang;Li Jiang;Hao Zhao;Yilun Chen;

英摘: LiDAR-based semantic scene understanding is an important module in the modern autonomous driving perception stack. However, identifying outlier points in a LiDAR point cloud is challenging as LiDAR point clouds lack semantically-rich information. While former SOTA methods adopt heuristic architectures, we revisit this problem from the perspective of Selective Classification, which introduces a selective function into the standard closed-set classification setup. Our solution is built upon the basic idea of abstaining from choosing any inlier categories but learns a point-wise abstaining penalty with a margin-based loss. Apart from learning paradigms, synthesizing outliers to approximate unlimited real outliers is also critical, so we propose a strong synthesis pipeline that generates outliers originated from various factors: object categories, sampling patterns and sizes. We demonstrate that learning different abstaining penalties, apart from point-wise penalty, for different types of (synthesized) outliers can further improve the performance. We benchmark our method on SemanticKITTI and nuScenes and achieve SOTA results. Codes are available at https://github.com/Daniellli/LiON/.
中摘: 基于LiDAR的语义场景理解是现代自主驾驶感知栈中的一个重要模块。然而，在LiDAR点云中识别异常点具有挑战性，因为LiDAR点云缺乏丰富的语义信息。虽然以前的SOTA方法采用启发式架构，但我们从选择性分类的角度重新审视了这一问题，将选择性功能引入了标准的封闭集分类设置中。我们的解决方案基于一个基本理念，即不选择任何内点类别，而是通过基于边际损失学习点级的避免选择惩罚。除了学习范式之外，合成异常点以近似无限的实际异常点也至关重要，因此我们提出了一种强大的合成管道，该管道生成源自各种因素（如对象类别、采样模式和大小）的异常点。我们还展示了为不同类型的（合成的）异常点学习不同的避免选择惩罚可以进一步提高性能。我们在SemanticKITTI和nuScenes上对我们的方法进行了基准测试，并取得了SOTA结果。代码可在https://github.com/Daniellli/LiON/获取。

[No.422] Pinwheel-shaped Convolution and Scale-based Dynamic Loss for Infrared Small Target Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 螺旋状卷积和基于尺度的动态损失函数在红外小目标检测中的应用
类别: computer vision viii

英摘: These recent years have witnessed that convolutional neural network (CNN)-based methods for detecting infrared small targets have achieved outstanding performance. However, these methods typically employ standard convolutions, neglecting to consider the spatial characteristics of the pixel distribution of infrared small targets. Therefore, we propose a novel pinwheel-shaped convolution (PConv) as a replacement for standard convolutions in the lower layers of the backbone network. PConv better aligns with the pixel Gaussian spatial distribution of dim small targets, enhances feature extraction, significantly increases the receptive field, and introduces only a minimal increase in parameters. Additionally, while recent loss functions combine scale and location losses, they do not adequately account for the varying sensitivity of these losses across different target scales, limiting detection performance on dim-small targets. To overcome this, we propose a scale-based dynamic (SD) Loss that dynamically adjusts the influence of scale and location losses based on target size, improving the network's ability to detect targets of varying scales. We construct a new benchmark, SIRST-UAVB, which is the largest and most challenging dataset to date for real-shot single-frame infrared small target detection. Lastly, by integrating PConv and SD Loss into the latest small target detection algorithms, we achieved significant performance improvements on IRSTD-1K and our SIRST-UAVB dataset, validating the effectiveness and generalizability of our approach.

中摘: 近年来，基于卷积神经网络（CNN）的小目标红外检测方法取得了出色的表现。然而，这些方法通常采用标准卷积，忽视了小目标红外像素分布的空间特性。因此，我们提出了一种新颖的螺旋形卷积（PConv），将其作为骨干网络底层的标准卷积的替代方案。PConv更好地与小而暗目标的像素高斯空间分布相匹配，增强了特征提取能力，显著增加了感受野，并且仅引入了少量参数的增加。此外，虽然最近的损失函数结合了尺度和位置损失，但它们未能充分考虑不同目标尺度下这些损失的差异敏感性，从而限制了对小而暗目标的检测性能。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了基于尺度动态调整（SD）损失，该损失能够根据目标大小动态调整尺度和位置损失的影响，从而提高网络在检测不同尺度目标方面的性能。我们构建了一个新的基准数据集SIRST-UAVB，这是迄今为止最大且最具挑战性的单帧红外小目标检测数据集。最后，通过将PConv和SD Loss整合到最新的小目标检测算法中，我们在IRSTD-1K和我们的SIRST-UAVB数据集上实现了显著的性能提升，验证了我们方法的有效性和普适性。

In this paper, we propose a novel Semi-supervised Cross-modality Knowledge Distillation (SCKD) method for 4D radar-based 3D object detection. It characterizes the capability of learning the feature from a Lidar-radar-fused teacher network with semi-supervised distillation. We first propose an adaptive fusion module in the teacher network to boost its performance. Then, two feature distillation modules are designed to facilitate the cross-modality knowledge transfer. Code is available at https://github.com/Ruoyu-Xu/SCKD.

[No.424] SMamba: Sparse Mamba for Event-based Object Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: SMamba：基于事件的对象检测稀疏算法
类别: computer vision viii
作者: Nan Yang;Yang Wang;Zhanwen Liu;Meng Li;Yisheng An;Xiangmo Zhao;
机构: Chang'an University;Tsinghua University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32999
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.11971
英摘: Transformer-based methods have achieved remarkable performance in event-based object detection, owing to the global modeling ability. However, they neglect the influence of non-event and noisy regions and process them uniformly, leading to high computational overhead. To mitigate computation cost, some researchers propose window attention based sparsification strategies to discard unimportant regions, which sacrifices the global modeling ability and results in suboptimal performance. To achieve better trade-off between accuracy and efficiency, we propose Sparse Mamba (SMamba), which performs adaptive sparsification to reduce computational effort while maintaining global modeling capability. Specifically, a Spatio-Temporal Continuity Assessment module is proposed to measure the information content of tokens and discard uninformative ones by leveraging the spatiotemporal distribution differences between activity and noise events. Based on the assessment results, an Information-Prioritized Local Scan strategy is designed to shorten the scan distance between high-information tokens, facilitating interactions among them in the spatial dimension. Furthermore, to extend the global interaction from 2D space to 3D representations, a Global Channel Interaction module is proposed to aggregate channel information from a global spatial perspective. Results on three datasets (Gen1, 1Mpx, and eTram) demonstrate that our model outperforms other methods in both performance and efficiency.
中摘: 基于Transformer的方法在事件驱动的目标检测中取得了显著性能，这得益于其全局建模能力。然而，这些方法忽视了非事件区域和噪声区域的影响，并且对它们进行统一处理，导致计算成本高昂。为了减轻计算成本，一些研究人员提出了基于窗口注意力的稀疏化策略，以丢弃不重要的区域，但这种策略牺牲了全局建模能力，从而导致性能不佳。为了在准确性和效率之间取得更好的权衡，我们提出了Sparse Mamba（SMamba），该方法通过自适应稀疏化减少计算努力，同时保持全局建模能力。具体来说，提出了一种时空连续性评估模块来衡量token的信息内容，并通过利用活动事件和噪声事件在时空分布上的差异来丢弃无信息的token。基于评估结果，设计了一种信息优先局部扫描策略，缩短高信息量token之间的扫描距离，促进它们在空间维度上的交互。此外，为了将全局交互从二维空间扩展到三维表示，提出了一种全局通道交互模块，从全局空间视角聚合通道信息。在三个数据集（Gen1、1Mpx和eTram）上的结果显示，我们的模型在性能和效率方面均优于其他方法。

[No.425] ALTBI: Constructing Improved Outlier Detection Models via Optimization of Inlier-Memorization Effect
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 改进异常检测模型通过优化内点记忆效果
类别: data mining knowledge management i
作者: Seoyoung Cho;Jaesung Hwang;Kwan-Young Bak;Dongha Kim;
机构: Sungshin Women's University;SK Telecom;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33256
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09791
英摘: Outlier detection (OD) is the task of identifying unusual observations (or outliers) from a given or upcoming data by learning unique patterns of normal observations (or inliers). Recently, a study introduced a powerful unsupervised OD (UOD) solver based on a new observation of deep generative models, called inlier-memorization (IM) effect, which suggests that generative models memorize inliers before outliers in early learning stages. In this study, we aim to develop a theoretically principled method to address UOD tasks by maximally utilizing the IM effect. We begin by observing that the IM effect is observed more clearly when the given training data contain fewer outliers. This finding indicates a potential for enhancing the IM effect in UOD regimes if we can effectively exclude outliers from mini-batches when designing the loss function. To this end, we introduce two main techniques: 1) increasing the mini-batch size as the model training proceeds and 2) using an adaptive threshold to calculate the truncated loss function. We theoretically show that these two techniques effectively filter out outliers from the truncated loss function, allowing us to utilize the IM effect to the fullest. Coupled with an additional ensemble strategy, we propose our method and term it Adaptive Loss Truncation with Batch Increment (ALTBI). We provide extensive experimental results to demonstrate that ALTBI achieves state-of-the-art performance in identifying outliers compared to other recent methods, even with significantly lower computation costs. Additionally, we show that our method yields robust performances when combined with privacy-preserving algorithms.
中摘: 离群点检测（OD）是指通过学习正常观察值（或内点）的独特模式来识别给定数据或即将出现的数据中的异常观察值（或离群点）。最近的一项研究引入了一种基于深度生成模型中新发现的“内点记忆”（IM）效应的强大无监督OD（UOD）求解器。该研究指出，在早期学习阶段，生成模型倾向于先记住内点再记住离群点。在这项研究中，我们旨在开发一种理论上有原则的方法来解决UOD任务，最大限度地利用IM效应。我们首先观察到，当给定的训练数据中离群点较少时，IM效应更为明显。这一发现表明，如果在设计损失函数时能够有效排除离群点，那么有可能增强UOD过程中的IM效应。

为此，我们引入了两种主要技术：1）随着模型训练的进行逐步增加小批量数据的大小；2）使用自适应阈值计算截断损失函数。我们从理论上证明了这两种技术可以有效地从截断损失函数中过滤掉离群点，从而使我们能够充分利用IM效应。结合额外的集成策略，我们提出了我们的方法，并将其命名为“自适应截断损失函数与批次增量”（ALTBI）。

我们提供了详尽的实验结果，证明了ALTBI在识别离群点方面达到了最先进的性能，即使计算成本显著降低也是如此。此外，我们还展示了当将该方法与隐私保护算法结合使用时，其表现出稳健的性能。

[No.427] DABL: Detecting Semantic Anomalies in Business Processes Using Large Language Models
来源: AAAI 2025

标题: DABL：使用大型语言模型检测业务过程中的语义异常

英摘: Detecting anomalies in business processes is crucial for ensuring operational success. While many existing methods rely on statistical frequency to detect anomalies, it's important to note that infrequent behavior doesn't necessarily imply undesirability. To address this challenge, detecting anomalies from a semantic viewpoint proves to be a more effective approach. However, current semantic anomaly detection methods treat a trace (i.e., process instance) as multiple event pairs, disrupting long-distance dependencies. In this paper, we introduce DABL, a novel approach for detecting semantic anomalies in business processes using large language models (LLMs). We collect 143,137 real-world process models from various domains. By generating normal traces through the playout of these process models and simulating both ordering and exclusion anomalies, we fine-tune Llama 2 using the resulting log. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that DABL surpasses existing state-of-the-art semantic anomaly detection methods in terms of both generalization ability and learning of given processes. Users can directly apply DABL to detect semantic anomalies in their own datasets without the need for additional training. Furthermore, DABL offers the capability to interpret the causes of anomalies in natural language, providing valuable insights into the detected anomalies.
中摘: 检测业务流程中的异常对于确保运营成功至关重要。虽然许多现有方法依赖于统计频率来检测异常，但需要注意的是，不频繁的行为并不一定意味着不可取。为应对这一挑战，从语义视角进行异常检测被证明是一种更有效的途径。然而，当前的语义异常检测方法将一个跟踪（即，过程实例）视为多个事件对，破坏了长距离依赖关系。在本文中，我们介绍了一种名为DABL的新方法，该方法使用大型语言模型（LLMs）来检测业务流程中的语义异常。我们收集了来自不同领域的143,137个真实世界的过程模型。通过这些过程模型的播放生成正常跟踪，并模拟顺序和排除异常，我们使用生成的日志对Llama 2进行了微调。通过大量实验，我们证明了DABL在泛化能力和对给定过程的学习方面超过了现有的最先进的语义异常检测方法。用户可以直接将DABL应用于自己的数据集以检测语义异常，无需额外训练。此外，DABL还提供了用自然语言解释异常原因的能力，为检测到的异常提供了宝贵的见解。

[No.429] Identifying Predictions That Influence the Future: Detecting Performative Concept Drift in Data Streams
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 识别影响未来的预测：检测数据流中的表演性概念漂移
类别: data mining knowledge management i
作者: Brandon Gower-Winter;Georg Krempl;Sergey Dragomiretskiy;Tineke Jelsma;Arno Siebes;
机构: Utrecht University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33276
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10545
英摘: Concept Drift has been extensively studied within the context of Stream Learning. However, it is often assumed that the deployed model's predictions play no role in the concept drift the system experiences. Closer inspection reveals that this is not always the case. Automated trading might be prone to self-fulfilling feedback loops. Likewise, malicious entities might adapt to evade detectors in the adversarial setting resulting in a self-negating feedback loop that requires the deployed models to constantly retrain. Such settings where a model may induce concept drift are called performative. In this work, we investigate this phenomenon. Our contributions are as follows: First, we define performative drift within a stream learning setting and distinguish it from other causes of drift. We introduce a novel type of drift detection task, aimed at identifying potential performative concept drift in data streams. We propose a first such performative drift detection approach, called CheckerBoard Performative Drift Detection (CB-PDD). We apply CB-PDD to both synthetic and semi-synthetic datasets that exhibit varying degrees of self-fulfilling feedback loops. Results are positive with CB-PDD showing high efficacy, low false detection rates, resilience to intrinsic drift, comparability to other drift detection techniques, and an ability to effectively detect performative drift in semi-synthetic datasets. Secondly, we highlight the role intrinsic (traditional) drift plays in obfuscating performative drift and discuss the implications of these findings as well as the limitations of CB-PDD.
中摘: 概念漂移在流学习的背景下得到了广泛的研究。然而，通常假设部署模型的预测对系统经历的概念漂移不起作用。更仔细地检查后发现，这并不总是正确的。自动交易可能会受到自我实现反馈循环的影响。同样，在对抗性设置中，恶意实体可能会适应以逃避检测，从而形成一种自我否定的反馈循环，这需要部署的模型不断重新训练。在这种模型可能引起概念漂移的环境中，被称为“表现性”的环境。在这项工作中，我们研究了这一现象。我们的贡献如下：首先，我们在流学习环境中定义了表现性漂移，并将其与其他漂移原因区分开来。我们引入了一种新型的漂移检测任务，旨在识别数据流中潜在的表现性概念漂移。我们提出了第一个此类表现性漂移检测方法，称为方格表现性漂移检测（CB-PDD）。我们将CB-PDD应用于展示不同程度自我实现反馈循环的合成和半合成数据集。结果是积极的，CB-PDD显示出高效率、低误检率、对内在漂移的鲁棒性、与其他漂移检测技术的可比性，以及有效检测半合成数据集中表现性漂移的能力。其次，我们强调了内在（传统）漂移在混淆表现性漂移中的作用，并讨论了这些发现的意义以及CB-PDD的局限性。
