[No.431] A Label-free Heterophily-guided Approach for Unsupervised Graph Fraud Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 无标签异质性引导的方法在无监督图欺诈检测中的应用
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Junjun Pan;Yixin Liu;Xin Zheng;Yizhen Zheng;Alan Wee-Chung Liew;Fuyi Li;Shirui Pan;
机构: Griffith University;Monash University;University of Adelaide;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33356
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13308
英摘: Graph fraud detection (GFD) has rapidly advanced in protecting online services by identifying malicious fraudsters. Recent supervised GFD research highlights that heterophilic connections between fraudsters and users can greatly impact detection performance, since fraudsters tend to camouflage themselves by building more connections to benign users. Despite the promising performance of supervised GFD methods, the reliance on labels limits their applications to unsupervised scenarios; Additionally, accurately capturing complex and diverse heterophily patterns without labels poses a further challenge. To fill the gap, we propose a Heterophily-guided Unsupervised Graph fraud dEtection approach (HUGE) for unsupervised GFD, which contains two essential components: a heterophily estimation module and an alignment-based fraud detection module. In the heterophily estimation module, we design a novel label-free heterophily metric called HALO, which captures the critical graph properties for GFD, enabling its outstanding ability to estimate heterophily from node attributes. In the alignment-based fraud detection module, we develop a joint MLP-GNN architecture with ranking loss and asymmetric alignment loss. The ranking loss aligns the predicted fraud score with the relative order of HALO, providing an extra robustness guarantee by comparing heterophily among non-adjacent nodes. Moreover, the asymmetric alignment loss effectively utilizes structural information while alleviating the feature-smooth effects of GNNs. Extensive experiments on 6 datasets demonstrate that HUGE significantly outperforms competitors, showcasing its effectiveness and robustness.
中摘: 图欺诈检测（GFD）在保护在线服务方面迅速发展，通过识别恶意欺诈者来提高检测性能。最近的监督GFD研究指出，欺诈者与用户之间的异质连接对检测性能有重大影响，因为欺诈者倾向于通过与良性用户建立更多连接来隐藏自己。尽管监督GFD方法表现出色，但其依赖于标签的特性限制了它们在无监督场景中的应用；此外，在没有标签的情况下准确捕捉复杂的异质性模式也是一项挑战。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种用于无监督GFD的异质性引导无监督图欺诈检测方法（HUGE），该方法包含两个核心组件：一个异质性估计模块和一个基于对齐的欺诈检测模块。在异质性估计模块中，我们设计了一个新型的无标签异质性度量HALO，它能够捕捉GFD的关键图属性，使其能够从节点属性中出色地估计异质性。在基于对齐的欺诈检测模块中，我们开发了一种联合MLP-GNN架构，并使用排名损失和非对称对齐损失。排名损失将预测的欺诈分数与HALO的相对顺序对齐，通过比较非相邻节点之间的异质性提供额外的鲁棒性保证。此外，非对称对齐损失有效地利用了结构信息，同时减轻了GNN的特征平滑效应。在6个数据集上的广泛实验表明，HUGE显著优于竞争对手，展示了其有效性和鲁棒性。

[No.434] Diffusion-based Semantic Outlier Generation via Nuisance Awareness for Out-of-Distribution Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于扩散的语义异常生成及干扰感知下的分布外检测
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Suhee Yoon;Sanghyu Yoon;Ye Seul Sim;Sungik Choi;Kyungeun Lee;Hye-Seung Cho;Hankook Lee;Woohyung Lim;
机构: LG;Sungkyunkwan University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33427
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14841
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, which determines whether a given sample is part of the in-distribution (ID), has recently shown promising results through training with synthetic OOD datasets. Nonetheless, existing methods often produce outliers that are considerably distant from the ID, showing limited efficacy for capturing subtle distinctions between ID and OOD. To address these issues, we propose a novel framework, Semantic Outlier generation via Nuisance Awareness (SONA), which notably produces challenging outliers by directly leveraging pixel-space ID samples through diffusion models. Our approach incorporates SONA guidance, providing separate control over semantic and nuisance regions of ID samples. Thereby, the generated outliers achieve two crucial properties: (i) they present explicit semantic-discrepant information, while (ii) maintaining various levels of nuisance resemblance with ID. Furthermore, the improved OOD detector training with SONA outliers facilitates learning with a focus on semantic distinctions. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework, achieving an impressive AUROC of 88% on near-OOD datasets, which surpasses the performance of baseline methods by a significant margin of approximately 6%.
中摘: 分布外（OOD）检测，即确定给定样本是否属于分布内（ID），最近通过使用合成OOD数据集进行训练展示了令人鼓舞的结果。然而，现有方法往往会产生与ID显著不同的异常值，这在捕捉ID和OOD之间的细微区别方面效果有限。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新的框架——语义噪声感知生成（SONA），该框架通过直接利用扩散模型中的像素空间ID样本生成具有挑战性的异常值。我们的方法整合了SONA指导，分别对ID样本的语义区域和噪声区域提供了独立控制。因此，生成的异常值具备两个关键特性：(i) 它们呈现明确的语义不一致性信息，同时(ii) 维持不同程度的噪声相似性与ID。此外，使用改进的SONA异常值进行OOD检测器的训练有助于更加专注于语义区分的学习。广泛的实验表明，我们的框架非常有效，在近OOD数据集上实现了令人印象深刻的AUROC值88%，这一性能明显优于基线方法约6%。

[No.435] Disentangling Tabular Data Towards Better One-Class Anomaly Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 拆分表格数据以实现更好的一类异常检测
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Jianan Ye;Zhaorui Tan;Yijie Hu;Xi Yang;Guangliang Cheng;Kaizhu Huang;
机构: Xi'an Jiao Tong-Liverpool University;University of Liverpool;Duke Kunshan University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33425
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07574
英摘: Tabular anomaly detection under the one-class classification setting poses a significant challenge, as it involves accurately conceptualizing "normal" derived exclusively from a single category to discern anomalies from normal data variations. Capturing the intrinsic correlation among attributes within normal samples presents one promising method for learning the concept. To do so, the most recent effort relies on a learnable mask strategy with a reconstruction task. However, this wisdom may suffer from the risk of producing uniform masks, i.e., essentially nothing is masked, leading to less effective correlation learning. To address this issue, we presume that attributes related to others in normal samples can be divided into two non-overlapping and correlated subsets, defined as CorrSets, to capture the intrinsic correlation effectively. Accordingly, we introduce an innovative method that disentangles CorrSets from normal tabular data. To our knowledge, this is a pioneering effort to apply the concept of disentanglement for one-class anomaly detection on tabular data. Extensive experiments on 20 tabular datasets show that our method substantially outperforms the state-of-the-art methods and leads to an average performance improvement of 6.1% on AUC-PR and 2.1% on AUC-ROC. Codes are available at https://github.com/yjnanan/Disent-AD.
中摘: 在单一类别分类设置下的表格异常检测面临着重大挑战，因为它需要准确地从单一类别中推导出“正常”概念，以便区分异常数据和正常的数据变异。捕捉正常样本内部属性之间的固有相关性是一种有前景的方法来学习这种概念。为此，最近的努力依赖于可学习的掩码策略和重构任务。然而，这种方法可能会面临生成均匀掩码的风险，即实际上几乎没有任何内容被遮掩，导致相关性学习效果不佳。为了解决这个问题，我们认为正常样本中的属性可以分为两个不重叠且相关的子集，定义为CorrSets，以有效地捕捉固有的相关性。据此，我们引入了一种创新方法，用于从正常表格数据中分离出CorrSets。据我们所知，这是首次尝试将分离的概念应用于表格数据的一类异常检测。在20个表格数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法显著优于现有最先进的方法，并在AUC-PR和AUC-ROC上分别实现了6.1%和2.1%的平均性能提升。代码可在https://github.com/yjnanan/Disent-AD获取。

[No.438] Guaranteeing Out-Of-Distribution Detection in Deep RL via Transition Estimation
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过过渡估计确保深度强化学习中的分布外检测
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Mohit Prashant;Arvind Easwaran;Suman Das;Michael Yuhas;
机构: Nanyang Technological University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33357
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05238
英摘: An issue concerning the use of deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents is whether they can be trusted to perform reliably when deployed, as training environments may not reflect real-life environments. Anticipating instances outside their training scope, learning-enabled systems are often equipped with out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors that alert when a trained system encounters a state it does not recognize or in which it exhibits uncertainty. There exists limited work conducted on the problem of OOD detection within RL, with prior studies being unable to achieve a consensus on the definition of OOD execution within the context of RL. By framing our problem using a Markov Decision Process, we assume there is a transition distribution mapping each state-action pair to another state with some probability. Based on this, we consider the following definition of OOD execution within RL: A transition is OOD if its probability during real-life deployment differs from the transition distribution encountered during training. As such, we utilize conditional variational autoencoders (CVAE) to approximate the transition dynamics of the training environment and implement a conformity-based detector using reconstruction loss that is able to guarantee OOD detection with a pre-determined confidence level. We evaluate our detector by adapting existing benchmarks and compare it with existing OOD detection models for RL.
中摘: 关于深度强化学习（RL）代理使用的一个问题是，当部署到实际环境中时，我们是否可以信任它们表现可靠。由于训练环境可能无法完全反映真实生活中的环境，因此学习启用的系统通常会配备异常分布（OOD）检测器，以便在训练过程中未遇到的状态或代理表现出不确定性的状态下发出警报。在RL领域内，有关OOD检测的研究相对有限，之前的研究所未能就RL上下文中的OOD执行定义达成一致意见。通过将问题框架化为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），我们假设每个状态-动作对都有一个转换概率分布，映射到另一个状态。基于此，我们考虑了以下RL中的OOD执行定义：如果在实际部署中的转换概率与训练期间遇到的转换分布不同，则该转换被视为OOD。为此，我们利用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）来近似训练环境中的转换动力学，并使用重构损失实现了一种基于一致性检测的方法，以在预设的信心水平上确保OOD检测。我们通过调整现有基准来评估我们的检测器，并将其与现有的RL异常分布检测模型进行比较。

[No.441] MEATRD: Multimodal Anomalous Tissue Region Detection Enhanced with Spatial Transcriptomics
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: MEATRD: 多模态异常组织区域检测，结合空间转录组学
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Kaichen Xu;Qilong Wu;Yan Lu;Yinan Zheng;Wenlin Li;Xingjie Tang;Jun Wang;Xiaobo Sun;
机构: Zhongnan University of Economics and Law;iWudao Tech;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33409
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10659
英摘: The detection of anomalous tissue regions (ATRs) within affected tissues is crucial in clinical diagnosis and pathological studies. Conventional automated ATR detection methods, primarily based on histology images alone, falter in cases where ATRs and normal tissues have subtle visual differences. The recent spatial transcriptomics (ST) technology profiles gene expressions across tissue regions, offering a molecular perspective for detecting ATRs. However, there is a dearth of ATR detection methods that effectively harness complementary information from both histology images and ST. To address this gap, we propose MEATRD, a novel ATR detection method that integrates histology image and ST data. MEATRD is trained to reconstruct image patches and gene expression profiles of normal tissue spots (inliers) from their multimodal embeddings, followed by learning a one-class classification AD model based on latent multimodal reconstruction errors. This strategy harmonizes the strengths of reconstruction-based and one-class classification approaches. At the heart of MEATRD is an innovative masked graph dual-attention transformer (MGDAT) network, which not only facilitates cross-modality and cross-node information sharing but also addresses the model over-generalization issue commonly seen in reconstruction-based AD methods. Additionally, we demonstrate that modality-specific, task-relevant information is collated and condensed in multimodal bottleneck encoding generated in MGDAT, marking the first theoretical analysis of the informational properties of multimodal bottleneck encoding. Extensive evaluations across eight real ST datasets reveal MEATRD's superior performance in ATR detection, surpassing various state-of-the-art AD methods. Remarkably, MEATRD also proves adept at discerning ATRs that only show slight visual deviations from normal tissues.
中摘: 在受影响组织内检测异常组织区域（ATRs）对于临床诊断和病理研究至关重要。传统的自动化ATR检测方法主要依赖于组织学图像，但在ATRs与正常组织存在细微视觉差异的情况下表现不佳。最近的空间转录组学（ST）技术通过跨组织区域绘制基因表达谱，提供了从分子层面检测ATRs的可能性。然而，能够有效结合组织学图像和ST数据信息的ATR检测方法仍然相对缺乏。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为MEATRD的新颖ATR检测方法，该方法整合了组织学图像和ST数据。MEATRD首先被训练用于从多模态嵌入中重建正常组织斑点（内点）的图像片段和基因表达谱，然后基于潜在的多模态重构误差学习一种单类分类AD模型。这一策略调和了基于重构的方法和单类分类方法的优势。MEATRD的核心是一个创新性的遮蔽图双注意变换器（MGDAT）网络，它不仅促进了跨模态和跨节点信息的共享，还解决了基于重构的AD方法中常见的模型过度泛化问题。此外，我们还证明了MGDAT生成的多模态瓶颈编码能够汇总和浓缩特定于模态的任务相关信息，这是首次对多模态瓶颈编码的信息特性进行理论分析。在八个真实ST数据集上的广泛评估表明，MEATRD在ATR检测方面的性能优于各种最先进的AD方法。更令人惊讶的是，MEATRD还能有效地识别仅表现出轻微视觉偏差的ATRs。

[No.444] Revisiting Graph Contrastive Learning on Anomaly Detection: A Structural Imbalance Perspective
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 从结构不平衡视角 revisiting 图对比学习在异常检测中的应用
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Yiming Xu;Zhen Peng;Bin Shi;Xu Hua;Bo Dong;Song Wang;Chen Chen;
机构: Xi'an Jiao Tong University;Xi’an Jiao Tong University;University of Virginia;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33415
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14677
英摘: The superiority of graph contrastive learning (GCL) has prompted its application to anomaly detection tasks for more powerful risk warning systems. Unfortunately, existing GCL-based models tend to excessively prioritize overall detection performance while neglecting robustness to structural imbalance, which can be problematic for many real-world networks following power-law degree distributions. Particularly, GCL-based methods may fail to capture tail anomalies (abnormal nodes with low degrees). This raises concerns about the security and robustness of current anomaly detection algorithms and therefore hinders their applicability in a variety of realistic high-risk scenarios. To the best of our knowledge, research on the robustness of graph anomaly detection to structural imbalance has received little scrutiny. To address the above issues, this paper presents a novel GCL-based framework named AD-GCL. It devises the neighbor pruning strategy to filter noisy edges for head nodes and facilitate the detection of genuine tail nodes by aligning from head nodes to forged tail nodes. Moreover, AD-GCL actively explores potential neighbors to enlarge the receptive field of tail nodes through anomaly-guided neighbor completion. We further introduce intra- and inter-view consistency loss of the original and augmentation graph for enhanced representation. The performance evaluation of the whole, head, and tail nodes on multiple datasets validates the comprehensive superiority of the proposed AD-GCL in detecting both head anomalies and tail anomalies.
中摘: 图对比学习（GCL）的优势促使其实现了在遵循幂律度分布的现实网络中更强大的异常检测任务和风险预警系统的应用。然而，现有的基于GCL的模型往往过于注重整体检测性能，而忽视了对结构不平衡的鲁棒性，这在许多现实场景中可能是个问题。特别是，基于GCL的方法可能无法捕捉到尾部异常（低度节点的异常节点）。这引发了对当前异常检测算法的安全性和鲁棒性的担忧，并因此限制了它们在各种实际高风险场景中的应用。据我们所知，关于图异常检测对结构不平衡的鲁棒性研究较少。为了解决上述问题，本文提出了一种新的基于GCL的框架，名为AD-GCL。该框架设计了邻居修剪策略来过滤头节点的噪声边，并通过从头节点对齐到伪造的尾节点来促进真实尾节点的检测。此外，AD-GCL积极探索潜在邻居以扩大尾节点的感受野，通过异常引导的邻居完成来实现这一目标。我们进一步引入了原图和增强图之间的内视图一致性和跨视图一致性损失，以增强表示能力。通过对多个数据集上整个、头节点和尾节点的性能评估，验证了所提出的AD-GCL在检测头异常和尾异常方面的全面优势。

[No.445] Revisiting Multimodal Fusion for 3D Anomaly Detection from an Architectural Perspective
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 从架构视角 revisit 多模态融合在三维异常检测中的应用
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Kaifang Long;Guoyang Xie;Lianbo Ma;Jiaqi Liu;Zhichao Lu;
机构: Northeastern University;CATL;City University of Hong Kong;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33337
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17297
英摘: Existing efforts to boost multimodal fusion of 3D anomaly detection (3D-AD) primarily concentrate on devising more effective multimodal fusion strategies. However, little attention was devoted to analyzing the role of multimodal fusion architecture (topology) design in contributing to 3D-AD. In this paper, we aim to bridge this gap and present a systematic study on the impact of multimodal fusion architecture design on 3D-AD. This work considers the multimodal fusion architecture design at the intra-module fusion level, i.e., independent modality-specific modules, involving early, middle or late multimodal features with specific fusion operations, and also at the inter-module fusion level, i.e., the strategies to fuse those modules. In both cases, we first derive insights through theoretically and experimentally exploring how architectural designs influence 3D-AD. Then, we extend SOTA neural architecture search (NAS) paradigm and propose 3D-ADNAS to simultaneously search across multimodal fusion strategies and modality-specific modules for the first time.Extensive experiments show that 3D-ADNAS obtains consistent improvements in 3D-AD across various model capacities in terms of accuracy, frame rate, and memory usage, and it exhibits great potential in dealing with few-shot 3D-AD tasks.
中摘: 现有的增强多模态融合的3D异常检测（3D-AD）的努力主要集中在设计更有效的多模态融合策略上。然而，很少有人关注多模态融合架构（拓扑结构）设计对3D-AD贡献的作用。本文旨在弥合这一差距，并系统研究多模态融合架构设计对3D-AD的影响。这项工作考虑了在模块内融合层面上的多模态融合架构设计，即独立的模态特定模块，涉及早期、中期或晚期多模态特征以及具体的融合操作。同时，也考虑了模块间融合层面的设计，即如何融合这些模块的策略。在两种情况下，我们首先通过理论和实验探索架构设计如何影响3D-AD，然后扩展当前最先进的神经架构搜索（NAS）范式，首次提出了3D-ADNAS，同时在多模态融合策略和模态特定模块之间进行搜索。广泛的实验证明，3D-ADNAS在准确率、帧率和内存使用方面的一致性改进适用于各种模型容量的3D-AD任务，并且在处理少样本3D-AD任务方面展现出巨大的潜力。

英摘: The vast, complex, and dynamic nature of social message data has posed challenges to social event detection (SED). In response to the challenges, this work introduces an unsupervised framework, HyperSED (Hyperbolic SED). Specifically, the proposed framework first models social messages into semantic-based message anchors, and then leverages the structure of the anchor graph and the expressiveness of the hyperbolic space to acquire structure- and geometry-aware anchor representations. [No.449] Quickest Detection of Adversarial Attacks Against Correlated Equilibria
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect

英摘: We consider correlated equilibria in strategic games in an adversarial environment, where an adversary can compromise the public signal used by the players for choosing their strategies, while players aim at detecting a potential attack as soon as possible to avoid loss of utility. We model the interaction between the adversary and the players as a zero-sum game and we derive the maxmin strategies for both the defender and the attacker using the framework of quickest change detection. We define a class of adversarial strategies that achieve the optimal trade-off between attack impact and attack detectability and show that a generalized CUSUM scheme is asymptotically optimal for the detection of the attacks. 英摘: Deep learning models have recently shown great success in classifying epileptic patients using EEG recordings. [No.451] MTVHunter: Smart Contracts Vulnerability Detection Based on Multi-Teacher Knowledge Translation

中摘: 智能合约与加密货币交易紧密相关，引发了对重大安全问题导致的财务损失的广泛担忧。为了应对这一问题，开发了多种工具来识别智能合约中的漏洞。然而，当面对智能合约字节码时，这些工具无法同时克服两个挑战：（i）由于大量无关指令的强大干扰；（ii）由于数据和控制流依赖不完整而导致字节码缺失语义。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于多教师的字节码漏洞检测方法，即多教师漏洞猎手（MTVHunter），该方法在多教师指导下有效去除噪声并补充字节码缺失的语义。具体而言，我们首先提出了一种指令去噪教师，通过抽象漏洞模式并进一步反映在合约嵌入中，消除噪声干扰。其次，我们设计了一种新型的语义补充教师，利用神经元蒸馏技术，有效地从源代码中提取必要的语义以补充字节码。特别地，提出的神经元蒸馏加速了这一语义填充过程，将知识传递转化为回归任务。我们在229,178个真实世界智能合约上进行了实验，涉及四种常见类型的漏洞。广泛实验表明，MTVHunter在性能上显著优于现有最先进的方法。

[No.454] Unveiling the Threat of Fraud Gangs to Graph Neural Networks: Multi-Target Graph Injection Attacks Against GNN-Based Fraud Detectors

标题: 揭露基于图神经网络的欺诈团伙威胁：针对基于GNN的欺诈检测器的多目标图注入攻击

英摘: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have emerged as an effective tool for fraud detection, identifying fraudulent users, and uncovering malicious behaviors. However, attacks against GNN-based fraud detectors and their risks have rarely been studied, thereby leaving potential threats unaddressed. Recent findings suggest that frauds are increasingly organized as gangs or groups. In this work, we design attack scenarios where fraud gangs aim to make their fraud nodes misclassified as benign by camouflaging their illicit activities in collusion. Based on these scenarios, we study adversarial attacks against GNN-based fraud detectors by simulating attacks of fraud gangs in three real-world fraud cases: spam reviews, fake news, and medical insurance frauds. We define these attacks as multi-target graph injection attacks and propose MonTi, a transformer-based Multi-target one-Time graph injection attack model. MonTi simultaneously generates attributes and edges of all attack nodes with a transformer encoder, capturing interdependencies between attributes and edges more effectively than most existing graph injection attack methods that generate these elements sequentially. Additionally, MonTi adaptively allocates the degree budget for each attack node to explore diverse injection structures involving target, candidate, and attack nodes, unlike existing methods that fix the degree budget across all attack nodes. Experiments show that MonTi outperforms the state-of-the-art graph injection attack methods on five real-world graphs.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）已成为欺诈检测、识别欺诈用户和揭露恶意行为的有效工具。然而，针对基于GNN的欺诈检测器的攻击及其风险研究很少，因此潜在威胁往往未被解决。最近的研究表明，欺诈行为越来越多地组织成团伙或团体。在这项工作中，我们设计了攻击场景，其中欺诈团伙旨在通过共谋掩盖其非法活动，使其欺诈节点被误分类为良性。基于这些场景，我们通过模拟欺诈团伙在三个真实世界的欺诈案例中的攻击，研究了对抗性攻击对基于GNN的欺诈检测器的影响：垃圾评论、假新闻和医疗保险欺诈。我们将这些攻击定义为多目标图注入攻击，并提出了一种基于转换器的多目标一次性图注入攻击模型MonTi。MonTi同时使用转换器编码器生成所有攻击节点的属性和边，比大多数现有按顺序生成这些元素的图注入攻击方法更有效地捕捉属性和边之间的相互依赖关系。此外，MonTi会根据每个攻击节点自适应分配度预算，以探索涉及目标节点、候选节点和攻击节点的多样化注入结构，不同于现有方法在整个攻击节点上固定度预算。实验结果显示，MonTi在五个真实世界的图数据集上优于最先进的图注入攻击方法。

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12850
英摘: Many unsupervised visual anomaly detection methods train an auto-encoder to reconstruct normal samples and then leverage the reconstruction error map to detect and localize the anomalies. However, due to the powerful modeling and generalization ability of neural networks, some anomalies can also be well reconstructed, resulting in unsatisfactory detection and localization accuracy. In this paper, a small coarsely-labeled anomaly dataset is first collected. Then, a coarse-knowledge-aware adversarial learning method is developed to align the distribution of reconstructed features with that of normal features. The alignment can effectively suppress the auto-encoder's reconstruction ability on anomalies and thus improve the detection accuracy. Considering that anomalies often only occupy very small areas in anomalous images, a patch-level adversarial learning strategy is further developed. Although no patch-level anomalous information is available, we rigorously prove that by simply viewing any patch features from anomalous images as anomalies, the proposed knowledge-aware method can also align the distribution of reconstructed patch features with the normal ones. Experimental results on four medical datasets and two industrial datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in improving the detection and localization performance.
中摘: 许多无监督视觉异常检测方法训练一个自编码器来重建正常样本，然后利用重构误差图来检测和定位异常。然而，由于神经网络的强大建模能力和泛化能力，一些异常也可能被很好地重建，导致检测和定位精度不令人满意。在本文中，首先收集了一个小型粗略标注的异常数据集。接着，开发了一种粗知觉信息感知的对抗学习方法，以使重构特征的分布与正常特征的分布对齐。这种对齐可以有效地抑制自编码器在异常上的重构能力，从而提高检测精度。考虑到异常通常仅占据异常图像中很小的区域，进一步开发了一种像素级对抗学习策略。尽管没有像素级的异常信息可用，但我们严格证明了通过将任何来自异常图像的像素特征视作异常，所提出的知识感知方法也可以使重构像素特征的分布与正常特征的分布对齐。在四个医学数据集和两个工业数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在提高检测和定位性能方面是有效的。

[No.456] Differential Alignment for Domain Adaptive Object Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 域自适应目标检测的差分对齐
类别: machine learning ii
作者: Xinyu He;Xinhui Li;Xiaojie Guo;

英摘: Domain adaptive object detection (DAOD) aims to generalize an object detector trained on labeled source-domain data to a target domain without annotations, the core principle of which is \emph{source-target feature alignment}. Typically, existing approaches employ adversarial learning to align the distributions of the source and target domains as a whole, barely considering the varying significance of distinct regions, say instances under different circumstances and foreground \emph{vs} background areas, during feature alignment. To overcome the shortcoming, we investigates a differential feature alignment strategy. Specifically, a prediction-discrepancy feedback instance alignment module (dubbed PDFA) is designed to adaptively assign higher weights to instances of higher teacher-student detection discrepancy, effectively handling heavier domain-specific information. Additionally, an uncertainty-based foreground-oriented image alignment module (UFOA) is proposed to explicitly guide the model to focus more on regions of interest. Extensive experiments on widely-used DAOD datasets together with ablation studies are conducted to demonstrate the efficacy of our proposed method and reveal its superiority over other SOTA alternatives. Our code is available at https://github.com/EstrellaXyu/Differential-Alignment-for-DAOD.
中摘: 领域自适应目标检测（Domain Adaptive Object Detection，DAOD）旨在将在一个有标注的源领域数据上训练的目标检测器推广到没有标注的目标领域。其核心原则是“源领域-目标领域特征对齐”。现有方法通常采用对抗学习来整体对齐源领域和目标领域的分布，但在特征对齐过程中很少考虑不同区域的重要性差异，例如在不同情境下的实例以及前景与背景区域的重要性差异。为克服这一不足，我们研究了一种差异化的特征对齐策略。具体来说，设计了一个预测差异反馈实例对齐模块（称为PDFA），该模块能够自适应地赋予更高权重给教师-学生检测差异较大的实例，有效处理更为具体的领域信息。此外，我们还提出了一种基于不确定性指导的前景导向图像对齐模块（称为UFOA），以明确引导模型更多关注感兴趣区域。我们在广泛使用的DAOD数据集上进行了大量的实验，并通过消融研究展示了我们所提出方法的有效性，并揭示了其在与其他SOTA替代方案相比的优势。我们的代码可在https://github.com/EstrellaXyu/Differential-Alignment-for-DAOD获取。

[No.457] Graph Mixture of Experts and Memory-augmented Routers for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于图的专家混合与记忆增强型路由的多变量时间序列异常检测
类别: machine learning ii
作者: Xiaoyu Huang;Weidong Chen;Bo Hu;Zhendong Mao;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33921
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.19108
英摘: Multivariate time series (MTS) anomaly detection is a critical task that involves identifying abnormal patterns or events in data that consist of multiple interrelated time series. In order to better model the complex interdependence between entities and the various inherent characteristics of each entity, the GNN based methods are widely adopted by existing methods. In each layer of GNN, node features aggregate information from their neighboring nodes to update their information. In doing so, from shallow layer to deep layer in GNN, original individual node features continue to be weakened and more structural information,i.e., from short-distance neighborhood to long-distance neighborhood, continues to be enhanced. However, research to date has largely ignored the understanding of how hierarchical graph information is represented and their characteristics that can benefit anomaly detection. Existing methods simply leverage the output from the last layer of GNN for anomaly estimation while neglecting the essential information contained in the intermediate GNN layers. To address such limitations, in this paper, we propose a Graph Mixture of Experts (Graph-MoE) network for multivariate time series anomaly detection, which incorporates the mixture of experts (MoE) module to adaptively represent and integrate hierarchical multi-layer graph information into entity representations. It is worth noting that our Graph-MoE can be integrated into any GNN-based MTS anomaly detection method in a plug-and-play manner. In addition, the memory-augmented routers are proposed in this paper to capture the correlation temporal information in terms of the global historical features of MTS to adaptively weigh the obtained entity representations to achieve successful anomaly estimation. Extensive experiments on five challenging datasets prove the superiority of our approach and each proposed module.
中摘: 基于图神经网络（GNN）的多变量时间序列（MTS）异常检测是一个关键任务，涉及在多个相互关联的时间序列数据中识别异常模式或事件。为了更好地建模实体之间的复杂依赖关系以及每个实体的各种内在特征，现有的方法广泛采用了基于GNN的方法。在每层GNN中，节点特征会从其邻近节点汇总信息以更新自身的信息。因此，在GNN的浅层到深层中，原始的个体节点特征逐渐被削弱，而更多的结构信息则逐渐增强，即从短距离邻域到长距离邻域。然而，迄今为止的研究大多忽视了对层次化图信息如何表示及其能够为异常检测带来哪些特征的理解。现有方法仅仅利用GNN最后一层的输出来进行异常估计，而忽略了中间GNN层中包含的重要信息。为了解决这些局限性，本文提出了一种用于多变量时间序列异常检测的图专家混合网络（Graph-MoE），该网络结合了专家混合模块（MoE），以适应性地表示和整合多层次多层图信息到实体表示中。值得注意的是，我们的Graph-MoE可以以插拔方式集成到任何基于GNN的MTS异常检测方法中。此外，本文提出了记忆增强路由器，以捕获MTS全局历史特征中的时间相关性信息，并适应性地加权获得的实体表示，以实现成功的异常估计。在五个具有挑战性的数据集上的广泛实验证明了我们方法的优势以及每个提出的模块的有效性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.458] Out-of-Distribution Detection with Prototypical Outlier Proxy
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于原型异常代理的分布外检测
类别: machine learning ii
作者: Mingrong Gong;Chaoqi Chen;Qingqiang Sun;Yue Wang;Hui Huang;
机构: Shenzhen University;Great Bay University;University College London;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33850
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16884
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is a crucial task for deploying deep learning models in the wild. One of the major challenges is that well-trained deep models tend to perform over-confidence on unseen test data. Recent research attempts to leverage real or synthetic outliers to mitigate the issue, which may significantly increase computational costs and be biased toward specific outlier characteristics. In this paper, we propose a simple yet effective framework, Prototypical Outlier Proxy (POP), which introduces virtual OOD prototypes to reshape the decision boundaries between ID and OOD data. Specifically, we transform the learnable classifier into a fixed one and augment it with a set of prototypical weight vectors. Then, we introduce a hierarchical similarity boundary loss to impose adaptive penalties depending on the degree of misclassification. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of POP. Notably, POP achieves average FPR95 reductions of 7.70%, 6.30%, and 5.42% over the second-best methods on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-200, respectively. Moreover, compared to the recent method NPOS, which relies on outlier synthesis, POP trains 7.2X faster and performs inference 19.5X faster. The source code is available at: https://github.com/gmr523/pop.
中摘: Out-of-distribution（OOD）检测是将深度学习模型部署到实际环境中的关键任务之一。主要挑战在于，经过良好训练的深度模型往往会对外部未见过的数据表现出过度自信。最近的研究试图利用真实或合成的异常值来缓解这一问题，但这种方法可能会显著增加计算成本，并且可能偏向特定的异常值特征。在本文中，我们提出了一种简单而有效的框架——原型异常代理（POP），该框架引入了虚拟OOD原型以重塑ID和OOD数据之间的决策边界。具体而言，我们将可学习分类器转变为固定分类器，并用一组原型权重向量对其进行增强。然后，我们引入了一种分层相似性边界损失，根据错误分类的程度施加自适应惩罚。在各种基准测试上的广泛实验表明了POP的有效性。值得注意的是，与CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-200上的第二优方法相比，POP分别实现了平均FPR95降低7.70%、6.30%和5.42%。此外，与依赖于异常值合成的近期方法NPOS相比，POP的训练速度提高了7.2倍，推理速度提高了19.5倍。源代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/gmr523/pop。

[No.459] Queries, Representation & Detection: The Next 100 Model Fingerprinting Schemes
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 查询、表示与检测：下一个百模指纹方案
类别: machine learning ii
作者: Augustin Godinot;Erwan Le Merrer;Camilla Penzo;Francois Taiani;Gilles Tredan;
机构: Université de Rennes;INRIA;Institut de Recherche en Informatique et Systèmes Aléatoires;PEReN;LAAS - Laboratoire d'analyse et d'architecture des systèmes;

英摘: The deployment of machine learning models in operational contexts represents a significant investment for any organisation. Consequently, the risk of these models being misappropriated by competitors needs to be addressed. In recent years, numerous proposals have been put forth to detect instances of model stealing. However, these proposals operate under implicit and disparate data and model access assumptions; as a consequence, it remains unclear how they can be effectively compared to one another. Our evaluation shows that a simple baseline that we introduce performs on par with existing state-of-the-art fingerprints, which, on the other hand, are much more complex. To uncover the reasons behind this intriguing result, this paper introduces a systematic approach to both the creation of model fingerprinting schemes and their evaluation benchmarks. By dividing model fingerprinting into three core components -- Query, Representation and Detection (QuRD) -- we are able to identify $\sim100$ previously unexplored QuRD combinations and gain insights into their performance. Finally, we introduce a set of metrics to compare and guide the creation of more representative model stealing detection benchmarks. Our approach reveals the need for more challenging benchmarks and a sound comparison with baselines. To foster the creation of new fingerprinting schemes and benchmarks, we open-source our fingerprinting toolbox.
中摘: 将机器学习模型部署在实际应用中是任何组织的一项重大投资。因此，这些模型被竞争对手不当利用的风险需要得到解决。近年来，提出了许多方法来检测模型窃取的行为。然而，这些方法隐含了不同的数据和模型访问假设；因此，尚不清楚它们如何能够有效地相互比较。我们的评估表明，我们引入的一个简单基线方案与现有的最先进的指纹方案表现相当，而后者则要复杂得多。为了探究这一引人注目的结果背后的原因，本文提出了一种系统的方法，用于创建模型指纹方案及其评估基准。通过将模型指纹划分为三个核心组件——查询（Query）、表示（Representation）和检测（Detection，简称QuRD），我们能够识别出约100种此前未被探索过的QuRD组合，并深入了解它们的性能。最后，我们提出了一组度量标准，用于比较和指导更具有代表性的模型窃取检测基准的创建。我们的方法揭示了需要更具挑战性的基准以及与基线进行更坚实比较的需求。为了促进新指纹方案和基准的创建，我们开源了我们的指纹工具箱。

[No.460] Structural Entropy Guided Unsupervised Graph Out-Of-Distribution Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 结构熵引导的无监督图离群点检测
类别: machine learning ii
作者: Yue Hou;He Zhu;Ruomei Liu;Yingke Su;Jinxiang Xia;Junran Wu;Ke Xu;
机构: Beihang University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33897
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03241
英摘: With the emerging of huge amount of unlabeled data, unsupervised out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is vital for ensuring the reliability of graph neural networks (GNNs) by identifying OOD samples from in-distribution (ID) ones during testing, where encountering novel or unknown data is inevitable. Existing methods often suffer from compromised performance due to redundant information in graph structures, which impairs their ability to effectively differentiate between ID and OOD data. To address this challenge, we propose SEGO, an unsupervised framework that integrates structural entropy into OOD detection regarding graph classification. Specifically, within the architecture of contrastive learning, SEGO introduces an anchor view in the form of coding tree by minimizing structural entropy. The obtained coding tree effectively removes redundant information from graphs while preserving essential structural information, enabling the capture of distinct graph patterns between ID and OOD samples. Furthermore, we present a multi-grained contrastive learning scheme at local, global, and tree levels using triplet views, where coding trees with essential information serve as the anchor view. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of SEGO, demonstrating superior performance over state-of-the-art baselines in OOD detection. Specifically, our method achieves the best performance on 9 out of 10 dataset pairs, with an average improvement of 3.7\% on OOD detection datasets, significantly surpassing the best competitor by 10.8\% on the FreeSolv/ToxCast dataset pair.
中摘: 随着大量未标记数据的出现，无监督分布外（OOD）检测对于确保图神经网络（GNNs）在测试过程中识别出分布外样本（从分布内样本中区分出来），从而保证其可靠性变得至关重要。在处理新数据或未知数据时，这种情况不可避免。现有方法常常由于图结构中的冗余信息而导致性能下降，这削弱了它们有效区分分布内和分布外数据的能力。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出了一种名为SEGO的无监督框架，该框架将结构熵整合到图分类的OOD检测中。具体而言，在对比学习的架构中，SEGO通过最小化结构熵引入了一个锚视图，以编码树的形式呈现。获得的编码树有效地从图中移除了冗余信息，同时保留了关键的结构信息，使SEGO能够捕捉分布内和分布外样本之间的不同图模式。此外，我们在局部、全局和树三个层次上提出了基于三元组视图的多粒度对比学习方案，其中包含关键信息的编码树作为锚视图。在真实世界数据集上的广泛实验验证了SEGO的有效性，展示了其在OOD检测方面优于最先进的基线方法的表现。具体来说，我们的方法在10个数据集对中的9个上取得了最佳性能，平均在OOD检测数据集上提高了3.7%，而在FreeSolv/ToxCast数据集对上，显著超越了最佳竞争对手10.8%。

[No.463] Detecting and Corrupting Convolution-based Unlearnable Examples
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于卷积的不可学习示例的检测与破坏
类别: machine learning iii
作者: Minghui Li;Xianlong Wang;Zhifei Yu;Shengshan Hu;Ziqi Zhou;Longling Zhang;Leo Yu Zhang;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;Hubei Engineering Research Center on Big Data Security;Griffith University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.18403
英摘: Convolution-based unlearnable examples (UEs) employ class-wise multiplicative convolutional noise to training samples, severely compromising model performance. This fire-new type of UEs have successfully countered all defense mechanisms against UEs. The failure of such defenses can be attributed to the absence of norm constraints on convolutional noise, leading to severe blurring of image features. To address this, we first design an Edge Pixel-based Detector (EPD) to identify convolution-based UEs. Upon detection of them, we propose the first defense scheme against convolution-based UEs, COrrupting these samples via random matrix multiplication by employing bilinear INterpolation (COIN) such that disrupting the distribution of class-wise multiplicative noise. To evaluate the generalization of our proposed COIN, we newly design two convolution-based UEs called VUDA and HUDA to expand the scope of convolution-based UEs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of detection scheme EPD and that our defense COIN outperforms 11 state-of-the-art (SOTA) defenses, achieving a significant improvement on the CIFAR and ImageNet datasets.
中摘: 基于卷积的不可遗忘样本（Unlearnable Examples, UEs）通过在训练样本上应用类别级乘法卷积噪声来严重损害模型性能。这种全新的UEs类型已经成功地对抗了所有针对UEs的防御机制。这些防御机制的失败可以归因于缺乏对卷积噪声的范数约束，导致图像特征严重模糊。为了解决这一问题，我们首先设计了一种基于边缘像素的检测器（Edge Pixel-based Detector, EPD），以识别基于卷积的UEs。一旦检测到这些样本，我们提出了针对基于卷积的UEs的第一个防御方案——COrrupting这些样本通过使用双线性插值的随机矩阵乘法（COIN），从而破坏类别级乘法噪声的分布。为了评估我们提出的COIN方案的一般化能力，我们新设计了两种基于卷积的UEs，称为VUDA和HUDA，以扩大基于卷积的UEs的应用范围。广泛的实验表明，EPD检测方案的有效性，并且我们的防御COIN方案优于11种最先进的（State-of-the-Art, SOTA）防御措施，在CIFAR和ImageNet数据集上取得了显著的改进。

来源: AAAI 2025

英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is indispensable for deploying reliable machine learning systems in real-world scenarios. In this work, we discover an obvious correlation that OOD data usually possesses significant ID attributes. [No.468] Early Concept Drift Detection via Prediction Uncertainty
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 早期概念漂移检测通过预测不确定性
类别: machine learning iv
作者: Pengqian Lu;Jie Lu;Anjin Liu;Guangquan Zhang;
机构: University of Technology Sydney;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34105
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11158
英摘: Concept drift, characterized by unpredictable changes in data distribution over time, poses significant challenges to machine learning models in streaming data scenarios. Although error rate-based concept drift detectors are widely used, they often fail to identify drift in the early stages when the data distribution changes but error rates remain constant. This paper introduces the Prediction Uncertainty Index (PU-index), derived from the prediction uncertainty of the classifier, as a superior alternative to the error rate for drift detection. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that: (1) The PU-index can detect drift even when error rates remain stable. (2) Any change in the error rate will lead to a corresponding change in the PU-index. These properties make the PU-index a more sensitive and robust indicator for drift detection compared to existing methods. We also propose a PU-index-based Drift Detector (PUDD) that employs a novel Adaptive PU-index Bucketing algorithm for detecting drift. Empirical evaluations on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate PUDD's efficacy in detecting drift in structured and image data.
中摘: 概念漂移，表现为数据分布随时间出现不可预测的变化，对流式数据场景中的机器学习模型构成了重大挑战。尽管基于错误率的概念漂移检测器被广泛应用，但在数据分布发生变化而错误率保持不变的早期阶段，它们往往无法识别漂移现象。本文介绍了从分类器预测不确定性中衍生出的预测不确定性指数（PU-index），作为一种优于错误率的漂移检测替代方法。我们的理论分析表明：(1) PU-index即使在错误率保持稳定时也能检测到漂移。(2) 任何错误率的变化都将导致PU-index相应变化。这些特性使得PU-index相比现有方法更敏感且更稳健，适用于漂移检测。我们还提出了一种基于PU-index的漂移检测器（PUDD），该检测器采用了一种新颖的自适应PU-index桶算法来检测漂移。对合成和真实世界数据集的实证评估证明了PUDD在检测结构化数据和图像数据中的漂移方面具有有效性。

[No.469] GCAD: Anomaly Detection in Multivariate Time Series from the Perspective of Granger Causality
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: GCAD：从Granger因果性视角进行多变量时间序列异常检测
类别: machine learning iv
作者: Zehao Liu;Mengzhou Gao;Pengfei Jiao;
机构: Hangzhou Dianzi University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34096
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.13493
英摘: Multivariate time series anomaly detection has numerous real-world applications and is being extensively studied. Modeling pairwise correlations between variables is crucial. Existing methods employ learnable graph structures and graph neural networks to explicitly model the spatial dependencies between variables. However, these methods are primarily based on prediction or reconstruction tasks, which can only learn similarity relationships between sequence embeddings and lack interpretability in how graph structures affect time series evolution. In this paper, we designed a framework that models spatial dependencies using interpretable causal relationships and detects anomalies through changes in causal patterns. Specifically, we propose a method to dynamically discover Granger causality using gradients in nonlinear deep predictors and employ a simple sparsification strategy to obtain a Granger causality graph, detecting anomalies from a causal perspective. Experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed model achieves more accurate anomaly detection compared to baseline methods.
中摘: 多变量时间序列异常检测在实际应用中有着广泛的应用，并且这一领域正在被广泛研究。建模变量之间的成对相关性至关重要。现有方法通过可学习的图结构和图神经网络显式地建模变量之间的空间依赖关系。然而，这些方法主要基于预测或重构任务，只能学习序列嵌入之间的相似性关系，缺乏从图结构如何影响时间序列演变过程中的解释性。在本文中，我们设计了一个框架，该框架使用可解释的因果关系来建模空间依赖性，并通过因果模式的变化来进行异常检测。具体来说，我们提出了一种动态发现Granger因果性的方法，利用非线性深度预测器中的梯度变化，并采用简单的稀疏化策略获得一个Granger因果图，从而从因果角度进行异常检测。实验结果表明，所提出的模型在异常检测的准确性上优于基线方法。

[No.470] Rectified Lagrangian for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Modern Hopfield Networks
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 修正拉格朗日乘子法在现代霍普菲尔德网络中的离分布检测应用
类别: machine learning iv
作者: Ryo Moriai;Nakamasa Inoue;Masayuki Tanaka;Rei Kawakami;Satoshi Ikehata;Ikuro Sato;
机构: Institute of Science Tokyo;National Institute of Informatics;Denso IT Laboratory, Inc.;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34153
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14003
英摘: Modern Hopfield networks (MHNs) have recently gained significant attention in the field of artificial intelligence because they can store and retrieve a large set of patterns with an exponentially large memory capacity. A MHN is generally a dynamical system defined with Lagrangians of memory and feature neurons, where memories associated with in-distribution (ID) samples are represented by attractors in the feature space. One major problem in existing MHNs lies in managing out-of-distribution (OOD) samples because it was originally assumed that all samples are ID samples. To address this, we propose the rectified Lagrangian (RegLag), a new Lagrangian for memory neurons that explicitly incorporates an attractor for OOD samples in the dynamical system of MHNs. RecLag creates a trivial point attractor for any interaction matrix, enabling OOD detection by identifying samples that fall into this attractor as OOD. The interaction matrix is optimized so that the probability densities can be estimated to identify ID/OOD. We demonstrate the effectiveness of RecLag-based MHNs compared to energy-based OOD detection methods, including those using state-of-the-art Hopfield energies, across nine image datasets.
中摘: 现代霍普菲尔德网络（MHNs）最近在人工智能领域引起了广泛关注，因为它们能够存储和检索大量模式，并拥有指数级大的记忆容量。一般而言，MHN是一个定义了记忆神经元和特征神经元拉格朗日函数的动力系统，其中与分布内（ID）样本相关的记忆在特征空间中表现为吸引子。现有MHN的一个主要问题在于管理分布外（OOD）样本，因为在最初的设计中，假设所有样本都是ID样本。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了修正拉格朗日（RegLag），这是一种新的用于记忆神经元的拉格朗日函数，它在MHN的动力系统中明确地包含了OOD样本的吸引子。RecLag通过优化交互矩阵，使得可以通过估计概率密度来识别ID和OOD样本，从而实现OOD检测。任何交互矩阵都会产生一个平凡的点吸引子，RecLag通过识别落入这一吸引子中的样本作为OOD样本来进行OOD检测。我们展示了基于RecLag的MHNs在九个图像数据集上相较于基于能量的OOD检测方法（包括最先进的霍普菲尔德能量方法）的有效性。

[No.478] ITP: Instance-Aware Test Pruning for Out-of-Distribution Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: ITP：实例感知测试剪枝以进行分布外检测
类别: machine learning vi
作者: Haonan Xu;Yang Yang;
机构: Nanjing University of Science and Technology;Pazhou Lab;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/35479
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12566
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is crucial for ensuring the reliable deployment of deep models in real-world scenarios. Recently, from the perspective of over-parameterization, a series of methods leveraging weight sparsification techniques have shown promising performance. These methods typically focus on selecting important parameters for in-distribution (ID) data to reduce the negative impact of redundant parameters on OOD detection. However, we empirically find that these selected parameters may behave overconfidently toward OOD data and hurt OOD detection. To address this issue, we propose a simple yet effective post-hoc method called Instance-aware Test Pruning (ITP), which performs OOD detection by considering both coarse-grained and fine-grained levels of parameter pruning. Specifically, ITP first estimates the class-specific parameter contribution distribution by exploring the ID data. By using the contribution distribution, ITP conducts coarse-grained pruning to eliminate redundant parameters. More importantly, ITP further adopts a fine-grained test pruning process based on the right-tailed Z-score test, which can adaptively remove instance-level overconfident parameters. Finally, ITP derives OOD scores from the pruned model to achieve more reliable predictions. Extensive experiments on widely adopted benchmarks verify the effectiveness of ITP, demonstrating its competitive performance.
中摘: out-of-distribution（OOD）检测对于确保在实际场景中深度模型的可靠部署至关重要。最近，从过参数化视角出发，一系列利用权重稀疏化技术的方法显示出了令人鼓舞的效果。这些方法通常侧重于选择对in-distribution（ID）数据重要的参数，以减少冗余参数对OOD检测的负面影响。然而，我们通过实验发现，这些选定的参数可能会对OOD数据表现出过度自信，并损害OOD检测的效果。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种简单而有效的后处理方法，称为实例感知测试剪枝（Instance-aware Test Pruning, ITP），该方法通过考虑参数剪枝的粗粒度和细粒度层次来进行OOD检测。具体而言，ITP首先通过探索ID数据来估计类特定的参数贡献分布。借助贡献分布，ITP执行粗粒度剪枝以消除冗余参数。更重要的是，ITP进一步采用基于右尾Z分数检验的细粒度测试剪枝过程，可以自适应地移除实例级别的过度自信参数。最后，ITP从剪枝后的模型中推导出OOD评分，从而实现更可靠的预测。广泛采用的基准上的大量实验验证了ITP的有效性，展示了其竞争力的表现。

[No.479] PhysAug: A Physical-guided and Frequency-based Data Augmentation for Single-Domain Generalized Object Detection
来源: AAAI 2025

标题: 物理引导和频率为基础的数据增强方法：单域通用目标检测

英摘: Single-Domain Generalized Object Detection~(S-DGOD) aims to train on a single source domain for robust performance across a variety of unseen target domains by taking advantage of an object detector. However, the absence of real-world prior knowledge hinders data augmentation from contributing to the diversity of training data distributions. To address this issue, we propose PhysAug, a novel physical model-based non-ideal imaging condition data augmentation method, to enhance the adaptability of the S-DGOD tasks. Drawing upon the principles of atmospheric optics, we develop a universal perturbation model that serves as the foundation for our proposed PhysAug. Given that visual perturbations typically arise from the interaction of light with atmospheric particles, the image frequency spectrum is harnessed to simulate real-world variations during training. This approach fosters the detector to learn domain-invariant representations, thereby enhancing its ability to generalize across various settings. Without altering the network architecture or loss function, our approach significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art across various S-DGOD datasets. 中摘: 单域通用物体检测（S-DGOD）旨在通过利用物体检测器在单一源域进行训练，以稳健的方式应对各种未见过的目标域。现有的S-DGOD方法通常依赖于数据增强策略，包括视觉变换的组合，以提高检测器的泛化能力。然而，缺乏实际世界先验知识阻碍了数据增强对训练数据分布多样性的贡献。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了PhysAug，一种基于物理模型的非理想成像条件数据增强方法，以增强S-DGOD任务的适应性。借鉴大气光学原理，我们开发了一个通用的扰动模型，作为PhysAug的基础。由于视觉扰动通常源自光线与大气颗粒的相互作用，我们在训练过程中利用图像的频率谱来模拟实际世界的变异。这种方法促使检测器学习领域不变的表示，从而增强了其在各种设置下的泛化能力。在不改变网络架构或损失函数的情况下，我们的方法在多种S-DGOD数据集上显著优于现有最先进的方法。特别是在DWD和Cityscape-C基准上分别实现了7.3%和7.2%的基线改进，突显了其在实际世界设置中的增强泛化能力。

[No.483] Detecting Music Performance Errors with Transformers
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 使用变换器检测音乐表演错误
类别: natural language processing i
作者: Benjamin Shiue-Hal Chou;Purvish Jajal;Nicholas John Eliopoulos;Tim Nadolsky;Cheng-Yun Yang;Nikita Ravi;James C. Davis;Kristen Yeon-Ji Yun;Yung-Hsiang Lu;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02030
英摘: Beginner musicians often struggle to identify specific errors in their performances, such as playing incorrect notes or rhythms. There are two limitations in existing tools for music error detection: (1) Existing approaches rely on automatic alignment; therefore, they are prone to errors caused by small deviations between alignment targets.; (2) There is a lack of sufficient data to train music error detection models, resulting in over-reliance on heuristics. To address (1), we propose a novel transformer model, Polytune, that takes audio inputs and outputs annotated music scores. This model can be trained end-to-end to implicitly align and compare performance audio with music scores through latent space representations. To address (2), we present a novel data generation technique capable of creating large-scale synthetic music error datasets. Our approach achieves a 64.1% average Error Detection F1 score, improving upon prior work by 40 percentage points across 14 instruments. Additionally, compared with existing transcription methods repurposed for music error detection, our model can handle multiple instruments. Our source code and datasets are available at https://github.com/ben2002chou/Polytune.
中摘: 初学者音乐家往往难以识别其演奏中的具体错误，比如演奏错误的音符或节奏。现有音乐错误检测工具存在的两个限制是：（1）现有方法依赖于自动对齐，因此容易受到对齐目标之间微小偏差导致的错误影响；（2）缺乏足够的数据来训练音乐错误检测模型，导致过度依赖启发式方法。为了解决（1），我们提出了一种新的变压器模型Polytune，它可以接受音频输入并输出标注过的音乐谱。该模型可以通过潜在空间表示隐式地进行对齐和比较演奏音频与音乐谱。为了解决（2），我们提供了一种新型的数据生成技术，能够创建大规模的合成音乐错误数据集。我们的方法在14种乐器上实现了64.1%的平均错误检测F1分数，相比之前的工作提高了40个百分点。此外，与现有重新利用于音乐错误检测的转录方法相比，我们的模型能够处理多种乐器。我们的源代码和数据集可在https://github.com/ben2002chou/Polytune获取。

[No.484] Enhancing Uncertainty Modeling with Semantic Graph for Hallucination Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于语义图增强不确定性建模以检测幻觉
类别: natural language processing i
作者: Kedi Chen;Qin Chen;Jie Zhou;Xinqi Tao;Bowen Ding;Jingwen Xie;Mingchen Xie;Peilong Li;Zheng Feng;
机构: East China Normal University;Xiaohongshu Inc.;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34528
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02020
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) are prone to hallucination with non-factual or unfaithful statements, which undermines the applications in real-world scenarios. Recent researches focus on uncertainty-based hallucination detection, which utilizes the output probability of LLMs for uncertainty calculation and does not rely on external knowledge or frequent sampling from LLMs. Whereas, most approaches merely consider the uncertainty of each independent token, while the intricate semantic relations among tokens and sentences are not well studied, which limits the detection of hallucination that spans over multiple tokens and sentences in the passage. In this paper, we propose a method to enhance uncertainty modeling with semantic graph for hallucination detection. Specifically, we first construct a semantic graph that well captures the relations among entity tokens and sentences. Then, we incorporate the relations between two entities for uncertainty propagation to enhance sentence-level hallucination detection. Given that hallucination occurs due to the conflict between sentences, we further present a graph-based uncertainty calibration method that integrates the contradiction probability of the sentence with its neighbors in the semantic graph for uncertainty calculation. Extensive experiments on two datasets show the great advantages of our proposed approach. In particular, we obtain substantial improvements with 19.78% in passage-level hallucination detection.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）容易产生与事实不符或不忠实的陈述，这在实际应用场景中削弱了其效果。近期的研究重点在于基于不确定性的幻觉检测，这种方法利用LLM的输出概率进行不确定性计算，并且不依赖于外部知识或频繁从LLM中采样。然而，大多数方法仅仅考虑每个独立词元的不确定性，而忽视了词元和句子之间复杂的语义关系，这限制了对跨越多个词元和句子的幻觉检测能力。在本文中，我们提出了一种通过语义图增强不确定性建模来进行幻觉检测的方法。具体而言，我们首先构建了一个能够很好地捕捉实体词元和句子之间关系的语义图。然后，我们将两个实体之间的关系纳入不确定性传播中，以增强句子级别的幻觉检测。鉴于幻觉通常是由于句子之间的冲突引起的，我们进一步提出了一种基于图的不确定性校准方法，该方法将句子与其语义图中邻居的矛盾概率结合起来进行不确定性计算。在两个数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们提出的这种方法具有显著优势，特别是在段落级别的幻觉检测方面取得了29.78%的大幅改进。

[No.485] Imitate Before Detect: Aligning Machine Stylistic Preference for Machine-Revised Text Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 模仿优先于检测：调整机器 stylistic 预ference以检测机器修订文本
类别: natural language processing i
作者: Jiaqi Chen;Xiaoye Zhu;Tianyang Liu;Ying Chen;Chen Xinhui;Yiwen Yuan;Chak Tou Leong;Zuchao Li;Long Tang;Lei Zhang;Chenyu Yan;Guanghao Mei;Jie Zhang;Lefei Zhang;
机构: Fudan University;Stanford University;South China University of Technology;National University of Singapore;University of California, San Diego;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Wuhan University;Fenz AI;Carnegie Mellon University;Hong Kong Polytechnic University;Georgia Institute of Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34525
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10432
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized text generation, making detecting machine-generated text increasingly challenging. Although past methods have achieved good performance on detecting pure machine-generated text, those detectors have poor performance on distinguishing machine-revised text (rewriting, expansion, and polishing), which can have only minor changes from its original human prompt. As the content of text may originate from human prompts, detecting machine-revised text often involves identifying distinctive machine styles, e.g., worded favored by LLMs. However, existing methods struggle to detect machine-style phrasing hidden within the content contributed by humans. We propose the "Imitate Before Detect" (ImBD) approach, which first imitates the machine-style token distribution, and then compares the distribution of the text to be tested with the machine-style distribution to determine whether the text has been machine-revised. To this end, we introduce style preference optimization (SPO), which aligns a scoring LLM model to the preference of text styles generated by machines. The aligned scoring model is then used to calculate the style-conditional probability curvature (Style-CPC), quantifying the log probability difference between the original and conditionally sampled texts for effective detection. We conduct extensive comparisons across various scenarios, encompassing text revisions by six LLMs, four distinct text domains, and three machine revision types. Compared to existing state-of-the-art methods, our method yields a 13% increase in AUC for detecting text revised by open-source LLMs, and improves performance by 5% and 19% for detecting GPT-3.5 and GPT-4o revised text, respectively. Notably, our method surpasses the commercially trained GPT-Zero with just $1,000$ samples and five minutes of SPO, demonstrating its efficiency and effectiveness.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）已经彻底改变了文本生成的方式，使得检测机器生成的文本变得越来越具有挑战性。尽管过去的方法在检测纯机器生成的文本方面取得了良好的性能，但在区分机器修订的文本（重写、扩展和润色）方面表现不佳，这些修订文本可能仅比原始的人类提示有细微的变化。由于文本内容可能源自人类提示，因此检测机器修订的文本通常涉及识别特有的机器风格，例如大型语言模型偏好使用的词语。然而，现有方法难以检测隐藏在人类贡献内容中的机器风格表达。我们提出了一种“模仿再检测”（ImBD）的方法，该方法首先模仿机器风格的标记分布，然后将待测试文本的标记分布与机器风格的分布进行比较，以确定文本是否经过了机器修订。为此，我们引入了风格偏好优化（SPO），这是一种将评分LLM模型与机器生成文本风格的偏好对齐的技术。对齐后的评分模型用于计算风格条件概率曲率（Style-CPC），这是一种量化原始文本和条件采样文本之间对数概率差异的有效方法，有助于更准确地检测。我们在各种场景下进行了广泛的对比实验，涵盖了六种LLM的文本修订、四种不同的文本领域以及三种机器修订类型。与现有的先进方法相比，我们的方法在检测开源LLM修订的文本时AUC提高了13%，分别在检测GPT-3.5和GPT-4o修订的文本时提高了5%和19%。值得注意的是，我们的方法仅使用1000个样本和五分钟的SPO时间就超越了商业训练的GPT-Zero，这证明了其高效性和有效性。

[No.487] Region-Based Optimization in Continual Learning for Audio Deepfake Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于区域的优化在持续学习中的音频换音检测中
类别: natural language processing i
作者: Yujie Chen;Jiangyan Yi;Cunhang Fan;Jianhua Tao;Yong Ren;Siding Zeng;Chu Yuan Zhang;Xinrui Yan;Hao Gu;Jun Xue;Chenglong Wang;Zhao Lv;Xiaohui Zhang;
机构: Anhui University;Chinese Academy of Sciences;Tsinghua University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34535
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11551
英摘: Rapid advancements in speech synthesis and voice conversion bring convenience but also new security risks, creating an urgent need for effective audio deepfake detection. Although current models perform well, their effectiveness diminishes when confronted with the diverse and evolving nature of real-world deepfakes. To address this issue, we propose a continual learning method named Region-Based Optimization (RegO) for audio deepfake detection. Specifically, we use the Fisher information matrix to measure important neuron regions for real and fake audio detection, dividing them into four regions. First, we directly fine-tune the less important regions to quickly adapt to new tasks. Next, we apply gradient optimization in parallel for regions important only to real audio detection, and in orthogonal directions for regions important only to fake audio detection. For regions that are important to both, we use sample proportion-based adaptive gradient optimization. This region-adaptive optimization ensures an appropriate trade-off between memory stability and learning plasticity. Additionally, to address the increase of redundant neurons from old tasks, we further introduce the Ebbinghaus forgetting mechanism to release them, thereby promoting the capability of the model to learn more generalized discriminative features. Experimental results show our method achieves a 21.3% improvement in EER over the state-of-the-art continual learning approach RWM for audio deepfake detection. Moreover, the effectiveness of RegO extends beyond the audio deepfake detection domain, showing potential significance in other tasks, such as image recognition. The code is available at https://github.com/cyjie429/RegO
中摘: 快速发展的语音合成和声音转换技术带来了便利，但也创造了新的安全风险，迫切需要有效的音频深度伪造检测方法。尽管当前模型表现良好，但在面对真实世界深度伪造的多样性和演变时，其有效性会减弱。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为区域基优化（RegO）的持续学习方法，用于音频深度伪造检测。具体而言，我们使用Fisher信息矩阵来衡量真实音频和虚假音频检测的重要神经元区域，并将它们分为四个区域。首先，我们直接微调不重要的区域，以迅速适应新任务。其次，我们对仅对真实音频检测重要的区域并行应用梯度优化，在仅对虚假音频检测重要的区域则在正交方向上进行梯度优化。对于既重要于真实音频又重要于虚假音频的区域，我们采用基于样本比例的自适应梯度优化。这种区域适应性优化确保了记忆稳定性和学习灵活性之间的适当权衡。此外，为了应对来自旧任务的冗余神经元数量增加的问题，我们进一步引入了艾宾浩斯遗忘机制来释放这些冗余神经元，从而促进模型学习更通用的判别特征的能力。实验结果显示，我们的方法在音频深度伪造检测中的错误接受率（EER）上比最先进的持续学习方法RWM提高了21.3%。此外，RegO的有效性不仅限于音频深度伪造检测领域，在图像识别等其他任务中也显示出潜在的重要性。代码可在https://github.com/cyjie429/RegO获取。

[No.488] Zero-resource Hallucination Detection for Text Generation via Graph-based Contextual Knowledge Triples Modeling
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于图结构上下文知识三元组建模的零资源幻觉检测与文本生成
类别: natural language processing i
作者: Xinyue Fang;Zhen Huang;Zhiliang Tian;Minghui Fang;Ziyi Pan;Quntian Fang;Zhihua Wen;Hengyue Pan;Dongsheng Li;
机构: National University of Defense Technology;Zhejiang University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34559
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11283
英摘: LLMs obtain remarkable performance but suffer from hallucinations. Most research on detecting hallucination focuses on the questions with short and concrete correct answers that are easy to check the faithfulness. Hallucination detections for text generation with open-ended answers are more challenging. Some researchers use external knowledge to detect hallucinations in generated texts, but external resources for specific scenarios are hard to access. Recent studies on detecting hallucinations in long text without external resources conduct consistency comparison among multiple sampled outputs. To handle long texts, researchers split long texts into multiple facts and individually compare the consistency of each pairs of facts. However, these methods (1) hardly achieve alignment among multiple facts; (2) overlook dependencies between multiple contextual facts. In this paper, we propose a graph-based context-aware (GCA) hallucination detection for text generations, which aligns knowledge facts and considers the dependencies between contextual knowledge triples in consistency comparison. Particularly, to align multiple facts, we conduct a triple-oriented response segmentation to extract multiple knowledge triples. To model dependencies among contextual knowledge triple (facts), we construct contextual triple into a graph and enhance triples' interactions via message passing and aggregating via RGCN. To avoid the omission of knowledge triples in long text, we conduct a LLM-based reverse verification via reconstructing the knowledge triples. Experiments show that our model enhances hallucination detection and excels all baselines.
中摘: 大语言模型（LLMs）在获得出色性能的同时，也面临着幻觉的问题。大多数关于检测幻觉的研究集中在那些具有短且具体正确答案的问题上，这些问题的答案容易验证其准确性。而对于生成开放性答案的文本时检测幻觉，则更为困难。一些研究人员利用外部知识来检测生成文本中的幻觉，但特定场景下的外部资源难以获取。最近的一些研究，在没有外部资源的情况下检测长文本中的幻觉，通过对比多个采样输出的一致性来进行。为了处理长文本，研究人员将长文本拆分为多个事实，并分别比较每对事实的一致性。然而，这些方法（1）难以实现多个事实之间的对齐；（2）忽视了多个上下文事实之间的依赖关系。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于图的上下文感知（GCA）幻觉检测方法，该方法对齐知识事实并在一致性比较中考虑上下文知识三元组之间的依赖关系。特别地，为了对齐多个事实，我们进行了一种面向三元组的响应分割，以提取多个知识三元组。为了建模上下文知识三元组（事实）之间的依赖关系，我们将上下文三元组构建为一个图，并通过消息传递和RGCN聚合增强三元组之间的交互。为了防止在长文本中遗漏知识三元组，我们通过重建知识三元组来进行基于大语言模型的逆向验证。实验表明，我们的模型增强了幻觉检测，并优于所有基线方法。

[No.490] Confidence Estimation for Error Detection in Text-to-SQL Systems
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 文本到SQL系统的错误检测中的置信度估计
类别: natural language processing ii

英摘: Text-to-SQL enables users to interact with databases through natural language, simplifying the retrieval and synthesis of information. Despite the success of large language models (LLMs) in converting natural language questions into SQL queries, their broader adoption is limited by two main challenges: achieving robust generalization across diverse queries and ensuring interpretative confidence in their predictions. To tackle these issues, our research investigates the integration of selective classifiers into Text-to-SQL systems. We analyse the trade-off between coverage and risk using entropy based confidence estimation with selective classifiers and assess its impact on the overall performance of Text-to-SQL models. Additionally, we explore the models' initial calibration and improve it with calibration techniques for better model alignment between confidence and accuracy. Our experimental results show that encoder-decoder T5 is better calibrated than in-context-learning GPT 4 and decoder-only Llama 3, thus the designated external entropy-based selective classifier has better performance. The study also reveal that, in terms of error detection, selective classifier with a higher probability detects errors associated with irrelevant questions rather than incorrect query generations.
中摘: Text-to-SQL 使用户能够通过自然语言与数据库进行交互，简化信息的检索和合成。尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）在将自然语言问题转换为SQL查询方面取得了成功，但它们更广泛的采用受到了两个主要挑战的限制：在不同类型的查询之间实现稳健的一般化以及确保对其预测解释的信心。为了应对这些问题，我们的研究探讨了将选择性分类器集成到Text-to-SQL系统中的方法。我们使用基于熵的信任度估计来分析选择性分类器在覆盖范围和风险之间的权衡，并评估其对Text-to-SQL模型整体性能的影响。此外，我们还探索了模型的初始校准，并通过校准技术改进它，以提高模型之间的信任度与准确性的匹配度。实验结果显示，编码器-解码器T5比上下文学习GPT 4和仅解码器Llama 3更具校准性，因此指定的外部熵基选择性分类器具有更好的性能。该研究还表明，在错误检测方面，概率较高的选择性分类器更倾向于检测与无关问题相关的错误，而不是查询生成错误。

[No.492] Detecting and Mitigating Hallucination in Large Vision Language Models via Fine-Grained AI Feedback
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过精细粒度的人工智能反馈检测和缓解大型视觉语言模型中的幻觉问题
类别: natural language processing iii
作者: Wenyi Xiao;Ziwei Huang;Leilei Gan;Wanggui He;Haoyuan Li;Zhelun Yu;Fangxun Shu;Hao Jiang;Linchao Zhu;

类型: Technical

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14233
英摘: The rapidly developing Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) have shown notable capabilities on a range of multi-modal tasks, but still face the hallucination phenomena where the generated texts do not align with the given contexts, significantly restricting the usages of LVLMs. Most previous work detects and mitigates hallucination at the coarse-grained level or requires expensive annotation (e.g., labeling by proprietary models or human experts). To address these issues, we propose detecting and mitigating hallucinations in LVLMs via fine-grained AI feedback. The basic idea is that we generate a small-size sentence-level hallucination annotation dataset by proprietary models, whereby we train a hallucination detection model which can perform sentence-level hallucination detection, covering primary hallucination types (i.e., object, attribute, and relationship). Then, we propose a detect-then-rewrite pipeline to automatically construct preference dataset for training hallucination mitigating model. Furthermore, we propose differentiating the severity of hallucinations, and introducing a Hallucination Severity-Aware Direct Preference Optimization (HSA-DPO) for mitigating hallucination in LVLMs by incorporating the severity of hallucinations into preference learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.
中摘: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的快速发展显示了其在多种跨模态任务上的显著能力，但仍然面临生成文本与给定上下文不一致的现象，这极大地限制了LVLMs的应用。大多数先前的工作在粗粒度级别检测和缓解幻觉现象，或者需要昂贵的标注（例如，由专有模型或专家人工标注）。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种通过细粒度AI反馈来检测和缓解LVLMs幻觉的方法。基本思路是使用专有模型生成一个小型的句子级幻觉标注数据集，然后训练一个幻觉检测模型，该模型可以在句子级别上进行幻觉检测，涵盖主要的幻觉类型（即对象、属性和关系）。接着，我们提出了一个“检测-重写”管道，自动构建偏好数据集用于训练缓解幻觉的模型。此外，我们还提出了区分幻觉严重程度的方法，并引入了感知幻觉严重程度的直接偏好优化（HSA-DPO），通过将幻觉严重程度纳入偏好学习中来缓解LVLMs中的幻觉现象。广泛的实验表明了我们方法的有效性。

[No.493] Is Sarcasm Detection a Step-by-Step Reasoning Process in Large Language Models?

机构: University of Copenhagen;Tianjin University;Hong Kong Polytechnic University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34756
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12725
英摘: Elaborating a series of intermediate reasoning steps significantly improves the ability of large language models (LLMs) to solve complex problems, as such steps would evoke LLMs to think sequentially. However, human sarcasm understanding is often considered an intuitive and holistic cognitive process, in which various linguistic, contextual, and emotional cues are integrated to form a comprehensive understanding, in a way that does not necessarily follow a step-by-step fashion. To verify the validity of this argument, we introduce a new prompting framework (called SarcasmCue) containing four sub-methods, viz. chain of contradiction (CoC), graph of cues (GoC), bagging of cues (BoC) and tensor of cues (ToC), which elicits LLMs to detect human sarcasm by considering sequential and non-sequential prompting methods. Through a comprehensive empirical comparison on four benchmarks, we highlight three key findings: (1) CoC and GoC show superior performance with more advanced models like GPT-4 and Claude 3.5, with an improvement of 3.5%. (2) ToC significantly outperforms other methods when smaller LLMs are evaluated, boosting the F1 score by 29.7% over the best baseline. (3) Our proposed framework consistently pushes the state-of-the-art (i.e., ToT) by 4.2%, 2.0%, 29.7%, and 58.2% in F1 scores across four datasets. This demonstrates the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework.
中摘: 详细阐述一系列中间推理步骤显著提高了大型语言模型（LLMs）解决复杂问题的能力，因为这样的步骤促使LLMs进行顺序性思考。然而，人类理解讽刺通常被认为是一种直观且整体的认知过程，在这种过程中，各种语言、上下文和情感线索被综合运用以形成全面的理解，不一定遵循逐步的方式。为了验证这一论点的有效性，我们引入了一个新的提示框架（称为SarcasmCue），该框架包含四个子方法：矛盾链（CoC）、线索图（GoC）、线索袋装（BoC）和线索张量（ToC）。这些方法通过考虑顺序性和非顺序性的提示方法来促使LLMs检测人类的讽刺。通过对四个基准测试进行全面的经验比较，我们突显了三个关键发现：(1) CoC和GoC在更先进的模型如GPT-4和Claude 3.5上表现出色，性能提高了3.5%。(2) 当评估较小的LLMs时，ToC显著优于其他方法，将F1分数提高了29.7%，超过最佳基线。(3) 我们提出的框架在四个数据集的F1分数上持续推动最先进的技术水平，分别为4.2%、2.0%、29.7%和58.2%。这表明所提出框架的有效性和稳定性。

英摘: Reliable failure detection holds paramount importance in safety-critical applications. Yet, neural networks are known to produce overconfident predictions for misclassified samples. This research introduces an innovative strategy, leveraging human-level concepts for a dual purpose: to reliably detect when a model fails and to transparently interpret why. 英摘: Enabling object detectors to recognize out-of-distribution (OOD) objects is vital for building reliable systems. A primary obstacle stems from the fact that models frequently do not receive supervisory signals from unfamiliar data, leading to overly confident predictions regarding OOD objects. Despite previous progress that estimates OOD uncertainty based on the detection model and in-distribution (ID) samples, we explore using pre-trained vision-language representations for object-level OOD detection. 中摘: 使目标检测器能够识别分布外（OOD）目标对于构建可靠系统至关重要。主要障碍在于模型通常不会接收到不熟悉数据的监督信号，导致其对OOD目标做出过于自信的预测。尽管之前的研究基于检测模型和分布内（ID）样本估计OOD不确定性取得了进展，我们探索了使用预训练的视觉-语言表示进行对象级别OOD检测的方法。首先，我们讨论了将基于图像级别的CLIP方法应用于对象级别场景时的局限性。在此基础上，我们提出了RUNA，这是一种新颖的框架，利用双编码器架构捕捉丰富的上下文信息，并采用区域不确定性对齐机制有效地区分ID对象和OOD对象。我们还引入了一种少量样本微调方法，进一步对区域级语义表示进行对齐，以提高模型区分相似对象的能力。我们的实验表明，在具有多样化和复杂对象实例的挑战性场景中，RUNA在对象级别OOD检测方面显著超越了现有最先进的方法。

[No.497] Transfer Learning of Real Image Features with Soft Contrastive Loss for Fake Image Detection
来源: AAAI 2025

标题: 基于软对比损失的真实图像特征迁移学习在假图像检测中的应用

英摘: In the last few years, the artifact patterns in fake images synthesized by different generative models have been inconsistent, leading to the failure of previous research that relied on spotting subtle differences between real and fake. In our preliminary experiments, we find that the artifacts in fake images always change with the development of the generative model, while natural images exhibit stable statistical properties. In this paper, we employ natural traces shared only by real images as an additional target for a classifier. Specifically, we introduce a self-supervised feature mapping process for natural trace extraction and develop a transfer learning based on soft contrastive loss to bring them closer to real images and further away from fake ones. This motivates the detector to make decisions based on the proximity of images to the natural traces. To conduct a comprehensive experiment, we built a high-quality and diverse dataset that includes generative models comprising GANs and diffusion models, to evaluate the effectiveness in generalizing unknown forgery techniques and robustness in surviving different transformations. Experimental results show that our proposed method gives 96.2% mAP significantly outperforms the baselines. [No.498] A General Black-box Adversarial Attack on Graph-based Fake News Detectors
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于图的假新闻检测器的一般黑盒对抗攻击

作者: Peican Zhu;Zechen Pan;Yang Liu;Jiwei Tian;Keke Tang;Zhen Wang;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15744
英摘: Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based fake news detectors apply various methods to construct graphs, aiming to learn distinctive news embeddings for classification. Since the construction details are unknown for attackers in a black-box scenario, it is unrealistic to conduct the classical adversarial attacks that require a specific adjacency matrix. In this paper, we propose the first general black-box adversarial attack framework, i.e., General Attack via Fake Social Interaction (GAFSI), against detectors based on different graph structures. Specifically, as sharing is an important social interaction for GNN-based fake news detectors to construct the graph, we simulate sharing behaviors to fool the detectors. Firstly, we propose a fraudster selection module to select engaged users leveraging local and global information. In addition, a post injection module guides the selected users to create shared relations by sending posts. The sharing records will be added to the social context, leading to a general attack against different detectors. Experimental results on empirical datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of GAFSI.
中摘: 基于图神经网络（GNN）的假新闻检测器通过构建各种图形方法来学习具有区分性的新闻嵌入以进行分类。由于在黑盒场景下攻击者不了解图形构建的细节，因此进行需要特定邻接矩阵的经典对抗攻击是不现实的。本文提出了一种针对不同图形结构的假新闻检测器的一般性黑盒对抗攻击框架，即“通过虚假社交互动的一般性攻击”（GAFSI）。具体而言，鉴于分享行为对于基于GNN的假新闻检测器构建图形至关重要，我们模拟了分享行为来欺骗检测器。首先，我们提出了一种欺诈用户选择模块，利用局部和全局信息选择参与分享的用户。此外，一个帖子注入模块指导选定的用户通过发送帖子来创建共享关系，这些分享记录将被添加到社交上下文中，从而对不同的检测器发起一般性攻击。实验结果表明，GAFSI的有效性。

[No.499] BadFusion: 2D-Oriented Backdoor Attacks against 3D Object Detection
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: BadFusion: 面向2D的后门攻击针对3D物体检测
类别: AI Ethics, Trust, Fairness
作者: Saket S. Chaturvedi;Lan Zhang;Wenbin Zhang;Pan He;Xiaoyong Yuan;
机构: Clemson University;Florida International University;Auburn University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/39
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.03884
英摘: 3D object detection plays an important role in autonomous driving; however, its vulnerability to backdoor attacks has become evident. By injecting ''triggers'' to poison the training dataset, backdoor attacks manipulate the detector's prediction for inputs containing these triggers. Existing backdoor attacks against 3D object detection primarily poison 3D LiDAR signals, where large-sized 3D triggers are injected to ensure their visibility within the sparse 3D space, rendering them easy to detect and impractical in real-world scenarios.
  In this paper, we delve into the robustness of 3D object detection, exploring a new backdoor attack surface through 2D cameras. Given the prevalent adoption of camera and LiDAR signal fusion for high-fidelity 3D perception, we investigate the latent potential of camera signals to disrupt the process. Although the dense nature of camera signals enables the use of nearly imperceptible small-sized triggers to mislead 2D object detection, realizing 2D-oriented backdoor attacks against 3D object detection is non-trivial. The primary challenge emerges from the fusion process that transforms camera signals into a 3D space, compromising the association with the 2D trigger to the target output. To tackle this issue, we propose an innovative 2D-oriented backdoor attack against LiDAR-camera fusion methods for 3D object detection, named BadFusion, for preserving trigger effectiveness throughout the entire fusion process. The evaluation demonstrates the effectiveness of BadFusion, achieving a significantly higher attack success rate compared to existing 2D-oriented attacks.
中摘: 三维物体检测在自动驾驶中扮演着重要角色，然而其对后门攻击的脆弱性已经变得明显。通过向训练数据集注入“触发器”来污染数据，后门攻击会操控检测器对包含这些触发器的输入的预测。现有的针对三维物体检测的后门攻击主要污染3D LiDAR信号，在稀疏的三维空间中注入大型三维触发器以确保其可见性，使其容易被检测并在实际场景中不切实际。

在本文中，我们探讨了三维物体检测的鲁棒性，通过2D摄像头开辟了一个新的后门攻击表面。鉴于相机和LiDAR信号融合广泛用于高精度三维感知，我们研究了相机信号潜藏的破坏潜力。尽管相机信号的密集特性允许使用几乎不可察觉的小型触发器误导二维物体检测，但实现面向二维的后门攻击以影响三维物体检测并不简单。主要挑战来自于将相机信号转换为三维空间的过程，这会破坏2D触发器与目标输出之间的关联。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为BadFusion的创新性面向二维的后门攻击方法，专门针对三维物体检测中的LiDAR-相机融合方法，以确保在整个融合过程中触发器的有效性。评估结果表明，BadFusion的有效性显著高于现有面向二维的后门攻击方法，实现了更高的攻击成功率。

[No.500] Detecting and Understanding Vulnerabilities in Language Models via Mechanistic Interpretability
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过机制可解释性检测和理解语言模型中的漏洞
类别: AI Ethics, Trust, Fairness
作者: Jorge García-Carrasco;Alejandro Maté;Juan Trujillo;
机构: University of Alicante;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/43
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.19842
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs), characterized by being trained on broad amounts of data in a self-supervised manner, have shown impressive performance across a wide range of tasks. Indeed, their generative abilities have aroused interest on the application of LLMs across a wide range of contexts. However, neural networks in general, and LLMs in particular, are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where an imperceptible change to the input can mislead the output of the model. This is a serious concern that impedes the use of LLMs on high-stakes applications, such as healthcare, where a wrong prediction can imply serious consequences. Even though there are many efforts on making LLMs more robust to adversarial attacks, there are almost no works that study \emph{how} and \emph{where} these vulnerabilities that make LLMs prone to adversarial attacks happen. Motivated by these facts, we explore how to localize and understand vulnerabilities, and propose a method, based on Mechanistic Interpretability (MI) techniques, to guide this process. Specifically, this method enables us to detect vulnerabilities related to a concrete task by (i) obtaining the subset of the model that is responsible for that task, (ii) generating adversarial samples for that task, and (iii) using MI techniques together with the previous samples to discover and understand the possible vulnerabilities. We showcase our method on a pretrained GPT-2 Small model carrying out the task of predicting 3-letter acronyms to demonstrate its effectiveness on locating and understanding concrete vulnerabilities of the model.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs），因其在自我监督的方式下训练于广泛的数据集，已经在多种任务上展示了令人印象深刻的性能。事实上，它们的生成能力引起了对LLMs在各种上下文中的应用的兴趣。然而，一般来说，神经网络，尤其是LLMs，已知容易受到对抗攻击的影响，在输入中进行几乎不可察觉的变化就可以误导模型的输出。这种现象是一个严重的问题，它阻碍了LLMs在高风险应用领域，如医疗保健中的使用，因为错误的预测可能会带来严重的后果。尽管有许多努力使LLMs更能抵抗对抗攻击，但几乎没有研究探讨这些漏洞是如何以及在哪里发生的。鉴于这些事实，我们探索如何定位和理解这些漏洞，并提出一种基于机制可解释性（Mechanistic Interpretability, MI）技术的方法来指导这一过程。具体而言，该方法通过以下步骤检测与特定任务相关的漏洞：(i) 获取负责该任务的模型子集，(ii) 为该任务生成对抗样本，以及(iii) 使用MI技术结合上述样本来发现并理解可能的漏洞。我们通过在一个预训练的GPT-2 Small模型上执行预测3字母缩写的任务，展示了该方法的有效性，以证明其在定位和理解模型的具体漏洞方面的效果。

[No.504] Ten Words Only Still Help: Improving Black-Box AI-Generated Text Detection via Proxy-Guided Efficient Re-Sampling
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 仅十个词仍有帮助：通过代理引导高效重采样提高黑盒AI生成文本检测
类别: AI Ethics, Trust, Fairness
作者: Yuhui Shi;Qiang Sheng;Juan Cao;Hao Mi;Beizhe Hu;Danding Wang;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.09199
英摘: With the rapidly increasing application of large language models (LLMs), their abuse has caused many undesirable societal problems such as fake news, academic dishonesty, and information pollution. This makes AI-generated text (AIGT) detection of great importance. Among existing methods, white-box methods are generally superior to black-box methods in terms of performance and generalizability, but they require access to LLMs' internal states and are not applicable to black-box settings. In this paper, we propose to estimate word generation probabilities as pseudo white-box features via multiple re-sampling to help improve AIGT detection under the black-box setting. Specifically, we design POGER, a proxy-guided efficient re-sampling method, which selects a small subset of representative words (e.g., 10 words) for performing multiple re-sampling in black-box AIGT detection. Experiments on datasets containing texts from humans and seven LLMs show that POGER outperforms all baselines in macro F1 under black-box, partial white-box, and out-of-distribution settings and maintains lower re-sampling costs than its existing counterparts.
中摘: 随着大型语言模型（LLMs）的应用迅速增加，它们的滥用已经引发了诸如假新闻、学术不诚实和信息污染等许多不良社会问题。这使得对AI生成文本（AIGT）的检测变得非常重要。在现有方法中，白盒方法在性能和泛化能力方面通常优于黑盒方法，但它们需要访问LLMs的内部状态，并且不适用于黑盒设置。在本文中，我们提出了一种通过多次采样估计单词生成概率的方法，以帮助改进在黑盒设置下的AIGT检测。具体来说，我们设计了POGER，一种代理引导的高效重采样方法，它选择一小部分代表性单词（例如，10个单词）来进行黑盒AIGT检测中的多次重采样。实验结果显示，在包含人类和七种LLM文本的数据集中，POGER在黑盒、部分白盒和分布外设置下的宏F1值上均优于所有基线方法，并且其重采样成本低于现有的同类方法。

[No.508] DCDet: Dynamic Cross-based 3D Object Detector
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: DCDet：动态交叉基于3D物体检测器
类别: Computer Vision
作者: Shuai Liu;Boyang Li;Zhiyu Fang;Kai Huang;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/124
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.07240
英摘: Recently, significant progress has been made in the research of 3D object detection. However, most prior studies have focused on the utilization of center-based or anchor-based label assignment schemes. Alternative label assignment strategies remain unexplored in 3D object detection. We find that the center-based label assignment often fails to generate sufficient positive samples for training, while the anchor-based label assignment tends to encounter an imbalanced issue when handling objects of varying scales. To solve these issues, we introduce a dynamic cross label assignment (DCLA) scheme, which dynamically assigns positive samples for each object from a cross-shaped region, thus providing sufficient and balanced positive samples for training. Furthermore, to address the challenge of accurately regressing objects with varying scales, we put forth a rotation-weighted Intersection over Union (RWIoU) metric to replace the widely used L1 metric in regression loss. Extensive experiments demonstrate the generality and effectiveness of our DCLA and RWIoU-based regression loss. The Code will be available at https://github.com/Say2L/DCDet.git.
中摘: 最近，在3D物体检测的研究中取得了显著进展。然而，大多数先前的研究主要集中在中心基或锚基标签分配方案的应用上。在3D物体检测中，替代的标签分配策略尚未被探索。我们发现，中心基标签分配经常无法为训练生成足够的正样本，而锚基标签分配在处理不同尺度的物体时容易遇到不平衡的问题。为了解决这些问题，我们引入了一种动态交叉标签分配（DCLA）方案，该方案能够从交叉形区域动态分配正样本，从而为训练提供充足的、平衡的正样本。此外，为了准确回归不同尺度的物体，我们提出了旋转加权交并比（RWIoU）度量来替换广泛使用的L1度量作为回归损失。大量的实验表明，我们的DCLA和基于RWIoU的回归损失具有普遍适用性和有效性。代码将在https://github.com/Say2L/DCDet.git获取。
