[No.514] OD-DETR: Online Distillation for Stabilizing Training of Detection Transformer
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: OD-DETR：在线蒸馏以稳定检测变换器的训练
类别: Computer Vision
作者: Shengjian Wu;Li Sun;Qingli Li;
机构: East China Normal University;FinVolution Group;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/160
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05791
英摘: DEtection TRansformer (DETR) becomes a dominant paradigm, mainly due to its common architecture with high accuracy and no post-processing. However, DETR suffers from unstable training dynamics. It consumes more data and epochs to converge compared with CNN-based detectors. This paper aims to stabilize DETR training through the online distillation. It utilizes a teacher model, accumulated by Exponential Moving Average (EMA), and distills its knowledge into the online model in following three aspects. First, the matching relation between object queries and ground truth (GT) boxes in the teacher is employed to guide the student, so queries within the student are not only assigned labels based on their own predictions, but also refer to the matching results from the teacher. Second, the teacher's initial query is given to the online student, and its prediction is directly constrained by the corresponding output from the teacher. Finally, the object queries from teacher's different decoding stages are used to build the auxiliary groups to accelerate the convergence. For each GT, two queries with the least matching costs are selected into this extra group, and they predict the GT box and participate the optimization. Extensive experiments show that the proposed OD-DETR successfully stabilizes the training, and significantly increases the performance without bringing in more parameters.
中摘: DEtection TRansformer（DETR）成为主导范式，主要是因为它具有通用架构，准确度高且无需后处理。然而，DETR在训练动态上存在不稳定性。与基于CNN的检测器相比，它需要更多的数据和迭代次数才能收敛。本文旨在通过在线蒸馏稳定DETR的训练。该方法利用了通过指数移动平均（EMA）积累的教师模型，并从以下三个方面将教师的知识传授给在线学生模型：首先，教师中的对象查询与地面真实（GT）框之间的匹配关系被用来引导学生，因此学生中的查询不仅根据自身的预测进行标签分配，还会参考教师的匹配结果。其次，教师的初始查询提供给在线学生，并直接由教师相应输出进行约束。最后，教师不同解码阶段的对象查询用于构建辅助组以加速收敛。对于每个GT，选择两个匹配成本最低的对象查询进入这个额外组，它们预测GT框并参与优化。大量实验表明，所提出的OD-DETR成功地稳定了训练，并显著提高了性能而没有增加更多参数。

[No.515] RoboFusion: Towards Robust Multi-Modal 3D Object Detection via SAM
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: RoboFusion：通过SAM实现稳健的多模态3D物体检测
类别: Computer Vision
作者: Ziying Song;Guoxing Zhang;Lin Liu;Lei Yang;Shaoqing Xu;Caiyan Jia;Feiyang Jia;Li Wang;
机构: Beijing Jiao Tong University;Beijing Key Lab of Traffic Data Analysis and Mining;Hebei University of Science and Technology;Tsinghua University;University of Macau;Beijing Institute of Technology;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.03907
英摘: Multi-modal 3D object detectors are dedicated to exploring secure and reliable perception systems for autonomous driving (AD).Although achieving state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on clean benchmark datasets, they tend to overlook the complexity and harsh conditions of real-world environments. With the emergence of visual foundation models (VFMs), opportunities and challenges are presented for improving the robustness and generalization of multi-modal 3D object detection in AD. Therefore, we propose RoboFusion, a robust framework that leverages VFMs like SAM to tackle out-of-distribution (OOD) noise scenarios. We first adapt the original SAM for AD scenarios named SAM-AD. To align SAM or SAM-AD with multi-modal methods, we then introduce AD-FPN for upsampling the image features extracted by SAM. We employ wavelet decomposition to denoise the depth-guided images for further noise reduction and weather interference. At last, we employ self-attention mechanisms to adaptively reweight the fused features, enhancing informative features while suppressing excess noise. In summary, RoboFusion significantly reduces noise by leveraging the generalization and robustness of VFMs, thereby enhancing the resilience of multi-modal 3D object detection. Consequently, RoboFusion achieves SOTA performance in noisy scenarios, as demonstrated by the KITTI-C and nuScenes-C benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/adept-thu/RoboFusion.
中摘: 多模态3D物体检测器专门致力于为自动驾驶（AD）探索安全可靠的感知系统。尽管在干净的数据基准集上实现了最先进的（SOTA）性能，它们往往忽视了真实环境中的复杂性和恶劣条件。随着视觉基础模型（VFMs）的出现，这为提高多模态3D物体检测在AD中的鲁棒性和泛化能力带来了机遇和挑战。因此，我们提出了一种名为RoboFusion的鲁棒框架，利用如SAM这样的VFMs来应对分布外（OOD）噪声场景。我们首先将原始SAM适应于AD场景，命名为SAM-AD。为了使SAM或SAM-AD与多模态方法相匹配，我们引入了AD-FPN来放大SAM提取的图像特征。我们采用小波分解对深度引导图像进行去噪，进一步减少噪声和天气干扰。最后，我们运用自注意力机制自适应地重新加权融合特征，增强信息性特征的同时抑制多余噪声。总之，RoboFusion通过利用VFMs的泛化能力和鲁棒性显著减少了噪声，从而增强了多模态3D物体检测的抗干扰性。因此，RoboFusion在嘈杂场景下的性能达到了SOTA水平，如KITTI-C和nuScenes-C基准所展示的那样。代码可在https://github.com/adept-thu/RoboFusion获取。

[No.516] SEMv3: A Fast and Robust Approach to Table Separation Line Detection
来源: IJCAI 2025

英摘: Table structure recognition (TSR) aims to parse the inherent structure of a table from its input image. The `"split-and-merge" paradigm is a pivotal approach to parse table structure, where the table separation line detection is crucial. However, challenges such as wireless and deformed tables make it demanding. In this paper, we adhere to the "split-and-merge" paradigm and propose SEMv3 (SEM: Split, Embed and Merge), a method that is both fast and robust for detecting table separation lines. During the split stage, we introduce a Keypoint Offset Regression (KOR) module, which effectively detects table separation lines by directly regressing the offset of each line relative to its keypoint proposals. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate that our proposed KOR module can detect table separation lines quickly and accurately. Furthermore, on public datasets (e.g. WTW, ICDAR-2019 cTDaR Historical and iFLYTAB), SEMv3 achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance. [No.518] Unified Physical-Digital Face Attack Detection
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 统一物理与数字空间面部攻击检测
类别: Computer Vision
作者: Hao Fang;Ajian Liu;Haocheng Yuan;Junze Zheng;Dingheng Zeng;Yanhong Liu;Jiankang Deng;Sergio Escalera;Xiaoming Liu;Jun Wan;Zhen Lei;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;Macau University of Science and Technology;Mashang Consumer Finance;Imperial College London;Computer Vision Center;Michigan State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/83
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.17699
英摘: Face Recognition (FR) systems can suffer from physical (i.e., print photo) and digital (i.e., DeepFake) attacks. However, previous related work rarely considers both situations at the same time. This implies the deployment of multiple models and thus more computational burden. The main reasons for this lack of an integrated model are caused by two factors: (1) The lack of a dataset including both physical and digital attacks with ID consistency which means the same ID covers the real face and all attack types; (2) Given the large intra-class variance between these two attacks, it is difficult to learn a compact feature space to detect both attacks simultaneously. To address these issues, we collect a Unified physical-digital Attack dataset, called UniAttackData. The dataset consists of $1,800$ participations of 2 and 12 physical and digital attacks, respectively, resulting in a total of 29,706 videos. Then, we propose a Unified Attack Detection framework based on Vision-Language Models (VLMs), namely UniAttackDetection, which includes three main modules: the Teacher-Student Prompts (TSP) module, focused on acquiring unified and specific knowledge respectively; the Unified Knowledge Mining (UKM) module, designed to capture a comprehensive feature space; and the Sample-Level Prompt Interaction (SLPI) module, aimed at grasping sample-level semantics. These three modules seamlessly form a robust unified attack detection framework. Extensive experiments on UniAttackData and three other datasets demonstrate the superiority of our approach for unified face attack detection.
中摘: 面部识别（FR）系统可能遭受物理（即，打印照片）和数字（即，Deepfake）攻击。然而，之前的相关研究很少同时考虑这两种情况。这暗示着需要部署多个模型，从而增加了计算负担。缺乏一个包含物理和数字攻击且具有ID一致性（即，同一个ID涵盖真实人脸和所有攻击类型）的数据集是导致这种缺乏集成模型的主要原因之一；此外，由于这两种攻击之间的高类内变异性，很难学习到一个紧凑的特征空间来同时检测这两种攻击。为了解决这些问题，我们收集了一个统一的物理-数字攻击数据集，称为UniAttackData。该数据集包括2种和12种物理及数字攻击的共计1,800个参与样本，总共产生了29,706个视频。然后，我们提出了一种基于视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的统一攻击检测框架，名为UniAttackDetection，该框架包含三个主要模块：教师-学生提示（TSP）模块，专注于分别获取统一和特定的知识；统一知识挖掘（UKM）模块，旨在捕捉一个全面的特征空间；以及样本级提示交互（SLPI）模块，目标是掌握样本级别的语义。这三个模块无缝地形成了一个稳健的统一攻击检测框架。在UniAttackData及其他三个数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法在统一面部攻击检测方面具有优越性。

[No.519] Unified Unsupervised Salient Object Detection via Knowledge Transfer
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 统一的知识迁移无监督显著对象检测
类别: Computer Vision
作者: Yao Yuan;Wutao Liu;Pan Gao;Qun Dai;Jie Qin;

英摘: Recently, unsupervised salient object detection (USOD) has gained increasing attention due to its annotation-free nature. However, current methods mainly focus on specific tasks such as RGB and RGB-D, neglecting the potential for task migration. In this paper, we propose a unified USOD framework for generic USOD tasks. Firstly, we propose a Progressive Curriculum Learning-based Saliency Distilling (PCL-SD) mechanism to extract saliency cues from a pre-trained deep network. This mechanism starts with easy samples and progressively moves towards harder ones, to avoid initial interference caused by hard samples. Afterwards, the obtained saliency cues are utilized to train a saliency detector, and we employ a Self-rectify Pseudo-label Refinement (SPR) mechanism to improve the quality of pseudo-labels. Finally, an adapter-tuning method is devised to transfer the acquired saliency knowledge, leveraging shared knowledge to attain superior transferring performance on the target tasks. Extensive experiments on five representative SOD tasks confirm the effectiveness and feasibility of our proposed method. Code and supplement materials are available at https://github.com/I2-Multimedia-Lab/A2S-v3.
中摘: 近年来，无监督显著对象检测（USOD）由于其无需标注的特点而引起了越来越多的关注。然而，当前的方法主要集中在特定任务上，如RGB和RGB-D，忽视了任务迁移的潜在可能性。在本文中，我们提出了一种统一的USOD框架，适用于通用的USOD任务。首先，我们提出了一种基于渐进式课程学习的显著性提炼（PCL-SD）机制，用于从预训练的深度网络中提取显著性线索。该机制从简单的样本开始，逐步过渡到更难的样本，以避免初始阶段由难样本引起的干扰。接着，获取到的显著性线索被用于训练显著性检测器，并采用一种自我校正伪标签精炼（SPR）机制来提高伪标签的质量。最后，我们设计了一种适配器调优方法，利用共享的知识在目标任务上实现更好的迁移性能。在五个代表性SOD任务上的广泛实验验证了我们所提方法的有效性和可行性。相关代码和补充材料可在https://github.com/I2-Multimedia-Lab/A2S-v3获取。

[No.522] Large Language Model Guided Knowledge Distillation for Time Series Anomaly Detection
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 大型语言模型引导的知识蒸馏在时间序列异常检测中的应用
类别: Data Mining
作者: Chen Liu;Shibo He;Qihang Zhou;Shizhong Li;Wenchao Meng;
机构: Zhejiang University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/239
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.15123
英摘: Self-supervised methods have gained prominence in time series anomaly detection due to the scarcity of available annotations. Nevertheless, they typically demand extensive training data to acquire a generalizable representation map, which conflicts with scenarios of a few available samples, thereby limiting their performance. To overcome the limitation, we propose \textbf{AnomalyLLM}, a knowledge distillation-based time series anomaly detection approach where the student network is trained to mimic the features of the large language model (LLM)-based teacher network that is pretrained on large-scale datasets. During the testing phase, anomalies are detected when the discrepancy between the features of the teacher and student networks is large. To circumvent the student network from learning the teacher network's feature of anomalous samples, we devise two key strategies. 1) Prototypical signals are incorporated into the student network to consolidate the normal feature extraction. 2) We use synthetic anomalies to enlarge the representation gap between the two networks. AnomalyLLM demonstrates state-of-the-art performance on 15 datasets, improving accuracy by at least 14.5\% in the UCR dataset.
中摘: 自监督方法在时间序列异常检测中因其可用标注数据稀缺而备受关注。然而，这些方法通常需要大量的训练数据来获取可泛化的特征映射，这与样本数量有限的场景相冲突，从而限制了它们的表现。为克服这一局限，我们提出了一种名为**AnomalyLLM**的时间序列异常检测方法，该方法基于知识蒸馏，其中学生网络被训练以模仿大型语言模型（LLM）为基础的预训练教师网络的特征。在测试阶段，当教师网络和学生网络的特征差异较大时，会检测到异常。为了防止学生网络学习教师网络中的异常样本特征，我们设计了两种关键策略。1）将原型信号纳入学生网络，以巩固正常特征的提取。2）使用合成异常来扩大两个网络之间的表示差距。AnomalyLLM在15个数据集上展示了最先进的性能，在UCR数据集上至少提高了14.5%的准确性。

[No.527] Sub-Adjacent Transformer: Improving Time Series Anomaly Detection with Reconstruction Error from Sub-Adjacent Neighborhoods
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 邻近子变换器：通过子邻域重建误差提高时间序列异常检测性能
类别: Data Mining
作者: Wenzhen Yue;Xianghua Ying;Ruohao Guo;DongDong Chen;Ji Shi;Bowei Xing;Yuqing Zhu;Taiyan Chen;
机构: Peking University;Microsoft;AI;Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/279
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18948
英摘: In this paper, we present the Sub-Adjacent Transformer with a novel attention mechanism for unsupervised time series anomaly detection. Unlike previous approaches that rely on all the points within some neighborhood for time point reconstruction, our method restricts the attention to regions not immediately adjacent to the target points, termed sub-adjacent neighborhoods. Our key observation is that owing to the rarity of anomalies, they typically exhibit more pronounced differences from their sub-adjacent neighborhoods than from their immediate vicinities. By focusing the attention on the sub-adjacent areas, we make the reconstruction of anomalies more challenging, thereby enhancing their detectability. Technically, our approach concentrates attention on the non-diagonal areas of the attention matrix by enlarging the corresponding elements in the training stage. To facilitate the implementation of the desired attention matrix pattern, we adopt linear attention because of its flexibility and adaptability. Moreover, a learnable mapping function is proposed to improve the performance of linear attention. Empirically, the Sub-Adjacent Transformer achieves state-of-the-art performance across six real-world anomaly detection benchmarks, covering diverse fields such as server monitoring, space exploration, and water treatment.
中摘: 在本文中，我们提出了一种次邻接变换器（Sub-Adjacent Transformer），并引入了一种新的注意力机制用于无监督的时间序列异常检测。与之前依赖于目标点周围某个区域内的所有点进行时间点重构的方法不同，我们的方法将注意力限制在不立即邻近目标点的区域，称为次邻接区域。我们关键的观察是由于异常的稀有性，它们通常与次邻接区域之间的差异比与直接邻近区域之间的差异更为显著。通过将注意力集中在次邻接区域，我们使异常的重构更具挑战性，从而提高了其可检测性。技术上，我们的方法在训练阶段通过扩大注意矩阵的非对角元素来集中注意力。为了实现所需的注意矩阵模式，我们采用了线性注意力，因为它具有灵活性和适应性。此外，我们还提出了一种可学习的映射函数以提高线性注意力的性能。实验证明，次邻接变换器在六个真实世界的异常检测基准测试中实现了最先进的性能，涵盖了诸如服务器监控、太空探索和水处理等不同的领域。

[No.528] Towards Dynamic Trend Filtering through Trend Point Detection with Reinforcement Learning
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过强化学习进行趋势点检测以实现动态趋势过滤
类别: Data Mining
作者: Jihyeon Seong;Sekwang Oh;Jaesik Choi;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;INEEJI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/257
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03665
英摘: Trend filtering simplifies complex time series data by applying smoothness to filter out noise while emphasizing proximity to the original data. However, existing trend filtering methods fail to reflect abrupt changes in the trend due to `approximateness,' resulting in constant smoothness. This approximateness uniformly filters out the tail distribution of time series data, characterized by extreme values, including both abrupt changes and noise. In this paper, we propose Trend Point Detection formulated as a Markov Decision Process (MDP), a novel approach to identifying essential points that should be reflected in the trend, departing from approximations. We term these essential points as Dynamic Trend Points (DTPs) and extract trends by interpolating them. To identify DTPs, we utilize Reinforcement Learning (RL) within a discrete action space and a forecasting sum-of-squares loss function as a reward, referred to as the Dynamic Trend Filtering network (DTF-net). DTF-net integrates flexible noise filtering, preserving critical original subsequences while removing noise as required for other subsequences. We demonstrate that DTF-net excels at capturing abrupt changes compared to other trend filtering algorithms and enhances forecasting performance, as abrupt changes are predicted rather than smoothed out.
中摘: 趋势滤波简化了复杂的时序数据，通过平滑处理来过滤噪声，同时强调与原始数据的接近性。然而，现有的趋势滤波方法由于“近似性”未能反映趋势中的突变变化，导致平滑度保持恒定。这种近似性会均匀地过滤掉时序数据尾部分布中的极端值，包括突变变化和噪声。在本文中，我们提出了一种趋势点检测方法，将其表述为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），这是一种识别应反映在趋势中的关键点的新颖方法，不同于现有的近似方法。我们将这些关键点称为动态趋势点（DTPs），并通过插值提取趋势。为了识别DTPs，我们利用离散动作空间内的强化学习（RL）以及一种预测平方和损失函数作为奖励，称为动态趋势滤波网络（DTF-net）。DTF-net结合了灵活的噪声过滤功能，在保留关键原始子序列的同时，根据需要去除其他子序列中的噪声。我们证明，与其它趋势滤波算法相比，DTF-net在捕捉突变变化方面表现出色，并能提升预测性能，因为它预测的是突变变化而非平滑处理。

[No.529] Unsupervised Anomaly Detection via Masked Diffusion Posterior Sampling
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 无监督异常检测通过掩码扩散后验采样
类别: Data Mining
作者: Di Wu;Shicai Fan;Xue Zhou;Li Yu;Yuzhong Deng;Jianxiao Zou;Baihong Lin;
机构: University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/270
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17900
英摘: Reconstruction-based methods have been commonly used for unsupervised anomaly detection, in which a normal image is reconstructed and compared with the given test image to detect and locate anomalies. Recently, diffusion models have shown promising applications for anomaly detection due to their powerful generative ability. However, these models lack strict mathematical support for normal image reconstruction and unexpectedly suffer from low reconstruction quality. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel and highly-interpretable method named Masked Diffusion Posterior Sampling (MDPS). In MDPS, the problem of normal image reconstruction is mathematically modeled as multiple diffusion posterior sampling for normal images based on the devised masked noisy observation model and the diffusion-based normal image prior under Bayesian framework. Using a metric designed from pixel-level and perceptual-level perspectives, MDPS can effectively compute the difference map between each normal posterior sample and the given test image. Anomaly scores are obtained by averaging all difference maps for multiple posterior samples. Exhaustive experiments on MVTec and BTAD datasets demonstrate that MDPS can achieve state-of-the-art performance in normal image reconstruction quality as well as anomaly detection and localization.
中摘: 基于重建的方法常用于无监督异常检测，在这些方法中，正常图像被重建并与给定的测试图像进行比较以检测和定位异常。最近，扩散模型因其强大的生成能力在异常检测方面显示出有前景的应用。然而，这些模型缺乏严格的数学支持来进行正常图像的重建，并且意外地遭受了低质量的重建问题。为了解决这些问题，本文提出了一种新颖且高度可解释的方法，名为掩码扩散后验采样（Masked Diffusion Posterior Sampling, MDPS）。在MDPS中，正常图像的重建问题在贝叶斯框架下通过设计的掩码噪声观测模型和基于扩散的正常图像先验进行了数学建模。从像素级和感知级的角度设计了一个度量标准，MDPS可以有效地计算每个正常后验样本与给定测试图像之间的差异图。异常分数通过多个后验样本的所有差异图的平均值获得。在MVTec和BTAD数据集上的 exhaustive 实验表明，MDPS 可以在正常图像重建质量和异常检测及定位方面达到最先进的性能。

[No.539] Are Watermarks Bugs for Deepfake Detectors? Rethinking Proactive Forensics
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: 水印是Deepfake检测器的陷阱吗？重新思考主动取证方法
类别: Multidisciplinary Topics and Applications
作者: Xiaoshuai Wu;Xin Liao;Bo Ou;Yuling Liu;Zheng Qin;
机构: Hunan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/673
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17867
英摘: AI-generated content has accelerated the topic of media synthesis, particularly Deepfake, which can manipulate our portraits for positive or malicious purposes. Before releasing these threatening face images, one promising forensics solution is the injection of robust watermarks to track their own provenance. However, we argue that current watermarking models, originally devised for genuine images, may harm the deployed Deepfake detectors when directly applied to forged images, since the watermarks are prone to overlap with the forgery signals used for detection. To bridge this gap, we thus propose AdvMark, on behalf of proactive forensics, to exploit the adversarial vulnerability of passive detectors for good. Specifically, AdvMark serves as a plug-and-play procedure for fine-tuning any robust watermarking into adversarial watermarking, to enhance the forensic detectability of watermarked images; meanwhile, the watermarks can still be extracted for provenance tracking. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed AdvMark, leveraging robust watermarking to fool Deepfake detectors, which can help improve the accuracy of downstream Deepfake detection without tuning the in-the-wild detectors. We believe this work will shed some light on the harmless proactive forensics against Deepfake.
中摘: AI生成的内容加速了媒体合成这一话题的发展，特别是深度伪造（Deepfake），它可以为了积极或恶意的目的操控我们的肖像。在发布这些威胁性的面部图像之前，一种有希望的解决方案是在其中注入稳健的水印以追踪其来源。然而，我们认为当前为真实图像设计的水印模型，在直接应用于伪造图像时可能会损害部署的深度伪造检测器，因为水印容易与用于检测的伪造信号重叠。为了弥合这一差距，我们因此提出AdvMark，旨在利用被动检测器的对抗性漏洞进行积极的追踪。具体来说，AdvMark 作为一项即插即用的流程，可以将任何稳健的水印调整为对抗性水印，以增强带有水印图像的取证可检测性；同时，这些水印仍然可以被提取出来进行来源追踪。广泛的实验表明，AdvMark 的有效性，它利用稳健的水印来欺骗深度伪造检测器，可以在不调校野外检测器的情况下提高下游深度伪造检测的准确性。我们认为这项工作将有助于揭示积极的无害取证对抗深度伪造的方法。

[No.540] Delocate: Detection and Localization for Deepfake Videos with Randomly-Located Tampered Traces
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: Delocate：具有随机定位篡改痕迹的深度假视频检测与定位
类别: Multidisciplinary Topics and Applications
作者: Juan Hu;Xin Liao;Difei Gao;Satoshi Tsutsui;Qian Wang;Zheng Qin;Mike Zheng Shou;
机构: Hunan University;National University of Singapore;Nanyang Technological University;Wuhan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/648
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.13516
英摘: Deepfake videos are becoming increasingly realistic, showing few tampering traces on facial areasthat vary between frames. Consequently, existing Deepfake detection methods struggle to detect unknown domain Deepfake videos while accurately locating the tampered region. To address thislimitation, we propose Delocate, a novel Deepfake detection model that can both recognize andlocalize unknown domain Deepfake videos. Ourmethod consists of two stages named recoveringand localization. In the recovering stage, the modelrandomly masks regions of interest (ROIs) and reconstructs real faces without tampering traces, leading to a relatively good recovery effect for realfaces and a poor recovery effect for fake faces. Inthe localization stage, the output of the recoveryphase and the forgery ground truth mask serve assupervision to guide the forgery localization process. This process strategically emphasizes the recovery phase of fake faces with poor recovery, facilitating the localization of tampered regions. Ourextensive experiments on four widely used benchmark datasets demonstrate that Delocate not onlyexcels in localizing tampered areas but also enhances cross-domain detection performance.
中摘: 深伪视频越来越逼真，面部区域在不同帧之间变化时几乎看不到篡改痕迹。因此，现有的深伪检测方法难以准确检测未知领域的深伪视频，同时又能精确定位篡改区域。为了解决这一限制，我们提出了Delocate，一种新颖的深伪检测模型，能够识别和定位未知领域的深伪视频。该方法由两个阶段组成，分别为恢复和定位。在恢复阶段，模型随机遮蔽感兴趣区域（ROIs），并重建无篡改痕迹的真实面部，从而对真实面部有相对较好的恢复效果，而对假面孔部则有较差的恢复效果。在定位阶段，恢复阶段的输出和伪造的ground truth遮罩作为监督，指导伪造定位过程。这个过程战略性地强调了恢复阶段表现不佳的假面孔部，有助于定位篡改区域。我们在四个广泛使用的基准数据集上的 extensive 实验表明，Delocate 不仅在定位篡改区域方面表现出色，还提高了跨域检测性能。

链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/677
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17454
英摘: Fined-grained anomalous cell detection from affected tissues is critical for clinical diagnosis and pathological research. Single-cell sequencing data provide unprecedented opportunities for this task. However, current anomaly detection methods struggle to handle domain shifts prevalent in multi-sample and multi-domain single-cell sequencing data, leading to suboptimal performance. Moreover, these methods fall short of distinguishing anomalous cells into pathologically distinct subtypes. In response, we propose ACSleuth, a novel, reconstruction deviation-guided generative framework that integrates the detection, domain adaptation, and fine-grained annotating of anomalous cells into a methodologically cohesive workflow. Notably, we present the first theoretical analysis of using reconstruction deviations output by generative models for anomaly detection in lieu of domain shifts. This analysis informs us to develop a novel and superior maximum mean discrepancy-based anomaly scorer in ACSleuth. Extensive benchmarks over various single-cell data and other types of tabular data demonstrate ACSleuth's superiority over the state-of-the-art methods in identifying and subtyping anomalies in multi-sample and multi-domain contexts. Our code is available at https://github.com/Catchxu/ACsleuth.
中摘: 精细的异常细胞检测对于临床诊断和病理研究至关重要。单细胞测序数据为这一任务提供了前所未有的机会。然而，当前的异常检测方法难以处理多样本和多领域单细胞测序数据中存在的领域偏移问题，导致性能不佳。此外，这些方法在区分异常细胞的不同病理亚型方面也存在不足。为应对这些问题，我们提出了一种名为ACSleuth的新颖框架，该框架通过生成模型引导的重建偏差进行集成，将异常细胞的检测、领域适应以及精细注释整合到一个方法论上连贯的工作流程中。值得注意的是，我们首次对使用生成模型输出的重建偏差进行异常检测进行了理论分析，而不是依赖于领域偏移。这一分析促使我们开发了ACSleuth中的新型且更优的最大均值偏差基异常评分器。在多种单细胞数据和其他类型的表格数据上的广泛基准测试表明，ACSleuth在多样本和多领域背景下识别和亚型划分异常方面优于最先进的方法。我们的代码可在https://github.com/Catchxu/ACsleuth获取。

[No.542] Dynamicity-aware Social Bot Detection with Dynamic Graph Transformers
来源: IJCAI 2025

英摘: Detecting social bots has evolved into a pivotal yet intricate task, aimed at combating the dissemination of misinformation and preserving the authenticity of online interactions. While earlier graph-based approaches, which leverage topological structure of social networks, yielded notable outcomes, they overlooked the inherent dynamicity of social networks -- In reality, they largely depicted the social network as a static graph and solely relied on its most recent state. Due to the absence of dynamicity modeling, such approaches are vulnerable to evasion, particularly when advanced social bots interact with other users to camouflage identities and escape detection. To tackle these challenges, we propose BotDGT, a novel framework that not only considers the topological structure, but also effectively incorporates dynamic nature of social network. Specifically, we characterize a social network as a dynamic graph. A structural module is employed to acquire topological information from each historical snapshot. Additionally, a temporal module is proposed to integrate historical context and model the evolving behavior patterns exhibited by social bots and legitimate users. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of BotDGT against the leading methods that neglected the dynamic nature of social networks in terms of accuracy, recall, and F1-score.

[No.551] DVPE: Divided View Position Embedding for Multi-View 3D Object Detection
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: detect
标题: DVPE: 分割视角位置嵌入在多视图三维目标检测中的应用
类别: Robotics
作者: Jiasen Wang;Zhenglin Li;Ke Sun;Xianyuan Liu;Yang Zhou;
机构: Shanghai University;University of Sheffield;
类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16955
英摘: Sparse query-based paradigms have achieved significant success in multi-view 3D detection for autonomous vehicles. Current research faces challenges in balancing between enlarging receptive fields and reducing interference when aggregating multi-view features. Moreover, different poses of cameras present challenges in training global attention models. To address these problems, this paper proposes a divided view method, in which features are modeled globally via the visibility crossattention mechanism, but interact only with partial features in a divided local virtual space. This effectively reduces interference from other irrelevant features and alleviates the training difficulties of the transformer by decoupling the position embedding from camera poses. Additionally, 2D historical RoI features are incorporated into the object-centric temporal modeling to utilize highlevel visual semantic information. The model is trained using a one-to-many assignment strategy to facilitate stability. Our framework, named DVPE, achieves state-of-the-art performance (57.2% mAP and 64.5% NDS) on the nuScenes test set. Codes will be available at https://github.com/dop0/DVPE.

[No.552] A Community-Centric Perspective for Characterizing and Detecting Anti-Asian Violence-Provoking Speech
来源: ACL 2025

标题: 以社区为中心的观点来描述和检测反亚裔暴力言论

作者: Gaurav Verma;Rynaa Grover;Jiawei Zhou;Binny Mathew;Jordan Kraemer;Munmun Choudhury;Srijan Kumar;

英摘: Violence-provoking speech -- speech that implicitly or explicitly promotes violence against the members of the targeted community, contributed to a massive surge in anti-Asian crimes during the pandemic. While previous works have characterized and built tools for detecting other forms of harmful speech, like fear speech and hate speech, our work takes a community-centric approach to studying anti-Asian violence-provoking speech. Using data from ~420k Twitter posts spanning a 3-year duration (January 1, 2020 to February 1, 2023), we develop a codebook to characterize anti-Asian violence-provoking speech and collect a community-crowdsourced dataset to facilitate its large-scale detection using state-of-the-art classifiers. We contrast the capabilities of natural language processing classifiers, ranging from BERT-based to LLM-based classifiers, in detecting violence-provoking speech with their capabilities to detect anti-Asian hateful speech. In contrast to prior work that has demonstrated the effectiveness of such classifiers in detecting hateful speech ($F_1 = 0.89$), our work shows that accurate and reliable detection of violence-provoking speech is a challenging task ($F_1 = 0.69$). We discuss the implications of our findings, particularly the need for proactive interventions to support Asian communities during public health crises. The resources related to the study are available at https://claws-lab.github.io/violence-provoking-speech/.

[No.553] A Glitch in the Matrix? Locating and Detecting Language Model Grounding with Fakepedia
来源: ACL 2025
命中: detect
标题: 《矩阵中的故障？》：通过假维基定位和检测语言模型语义 grounding
类别: 
作者: Giovanni Monea;Maxime Peyrard;Martin Josifoski;Vishrav Chaudhary;Jason Eisner;Emre Kiciman;Hamid Palangi;Barun Patra;Robert West;
机构: EPFL;Universite Grenoble Alpes;Microsoft;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.369/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.02073
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have an impressive ability to draw on novel information supplied in their context. Yet the mechanisms underlying this contextual grounding remain unknown, especially in situations where contextual information contradicts factual knowledge stored in the parameters, which LLMs also excel at recalling. Favoring the contextual information is critical for retrieval-augmented generation methods, which enrich the context with up-to-date information, hoping that grounding can rectify outdated or noisy stored knowledge. We present a novel method to study grounding abilities using Fakepedia, a novel dataset of counterfactual texts constructed to clash with a model's internal parametric knowledge. In this study, we introduce Fakepedia, a counterfactual dataset designed to evaluate grounding abilities when the internal parametric knowledge clashes with the contextual information. We benchmark various LLMs with Fakepedia and conduct a causal mediation analysis of LLM components when answering Fakepedia queries, based on our Masked Grouped Causal Tracing (MGCT) method. Through this analysis, we identify distinct computational patterns between grounded and ungrounded responses. We finally demonstrate that distinguishing grounded from ungrounded responses is achievable through computational analysis alone. Our results, together with existing findings about factual recall mechanisms, provide a coherent narrative of how grounding and factual recall mechanisms interact within LLMs.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）具有从其上下文中获取新颖信息的强大能力。然而，这些模型在上下文信息与参数中存储的事实知识相矛盾的情况下，其上下文关联机制仍然未知。特别是在这种情况下，偏好上下文信息对于增强生成方法的检索至关重要，这些方法希望通过增加最新的信息来丰富上下文，希望以此纠正过时或噪音较大的存储知识。我们提出了一种新的方法来研究关联能力，利用Fakepedia，这是一种设计用来与模型内部参数知识产生冲突的虚构文本数据集。在这个研究中，我们介绍了Fakepedia，这是一个反事实数据集，旨在评估当内部参数知识与上下文信息产生冲突时的语言模型的关联能力。我们使用Fakepedia对各种LLMs进行了基准测试，并基于我们的掩蔽分组因果追踪（MGCT）方法，对LLM组件在回答Fakepedia查询时进行了因果中介分析。通过这种分析，我们识别出了有联系和无联系响应之间不同的计算模式。最后，我们证明仅通过计算分析即可区分有联系和无联系的响应。我们的结果，结合现有关于事实回忆机制的研究发现，提供了一个关于LLMs中关联机制和事实回忆机制相互作用的连贯叙述。

[No.554] AFaCTA: Assisting the Annotation of Factual Claim Detection with Reliable LLM Annotators

英摘: With the rise of generative AI, automated fact-checking methods to combat misinformation are becoming more and more important. However, factual claim detection, the first step in a fact-checking pipeline, suffers from two key issues that limit its scalability and generalizability: (1) inconsistency in definitions of the task and what a claim is, and (2) the high cost of manual annotation. To address (1), we review the definitions in related work and propose a unifying definition of factual claims that focuses on verifiability. To address (2), we introduce AFaCTA (Automatic Factual Claim deTection Annotator), a novel framework that assists in the annotation of factual claims with the help of large language models (LLMs). AFaCTA calibrates its annotation confidence with consistency along three predefined reasoning paths. Extensive evaluation and experiments in the domain of political speech reveal that AFaCTA can efficiently assist experts in annotating factual claims and training high-quality classifiers, and can work with or without expert supervision. Our analyses also result in PoliClaim, a comprehensive claim detection dataset spanning diverse political topics.
中摘: 随着生成式AI的发展，对抗虚假信息的自动化事实核查方法变得越来越重要。然而，在事实核查管道中的第一步——事实声明检测方面，存在两个关键问题限制了其可扩展性和通用性：（1）任务定义和声明含义的一致性问题；（2）手动标注的成本高昂。为了解决这些问题，我们回顾了相关工作的定义，并提出了一种聚焦于可验证性的统一的事实声明定义。为了应对第二个问题，我们引入了AFaCTA（自动事实声明标注助手），这是一种新颖的框架，通过大型语言模型（LLMs）的帮助来辅助事实声明的标注。AFaCTA利用三个预定义的推理路径来校准其标注置信度。在政治演讲领域的广泛评估和实验表明，AFaCTA能够高效地协助专家进行事实声明的标注和高质量分类器的训练，并且可以在有专家监督或无专家监督的情况下工作。我们的分析还产生了PoliClaim，这是一个涵盖多种政治主题的综合声明检测数据集。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.555] AI ‘News’ Content Farms Are Easy to Make and Hard to Detect: A Case Study in Italian
来源: ACL 2025
命中: detect
标题: AI “新闻”内容农场容易建立但难以检测：意大利案例研究
类别: 
作者: Giovanni Puccetti;Anna Rogers;Chiara Alzetta;Felice Dell’Orletta;Andrea Esuli;
机构: Istituto di Scienza e Tecnologia dell’Informazione "A. Faedo";IT University of Copenhagen;Istituto di Linguistica Computazionale "Antonio Zampolli";
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.817/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12128
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as "content farm" models (CFMs), to generate synthetic text that could pass for real news articles. This is already happening even for languages that do not have high-quality monolingual LLMs. We show that fine-tuning Llama (v1), mostly trained on English, on as little as 40K Italian news articles, is sufficient for producing news-like texts that native speakers of Italian struggle to identify as synthetic.
  We investigate three LLMs and three methods of detecting synthetic texts (log-likelihood, DetectGPT, and supervised classification), finding that they all perform better than human raters, but they are all impractical in the real world (requiring either access to token likelihood information or a large dataset of CFM texts). We also explore the possibility of creating a proxy CFM: an LLM fine-tuned on a similar dataset to one used by the real "content farm". We find that even a small amount of fine-tuning data suffices for creating a successful detector, but we need to know which base LLM is used, which is a major challenge.
  Our results suggest that there are currently no practical methods for detecting synthetic news-like texts 'in the wild', while generating them is too easy. We highlight the urgency of more NLP research on this problem.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用作“内容农场”模型（CFMs），生成能够通过真实新闻文章检验的合成文本。即使对于几乎没有高质量单语LLM的语言，这种情况也已经发生。我们发现，仅对意大利语新闻文章进行40K篇的微调，Llama（v1）主要在英语上训练过的模型，就足以产生意大利语母语者难以辨识为合成文本的类似新闻的文章。

我们研究了三种LLM和三种检测合成文本的方法（对数似然性、DetectGPT和监督分类），发现它们都比人类评审员表现更好，但在实际应用中却都不实用（要么需要访问标记概率信息，要么需要大量CFM文本的数据集）。我们还探讨了创建代理CFM的可能性：一个在类似数据集上微调的LLM，该数据集与实际“内容农场”使用的数据集相似。我们发现，即使是少量的微调数据也足以创建成功的检测器，但我们需要知道使用了哪个基础LLM，这是一个主要挑战。

我们的研究结果表明，在现实世界中目前尚无实用的方法来检测合成的类似新闻的文章，而生成它们却过于容易。这突显了对该问题进行更多自然语言处理（NLP）研究的紧迫性。

[No.556] An Entropy-based Text Watermarking Detection Method
来源: ACL 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于熵的文本水印检测方法
类别: 
作者: Yijian Lu;Aiwei Liu;Dianzhi Yu;Jingjing Li;Irwin King;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;Tsinghua University;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.630/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13485
英摘: Text watermarking algorithms for large language models (LLMs) can effectively identify machine-generated texts by embedding and detecting hidden features in the text. Although the current text watermarking algorithms perform well in most high-entropy scenarios, its performance in low-entropy scenarios still needs to be improved. In this work, we opine that the influence of token entropy should be fully considered in the watermark detection process, $i.e.$, the weight of each token during watermark detection should be customized according to its entropy, rather than setting the weights of all tokens to the same value as in previous methods. Specifically, we propose \textbf{E}ntropy-based Text \textbf{W}atermarking \textbf{D}etection (\textbf{EWD}) that gives higher-entropy tokens higher influence weights during watermark detection, so as to better reflect the degree of watermarking. Furthermore, the proposed detection process is training-free and fully automated. From the experiments, we demonstrate that our EWD can achieve better detection performance in low-entropy scenarios, and our method is also general and can be applied to texts with different entropy distributions. Our code and data is available\footnote{\url{https://github.com/luyijian3/EWD}}. Additionally, our algorithm could be accessed through MarkLLM \cite{pan2024markllm}\footnote{\url{https://github.com/THU-BPM/MarkLLM}}.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的文本水印算法可以通过在文本中嵌入和检测隐藏特征来有效识别机器生成的文本。尽管当前的文本水印算法在大多数高熵场景下表现良好，但在低熵场景下的性能仍有待提高。在本工作中，我们认为应在水印检测过程中充分考虑token的熵的影响，即在水印检测过程中，应根据每个token的熵为其分配定制化的权重，而不是像之前的方法那样将所有token的权重设置为相同值。具体来说，我们提出了基于熵的文本水印检测（EWD），该方法在水印检测过程中赋予更高熵的token更高的影响权重，以便更好地反映水印的程度。此外，所提出的检测过程无需训练且完全自动化。从实验结果来看，我们的EWD在低熵场景下的检测性能更好，并且我们的方法具有通用性，可以应用于不同熵分布的文本。我们的代码和数据可在以下链接获取：[https://github.com/luyijian3/EWD]。另外，我们的算法可以通过MarkLLM [1]访问：[https://github.com/THU-BPM/MarkLLM]。

[No.557] ‘Quis custodiet ipsos custodes?’ Who will watch the watchmen? On Detecting AI-generated peer-reviews
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: “谁来监督这些监督者？”——检测AI生成的同行评审
类别: 
作者: Sandeep Kumar;Mohit Sahu;Vardhan Gacche;Tirthankar Ghosal;Asif Ekbal;
机构: Indian Institute of Technology Patna;Oak Ridge National Laboratory;IIT Jodhpur;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09770
英摘: The integrity of the peer-review process is vital for maintaining scientific rigor and trust within the academic community. With the steady increase in the usage of large language models (LLMs) like ChatGPT in academic writing, there is a growing concern that AI-generated texts could compromise scientific publishing, including peer-reviews. Previous works have focused on generic AI-generated text detection or have presented an approach for estimating the fraction of peer-reviews that can be AI-generated. Our focus here is to solve a real-world problem by assisting the editor or chair in determining whether a review is written by ChatGPT or not. To address this, we introduce the Term Frequency (TF) model, which posits that AI often repeats tokens, and the Review Regeneration (RR) model, which is based on the idea that ChatGPT generates similar outputs upon re-prompting. We stress test these detectors against token attack and paraphrasing. Finally, we propose an effective defensive strategy to reduce the effect of paraphrasing on our models. Our findings suggest both our proposed methods perform better than the other AI text detectors. Our RR model is more robust, although our TF model performs better than the RR model without any attacks. We make our code, dataset, and model public.
中摘: 同行评审过程的完整性对于维护学术界的科学严谨性和信任至关重要。随着大型语言模型（如ChatGPT）在学术写作中的使用逐渐增加，人们越来越担心AI生成的文本可能会损害科学出版，包括同行评审。以往的研究主要集中在通用的AI生成文本检测上，或者提出了估算可以由AI生成的同行评审比例的方法。在这里，我们的重点是通过辅助编辑或主席来确定一篇评论是否由ChatGPT撰写，从而解决一个实际问题。为此，我们引入了词频（TF）模型，该模型认为AI经常重复单词；以及基于ChatGPT在重新提示时生成类似输出这一理念的评论再生（RR）模型。我们对这些检测器进行了压力测试，以应对令牌攻击和改写。最后，我们提出了一种有效的防御策略，以减少改写对我们模型的影响。我们的研究发现表明，我们提出的方法都优于其他AI文本检测器。尽管在没有攻击的情况下，我们的TF模型表现优于RR模型，但RR模型更为稳健。我们将我们的代码、数据集和模型公开。

[No.559] A new approach for fine-tuning sentence transformers for intent classification and out-of-scope detection tasks
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 一种新的方法用于微调句向量模型进行意图分类和超出范围检测任务
类别: 
作者: Tianyi Zhang;Atta Norouzian;Aanchan Mohan;Frederick Ducatelle;
机构: Northeastern University;University of Victoria;Cerence Inc;
类型: Industry
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-industry.68/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13649
英摘: In virtual assistant (VA) systems it is important to reject or redirect user queries that fall outside the scope of the system. One of the most accurate approaches for out-of-scope (OOS) rejection is to combine it with the task of intent classification on in-scope queries, and to use methods based on the similarity of embeddings produced by transformer-based sentence encoders. Typically, such encoders are fine-tuned for the intent-classification task, using cross-entropy loss. Recent work has shown that while this produces suitable embeddings for the intent-classification task, it also tends to disperse in-scope embeddings over the full sentence embedding space. This causes the in-scope embeddings to potentially overlap with OOS embeddings, thereby making OOS rejection difficult. This is compounded when OOS data is unknown. To mitigate this issue our work proposes to regularize the cross-entropy loss with an in-scope embedding reconstruction loss learned using an auto-encoder. Our method achieves a 1-4% improvement in the area under the precision-recall curve for rejecting out-of-sample (OOS) instances, without compromising intent classification performance.
中摘: 在虚拟助手（VA）系统中，拒绝或重定向超出系统范围的用户查询非常重要。一种最准确的超出范围（OOS）拒绝方法是将其与对范围内查询的意图分类任务结合起来，并使用基于变压器句编码器生成的嵌入相似性的方法。通常，这些编码器会针对意图分类任务进行微调，使用交叉熵损失。最近的研究表明，虽然这种方法产生了适合意图分类的嵌入，但它也倾向于将范围内嵌入分散在整个句子嵌入空间中。这导致范围内嵌入可能与超出范围嵌入重叠，从而使超出范围的拒绝变得更加困难。当超出范围的数据未知时，这一问题更为严重。为缓解这一问题，我们的研究提出用自编码器学习的范围内嵌入重建损失来正则化交叉熵损失。我们的方法在拒绝未见过的样本（OOS）实例方面实现了1-4%的改进，同时不牺牲意图分类性能。

[No.560] A Pointer Network-based Approach for Joint Extraction and Detection of Multi-Label Multi-Class Intents
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于指针网络的方法：联合提取和检测多标签多类意图
类别: 
作者: Ankan Mullick;Sombit Bose;Abhilash Nandy;Gajula Sai Chaitanya;Pawan Goyal;

英摘: In task-oriented dialogue systems, intent detection is crucial for interpreting user queries and providing appropriate responses. Existing research primarily addresses simple queries with a single intent, lacking effective systems for handling complex queries with multiple intents and extracting different intent spans. Additionally, there is a notable absence of multilingual, multi-intent datasets. This study addresses three critical tasks: extracting multiple intent spans from queries, detecting multiple intents, and developing a multi-lingual multi-label intent dataset. We introduce a novel multi-label multi-class intent detection dataset (MLMCID-dataset) curated from existing benchmark datasets. We also propose a pointer network-based architecture (MLMCID) to extract intent spans and detect multiple intents with coarse and fine-grained labels in the form of sextuplets. 来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect

We aim to provide a detailed overview of existing detection strategies and benchmarks, scrutinizing their differences and identifying key challenges and prospects in the field, advocating for more adaptable and robust models to enhance detection accuracy. We also posit the necessity for a multi-faceted approach to defend against various attacks to counter the rapidly advancing capabilities of LLMs. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first comprehensive survey on the detection in the era of LLMs. We hope it will provide a broad understanding of the current landscape of LLMs-generated content detection, offering a guiding reference for researchers and practitioners striving to uphold the integrity of digital information in an era increasingly dominated by synthetic content. [No.563] Authorship Obfuscation in Multilingual Machine-Generated Text Detection
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 多语言机器生成文本作者混淆检测
类别: 
作者: Dominik Macko;Robert Moro;Adaku Uchendu;Ivan Srba;Jason S Lucas;Michiharu Yamashita;Nafis Irtiza Tripto;Dongwon Lee;Jakub Simko;Maria Bielikova;
机构: Kempelen Institute of Intelligent Technologies;Massachusetts Institute of Technology Lincoln Laboratory;Pennsylvania State University;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.369/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.07867
英摘: High-quality text generation capability of recent Large Language Models (LLMs) causes concerns about their misuse (e.g., in massive generation/spread of disinformation). Machine-generated text (MGT) detection is important to cope with such threats. However, it is susceptible to authorship obfuscation (AO) methods, such as paraphrasing, which can cause MGTs to evade detection. So far, this was evaluated only in monolingual settings. Thus, the susceptibility of recently proposed multilingual detectors is still unknown. We fill this gap by comprehensively benchmarking the performance of 10 well-known AO methods, attacking 37 MGT detection methods against MGTs in 11 languages (i.e., 10 $\times$ 37 $\times$ 11 = 4,070 combinations). We also evaluate the effect of data augmentation on adversarial robustness using obfuscated texts. The results indicate that all tested AO methods can cause evasion of automated detection in all tested languages, where homoglyph attacks are especially successful. However, some of the AO methods severely damaged the text, making it no longer readable or easily recognizable by humans (e.g., changed language, weird characters).
中摘: 近年来大型语言模型（LLMs）的高质量文本生成能力引发了对其误用的担忧（例如，在大规模生成/传播虚假信息方面的应用）。机器生成文本（MGT）检测对于应对这些威胁至关重要。然而，这种方法容易受到作者身份模糊化（AO）方法的影响，如改写，这可能导致MGT逃避检测。迄今为止，这种影响仅在单语环境中进行了评估。因此，最近提出的多语言检测器的脆弱性仍然未知。我们通过全面基准测试10种知名AO方法，攻击37种MGT检测方法来填补这一空白，针对11种语言中的MGT进行检测（即，10×37×11=4,070种组合）。我们还使用模糊化的文本评估数据增强对对抗鲁棒性的影响。结果显示，所有测试的AO方法都能导致所有测试语言中的自动化检测被绕过，其中同形字攻击尤其成功。然而，有些AO方法严重破坏了文本，使其变得不再可读或难以由人类识别（例如，改变语言、使用奇怪的字符）。

[No.564] AutoDetect: Towards a Unified Framework for Automated Weakness Detection in Large Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: AutoDetect：迈向统一的大语言模型自动化弱点检测框架
类别: 
作者: Jiale Cheng;Yida Lu;Xiaotao Gu;Pei Ke;Xiao Liu;Yuxiao Dong;Hongning Wang;Jie Tang;Minlie Huang;
机构: 
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.397/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16714
英摘: Although Large Language Models (LLMs) are becoming increasingly powerful, they still exhibit significant but subtle weaknesses, such as mistakes in instruction-following or coding tasks. As these unexpected errors could lead to severe consequences in practical deployments, it is crucial to investigate the limitations within LLMs systematically. Traditional benchmarking approaches cannot thoroughly pinpoint specific model deficiencies, while manual inspections are costly and not scalable. In this paper, we introduce a unified framework, AutoDetect, to automatically expose weaknesses in LLMs across various tasks. Inspired by the educational assessment process that measures students' learning outcomes, AutoDetect consists of three LLM-powered agents: Examiner, Questioner, and Assessor. The collaboration among these three agents is designed to realize comprehensive and in-depth weakness identification. Our framework demonstrates significant success in uncovering flaws, with an identification success rate exceeding 30% in prominent models such as ChatGPT and Claude. More importantly, these identified weaknesses can guide specific model improvements, proving more effective than untargeted data augmentation methods like Self-Instruct. Our approach has led to substantial enhancements in popular LLMs, including the Llama series and Mistral-7b, boosting their performance by over 10% across several benchmarks. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/thu-coai/AutoDetect.
中摘: 尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）正在变得越来越强大，但它们仍然表现出显著但微妙的弱点，例如在指令遵循或编程任务中的错误。由于这些意外错误可能在实际部署中导致严重后果，系统地调查LLMs的局限性至关重要。传统的基准测试方法无法彻底发现特定模型的缺陷，而人工检查则成本高昂且不可扩展。在这篇论文中，我们介绍了一个统一框架AutoDetect，用于自动在各种任务中揭示LLMs的弱点。受教育评估过程测量学生学习成果的启发，AutoDetect由三个LLM驱动的代理组成：考官、提问者和评估者。这三个代理之间的协作旨在实现全面深入的弱点识别。我们的框架在揭露缺陷方面取得了显著成功，在ChatGPT和Claude等知名模型中，缺陷识别成功率超过30%。更重要的是，这些识别出的弱点可以指导特定模型的改进，比未瞄准的数据增强方法如Self-Instruct更有效。我们的方法已经显著提升了流行的LLM，包括Llama系列和Mistral-7b，在多个基准测试中提高了其性能超过10%。代码和数据可在https://github.com/thu-coai/AutoDetect公开获取。

[No.565] Bi-DCSpell: A Bi-directional Detector-Corrector Interactive Framework for Chinese Spelling Check
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: Bi-DCSpell：一种双向检测-纠正交互框架用于中文拼写检查
类别: 
作者: Haiming Wu;Hanqing Zhang;Richeng Xuan;Dawei Song;
机构: Beijing Institute of Technology;China North Industries Group Corporation Limited;Beijing Academy of Artificial Intelligence;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.229/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01879
英摘: Chinese Spelling Check (CSC) aims to detect and correct potentially misspelled characters in Chinese sentences. Naturally, it involves the detection and correction subtasks, which interact with each other dynamically. Such interactions are bi-directional, i.e., the detection result would help reduce the risk of over-correction and under-correction while the knowledge learnt from correction would help prevent false detection. Current CSC approaches are of two types: correction-only or single-directional detection-to-correction interactive frameworks. Nonetheless, they overlook the bi-directional interactions between detection and correction. This paper aims to fill the gap by proposing a Bi-directional Detector-Corrector framework for CSC (Bi-DCSpell). Notably, Bi-DCSpell contains separate detection and correction encoders, followed by a novel interactive learning module facilitating bi-directional feature interactions between detection and correction to improve each other's representation learning. Extensive experimental results demonstrate a robust correction performance of Bi-DCSpell on widely used benchmarking datasets while possessing a satisfactory detection ability.
中摘: 中文拼写检查（CSC）旨在检测并纠正中文句子中可能拼错的字符。自然地，这涉及到检测和修正子任务之间的动态交互。这种交互是双向的，即检测结果有助于减少过度修正和不足修正的风险，而从修正中学到的知识也有助于防止误检。当前的CSC方法有两种类型：仅修正或单向检测到修正的交互框架。然而，它们忽视了检测与修正之间的双向交互。本文旨在通过提出一种双向检测-修正框架（Bi-DCSpell）来填补这一空白。值得注意的是，Bi-DCSpell包含独立的检测编码器和修正编码器，随后是一个新颖的交互学习模块，该模块促进检测与修正之间的双向特征交互，以提高彼此的表示学习能力。广泛的实验结果表明，Bi-DCSpell在广泛使用的基准数据集上表现出稳健的修正性能，同时具备满意的检测能力。

[No.571] Cross-Domain Audio Deepfake Detection: Dataset and Analysis
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 跨域音频换脸检测：数据集与分析
类别: 
作者: Yuang Li;Min Zhang;Mengxin Ren;Xiaosong Qiao;Miaomiao Ma;Daimeng Wei;Hao Yang;
机构: Huawei;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.286/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04904
英摘: Audio deepfake detection (ADD) is essential for preventing the misuse of synthetic voices that may infringe on personal rights and privacy. Recent zero-shot text-to-speech (TTS) models pose higher risks as they can clone voices with a single utterance. However, the existing ADD datasets are outdated, leading to suboptimal generalization of detection models. In this paper, we construct a new cross-domain ADD dataset comprising over 300 hours of speech data that is generated by five advanced zero-shot TTS models. To simulate real-world scenarios, we employ diverse attack methods and audio prompts from different datasets. Experiments show that, through novel attack-augmented training, the Wav2Vec2-large and Whisper-medium models achieve equal error rates of 4.1\% and 6.5\% respectively. Additionally, we demonstrate our models' outstanding few-shot ADD ability by fine-tuning with just one minute of target-domain data. Nonetheless, neural codec compressors greatly affect the detection accuracy, necessitating further research.
中摘: 音频换音检测（ADD）对于防止滥用合成语音以侵犯个人权利和隐私至关重要。最近的零样本文本转语音（TTS）模型带来了更高的风险，因为它们只需一句话就能克隆声音。然而，现有的ADD数据集已经过时，导致检测模型的泛化能力不佳。在本文中，我们构建了一个新的跨域ADD数据集，其中包括由五个先进的零样本TTS模型生成的超过300小时的语音数据。为了模拟真实世界场景，我们采用了来自不同数据集的多种攻击方法和音频提示。实验结果显示，通过新颖的攻击增强训练，Wav2Vec2-large和Whisper-medium模型分别实现了4.1%和6.5%的等错误率。此外，我们还通过仅使用一分钟的目标领域数据进行微调，展示了模型在少量样本ADD方面的出色能力。不过，神经编码压缩器极大地影响了检测准确性，需要进一步研究。

[No.573] D3CODE: Disentangling Disagreements in Data across Cultures on Offensiveness Detection and Evaluation
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: D3CODE：跨文化数据中的冒犯性检测与评估分歧剖析
类别: 
作者: Aida Mostafazadeh Davani;Mark Diaz;Dylan K Baker;Vinodkumar Prabhakaran;
机构: Google;DAIR Institute;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.1029/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10857
英摘: While human annotations play a crucial role in language technologies, annotator subjectivity has long been overlooked in data collection. Recent studies that have critically examined this issue are often situated in the Western context, and solely document differences across age, gender, or racial groups. As a result, NLP research on subjectivity have overlooked the fact that individuals within demographic groups may hold diverse values, which can influence their perceptions beyond their group norms. To effectively incorporate these considerations into NLP pipelines, we need datasets with extensive parallel annotations from various social and cultural groups. In this paper we introduce the \dataset dataset: a large-scale cross-cultural dataset of parallel annotations for offensive language in over 4.5K sentences annotated by a pool of over 4k annotators, balanced across gender and age, from across 21 countries, representing eight geo-cultural regions. The dataset contains annotators' moral values captured along six moral foundations: care, equality, proportionality, authority, loyalty, and purity. Our analyses reveal substantial regional variations in annotators' perceptions that are shaped by individual moral values, offering crucial insights for building pluralistic, culturally sensitive NLP models.
中摘: 尽管人类注释在语言技术中发挥着至关重要的作用，但在数据收集过程中，注释者的主观性长期以来一直被忽视。最近一些深入研究这一问题的研究多处于西方语境下，并且仅记录了年龄、性别或种族群体之间的差异。因此，自然语言处理（NLP）研究在处理主观性时忽略了这样一个事实：同一人口统计学群体内的个体可能持有不同的价值观，这些价值观可以超出其群体规范而影响他们的感知。为了有效将这些考虑因素纳入NLP管道中，我们需要包含来自各种社会和文化群体的大量平行注释的数据集。在本文中，我们介绍了\dataset 数据集：一个跨文化的大规模平行注释数据集，涵盖了超过4500个句子，由超过4000名注释者进行标注，这些注释者来自21个国家，代表八个地理文化区域，并在性别和年龄方面实现了平衡。该数据集包含了注释者沿六种道德基础——关怀、平等、比例性、权威、忠诚和纯洁——捕获的道德价值观。我们的分析揭示了注释者感知中的显著区域差异，这些差异是由个人道德价值观所塑造的，为构建多元文化和敏感的NLP模型提供了关键见解。

[No.575] DECOR: Improving Coherence in L2 English Writing with a Novel Benchmark for Incoherence Detection, Reasoning, and Rewriting
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: DECOR：一种新颖的不连贯检测、推理和重写基准以提高二语英语写作的连贯性
类别: 
作者: Xuanming Zhang;Anthony Diaz;Zixun Chen;Qingyang Wu;Kun Qian;Erik Voss;Zhou Yu;
机构: Columbia University;University of California, Davis;

链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.639/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19650
英摘: Coherence in writing, an aspect that second-language (L2) English learners often struggle with, is crucial in assessing L2 English writing. Existing automated writing evaluation systems primarily use basic surface linguistic features to detect coherence in writing. However, little effort has been made to correct the detected incoherence, which could significantly benefit L2 language learners seeking to improve their writing. To bridge this gap, we introduce DECOR, a novel benchmark that includes expert annotations for detecting incoherence in L2 English writing, identifying the underlying reasons, and rewriting the incoherent sentences. To our knowledge, DECOR is the first coherence assessment dataset specifically designed for improving L2 English writing, featuring pairs of original incoherent sentences alongside their expert-rewritten counterparts. Additionally, we fine-tuned models to automatically detect and rewrite incoherence in student essays. We find that incorporating specific reasons for incoherence during fine-tuning consistently improves the quality of the rewrites, achieving a result that is favored in both automatic and human evaluations.
中摘: 写作连贯性是评估二语（L2）英语写作能力的一个重要方面，而这一点往往是二语英语学习者面临的挑战。现有的自动作文评价系统主要依赖基本的语言表层特征来检测写作连贯性。然而，很少有研究致力于纠正检测出的不连贯之处，这对于寻求提高写作能力的二语语言学习者来说具有显著的好处。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了DECOR，这是一个新颖的基准，其中包括了专家标注来检测二语英语写作中的不连贯性、识别其背后的原因以及重写不连贯句子。据我们所知，DECOR是首个专门设计用于提高二语英语写作连贯性的评估数据集，它包含了原始不连贯句子及其专家重写版本的配对。此外，我们还微调了模型以自动检测并重写学生论文中的不连贯性。我们的研究表明，在微调过程中纳入具体的不连贯原因可以一致地提高重写质量，并在自动和人工评估中均得到了青睐。

[No.577] DetectBench: Can Large Language Model Detect and Piece Together Implicit Evidence?
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: DetectBench: 大型语言模型能够检测和拼凑隐含证据吗？
类别: 
作者: Zhouhong Gu;Lin Zhang;Xiaoxuan Zhu;Jiangjie Chen;Wenhao Huang;Yikai Zhang;Shusen Wang;Zheyu Ye;Yan Gao;Hongwei Feng;Yanghua Xiao;
机构: 
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.11/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12641
英摘: Detecting evidence within the context is a key step in the process of reasoning task. Evaluating and enhancing the capabilities of LLMs in evidence detection will strengthen context-based reasoning performance. This paper proposes a benchmark called DetectBench for verifying the ability to detect and piece together implicit evidence within a long context. DetectBench contains 3,928 multiple-choice questions, with an average of 994 tokens per question. Each question contains an average of 4.55 pieces of implicit evidence, and solving the problem typically requires 7.62 logical jumps to find the correct answer. To enhance the performance of LLMs in evidence detection, this paper proposes Detective Reasoning Prompt and Finetune. Experiments demonstrate that the existing LLMs' abilities to detect evidence in long contexts are far inferior to humans. However, the Detective Reasoning Prompt effectively enhances the capability of powerful LLMs in evidence detection, while the Finetuning method shows significant effects in enhancing the performance of weaker LLMs. Moreover, when the abilities of LLMs in evidence detection are improved, their final reasoning performance is also enhanced accordingly.
中摘: 在推理任务过程中，在上下文中检测证据是一个关键步骤。评估和提升语言模型（LLMs）在证据检测方面的能力将增强基于上下文的推理表现。本文提出了一项名为DetectBench的基准测试，用于验证检测和拼接长上下文中的隐含证据的能力。DetectBench包含3,928个多选题，每个问题平均有994个标记。每个问题平均包含4.55个隐含证据片段，解决问题通常需要进行7.62次逻辑跳跃以找到正确答案。为了提高语言模型在证据检测方面的性能，本文提出了侦探推理提示和微调方法。实验表明，现有的语言模型在长上下文中的证据检测能力远不及人类。然而，侦探推理提示有效地提升了强大语言模型在证据检测方面的能力，而微调方法则显著增强了较弱语言模型的性能。此外，当语言模型的证据检测能力得到提高时，它们的整体推理表现也随之提升。

[No.578] Detecting Ambiguous Utterances in an Intelligent Assistant

[No.579] Detecting Errors through Ensembling Prompts (DEEP): An End-to-End LLM Framework for Detecting Factual Errors
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 通过集成提示检测错误（DEEP）：一个端到端的大语言模型框架，用于检测事实性错误
类别: 
作者: Alex Chandler;Devesh Surve;Hui Su;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;Northeastern University;Fidelity Investments;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.728/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13009
英摘: Accurate text summarization is one of the most common and important tasks performed by Large Language Models, where the costs of human review for an entire document may be high, but the costs of errors in summarization may be even greater. We propose Detecting Errors through Ensembling Prompts (DEEP) - an end-to-end large language model framework for detecting factual errors in text summarization. Our framework uses a diverse set of LLM prompts to identify factual inconsistencies, treating their outputs as binary features, which are then fed into ensembling models. We then calibrate the ensembled models to produce empirically accurate probabilities that a text is factually consistent or free of hallucination. We demonstrate that prior models for detecting factual errors in summaries perform significantly worse without optimizing the thresholds on subsets of the evaluated dataset. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) balanced accuracy on the AggreFact-XSUM FTSOTA, TofuEval Summary-Level, and HaluEval Summarization benchmarks in detecting factual errors within transformer-generated text summaries. It does so without any fine-tuning of the language model or reliance on thresholding techniques not available in practical settings.
中摘: 准确的文字总结是大型语言模型执行的最常见和最重要的任务之一，其中对整个文档进行人工审核的成本可能很高，但总结中的错误成本可能会更高。我们提出了一种通过集成提示检测错误（DEEP）框架——这是一种端到端的大规模语言模型框架，用于在文本总结中检测事实错误。该框架使用一组多样的LLM提示来识别事实不一致，将它们的输出视为二元特征，然后输入到集成模型中。接着，我们校准这些集成模型以生成关于文本是否事实一致或无幻觉的实证准确概率。我们证明了之前用于检测摘要中事实错误的模型，在未优化评估数据集子集上的阈值的情况下表现显著较差。我们的框架在AggreFact-XSUM FTSOTA、TofuEval Summary-Level和HaluEval Summarization基准测试中实现了最先进的平衡准确性，用于检测Transformer生成的文本摘要中的事实错误，且无需对语言模型进行微调或依赖于实际应用场景中不可用的阈值技术。

[No.581] Detecting Machine-Generated Long-Form Content with Latent-Space Variables
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于潜在空间变量检测机器生成的长格式内容
类别: 
作者: Yufei Tian;Zeyu Pan;Nanyun Peng;
机构: University of California, Los Angeles;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.608/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03856
英摘: The increasing capability of large language models (LLMs) to generate fluent long-form texts is presenting new challenges in distinguishing machine-generated outputs from human-written ones, which is crucial for ensuring authenticity and trustworthiness of expressions. Existing zero-shot detectors primarily focus on token-level distributions, which are vulnerable to real-world domain shifts, including different prompting and decoding strategies, and adversarial attacks. We propose a more robust method that incorporates abstract elements, such as event transitions, as key deciding factors to detect machine versus human texts by training a latent-space model on sequences of events or topics derived from human-written texts. In three different domains, machine-generated texts, which are originally inseparable from human texts on the token level, can be better distinguished with our latent-space model, leading to a 31% improvement over strong baselines such as DetectGPT. Our analysis further reveals that, unlike humans, modern LLMs like GPT-4 generate event triggers and their transitions differently, an inherent disparity that helps our method to robustly detect machine-generated texts.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）生成流畅长篇文本的能力不断提高，这为区分机器生成的输出和人类撰写的文本带来了新的挑战，这对于确保表达的真实性和可信度至关重要。现有的零样本检测器主要关注于词元级别的分布，这些检测器容易受到现实世界领域变化的影响，包括不同的提示和解码策略，以及对抗性攻击。我们提出了一种更为 robust 的方法，通过在事件或主题序列上训练一个潜在空间模型，将抽象元素，如事件转换，作为关键决定因素来区分机器生成的文本与人类撰写的文本。在三个不同的领域中，原本在词元级别难以区分开来的机器生成文本，可以通过我们的潜在空间模型更好地加以区分，从而在诸如DetectGPT等强 baseline 上取得31%的改进。进一步的分析还表明，与人类不同，现代大型语言模型如GPT-4在生成事件触发器及其转换方面存在差异，这种固有的差异有助于我们的方法 robust 地检测机器生成的文本。

[No.583] Detecting Subtle Differences between Human and Model Languages Using Spectrum of Relative Likelihood
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 使用相对可能性谱检测人类语言与模型语言之间的细微差异
类别: 
作者: Yang Xu;Yu Wang;Hao An;Zhichen Liu;Yongyuan Li;
机构: Southern University of Science and Technology;Bielefeld University;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.564/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19874
英摘: Human and model-generated texts can be distinguished by examining the magnitude of likelihood in language. However, it is becoming increasingly difficult as language model's capabilities of generating human-like texts keep evolving. This study provides a new perspective by using the relative likelihood values instead of absolute ones, and extracting useful features from the spectrum-view of likelihood for the human-model text detection task. We propose a detection procedure with two classification methods, supervised and heuristic-based, respectively, which results in competitive performances with previous zero-shot detection methods and a new state-of-the-art on short-text detection. Our method can also reveal subtle differences between human and model languages, which find theoretical roots in psycholinguistics studies. Our code is available at https://github.com/CLCS-SUSTech/FourierGPT
中摘: 通过分析语言在概率上的差异，人类生成的文本和模型生成的文本可以被区分。然而，随着语言模型生成类人文本的能力不断提升，这种区分变得越来越困难。本研究提供了一个新的视角，通过使用相对概率值而非绝对值，并从概率频谱的角度提取有用的特征来进行人类与模型文本检测任务。我们提出了两种分类方法：监督学习和启发式方法，这两种方法在短文本检测方面分别达到了与之前零样本检测方法相当甚至更好的性能。我们的方法还可以揭示人类语言和模型语言之间的细微差异，这些差异在心理语言学的研究中有理论基础。我们的代码可在https://github.com/CLCS-SUSTech/FourierGPT获取。

[No.584] Detecting Temporal Ambiguity in Questions
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 检测问题中的时间歧义
类别: 
作者: Bhawna Piryani;Abdelrahman Abdallah;Jamshid Mozafari;Adam Jatowt;
机构: University of Innsbruck;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.562/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17046
英摘: Detecting and answering ambiguous questions has been a challenging task in open-domain question answering. Ambiguous questions have different answers depending on their interpretation and can take diverse forms. Temporally ambiguous questions are one of the most common types of such questions. In this paper, we introduce TEMPAMBIQA, a manually annotated temporally ambiguous QA dataset consisting of 8,162 open-domain questions derived from existing datasets. Our annotations focus on capturing temporal ambiguity to study the task of detecting temporally ambiguous questions. We propose a novel approach by using diverse search strategies based on disambiguated versions of the questions. We also introduce and test non-search, competitive baselines for detecting temporal ambiguity using zero-shot and few-shot approaches.
中摘: 在开放领域问答中，检测和回答含糊问题一直是一个具有挑战性的任务。含糊问题根据其不同的解释有不同的答案，并且可以采取多种形式。时间含糊问题是这类问题中最常见的类型之一。在本文中，我们介绍了TEMPAMBIQA，这是一个手工标注的时间含糊问答数据集，包含来自现有数据集的8,162个开放领域问题。我们的标注重点在于捕捉时间含糊性，以研究检测时间含糊问题的任务。我们提出了一种新颖的方法，通过基于去含糊化版本问题的多样化搜索策略来实现。我们还引入并测试了非搜索、竞争性基线方法，使用零样本和少样本方法检测时间含糊性。

[No.585] Detection and Measurement of Syntactic Templates in Generated Text
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 生成文本中的句法模板检测与测量
类别: 
作者: Chantal Shaib;Yanai Elazar;Junyi Jessy Li;Byron C Wallace;
机构: Northeastern University;Allen Institute for AI;University of Washington;University of Texas at Austin;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.368/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00211
英摘: Recent work on evaluating the diversity of text generated by LLMs has focused on word-level features. Here we offer an analysis of syntactic features to characterize general repetition in models, beyond frequent n-grams. Specifically, we define syntactic templates and show that models tend to produce templated text in downstream tasks at a higher rate than what is found in human-reference texts. We find that most (76%) templates in model-generated text can be found in pre-training data (compared to only 35% of human-authored text), and are not overwritten during fine-tuning processes such as RLHF. This connection to the pre-training data allows us to analyze syntactic templates in models where we do not have the pre-training data. We also find that templates as features are able to differentiate between models, tasks, and domains, and are useful for qualitatively evaluating common model constructions. Finally, we demonstrate the use of templates as a useful tool for analyzing style memorization of training data in LLMs.
中摘: 最近对评估由大规模语言模型（LLMs）生成的文本多样性的工作主要集中在词级特征上。在这里，我们提供了一种分析句法特征的方法，以表征模型中的普遍重复现象，超越频繁出现的n-克隆。具体来说，我们定义了句法模板，并展示了在下游任务中，模型倾向于以更高的频率生成模板化的文本，而这种现象在人类参考文本中并不常见。我们发现，在模型生成的文本中，大多数（76%）的模板可以在预训练数据中找到（相比之下，只有35%的人类撰写的文本包含这些模板），并且这些模板在如RLHF这样的微调过程中不会被覆盖。这种与预训练数据的联系使我们能够在没有预训练数据的情况下分析模型中的句法模板。我们还发现，将模板作为特征可以区分不同的模型、任务和领域，并且对于定性评估常见的模型构建方式非常有用。最后，我们展示了如何使用模板作为分析LLMs中训练数据风格记忆的有用工具。

[No.589] ECON: On the Detection and Resolution of Evidence Conflicts
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 经济：证据冲突的检测与解决
类别: 
作者: Cheng Jiayang;Chunkit Chan;Qianqian Zhuang;Lin Qiu;Tianhang Zhang;Tengxiao Liu;Yangqiu Song;Yue Zhang;Pengfei Liu;Zheng Zhang;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Amazon;Westlake University;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.447/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04068
英摘: The rise of large language models (LLMs) has significantly influenced the quality of information in decision-making systems, leading to the prevalence of AI-generated content and challenges in detecting misinformation and managing conflicting information, or "inter-evidence conflicts." This study introduces a method for generating diverse, validated evidence conflicts to simulate real-world misinformation scenarios. We evaluate conflict detection methods, including Natural Language Inference (NLI) models, factual consistency (FC) models, and LLMs, on these conflicts (RQ1) and analyze LLMs' conflict resolution behaviors (RQ2). Our key findings include: (1) NLI and LLM models exhibit high precision in detecting answer conflicts, though weaker models suffer from low recall; (2) FC models struggle with lexically similar answer conflicts, while NLI and LLM models handle these better; and (3) stronger models like GPT-4 show robust performance, especially with nuanced conflicts. For conflict resolution, LLMs often favor one piece of conflicting evidence without justification and rely on internal knowledge if they have prior beliefs.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的兴起显著影响了决策系统中的信息质量，导致AI生成内容的盛行，并带来了检测虚假信息和管理冲突信息或“相互矛盾的证据”方面的挑战。本研究介绍了一种生成多样化且验证过的证据冲突的方法，以模拟现实世界的虚假信息场景。我们评估了包括自然语言推理（NLI）模型、事实一致性（FC）模型和LLM在内的冲突检测方法的效果（RQ1），并分析了LLM在解决冲突方面的行为（RQ2）。我们的主要发现包括：（1）NLI和LLM模型在检测答案冲突方面表现出高精度，尽管较弱的模型召回率较低；（2）FC模型难以处理语义相似的答案冲突，而NLI和LLM模型在这方面表现更好；（3）如GPT-4这样的更强模型在处理细微冲突时表现出稳健性能。在冲突解决方面，LLM往往倾向于无根据地支持某一条矛盾的证据，并依赖其内部知识来解决冲突，尤其是当它们有先验信念时。

[No.597] Fine-Grained Detection of Solidarity for Women and Migrants in 155 Years of German Parliamentary Debates
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 155年内德国议会辩论中的女性和移民团结细粒度检测
类别: 
作者: Aida Kostikova;Dominik Beese;Benjamin Paassen;Ole Pütz;Gregor Wiedemann;Steffen Eger;
机构: Bielefeld University;Technische Universität Darmstadt;Hans-Bredow-Institut;University of Mannheim;Nuremberg University of Technology;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.337/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.04359
英摘: Solidarity is a crucial concept to understand social relations in societies. In this paper, we explore fine-grained solidarity frames to study solidarity towards women and migrants in German parliamentary debates between 1867 and 2022. Using 2,864 manually annotated text snippets (with a cost exceeding 18k Euro), we evaluate large language models (LLMs) like Llama 3, GPT-3.5, and GPT-4. We find that GPT-4 outperforms other LLMs, approaching human annotation quality. Using GPT-4, we automatically annotate more than 18k further instances (with a cost of around 500 Euro) across 155 years and find that solidarity with migrants outweighs anti-solidarity but that frequencies and solidarity types shift over time. Most importantly, group-based notions of (anti-)solidarity fade in favor of compassionate solidarity, focusing on the vulnerability of migrant groups, and exchange-based anti-solidarity, focusing on the lack of (economic) contribution. Our study highlights the interplay of historical events, socio-economic needs, and political ideologies in shaping migration discourse and social cohesion. We also show that powerful LLMs, if carefully prompted, can be cost-effective alternatives to human annotation for hard social scientific tasks.
中摘: 团结是一种理解社会关系的关键概念。在这篇论文中，我们探讨了细致的团结框架，以研究1867年至2022年间德国议会辩论中对女性和移民的团结情况。我们使用了2,864条人工标注的文本片段（成本超过18000欧元），评估了大型语言模型（LLM）如Llama 3、GPT-3.5和GPT-4。结果显示，GPT-4的表现优于其他LLM，接近于人类标注的质量。利用GPT-4，我们自动标注了超过18000个进一步的实例（成本约为500欧元），跨越了155年的时间，发现对移民的团结超过了反团结，但团结频率和类型随时间变化。最重要的是，基于群体的概念（反）团结逐渐淡出，取而代之的是富有同情心的团结，关注移民群体的脆弱性，以及基于交换的反团结，关注其缺乏（经济）贡献。我们的研究突显了历史事件、社会经济需求和政治意识形态在塑造移民话语和社会凝聚力方面的作用。同时，我们也展示了如果仔细提示，强大的LLM可以成为进行艰难的社会科学任务时成本效益较高的替代人类标注的选择。

[No.598] Fishing for Magikarp: Automatically Detecting Under-trained Tokens in Large Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 自动检测大型语言模型中的欠训练 token：寻找神奇鱼
类别:

机构: Cohere;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.649/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05417
英摘: The disconnect between tokenizer creation and model training in language models allows for specific inputs, such as the infamous SolidGoldMagikarp token, to induce unwanted model behaviour. Although such `glitch tokens', tokens present in the tokenizer vocabulary but that are nearly or entirely absent during model training, have been observed across various models, a reliable method to identify and address them has been missing. We present a comprehensive analysis of Large Language Model tokenizers, specifically targeting this issue of detecting under-trained tokens. Through a combination of tokenizer analysis, model weight-based indicators, and prompting techniques, we develop novel and effective methods for automatically detecting these problematic tokens. Our findings demonstrate the prevalence of such tokens across a diverse set of models and provide insights into improving the efficiency and safety of language models.
中摘: 语言模型中分词器创建与模型训练之间的脱节使得特定输入，如臭名昭著的SolidGoldMagikarp标记，能够引发模型不希望的行为。尽管这类“故障标记”，即存在于分词器词汇表但在模型训练过程中几乎或完全缺失的标记，在各种模型中已被观察到，但识别和解决它们的有效方法一直缺失。我们对大型语言模型分词器进行了全面分析，特别针对检测未充分训练的标记这一问题。通过结合分词器分析、基于模型权重的指标以及提示技术，我们开发了新的有效方法来自动检测这些有问题的标记。我们的研究结果表明，此类标记在多种不同的模型中普遍存在，并提供了提高语言模型效率和安全性的见解。

英摘: Through the advent of pre-trained language models, there have been notable advancements in abstractive summarization systems. [No.600] Generate then Refine: Data Augmentation for Zero-shot Intent Detection
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 生成然后细化：数据扩增在零样本意图检测中的应用
类别: 
作者: I-Fan Lin;Faegheh Hasibi;Suzan Verberne;
机构: Leiden University;Radboud University;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.768/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01953
英摘: In this short paper we propose a data augmentation method for intent detection in zero-resource domains. Existing data augmentation methods rely on few labelled examples for each intent category, which can be expensive in settings with many possible intents. We use a two-stage approach: First, we generate utterances for intent labels using an open-source large language model in a zero-shot setting. Second, we develop a smaller sequence-to-sequence model (the Refiner), to improve the generated utterances. The Refiner is fine-tuned on seen domains and then applied to unseen domains. We evaluate our method by training an intent classifier on the generated data, and evaluating it on real (human) data. We find that the Refiner significantly improves the data utility and diversity over the zero-shot LLM baseline for unseen domains and over common baseline approaches. Our results indicate that a two-step approach of a generative LLM in zero-shot setting and a smaller sequence-to-sequence model can provide high-quality data for intent detection.
中摘: 在这篇简短的文章中，我们提出了一种针对零资源领域意图检测的数据增强方法。现有的数据增强方法依赖于每个意图类别少量标注的例子，而在可能的意图种类众多的情况下，这可能会很昂贵。我们采用两阶段的方法：首先，在零样本设置下，使用开源的大语言模型生成意图标签的语句；其次，开发一个较小的序列到序列模型（Refiner），以改进生成的语句。Refiner 在已见领域进行微调，然后应用于未见领域。我们通过在生成的数据上训练一个意图分类器，并在真实（人类）数据上评估该分类器来评价我们的方法。结果显示，Refiner 显著提高了未见领域数据的实用性和多样性，超过了零样本大语言模型基线和常见的基线方法。我们的研究结果表明，零样本设置下的生成型大语言模型与一个小的序列到序列模型相结合的两步方法可以为意图检测提供高质量的数据。

[No.603] How You Prompt Matters! Even Task-Oriented Constraints in Instructions Affect LLM-Generated Text Detection
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 你的提示方式很重要！即使是指令中的任务导向约束也会影响LLM生成的文本检测。
类别: 
作者: Ryuto Koike;Masahiro Kaneko;Naoaki Okazaki;
机构: Tokyo Institute of Technology;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.841/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.08369
英摘: To combat the misuse of Large Language Models (LLMs), many recent studies have presented LLM-generated-text detectors with promising performance. When users instruct LLMs to generate texts, the instruction can include different constraints depending on the user's need. However, most recent studies do not cover such diverse instruction patterns when creating datasets for LLM detection. In this paper, we reveal that even task-oriented constraints -- constraints that would naturally be included in an instruction and are not related to detection-evasion -- cause existing powerful detectors to have a large variance in detection performance. We focus on student essay writing as a realistic domain and manually create task-oriented constraints based on several factors for essay quality. Our experiments show that the standard deviation (SD) of current detector performance on texts generated by an instruction with such a constraint is significantly larger (up to an SD of 14.4 F1-score) than that by generating texts multiple times or paraphrasing the instruction. We also observe an overall trend where the constraints can make LLM detection more challenging than without them. Finally, our analysis indicates that the high instruction-following ability of LLMs fosters the large impact of such constraints on detection performance.
中摘: 为了应对大型语言模型（LLMs）的滥用，许多近期的研究提出了具有出色性能的LLM生成文本检测器。当用户指示LLM生成文本时，指令可以包含不同的约束条件，这取决于用户的具体需求。然而，大多数近期的研究在创建LLM检测数据集时，并未涵盖如此多样的指令模式。在本文中，我们揭示了即使任务导向性的约束——这些约束自然会出现在指令中且与检测规避无关——也会导致现有的强大检测器在检测性能上出现显著差异。我们专注于学生作文写作这一现实领域，并基于几个因素手动创建任务导向性的约束，以提高作文质量。我们的实验表明，在存在此类约束的情况下，当前检测器性能的标准差（SD）相较于多次生成文本或重新表述指令要大得多（最高可达14.4 F1分数）。我们也观察到一个总体趋势，即这些约束会使LLM检测变得更加困难。最后，我们的分析表明，LLM高度的任务跟随能力促进了这类约束对检测性能的巨大影响。

[No.605] Intent Detection in the Age of LLMs
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: LLM时代下的意图检测
类别: 
作者: Gaurav Arora;Shreya Jain;Srujana Merugu;
机构: Amazon;Indian Institute of Technology Jammu;
类型: Industry
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-industry.114/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01627
英摘: Intent detection is a critical component of task-oriented dialogue systems (TODS) which enables the identification of suitable actions to address user utterances at each dialog turn. Traditional approaches relied on computationally efficient supervised sentence transformer encoder models, which require substantial training data and struggle with out-of-scope (OOS) detection. The emergence of generative large language models (LLMs) with intrinsic world knowledge presents new opportunities to address these challenges. In this work, we adapt 7 SOTA LLMs using adaptive in-context learning and chain-of-thought prompting for intent detection, and compare their performance with contrastively fine-tuned sentence transformer (SetFit) models to highlight prediction quality and latency tradeoff. We propose a hybrid system using uncertainty based routing strategy to combine the two approaches that along with negative data augmentation results in achieving the best of both worlds ( i.e. within 2% of native LLM accuracy with 50% less latency). To better understand LLM OOS detection capabilities, we perform controlled experiments revealing that this capability is significantly influenced by the scope of intent labels and the size of the label space. We also introduce a two-step approach utilizing internal LLM representations, demonstrating empirical gains in OOS detection accuracy and F1-score by >5% for the Mistral-7B model.
中摘: 意图检测是面向任务的对话系统（TODS）的关键组成部分，它能够在每次对话轮次中识别出适合应对用户陈述的行动。传统的方法依赖于计算效率高的监督学习句子变换器编码模型，这些模型需要大量的训练数据，并且在处理范围外（OOE）检测方面存在困难。随着具有内在世界知识的生成型大型语言模型（LLMs）的出现，为解决这些挑战提供了新的机会。在本研究中，我们使用自适应上下文学习和链式思考提示来适应7个最新的LLM模型进行意图检测，并将它们的表现与对比微调的句子变换器（SetFit）模型进行比较，以突出预测质量和延迟之间的权衡。我们提出了一种混合系统，利用基于不确定性的路由策略结合这两种方法，从而实现两全其美（即，与原生LLM相比准确率相差不到2%，但延迟减少50%）。为了更好地理解LLM的范围外（OOE）检测能力，我们进行了受控实验，揭示了这种能力显著受到意图标签范围和标签空间大小的影响。我们还引入了一种两步方法，利用内部LLM表示，在Mistral-7B模型上实现了OOE检测准确性和F1分数超过5%的实际提升。

[No.608] Lifelong Event Detection via Optimal Transport
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 基于最优传输的终身事件检测
类别: 
作者: Viet Dao;Van-Cuong Pham;Quyen Tran;Thanh-Thien Le;Linh Van Ngo;Thien Huu Nguyen;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08905
英摘: Continual Event Detection (CED) poses a formidable challenge due to the catastrophic forgetting phenomenon, where learning new tasks (with new coming event types) hampers performance on previous ones. In this paper, we introduce a novel approach, Lifelong Event Detection via Optimal Transport (LEDOT), that leverages optimal transport principles to align the optimization of our classification module with the intrinsic nature of each class, as defined by their pre-trained language modeling.
