[No.609] Lookback Lens: Detecting and Mitigating Contextual Hallucinations in Large Language Models Using Only Attention Maps
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 回溯镜头：仅使用注意力图检测和减轻大型语言模型中的上下文幻觉
类别: 
作者: Yung-Sung Chuang;Linlu Qiu;Cheng-Yu Hsieh;Ranjay Krishna;Yoon Kim;James R. Glass;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;University of Washington;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.84/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07071
英摘: When asked to summarize articles or answer questions given a passage, large language models (LLMs) can hallucinate details and respond with unsubstantiated answers that are inaccurate with respect to the input context. This paper describes a simple approach for detecting such contextual hallucinations. We hypothesize that contextual hallucinations are related to the extent to which an LLM attends to information in the provided context versus its own generations. Based on this intuition, we propose a simple hallucination detection model whose input features are given by the ratio of attention weights on the context versus newly generated tokens (for each attention head). We find that a linear classifier based on these lookback ratio features is as effective as a richer detector that utilizes the entire hidden states of an LLM or a text-based entailment model. The lookback ratio-based detector -- Lookback Lens -- is found to transfer across tasks and even models, allowing a detector that is trained on a 7B model to be applied (without retraining) to a larger 13B model. We further apply this detector to mitigate contextual hallucinations, and find that a simple classifier-guided decoding approach is able to reduce the amount of hallucination, for example by 9.6% in the XSum summarization task.
中摘: 当被要求总结文章或根据一段给定的文本回答问题时，大型语言模型（LLMs）可能会虚构细节，并给出与输入上下文不符的不实答案。本文描述了一个简单的方法来检测这种情境性幻觉。我们假设情境性幻觉与LLM关注给定上下文信息的程度与其自身生成内容的程度有关。基于这一直觉，我们提出了一种简单的幻觉检测模型，其输入特征由每个注意头对上下文与新生成词的比例注意力权重给出。我们发现，基于这些回溯比例特征的线性分类器与利用LLM整个隐藏状态或基于文本的蕴含模型的更丰富的检测器具有同等效果。基于回溯比例的检测器——“回溯镜头”（Lookback Lens），能够跨任务甚至跨模型进行迁移，使得在7B模型上训练的检测器无需重新训练即可应用于更大的13B模型。进一步地，我们将此检测器应用于减轻情境性幻觉，发现一个简单的分类器引导的解码方法能够减少幻觉的数量，例如，在XSum摘要任务中减少了9.6%的幻觉。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

作者: Chia-Wei Tang;Ting-Chih Chen;Kiet A. Nguyen;Kazi Sajeed Mehrab;Alvi Md Ishmam;Chris Thomas;

[No.611] Machine Translation Hallucination Detection for Low and High Resource Languages using Large Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: detect
标题: 使用大型语言模型进行低资源和高资源语言的机器翻译幻觉检测
类别: 
作者: Kenza Benkirane;Laura Gongas;Shahar Pelles;Naomi Fuchs;Joshua Darmon;Pontus Stenetorp;David Ifeoluwa Adelani;Eduardo Sánchez;
机构: 
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.564/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16470
英摘: Recent advancements in massively multilingual machine translation systems have significantly enhanced translation accuracy; however, even the best performing systems still generate hallucinations, severely impacting user trust. Detecting hallucinations in Machine Translation (MT) remains a critical challenge, particularly since existing methods excel with High-Resource Languages (HRLs) but exhibit substantial limitations when applied to Low-Resource Languages (LRLs). This paper evaluates sentence-level hallucination detection approaches using Large Language Models (LLMs) and semantic similarity within massively multilingual embeddings. Our study spans 16 language directions, covering HRLs, LRLs, with diverse scripts. We find that the choice of model is essential for performance. On average, for HRLs, Llama3-70B outperforms the previous state of the art by as much as 0.16 MCC (Matthews Correlation Coefficient). However, for LRLs we observe that Claude Sonnet outperforms other LLMs on average by 0.03 MCC. The key takeaway from our study is that LLMs can achieve performance comparable or even better than previously proposed models, despite not being explicitly trained for any machine translation task. However, their advantage is less significant for LRLs.
中摘: 近年来，在大规模多语言机器翻译系统方面的进步显著提高了翻译准确性；然而，即使性能最佳的系统也会生成幻觉，严重影响了用户的信任。在机器翻译（MT）中检测幻觉仍然是一个关键挑战，尤其是在现有方法在高资源语言（HRLs）上表现出色时，应用于低资源语言（LRLs）时却存在重大限制。本文评估了使用大型语言模型（LLMs）和语义相似性在大规模多语言嵌入中进行句级幻觉检测的方法。我们的研究涵盖了16种语言方向，包括高资源语言和低资源语言，以及多种不同的书写系统。我们发现，模型的选择对性能至关重要。对于高资源语言，Llama3-70B平均比之前的状态-of-the-art高出0.16个MCC（马修相关系数）。然而，对于低资源语言，Claude Sonnet平均比其他大型语言模型高出0.03个MCC。从我们的研究中得出的关键结论是，尽管大型语言模型并未明确针对任何机器翻译任务进行训练，它们仍能实现与先前提出的模型相当甚至更好的性能。不过，它们在低资源语言上的优势并不明显。
